LiteTrajEval derives compact domain-specific rule profiles offline, then preprocesses each trajectory online, marks heuristic failure signals, serializes it under a fixed global budget, and invokes a single rubric-guided LLM judge to produce structured diagnostic reports. Evaluated on public Magentic-One-style and $τ$-bench-style trajectory datasets, LiteTrajEval improves failure-localization alignment with human annotations by roughly 20--35 percentage points on Magentic-One and up to 23 percentage points on $τ$-retail compared with AgentRx, while reducing cost by about 6$\times$ and evaluation time by more than 8$\times$. This solution has also been deployed in our enterprise agentic platform.

</details>


### [100] [Multi-Task Evolution for Zero-Shot Cross-Problem Generalization using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03316)
*Zhouliang Xie,Changliang Zhou,Genghui Li,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MECo：基于LLM的多任务进化框架，通过跨任务种群性能的“迁移差距”引导启发式交互与重组，并以覆盖度选择构建紧凑启发式集合，实现零样本跨问题泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化问题多样，人工设计启发式需大量专业知识与反复搜索；LLM虽可自动生成/精炼启发式，但启发式搜索通常依赖目标问题的评估反馈，难以仅凭源任务反馈泛化到新问题定义。因此，零样本跨问题泛化是核心挑战。

Method: MECo维护“任务条件化”的启发式种群，用基于跨任务种群性能的迁移差距（transfer gap）指导种群间交互，实现启发式的迁移与重组；再用一种补充性选择准则，奖励每个成员对源任务组合的额外覆盖，从而构建紧凑启发式集合；该集合直接应用于目标任务，无需进一步搜索或自适应。

Result: 在车辆路径（VRP）与柔性作业车间调度（FJSP）的32个问题变体上，相同预算下MECo的平均成本低于8个自动启发式设计（AHD）基线；在域外（OOD）问题上，于两类问题族中均优于最强基线；将MECo框架与不同AHD方法结合，可同时提升其ID与OOD表现。

Conclusion: MECo通过多任务进化与迁移差距引导的种群交互，实现了跨问题零样本泛化，并在多个问题族和多种AHD方法上展现稳定增益，说明其框架具有跨方法的通用有效性。

Abstract: Designing effective heuristics for diverse combinatorial optimization problems requires substantial expertise and repeated search. Large language models (LLMs) automate heuristic generation and refinement, but heuristic search typically depends on evaluation feedback from the problem being optimized. Generalizing to new problem definitions using only source-task feedback therefore remains a central challenge. We introduce MECo, an LLM-driven multi-task evolutionary framework for zero-shot cross-problem generalization. MECo maintains task-conditioned heuristic populations and uses a transfer gap based on cross-task population performance to guide their interactions. These interactions enable the transfer and recombination of heuristics. A complementary selection criterion then constructs a compact heuristic set by rewarding each member's additional coverage of source combinations. The selected set is applied to target problems without further search or adaptation. Experiments on 32 problem variants across vehicle routing (VRP) and flexible job-shop scheduling (FJSP) show that MECo achieves the lowest mean costs compared with eight automated heuristic design (AHD) baselines under the same budgets. On out-of-domain problems, it outperforms the strongest baseline in each family. Moreover, integrating the framework of MECo with different AHD methods improves their ID and OOD performance in both families, supporting its effectiveness across different methods.

</details>


### [101] [Refinement Buys Intelligibility, Search Buys Identity: What Test-Time Compute Buys in Masked-Diffusion TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03320)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Ayush Pratap Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在掩码扩散编解码TTS上，模型深度（3-133M参数）与推理细化步数（T=1-16）对可懂度与说话人身份的增益并不对等：细化可闭合86.2%可懂度差距，但仅闭合46.4%身份差距（1.86倍非对称），且该差距在加长训练后衰减但不反转；深度与步数不可互换，剩余身份缺陷62%来自编解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式TTS同时消耗两类算力：模型深度（参数）与细化步数（推理预算）。作者质疑二者是否对所有能力等比例缩放，即深度与推理步数是否可相互替代，还是分别作用于不同瓶颈，从而决定算力应投向何处。

Method: 训练15个掩码扩散编解码TTS模型，深度覆盖19-133M参数、每档3个随机种子，数据为2,000小时语音；推理时扫描细化步数 T∈[1,16]。在174名留出说话人上做零样本评测：以ASR词错误率衡量可懂度，以说话人验证衡量身份一致性。对照实测下限分析增益闭合比例；用3倍、6倍训练调度重训检验稳健性；用Best-of-K搜索检验能否找回身份；用 B(d)B(T) 可分离拟合与替代模型比较（ΔAICc）；并做组件分析定位身份缺陷来源。

Result: 细化闭合86.2%的可懂度差距，仅闭合46.4%的身份差距，1.86倍非对称在多种误差指标下稳健。加长训练使差距由1.84降至1.36再降至1.23倍，但未反转：可懂度随步数饱和，身份仍持续提升。Best-of-K搜索在四个独立编码器上取得64.6-79.0%胜率，可弥补细化无法改善的身份。可分离 B(d)B(T) 拟合显著优于替代模型（ΔAICc=+69.3）。62%的剩余身份缺陷位于编解码器而非生成器。

Conclusion: 细化步数与模型深度针对不同瓶颈，二者不可互换，应分别独立优化；身份质量的主要剩余限制在编解码器，而推理端可通过Best-of-K搜索部分补偿。这为TTS算力分配提供了依据。

Abstract: Diffusion language models for text-to-speech combine two forms of computation: model depth (parameters) and refinement steps (inference budget). We ask whether they scale equally across capabilities. We train 15 masked-diffusion codec TTS models varying depth (19-133M parameters, 3 seeds) on 2,000 hours of speech and sweep refinement steps T in [1,16] at inference, measuring zero-shot synthesis via ASR word error rate (intelligibility) and speaker verification (identity) on 174 held-out speakers. Against measured floors, refinement closes 86.2% of the intelligibility range but only 46.4% of the identity range - a 1.86x asymmetry robust across multiple error metrics. Retraining at 3x and 6x schedule attenuates but does not reverse this gap (1.84 to 1.36 to 1.23x), because intelligibility saturates with steps while identity continues improving. Best-of-K search recovers speaker identity where refinement fails, with 64.6-79.0% win rates across four independent encoders. Depth and steps are not interchangeable: separable B(d)B(T) fits significantly better (Delta AICc=+69.3) than substitution models. Analysis shows 62% of remaining identity deficit lies in the codec, not the generator. We conclude that refinement and depth target different bottlenecks and should be optimized separately.

</details>


### [102] [Preserving Mathematical Reasoning in Compressed Diffusion Language Models via Trajectory-Aware Low-Rank Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03326)
*Tian Liang,Zishan Shao,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作针对扩散语言模型（dLLM）压缩中校准状态与推理状态不匹配的问题，提出轨迹感知的低秩压缩目标与Traj-MC估计方法，在同等压缩预算下更好地保持数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM压缩存在校准-推理不匹配：低秩压缩的校准通常在干净、完全可见的激活上进行，而推理时实际经过的是部分掩码的中间状态。由此引出两个问题：一是当逼近质量在轨迹分布状态上度量时，低秩最优性如何刻画；二是校准状态的选择是否影响压缩后数学推理能力的保持。

Method: 作者在腐蚀程度（corruption levels）与掩码实现（masking realizations）上构建轨迹感知的低秩目标函数；为高效估计该目标，提出Traj-MC，通过蒙特卡洛采样估计轨迹二阶矩，并具备采样状态下的精确最优性与总体一致性保证。

Result: 在匹配的压缩预算下，轨迹感知校准相比干净校准，在生成轨迹上提升了重构质量，并在数学推理基准上显著保留了更多数学推理能力。

Conclusion: 结果将轨迹感知低秩最优性与dLLM压缩后保留的推理能力联系起来，表明校准状态的选择对压缩后的推理保持至关重要。

Abstract: Diffusion language model (dLLM) compression faces a known challenge because calibration is typically performed on clean, fully visible activations, whereas inference traverses partially masked intermediate states. For low-rank compression, this raises two questions. First, can low-rank optimality still be characterized when approximation quality is measured over trajectory-distributed states, and second, does the choice of calibration states affect mathematical reasoning preservation under compression? We address these questions by formulating a trajectory-aware low-rank objective over corruption levels and masking realizations. To estimate this objective efficiently, we propose Traj-MC, which estimates the trajectory second moment through Monte Carlo sampling and yields exact sampled-state optimality and population consistency. Under matched compression budgets, trajectory-aware calibration improves reconstruction over the generation trajectory and preserves substantially more mathematical reasoning than clean calibration on mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Our results connect trajectory-aware low-rank optimality to the reasoning capability retained after dLLM compression. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/traj-mc.git.

</details>


### [103] [ReFract: Benchmarking Perspective Awareness in Language Model Agents with Text World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03356)
*Hainiu Xu,Vítor N. Lourenço,Mohnish Dubey,Yunfei Bai,Yulan He,Caroline Catmur,Aline Paes,Marco Caserta,Akash Chandrayan,Luca D'Angelo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReFract 基准，用于评估 LLM agent 的"视角感知"能力，即在工业故障排查等高风险场景中，agent 需根据用户角色调整行动与信息提供。150 条专家验证条目显示，SOTA LLM 最多仅解决 69% 任务，且超半数轨迹包含越权行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 正被部署于工业维护、设备故障排查等高风险场景，用户角色多样。Agent 行动会作用于真实物理设备，错误可能导致不可逆的设备损坏、生产损失或人员伤害。因此 agent 必须根据用户角色校准其行动与信息，尊重角色的知识边界与能力边界。然而现有基准普遍忽略这一需求：agent 需要推断角色意图，并仅通过该角色合法可用的工具行动，作者将此能力定义为"视角感知"（Perspective Awareness）。

Method: 作者构建 ReFract 基准，包含 150 条经专家验证的条目。条目以匿名化的领域支持对话查询为基础，据此构建"文本世界模型"（Text World Models）来模拟 agent 的作业环境，并组装具备视角感知的行动轨迹。任务设计要求 agent 对同一查询根据用户角色作出不同的响应。

Result: SOTA LLM 最多仅能解决 69% 的任务，且超过 50% 的轨迹包含试图执行违背视角（越权）行为的尝试。

Conclusion: ReFract 揭示"视角感知"是 agent 评估中一个独立且尚未解决的维度，推动研究者开发不仅校准"如何行动"、更要校准"为谁行动"的 agent。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as industrial maintenance and equipment fault troubleshooting, where workers occupy a variety of roles. A capable agent must therefore act in a way that is calibrated to user's role: taking actions and providing information that respect the role's knowledge and capability boundaries. Unlike coding, where mistakes are usually recoverable, agent responses in these settings are enacted on physical equipment, and can therefore cause irreversible equipment damage, production loss, or personnel harm. Existing benchmarks, however, largely overlook the need for agents to infer what a role intends and acting only through tools that role may legitimately use, a capability which we term Perspective Awareness. To this end, we introduce ReFract, a benchmark of 150 expert-validated entries in which an agent must act differently in response to the same query depending on user's role. Entries of ReFract are grounded in anonymized queries from domain support conversations, against which we construct Text World Models that simulate the agent's operating environments and assemble perspective-aware action trajectories. State-of-the-art LLMs solve at most 69% of the tasks with more than 50% of their trajectories contain attempts of taking perspective-violating actions. ReFract exposes perspective awareness as a distinct, largely unsolved axis of agent evaluation and motivates agents that calibrate not just how to act, but for whom.

</details>


### [104] [Geometry Meets Physics: Data-Efficient Pre-Training for Unstructured Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03363)
*Luis Medrano-Navarro,Giacomo Baldan,Qiang Liu,Benjamin Holzschuh,Jan Hagnberger,Mathias Niepert,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需磁盘数据的预训练框架，用于非结构化3D几何上的PDE神经代理模型，稳态问题采用几何驱动策略，瞬态问题采用物理驱动的在线合成数据策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化3D几何上的PDE神经代理模型泛化能力差，且大规模训练数据生成成本高；传统基于海量预计算数据的预训练方法在计算和数据效率上均不足。

Method: 提出无磁盘数据预训练框架：针对稳态问题，利用内在形状描述子进行几何驱动的表示学习；针对瞬态问题，采用基于在线生成合成PDE数据的物理驱动方法，实现可扩展预训练。

Result: 在多组实验中，该方法在微调阶段实现更快收敛、更高数据效率和更高精度，尤其在真实低数据场景下优势明显。

Conclusion: 该方法为大规模仿真提供了一条面向数据高效神经仿真器的实用路径。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models for Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) on unstructured 3D geometries are often limited by poor generalization and the high cost of generating large-scale training datasets. Consequently, pre-training on massive datasets of related PDE dynamics has emerged as a critical alternative to enhance the robustness and scalability of these models. However, this strategy is neither compute- nor data-efficient, as it relies on massive pre-computed data that is very costly to generate. In this work, we introduce a disk-data-free pre-training framework tailored to both steady-state and transient regimes. For steady-state problems, we propose a geometry-driven strategy that leverages intrinsic shape descriptors to learn representations of complex 3D domains. For transient problems, we introduce a physics-driven approach based on online generation of synthetic PDE data, enabling scalable pre-training without reliance on expensive datasets. Across multiple experiments, our approach achieves faster convergence, greater data efficiency, and higher accuracy during fine-tuning, particularly under realistic low-data regimes. This methodology provides a practical pathway toward data-efficient neural emulators for large-scale simulations.

</details>


### [105] [Multilingual GSM-Symbolic: What determines capability transfer across languages?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03367)
*Kenneth Enevoldsen,Riley Herchert,Sofie Mosegaard,Dan Saattrup Smart,Simon Enni,Isaac Chung,Sofie Bruun,Ayush Sunil Munot,Max Müller-Eberstein,Adnan El-Assadi,Elisa Bassignana,Gianluca Barmina,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Iben Nyholm Debess,Linda Freienthal,Lukas Galke Poech,Mike Zhang,Nicolas Legrand,Vladimir Salnikov,Yevhen Kostiuk,Zafar Hussain,Sagandeep Kaur,Agnes Toftgård,Marie Mattson,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出可扩展的多语言数学数据集 Multilingual GSM-Symbolic（3万条匹配问答对、15种语言），量化跨语言能力迁移的决定因素，并揭示模型规模、语言资源水平、推理与类型学距离的影响差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对一种语言中获得的能力如何迁移到另一种语言、以及何种因素主导这一迁移知之甚少；现有评测依赖不可比且易饱和的数据集，且很少联合考察迁移的决定因素。识别迁移的预测因子可避免对所有语言对进行穷举评测，并帮助开发者针对限制低资源语言表现的因素进行优化。

Method: 构建 Multilingual GSM-Symbolic：一个可扩展的多语言数学数据集，包含30,000条题目匹配的问答对，覆盖15种语言；采用符号化模板防止过拟合、保证泛化，可从单个样本生成数百万高质量变体。基于该数据集联合估计能力的主要决定因素及其交互效应。

Result: 量化出最大决定因素：模型规模（β=1.77）、语言资源水平（β=0.77）、推理（β=0.67）、类型学距离（β=-0.25）。这些因素可相互换算：32B模型在马拉地语上的表现相当于10B模型在英语上的表现。模型规模与推理能缩小低资源与高资源语言间的差距（β=-0.27与β=-0.20），但对类型学上距离较远的语言几乎没有效果。整体分析框架解释92%的语言间变异，但仅解释23%的模型×语言变异；对未见语言的性能预测误差在6.0pp以内（r=.96），加入目标语言仅10个模板的测量后可降至4.19pp。

Conclusion: 跨语言能力迁移主要由模型规模、语言资源水平、推理和类型学距离决定；模型规模与推理可有效缓解低资源语言的性能差距，但难以弥合类型学距离带来的差距。该框架能以极少下游数据实现较准确的未见语言性能预测，对模型开发者具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We understand little about how capabilities acquired in one language carry over to another, or what governs this transfer: evaluations rely on incomparable, saturation-prone datasets and rarely examine its determinants jointly. Identifying what predicts transfer would let us avoid exhaustive evaluation across all language pairs and let developers target the factors that limit performance in low-resource languages. To evaluate cross-lingual capability transfer, we introduce Multilingual GSM-Symbolic, an extensible multilingual mathematical dataset covering 30,000 item-matched question-answer pairs and spanning 15 languages. It utilises symbolic templates to prevent overfitting and ensure generalisation by allowing generation of millions of high-quality variations from a single sample. Using Multilingual GSM-Symbolic, we quantify the largest determinants of capability as model size ($β= 1.77$), language resource level ($β= 0.77$), reasoning ($β= 0.67$) and typological distance ($β= -0.25$). This joint estimation allows these determinants to be expressed in terms of one another: a 32B model evaluated in Marathi performs like a 10B model in English. Our findings have important implications for model developers, showing that model size and reasoning narrow the performance gap between low- and high-resource languages ($β= -0.27$ and $β= -0.20$, respectively), while similar levers have little or no effect on typologically distant languages.
  Overall, our analysis framework explains 92% of between-language variation, but only 23% of the model-by-language variation, and predicts a model's performance on an unseen language within 6.0pp (r=.96). Incorporating measurements from just 10 templates in the target language reduces this to 4.19pp, enabling reasonable estimates of performance with little or no downstream dataset.

</details>


### [106] [CVE2AP: Automated Generation of PDDL-Encoded Attack Paths via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03383)
*Lin Cui,Vincenzo Scotti,Raffaela Mirandola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CVE2AP，利用LLM从自然语言CVE描述自动生成PDDL编码的攻击路径，结合结构化提示与错误反馈迭代修正机制，实现高语法、可解性与语义质量的自动化AP建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击路径(AP)建模是网络安全分析的基础，而PDDL被广泛用于将AP编码为形式化、机器可验证的表示以支持漏洞利用与攻击演进的自动推理。然而现有AP建模方法主要依赖专家手工构建，难以扩展且无法跟上快速演变的网络威胁。LLM凭借其预训练知识与推理能力，有望将威胁情报自动转化为形式化表示，因此需要研究基于LLM的自动化AP生成方法。

Method: 提出CVE2AP方法：以自然语言CVE描述为输入，利用LLM自动生成PDDL编码的攻击路径。方法采用结构化提示，并引入错误反馈机制，基于规划器报告的语法错误与可解性错误迭代精化生成结果。在多个LLM与多种生成配置下进行系统性实证评估，从语法、可解性、语义三个维度衡量生成质量，并统计token消耗与生成时间。

Result: 实验表明CVE2AP能有效生成高质量PDDL编码攻击路径，最高达到86.9%语法正确率、78.6%可解性与93.1%语义正确率（基于LLM-as-expert评估）。其中GPT-5.5在质量-成本权衡上表现最佳，错误反馈机制带来最稳定的质量提升。

Conclusion: CVE2AP验证了LLM自动化生成PDDL攻击路径的可行性，通过结构化提示与错误反馈显著提升生成质量，为可扩展、可跟随威胁演进的AP建模提供了新途径，并揭示了不同LLM在质量与成本间的权衡特性。

Abstract: Attack Path (AP) modeling is fundamental to cybersecurity analysis, where the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL) has been widely adopted to encode APs into formal and machine-verifiable representations for automated reasoning about vulnerability exploitation, attack progression, and their potential impacts. However, existing AP modeling approaches largely rely on expert-driven manual construction, limiting their scalability and ability to keep pace with rapidly evolving cyber threats. Large language models (LLMs) are promising candidates, as their extensive pre-trained knowledge and reasoning capabilities enable them to interpret and transform threat intelligence into formal representations. In this paper, we propose \textbf{CVE2AP}, an LLM-based approach for automatically generating PDDL-encoded attack paths from natural language CVE (Common Vulnerability Exposure) descriptions. CVE2AP leverages structured prompting and incorporates an error-feedback mechanism that iteratively refines the generated paths using planner-reported syntactic and solvability errors. We conduct a systematic empirical evaluation across multiple LLMs and generation configurations, assessing generation quality across syntactic, solvability and semantic dimensions, together with token consumption and generation time. The results demonstrate that CVE2AP effectively generates high-quality PDDL-encoded attack paths, achieving up to 86.9\% syntax correctness, 78.6\% solvability, and 93.1\% semantic correctness under LLM-as-expert evaluation, while \texttt{GPT-5.5} offers the best quality-cost trade-off and error feedback yields the most consistent quality improvement.

</details>


### [107] [Benchmarking Candidate Coverage in Typed Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03387)
*Jiawen Lu,Tongtong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出配对候选覆盖基准协议，评估Laya与Jev在四个数据集上对"缺失参考答案"的识别与拒答行为，发现模型在检测缺失答案与误拒有效选项之间存在严重权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型化决策模型在请求时返回选项中的选择或分布，在其选项完整时的准确率并不能说明模型是否能识别参考答案缺失，或是否会避免拒绝有效候选，因此需要专门的拒答行为评估协议。

Method: 提出配对候选覆盖基准协议：将"存在/缺失"答案配对，保持候选数量、描述、成员与顺序一致，仅改变名称变体；对Laya与Jev在AG News、DBpedia、Emotion、TREC四个数据集上评测。使用300条校准文本与589条测试文本，每模型产生23,932条预测，并采用仅基于校准集的none-score阈值、能力条件分析、概率-精度敏感性与接口审计。

Result: 原生拒答行为差异显著：TREC五个候选且使用自然名称时，Laya检出97.2%的缺失答案案例，但误拒69.7%的存在对照；Jev分别为24.8%与0.0%。仅用校准none-score阈值后，两者变为33.9%/3.7%与45.0%/1.8%。DBpedia上Jev的覆盖度分数AUROC较高，支持更强工作点；Emotion上两模型完整集准确率均较弱。

Conclusion: 该初始基准表明分类、分数排序与拒答策略需要分别测量；结果为描述性且仅限参考答案缺失情形，不能说明自然范围外泛化、因果机制或提出新的拒答方法。

Abstract: Typed decision models return choices or distributions over answer options supplied at request time. Accuracy with complete options does not establish whether a model recognizes that a reference answer is missing or avoids rejecting valid candidates. We present a paired candidate-coverage benchmark protocol and an initial evaluation of Laya and Jev across AG News, DBpedia, Emotion, and TREC. The models receive identical frozen texts and requests: 300 calibration and 589 test texts yield 23,932 predictions per model. Present/absent pairs match ordinary candidate count, and name variants preserve descriptions, members, and order. Native rejection behavior differs sharply: at five TREC candidates with natural names, Laya detects 97.2% of missing-answer cases but falsely rejects 69.7% of present controls; Jev's rates are 24.8% and 0.0%. Calibration-only none-score thresholds change these rates to 33.9%/3.7% and 45.0%/1.8%, respectively. On DBpedia, Jev's high coverage-score AUROC supports a stronger operating point, whereas both models have weak complete-set accuracy on Emotion. Competence-conditioned analysis, probability-precision sensitivity, and interface audits show why classification, score ranking, and rejection policies need separate measurement. This initial benchmark is descriptive and limited to reference-label omission; it does not establish natural out-of-scope generalization, causal mechanisms, or a new rejection method.

</details>


### [108] [Becoming Suspicious Across Borders: Algorithmic Extraterritoriality and AI-Driven Financial Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03425)
*Georgios Pavlidis,Savvas Chatzichristofis,Eleni Gavriil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析AI驱动的金融监控如何重构反洗钱/反恐融资中的"怀疑"概念，提出"算法域外性"（algorithmic extraterritoriality）这一概念，将监管权力理解为经由数据基础设施中介而非正式司法管辖主张的形式，并指出被数据化的怀疑主体面临问责与可争议性的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，怀疑被理解为可识别司法管辖区内人类行为者的情景化法律判断；但随着AI成为金融监控的组成部分，怀疑越来越多地通过数据驱动过程生成，这一转变不仅是认识论的，也是空间性的，使传统理解不再充分，需要新的概念工具来分析监管权力与问责问题。

Method: 概念分析与理论建构：通过论证AI驱动金融监控依托跨国数据基础设施运行，提出并发展"算法域外性"概念，将监管权力界定为数据基础设施中介的形式，并分析个体如何被构成为数据化的怀疑主体。

Result: 监管触达不再取决于行为发生地，而取决于该行为是否在数据系统中变得可见；个体通过分散且不透明的评估过程被治理，怀疑变得更难以定位、解释和质疑，从而对问责性与可争议性构成挑战。

Conclusion: 需要以"算法域外性"重新理解AML/CFT中的怀疑与监管权力，关注数据基础设施中介的规训机制，并应对算法化怀疑带来的问责与争议困境。

Abstract: Suspicion is an important, yet elusive concept in anti-money laundering and counter-terrorist financing (AML/CFT), which allows for intervention below the threshold of proof. In its traditional form, suspicion can be understood as a situated legal judgement by human actors within identifiable jurisdictions. It is argued that this understanding is no longer adequate. As artificial intelligence (AI) becomes an integral part of financial surveillance, suspicion is increasingly produced through data-driven processes. This transformation is epistemic, but also spatial. Since AI-driven financial surveillance operates through transnational data infrastructures, regulatory reach is less a matter of where conduct occurs than a question of whether such conduct becomes visible within data systems. This article develops the concept of algorithmic extraterritoriality, understood as a form of regulatory power mediated by data infrastructures rather than formal assertions of jurisdiction. Moreover, since individuals are increasingly constituted as datafied subjects of suspicion, they are rendered governable through dispersed and opaque processes of evaluation. This constitutes a challenge for accountability and contestability because suspicion becomes more difficult to locate, explain or contest.

</details>


### [109] [Jumping the Line: Exploiting Length Predictions in LLM Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03430)
*Yuyang Dai,Rana Shahout,Mahmood Sharif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JIL，一种针对基于长度预测的LLM调度器的攻击方法，通过优化对抗后缀使轻量级输出长度探针低估请求长度，从而获取更高调度优先级。实验显示预测长度最多降低83.4%，对抗请求端到端平均加速最高1.53倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务中请求调度对降低完成时间至关重要。基于长度的策略（如SJF）偏好短请求，但输出长度在生成前未知，实际调度器依赖预测长度。这种依赖预测的机制存在被操纵的隐患，作者因此研究该调度信号的可攻击性。

Method: 以TRAIL为案例，JIL优化对抗后缀，使轻量级输出长度探针低估请求长度，从而在调度中获得更高优先级。在两个数据集、四种LLM、多种请求画像与部署配置下进行评测，并考察调度器端防御措施。

Result: 预测输出长度最多降低83.4%；端到端服务实验中对抗请求平均完成时间最多加快1.53倍。预测长度的下降幅度显著大于实际输出长度的变化，揭示调度器估计值与实际请求规模间的失配。响应效用因模型和任务而异，体现调度优势与响应质量间的权衡。将长度预测分组为粗粒度区间可削弱JIL的调度优势并缓解对良性请求的延迟。

Conclusion: JIL表明基于预测长度的LLM调度器易受信号操纵攻击，攻击者可在较小影响实际输出的情况下获取显著调度优势，且存在调度收益与响应质量的权衡；粗粒度区间分组是一种有效的缓解防御。

Abstract: Efficient request scheduling is increasingly important for reducing completion time in large language model (LLM) serving. Size-based policies such as Shortest Job First prioritize shorter requests, but output lengths are unknown before generation, so practical schedulers rely on predicted lengths. We introduce JIL, an attack on prediction-based LLM schedulers that manipulates the scheduling signal to obtain higher priority and reduce completion time. Using TRAIL as a case study, JIL optimizes an adversarial suffix that causes a lightweight output-length probe to underestimate a request's length. We evaluate JIL on two datasets and four LLMs across varied request profiles and deployment configurations. JIL reduces predicted output lengths by up to 83.4 percent, and adversarial requests complete up to 1.53 times faster on average in end-to-end serving experiments. The reduction in predicted length is substantially larger than the change in actual output length, revealing a mismatch between the scheduler's estimate and the request's realized size. Response utility varies across models and tasks, exposing a trade-off between scheduling advantage and response quality. We also evaluate scheduler-side defenses and find that grouping length predictions into coarse intervals reduces JIL's scheduling advantage and mitigates delays to benign requests.

</details>


### [110] [A Near-Zero Monitor Readout Is Not Evidence of Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03458)
*Zhe Zhou,Tianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 监控器读数值低并不能证明其实现了行为控制；离线判别力与低对齐读数均不足以作为证据，必须辅以带外行为检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带可验证奖励的后训练会诱发reward hacking，因此人们希望把监控器嵌入训练目标而非仅用于离线审计。但“监控读数低”常被当作干预有效的证据，本文质疑这一推断是否成立。

Method: 在代码生成环境中（其主要exploit在推理轨迹开头即可用），针对三类通过同一离线门槛的监控器训练策略：一个域内激活探针，以及两个以策略多早锁定自身最终答案为条件的惩罚项。比较同一监控族内低读数与行为结果的关系，并进行文本层面分析。

Result: 探针分数从首个记录训练步起就处于数值下限；所有前缀训练运行的训练分数中位数在终点为零。同一固定配置下、具有相同零中位训练分数的前缀训练运行，仅因随机种子的不同，就从hacking占比低的混合状态跨越到近乎纯粹的reward hacking。所有探针运行都落入hacking状态，但其下限读数源于探针验证位置与训练读取位置不匹配，而非第二种歧义。文本分析发现一种前缀失败模式：泛化的规划与填充外壳把exploit推迟到截断点之后，却未将其从最终输出中消除。

Conclusion: 低测量承诺度无法区分“低hacking占比”与“对exploit的延迟承诺”。离线判别力与低监控对齐读数都不是行为控制的充分证据，必须进行带外行为检查。本文刻画的是终点读数，而非其演化过程。

Abstract: Post-training with verifiable rewards can induce reward hacking, motivating the use of monitors within the training objective rather than solely for offline auditing. We show that a low monitor readout does not identify whether such an intervention controls behavior. In a code-generation environment whose dominant exploit is available at the start of the reasoning trace, we train policies against three monitors that pass the same offline gate: an in-domain activation probe and two penalties conditioned on how early the policy commits to its own final answer. The probe score is at its numerical floor from the first recorded training step, and the trained-score median is zero for every prefix-trained run at the endpoint. These readouts estimate different quantities, and we do not compare their scales; within each monitor family, however, low values do not establish behavioral control. Within one fixed configuration, prefix-trained runs with the same zero-median trained score range, by seed alone, from a mixed regime with a low hacking share to near-pure reward hacking. All probe runs reach the hacking regime, but their floor-level readout reflects a mismatch between the position where the probe was validated and the position where it was read during training, not a second instance of this ambiguity. Text-level analysis identifies a prefix failure mode: generic planning and filler shells postpone the exploit past the cut without eliminating it from the final output. Low measured commitment therefore does not distinguish a low hacking share from delayed commitment to the exploit. Offline discrimination and low monitor-aligned readouts are insufficient evidence of behavioral control; an out-of-band behavioral check is required. We characterize the endpoint readout, not its evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/zhezhou1106/spoof-cost.

</details>


### [111] [Efficient Reasoning Training Does Not Always Harm CoT Faithfulness and Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03509)
*Samuel Lewis-Lim,Xingwei Tan,Mario Sanger,Zhixue Zhao,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了高效推理训练（压缩思维链长度）对思维链忠实性与可监控性的影响，发现二者受长度压力的影响不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链（CoT）推理让人类可以审视大模型的作答过程并监督其行为，但带来更高的推理成本，因此需要训练模型用更少token解题。然而，人们担心此类训练会导致模型跳过关键推理步骤，使CoT不再忠实反映模型决策。由于不同效率方法对CoT施加长度压力的方式各异，且不同任务中忠实解释决策所需token量不同，这种问题是否以及何时发生尚不清楚。

Method: 作者用三种以不同方式施加长度压力的方法对大模型进行微调：固定生成预算、按样本的长度目标、以及组相对长度奖励。随后评估高效推理对两项指标的影响：忠实性（CoT在相关输入上反映模型决策的程度）与可监控性（CoT是否揭示输入干预改变输出）。

Result: 高效推理对忠实性与可监控性影响不同。在多数设置下忠实性下降，主要原因是训练后模型的一致性变差；而可监控性更稳健，即便CoT显著变短，模型仍会承认输入对其答案的影响。

Conclusion: 在提升推理效率的同时，CoT忠实性普遍受损而可监控性相对稳健。评价高效推理方法时需区分忠实性与可监控性，并应关注模型一致性下降这一导致忠实性损失的主要原因。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning allows humans to inspect how large language models reach their answers, and oversee model behaviour. This reasoning comes at an increased inference cost, motivating efficient methods that train models to solve tasks using fewer tokens. However, a common concern is that such training may cause models to skip important reasoning steps, so the CoT no longer faithfully reflects the model's decision. It is unclear whether or when this occurs in practice, since different efficiency methods apply length pressure to models' CoT in distinct ways, and faithfully explaining a model's decision takes more tokens on some tasks than others. To understand these dynamics, we fine-tune a variety of models with three methods that apply length pressure differently, namely a fixed generation budget, a per-example length target, and a group-relative length reward. We evaluate how efficient reasoning affects CoT faithfulness (i.e., how well the CoT reflects model decisions on related inputs) and monitorability (i.e., whether the CoT reveals when input interventions alter the output). We find that it affects faithfulness and monitorability differently. Faithfulness falls in most settings, primarily because the trained models are less consistent. Monitorability is more robust, as models keep acknowledging the influence on their answer even when the CoT is much shorter.

</details>


### [112] [Reasoning Models Are Accurate but Unsound on Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03519)
*Arman Behnam,Binghui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CERTID，一个形式化因果可识别性评测流水线，通过ID算法认证查询是否可识别，并用已知干预分布的SCM验证公式等价性，用于评估推理模型在因果识别任务上的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在判断因果效应能否从观测数据中恢复时存在两类失败：拒绝可识别查询，或对不可识别查询给出答案。后者更严重，因为没有任何观测数据能验证其声称的公式。而现有评测缺乏可证明不可识别的查询，且字符串匹配无法接受等价形式的正确公式。

Method: CERTID构建形式化识别流水线：使用健全且完备的ID算法认证效应在给定图与查询下是否可识别；通过干预分布已知的结构因果模型（SCM）验证返回公式；并发展理论结果以缓解结构泄漏、修复不可识别查询、建立评分保证。在1200个认证实例（4至50个顶点）上评测Gemini Flash、Gemini Pro、GPT5.5三个前沿推理模型。

Result: 准确率是可靠性的糟糕代理：在相同实例上，不可识别查询的虚假声称率在不同模型间相差达十七倍。模型对训练快照之后生成的图，判定可识别性的准确率为97–100%。

Conclusion: 仅靠准确率无法衡量模型在因果识别中的健全性，需要可证明不可识别的查询与形式化等价验证。CERTID提供了认证流程、验证器与逐实例记录，为因果推理模型评测建立更严格基准。

Abstract: A reasoning model asked whether a causal effect is recoverable from observational data can fail in two ways: it refuses an identifiable query or answers a nonidentifiable one. The latter is more consequential, as no observational data can validate the claimed formula. Measuring this failure requires queries that are provably non-identifiable, which prior evaluations lack, and grading that accepts correct formulas in any equivalent form, which string matching cannot provide. We build CERTID, a formal identification pipeline that addresses both limitations. CERTID uses the sound and complete causal identification algorithm ID to certify whether an effect is identifiable from a given graph and query, and verifies returned formulas against structural causal models whose interventional distributions are known exactly. CERTID further develops theoretical results to mitigate structural leakage, repair non-identifiable queries, and establish grading guarantees. We evaluate three frontier reasoning models (Gemini Flash, Gemini Pro, and GPT5.5) on 1,200 certified instances spanning 4 to 50 vertices. Accuracy proves a poor proxy for soundness: on identical instances, the false-claim rate on non-identifiable queries varies by seventeen-fold across models. We also find that models decide identifiability with 97-100% accuracy on graphs generated after the strongest model's training snapshot. Instances, the certification procedure, the verifier, and per-instance records are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/certid-D718.

</details>


### [113] [From Benchmarks to Production: A Text-to-SQL System for Complex Financial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03524)
*Arijit Sehanobish,Bruno Gomes Coelho,Guillaume Michel,Sophia Zhi,Valerie Faucon-Morin,Kristen Howell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLINT是一个面向生产金融数据库的领域专用Text-to-SQL系统，通过查询概念动态解析、相似查询模板检索与基于外键链的Schema链接三大组件，解决不透明整数键、多表连接等难点，在两个共359题的生产级金融Schema数据集上超越同等LLM支撑的各类SOTA基线，并已上线部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用Text-to-SQL系统在Spider、BIRD等学术基准上表现优异，因为这些基准的Schema较浅、列值通常为人类可读字符串。但在生产金融数据库中，概念以不透明的整数键存储而非可读文本，即便是简单查询也需多表连接，过滤谓词还引用不透明ID，导致通用方法准确率不足50%，存在明显落地差距。

Method: FLINT由三个关键组件构成：(1) 查找代理(lookup agent)，将自然语言概念动态解析为与具体问题相关的参考表约束；(2) 基于嵌入的检索，从紧凑的专家撰写查询模板库中召回结构相似的模板；(3) Schema链接，通过遍历外键链将大表Schema裁剪为相关子集，而非仅依赖名称相似度。

Result: 在两个共计359个问题的生产金融Schema数据集上进行评测，FLINT在使用相同LLM的前提下优于多种当前最先进的基线方法；系统已作为金融数据检索服务的一部分在生产环境部署。

Conclusion: 面向金融领域定制化设计（概念解析、模板检索、外键链驱动的Schema剪枝）能有效弥合通用Text-to-SQL在生产级不透明键、多连接场景下的性能差距，并已实现工业级落地。

Abstract: General-purpose Text-to-SQL systems achieve strong performance on academic benchmarks like Spider and BIRD, where schemas are relatively shallow and column values are often human readable. In production financial databases, where concepts are stored as opaque integer keys rather than human-readable strings, these methods fall below 50%, as even simple queries require multiple joins and filter predicates reference opaque IDs. We present Financial LINking Text-to-SQL (FLINT), a domain-specialized Text-to-SQL system that closes this gap through three key components: (1) a lookup agent that dynamically resolves natural-language concepts to question-specific reference table constraints, (2) embedding-based retrieval of structurally similar query templates from a compact, expert-authored bank, and (3) schema linking that prunes a large table schema to the relevant subset by traversing foreign-key chains, rather than relying on name similarity alone. We evaluate on two datasets totaling 359 questions over production financial schemas. FLINT outperforms various state-of-the-art baselines using the same LLM. The system is deployed in production as part of a financial data retrieval service.

</details>


### [114] [Recursive Harness Self-Improvement for Frontier Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03548)
*Wenlong Zhang,Zhengbo Jiao,Chenxu Zhang,Lekang Jiang,SiYuan Ma,Qituan Zhang,Guo Chen,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"任务-执行框架协同演化"框架，通过在线与任务后两阶段自我改进，让合成执行框架随任务分布一起演化，生成逐步变难的推理数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务级递归虽复用生成题目作为种子，但构建执行框架（harness）保持不变，导致任务难度分布演化受限，难以持续产出更难的推理数据。

Method: 任务-执行框架协同演化框架：在线自我改进将中间求解器失败转为可复用技能；任务后自我改进在每批后修订技能、提示与工作流，仅在成本有界增长且能生成更难的合法任务时采纳候选。模型权重与验证标准保持固定。

Result: 在数学、代码与科学领域，十四轮演化后平均求解器准确率从100.0%降至54.8%；消融显示两种更新调度的结合比固定框架递归或任一单独调度生成更难任务；数据提升下游SFT与GRPO表现，27B学生在10K数学样本上于APEX取得62.5% mean-16准确率，与部分前沿模型参考相当。

Conclusion: 在执行框架与任务同步自适应的条件下，可持续生成越来越难且具下游训练价值的推理数据。

Abstract: Generating progressively harder reasoning problems requires synthesis procedures that adapt as the task distribution evolves. Existing task-level recursion reuses generated problems as seeds but leaves the construction harness unchanged. We present task-harness co-evolution, a framework for recursive harness self-improvement (RSI) in reasoning-data synthesis. Online self-improvement converts intermediate solver failures into reusable skills during generation. Post-task self-improvement revises skills, prompts, and workflows after each batch, adopting candidates only when they generate harder valid tasks within a bounded cost increase. Model weights and verification criteria remain fixed. Across mathematics, coding, and science, mean solver accuracy decreases from 100.0% to 54.8% over fourteen evolution rounds. Ablations show that combining both update schedules produces harder tasks than fixed-harness recursion or either schedule alone. The resulting data improves downstream SFT and GRPO performance. In particular, a 27B student fine-tuned on 10K synthesized mathematics examples achieves 62.5% mean-16 accuracy on APEX, competitive with selected frontier-model references. These results support adapting the synthesis harness alongside the tasks to generate increasingly challenging data with downstream training value.

</details>


### [115] [Knowledge or Calculator? Decomposing the Skill Premium in Verifiable Financial Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03564)
*Jermyn Zhen Yong Bek,Zhuang Qiang Bok,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 FinSkillBench，一个包含 2,603 个时点情景、覆盖 12 个子任务的金融 AI 智能体评测套件，通过配对实验量化了"技能包"（人工策划的技能、文档与可执行工具）对模型表现的提升，发现策划技能包带来 +16.2 分的显著增益，且该增益高度依赖工作流类型与评测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融 AI 智能体不能仅停留在事实检索层面，投资工作流要求正确的定量执行、可靠使用程序性资源以及可审计的结构化输出。然而现有评测缺乏带隐藏可复现真值和任务特定确定性验证器的时点化基准，也就无法厘清"技能"到底带来多大增益、增益从何而来。

Method: 构建 FinSkillBench：2,603 个时点情景、12 个子任务（组合构建、风险管理、基本面分析），采用隐藏可再生的真值与任务特定确定性验证器。在 9 个模型 × 3 种资源条件下执行 17,820 个情景，对 8 个模型做配对分析；并将"策划增益"分解为人工撰写程序性文档与可执行领域工具两部分，辅以 10 种打分变体、聚类自助法，以及独立实现的第二套评测框架进行复现。

Result: 策划技能包将平均分从 0.366 提升至 0.528（+16.2 分）；单情景内生成的技能仅 +0.5 分，且消耗更多 token 与轮次。分解后：仅文档 +5.6 分，仅工具 +19.5 分，二者结合呈次可加性。增益强烈依赖工作流：数值密集任务由可执行工具主导，程序性或输出模式指导成为瓶颈时文档更重要，解释性任务两者皆有益。效应符号在 10 种打分变体和聚类自助法下稳定；第二套框架复现了方向性模式，但效应量级取决于工具与数据的暴露方式。

Conclusion: 可测量的"技能溢价"是整个模型—资源—评测框架系统的属性，而非仅由底层模型决定。因此金融智能体的能力评估必须连同资源供给与评测框架一并报告与解读。

Abstract: Financial AI agents must do more than retrieve facts: investment workflows require correct quantitative execution, reliable use of procedural resources, and auditable structured outputs. We introduce FinSkillBench, an evaluation suite of 2,603 point in time episodes across 12 subtasks in portfolio construction, risk management, and fundamental analysis, with hidden regenerable ground truth and task specific deterministic verifiers. Executing 17,820 episodes across 9 models and 3 resource conditions, the paired analysis across 8 models shows that curated skill packages raise mean scores by +16.2 points (0.366 to 0.528), whereas skills generated within a single episode add only +0.5 points while consuming more tokens and turns. We then decompose the curated premium by granting human authored procedural documents and executable domain tools separately: documents alone add +5.6 points, tools alone add +19.5 points, and their combination is subadditive. The premium is strongly workflow dependent: executable tools dominate numerically intensive workflows, documentation matters more when procedural or output schema guidance is the bottleneck, and interpretive tasks benefit from both. The effects are sign stable across 10 scoring variants and cluster bootstrap analyses, and an independently implemented second harness reproduces the directional pattern while showing that effect magnitudes depend on how tools and data are exposed. Overall, a measured "skill premium" is a property of the full model, resource, and harness system rather than of the underlying model alone.

</details>


### [116] [Learning to Assess Heartbeat Observability for mmWave Heart-Rate Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03570)
*Yuxuan Hu,Shilin Shan,Jianfei Yang,Feng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HEAR，一种双任务Transformer，从毫米波雷达相位谱中评估心跳可观测性并估计心率，仅用仿真训练即可零样本迁移到60/120 GHz真实数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达非接触心率测量需判断单次测量是否可靠。多个散射体回波的相干叠加即使在相似宏观观测几何下也会抑制心跳分量，因此可靠性评估必须直接基于采集到的测量信号，而非依赖几何条件。

Method: 构建可控多散射体FMCW仿真器，以心跳频带主峰与已知心率的吻合度自动生成可观测性标签；提出HEAR双任务Transformer，输入为频谱幅度及相对呼吸基频的频率以提供呼吸谐波上下文，联合预测可观测性分数与心率。

Result: 仅用仿真数据训练，零样本迁移至两个公开真实数据集（60 GHz与120 GHz，共134名受试者）。在120 GHz数据集上，基于分数的选择性预测将心率头MAE从全覆盖率17.9 BPM降至50%覆盖率1.6 BPM；该分数同样适用于多种现有估计器。端到端边缘设备处理延迟50.8 ms。

Conclusion: 直接从测量中学习心跳可观测性可实现可靠的选择性心率估计，仿真监督可有效迁移到真实场景，且计算轻量、适合边缘部署。

Abstract: Contactless heart-rate sensing with millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar requires assessing whether individual measurements support reliable estimation. We study learning to assess heartbeat observability, defined as the readability of the heartbeat component in an acquired phase spectrum, for selective heart-rate estimation. Coherent superposition of scatterer returns can suppress this component even under similar macroscopic observation geometry, motivating assessment directly from acquired measurements. To obtain training supervision across different observability conditions, we develop a controllable multi-scatterer frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) simulator. Agreement between the dominant heartbeat-band peak and the known heart rate provides an automatic observability label for each simulated measurement. We propose HEAR (Heartbeat Estimation with Assessed Reliability), a compact dual-task Transformer that jointly predicts an observability score and heart rate. Its input combines spectral magnitudes with frequencies relative to the respiration fundamental, providing context for respiratory harmonics. Trained solely on simulated observations, HEAR transfers zero-shot to two public real-world datasets collected at 60 and 120 GHz from 134 subjects. The same learned score supports selective prediction with both HEAR's own heart-rate head and multiple existing estimators. On the 120 GHz dataset, score-based selection reduces the HR head's mean absolute error from 17.9 BPM at full coverage to 1.6 BPM at 50% coverage. The complete pipeline achieves an end-to-end processing latency of 50.8 ms on an edge device. Project page: https://yuxuanhu9.github.io/HEAR/.

</details>


### [117] [HyperBrowseComp: A Multilingual and Multimodal Stress Test for Web-Browsing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03574)
*Alham Fikri Aji,Faiz Rizki Ramadhan,Zayd M. K. Zuhri,Seung Hun Eddie Han,Ryandito Diandaru,Qinrong Cui,Jan Christian Blaise Cruz,Badrinath Chandana,Peerawat Chomphooyod,Ahmed Attia,Jonibek Mansurov,Emilio Villa-Cueva,Canh Duong Nguyen,Imran Turganov,Minghao Wu,Peerat Limkonchotiwat,Irina Nikishina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HyperBrowseComp，一个多语言、多模态的浏览检索基准，包含 423 道由母语者人工编写并验证的难题，覆盖 13 种语言，用于评测模型在开放网络中的持久信息检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准往往难以同时考察多语言、多模态证据整合与多步线索追踪能力，且题目可能仅凭参数化知识即可回答，无法真实反映开放网络检索能力。

Method: 由母语或高熟练度使用者人工编写 423 道极难问题，覆盖 13 种语言；答案需简洁且可公开验证，需定位冷门证据、追踪多步线索链或查阅视频、扫描文档、图像、地图等异构来源；通过无联网模型评估过滤掉简单题；采用统一的 agent 协议，使用提供方原生搜索与共享外部检索框架评测多个模型，并对部分题目进行人工评测。

Result: 构建了覆盖 13 种语言、423 道人工验证问题的多语言多模态浏览基准，并通过原生搜索与统一检索框架评测多个模型，同时以人工评测对照模型表现与投入。

Conclusion: HyperBrowseComp 为跨语言、跨证据模态的持久信息检索提供了高难度测试平台，其难度源于在开放网络中寻找并连接证据。

Abstract: We introduce HyperBrowseComp, a multilingual and multimodal browsing benchmark comprising 423 manually authored and human-validated questions across 13 languages, written by native or highly proficient speakers. Questions are designed to be extremely challenging. Each question targets a concise, publicly verifiable answer whose discovery requires locating obscure evidence, following multi-step clue chains, or inspecting heterogeneous sources such as videos, scanned documents, images, or maps. Easier questions are filtered out by evaluating them with models without internet access to reduce the likelihood that they can be answered with parametric knowledge alone. We evaluate several models using provider-native search and a shared external retrieval harness under a common agent protocol. To contextualize model performance and effort, we also conduct a human evaluation on a sample of the questions. HyperBrowseComp provides a challenging testbed for persistent information seeking across languages and evidence modalities, with difficulty arising from discovering and connecting evidence on the open web.

</details>


### [118] [HazardWeaver: Scientific Route Selection for Hazard Analysis Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03591)
*Wangshu Zhu,Xueqi Cheng,Liang Wu,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出状态依赖的科学路径选择问题，并构建HazardWeaver系统与配套基准，用于灾害分析中的自动化方法选择与动态调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害理解与评估对防灾减灾至关重要，大语言模型推动的AI智能体在灾害分析中受到关注；但有效自动化要求智能体判断哪种科学方法适用于特定事件、且在当前数据和工具下可执行，并随新证据和执行结果动态调整选择。

Method: 将问题形式化为状态依赖的科学路径选择，提出HazardWeaver。系统包含：Hazard Knowledge Compiler提取与证据关联的科学适用条件；Hazard Capability Graph表示可执行科学能力并检查输入输出兼容性；Hazard Weaver Agent基于上述互补表示选择适用且可执行的路径、执行工作流并在分析状态变化时修正决策。同时构建Hazard Weaver Benchmark，含7个单灾种域和4类多灾种交互共141个实例，支持多有效科学路径，评估输出正确性、路径有效性和有依据的弃权。

Result: 在该基准上的大量实验表明，HazardWeaver优于现有智能体系统，在存在多条合格科学路径的任务上提升最大。代码已公开。

Conclusion: HazardWeaver为灾害分析中的方法选择与执行提供状态依赖的自动化框架，并通过专门基准验证其在多路径场景下的优势。

Abstract: Understanding and assessing natural hazards is essential for disaster preparedness and risk reduction. Recent advances in large language models have spurred growing interest in AI agents for hazard analysis, particularly their ability to integrate scientific data, models, and tools into automated workflows. However, effective automation requires agents to determine which scientific methods are appropriate for a given event and executable with the available data and tools. As new evidence and execution results become available, these conditions can change, requiring agents to reconsider their choices. We formulate this problem as state-dependent scientific route selection and introduce HazardWeaver. Specifically, HazardWeaver first leverages the Hazard Knowledge Compiler to extract evidence-linked conditions governing scientific applicability, then its Hazard Capability Graph represents executable scientific capabilities and checks compatibility between their inputs and outputs. Using these complementary representations, the Hazard Weaver Agent component selects applicable and executable routes, carries out their workflows, and revises its decisions as the analysis state changes. To evaluate both the scientific outputs and the decisions that produce them, we introduce the Hazard Weaver Benchmark, comprising 141 instances across seven single-hazard domains and four multi-hazard interaction classes. The benchmark accommodates multiple valid scientific routes and evaluates output correctness, route validity, and justified abstention. Extensive experiments on this benchmark show that HazardWeaver outperforms existing agent systems, with the largest gains on tasks with multiple eligible scientific routes. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/LabRAI/HazardWeaver.

</details>


### [119] [Low-Cost Video--Time Priors as a Strong Baseline for EEG--fNIRS Emotion Regression on Familiar Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03618)
*Minghao Kong,Jiurun Chen,Ying Gao,Xiangbin Meng,Rongjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 连续情绪回归中，视频-时间先验构成强且低成本基线，EEG-fNIRS仅为可选残差信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在熟悉视频场景下，参与者特异性响应可能主导预测，需检验生理信号（EEG-fNIRS）是否能提供额外残差修正。

Method: 采用五折被试留出评估（24名被试），比较训练参与者特异性响应、先验主导的固定融合，并进行源显式消融（视频身份、视频内时间）。

Result: 融合模型在内部与外部评估中MAE分别相差0.05与0.32以内；消融显示视频身份与视频内时间贡献最大，EEG-fNIRS增益较小且随被试和视频变化。

Conclusion: 视频-时间先验是熟悉视频情绪回归的强基线，EEG-fNIRS可作为可选的残差信号。

Abstract: Continuous emotion regression estimates moment-to-moment valence and arousal while a viewer watches a video. In familiar-video deployment, responses fron training participant-specific estimate, and prior-dominating fixed fusion tests whether physiology adds residual correction. In five-fold subject-held-out evaluation on 24was within 0.05 and 0.32 MAE of fusion in the internal and external evaluations, respectively. Source-explicit ablations showed that video identity and within-video tine accounted for most of the reduction, while EG-FNIRS gains were smaller and varied across participants and videos. These results identify the video-time prior as a strong, low-cost baseline and position EEG-fNIRS as an optional residual signal for familiar-video emotion regression.

</details>


### [120] [Depth as Time in One-Step Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03626)
*Arnold Caleb Asiimwe,William Yang,Sanghyuk Chun,Esin Tureci,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"深度即时间"（depth as time）经验观察：一步生成模型并未消除扩散的去噪时间计算，而是将其重排到网络深度中，可通过模型自身输出头解码中间层恢复，并据此实现16.6倍参数压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步生成模型（蒸馏或学习流映射）已能高质量出图，但多步扩散的去噪轨迹在压缩为单次前向传播后究竟发生什么，尚不清楚。作者由此提出该问题，探究时间维度的去噪计算是否真正被消除，还是被重新组织。

Method: 通过解码中间层（用模型自身输出头）探测单次前向传播中各深度的表示，检验其是否按采样步骤顺序展开去噪计算；对比不同运输任务训练的流映射（如MeanFlow与drifting模型）；并显式将逐层计算视为一个流，训练单个时间条件块来跨层去噪。

Result: 发现逐深度计算依赖流映射所训练的运输任务：MeanFlow在探测较短运输区间时，单次网络评估内同时呈现去噪与再加噪（denoise-then-renoise）；无时间索引运输任务的drifting模型则无此逐深度去噪。展示具有该现象更易跨层压缩：MeanFlow SiT-L/2可压缩16.6倍参数为单个时间条件块。

Conclusion: 扩散的时间计算并未被一步生成消除，而是跨网络深度被重组；将逐层计算显式视为流时，单时间条件块即可训练以跨层去噪，实现16.6倍压缩。这为一步生成模型的可解释性与压缩提供了新视角。

Abstract: The recent wave of one-step generative models, which compress the multi-step trajectory of diffusion via either distillation or learned flow maps, has reached an inflection point where they can generate high-quality images. Here, we ask a natural question that follows from these advances: what happens to the denoising trajectory of multi-step diffusion when generation is compressed into a single forward pass? We offer an empirical observation we call \textit{depth as time}: the denoising computation that multi-step diffusion performs across sampling steps appears to unfold across the depth of a single forward pass, and can be recovered by decoding intermediate layers with the model's own output head. Most interestingly, we show that this depthwise computation depends on the transport task a flow map is trained to solve. The most surprising case is MeanFlow, where probing shorter transport intervals reveals both denoising and renoising within a single network evaluation. In contrast, generators trained without a time-indexed transport task, such as drifting models, do not exhibit the same depthwise denoising. Consequently, we show that models that exhibit the depthwise denoising phenomenon are more compressible across the layerwise computation: a MeanFlow \texttt{SiT-L/2} model can be compressed by $16.6\times$ in parameters into a single time-conditioned block. We offer an explanation for this denoise-then-renoise behavior and show that, when we treat the layerwise computation explicitly as a flow, a single time-conditioned block can be trained to denoise across layers, compressing a MeanFlow \texttt{SiT-L/2} model by $16.6\times$ in parameters. Together, these results suggest that the temporal computation of diffusion is not eliminated by one-step generation, but reorganized across network depth.

</details>


### [121] [NeutronGym: Physics-Graded Neutron Instrument Design for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03631)
*Lijie Ding,Changwoo Do*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 NeutronGym——首个可执行的中子仪器设计环境，无 LLM 评委、分级评分，揭示语言模型更多是"回忆"而非"做"物理，但可通过强化学习获得真实设计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测无法可靠区分语言模型是真正进行物理设计推理还是复现记忆。需要一个评分不可争辩、可执行、能防记忆的科学仪器设计环境来检验并训练这种能力。

Method: NeutronGym：智能体经校验工具搭建仪器，McStas 进行射线追踪；层级阶梯从语法、运行、结构、科学四层自动评分，不使用 LLM 评委。程序化任务族提供同布局的无限实例并设留出参数区间；精选切片 McStasBench 含 16 个源自已发表仪器的任务，附记忆探测与沙箱。并在此奖励上做强化学习。

Result: 七个模型最多复现 16 个任务中的 7 个，无人检索到参考实现，也无人达到改进目标。RL 将 Qwen3-8B 在留出实例上从 11% 提升到 77%（第二种子 69%），超越未训练 Qwen3-32B，并在另外三个受限任务族各单种子复现。去掉阶梯部分给分则性能崩塌 60 分；仅靠奖励，训练模型在智能体仿真预算下只在给定闭式物理时达到经典优化器水平（77% 对 81%，此规模下差异不显著），前沿模型仍解出 98–99%。

Conclusion: 该环境可将物理设计能力从"回忆"转为可训练的"执行"，但当前增益高度依赖分层部分给分，且在缺乏闭式物理时仍落后经典方法；过程中通过四类无模型基线即可解出的失败任务设计（已发布探测工具）保证了结果可信度。

Abstract: Designing a scientific instrument tests whether language-model agents can do physics rather than recall it, provided the grading cannot be argued with. We introduce NeutronGym, to our knowledge the first executable environment for neutron instrument design: agents build instruments through validating tools, McStas ray-traces what they build, and a level-resolved ladder grades syntax, runtime, structure and science with no LLM judge. Procedural families supply unlimited instances of a fixed layout whose design parameters the agent must set, with held-out parameter regimes; a curated slice, McStasBench, adds 16 tasks from published instruments behind memorization probes and a sandbox. Seven models reproduce at most 7 of the 16, none retrieves a reference, and none meets an improvement target. The environment also trains. Reinforcement learning on its reward takes Qwen3-8B from 11% to 77% of held-out instances of a family whose targets come from a hidden design (69% at a second seed), past an untrained Qwen3-32B, and the recipe holds, at one seed each, on three further gated families. The analysis says what that gain is. Without the ladder's partial credit it collapses by 60 points. From reward alone the trained model reaches what a classical optimizer reaches, at the agent's simulation budget, only when handed the closed-form physics (77% against 81%, a gap that does not separate at this size), while frontier models still solve 98-99%. Getting a trustworthy result meant failing four task designs that no-model baselines could solve, and we release the probes that found them.

</details>


### [122] [Credit Where It Matters: Dependency-Aware Policy Optimization for Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03634)
*Yu Li,Guangfeng Cai,Long-Fei Li,Shuo Han,Shengtian Yang,Han Luo,Kaibing Yang,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DepGPO，一种利用命令间执行依赖关系进行信用分配的强化学习方法，用于终端智能体的多步任务训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹级与步级信用分配方法未显式追踪命令之间的读写依赖，导致训练信号可能被分配到无关操作上，削弱了对关键步骤的学习效果。

Method: 从执行轨迹构建命令依赖图，从任务验证器检查的资源反向追溯，沿路径将信用分配给相关写操作及其支撑读操作，并据此在步骤间重新分配轨迹优势值。

Result: 大量对比实验与消融实验表明，DepGPO 在复杂终端任务上提升了任务性能与训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 显式建模命令读写依赖可有效改善终端智能体强化学习中的信用分配质量。

Abstract: Terminal-using agents benefit from reinforcement learning (RL) in coding, debugging, and other multi-step terminal tasks. In these tasks, later commands often depend on information or intermediate results produced by earlier commands. However, existing trajectory-level and step-level credit assignment methods do not explicitly trace the read-write dependencies through which commands affect the final outcome. Consequently, training signals could still be assigned to irrelevant operations, weakening learning from relevant steps. In this paper, we propose Dependency-Aware Group Policy Optimization (DepGPO), which uses execution dependencies between commands to guide credit assignment for terminal agents. Specifically, we construct a command dependency graph from execution traces and trace backward from the resources inspected by the task verifier. We then assign credit to relevant writes and their supporting reads along these paths, and use it to redistribute trajectory advantages across steps. Extensive comparative experiments and ablation studies demonstrate that DepGPO improves task performance and training stability on complex terminal tasks.

</details>


### [123] [Do Large Language Models Know Colombian Law? A Reliability Benchmark for the Colombian Legal System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03639)
*Rubén Manrique,Michelle Castellanos,Jorge Morales,Juan David Gutiérrez,Antonio Barreto Rozo,Joaquín Vélez Navarro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个经专家验证的哥伦比亚法律体系LLM可靠性基准（1,042题，覆盖10个法律领域、3种题型），评测15个模型，发现闭卷选择题准确率最高0.905，但自由文本事实正确率不超过0.45，且存在"答案相关性与正确性脱钩"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于法律实践、教育与研究，但其在美国以外国家法律体系中的可靠性缺乏系统记录，尤其缺乏经专家验证的评测基准。

Method: 采用人机协同（human-in-the-loop）流水线，经多阶段专家评审构建含1,042个条目、覆盖10个法律领域与3种题型（闭卷多选、半开放、开放式IRAC）的基准；用适配各题型的指标评测15个当代闭源与开源模型。

Result: 闭卷题准确率跨度大（0.905—0.577）；自由文本事实正确率均不超过0.45（0-1刻度）。答案相关性与正确性呈负相关（Spearman rho = -0.46），即模型常"听起来切题但实为错误"。闭卷准确率与自由文本正确率排名高度相关（rho = 0.94）。独立LLM评审与盲审人类专家均复现自由文本排名（rho ≥ 0.88）。评审还显示模型引用的法条仅约一半正确，其余为错误或不存在。可靠性随法律领域系统性变化，并随问题复杂度呈倒U形分布。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在哥伦比亚法律任务中仍需专家监督；将答案锚定于权威来源是提升可靠性的可行路径。作者开源基准构建流水线以支持可复现评测。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support legal practice, education, and research, yet their reliability in national legal systems outside the United States remains largely undocumented. We introduce an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLM reliability on the Colombian legal system. The benchmark comprises 1,042 items spanning ten areas of law and three question formats (closed multiple-choice, semi-open, and open-ended IRAC), built through a human-in-the-loop pipeline with multi-stage expert review. We evaluate 15 contemporary proprietary and open-weight models with format-appropriate metrics. Accuracy on closed questions ranges widely, from 0.905 (Gemini 3.1 Pro) to 0.577, but on free-text legal answers factual correctness never exceeds 0.45 (on a 0-1 scale) for any model. We find a dissociation between answer relevancy and correctness (Spearman rho = -0.46): models reliably sound responsive while frequently being wrong, a pattern of particular concern for non-expert users. Closed-question accuracy and free-text correctness are strongly rank-correlated (rho = 0.94), so cheap multiple-choice screening predicts model ranking but overstates absolute reliability. An independent rubric-based LLM judge and blind human expert scoring both reproduce the free-text ranking (rho >= 0.88). The judge further reveals that only about half of the norms models cite are correct; the rest are wrong or non-existent. Reliability varies systematically by legal area and follows an inverted-U across question complexity. Our results indicate that current LLMs require expert supervision for Colombian legal tasks, and that grounding answers in authoritative sources is a promising path to higher reliability. We release the benchmark construction pipeline to support reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [124] [Transcriptome-informed multi-modal AI for predicting neoadjuvant therapy response from breast cancer biopsies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03693)
*Jungkyu Park,Dhruva Biswas,Joseph Cappadona,Cerise Tang,Ken G. Zeng,Bartosz Machura,Chuwen Liu,Paolo Tarantino,Coral Omene,Francisco J. Esteva,Rohit Bhargava,Marcin Braun,Kamila Paździerz,Jakub Czerwiński,Hanna Romańska-Knight,Albert Grinshpun,Bareket Daniel,Michele Buchinger,Frederick Howard,Piotr Wysocki,Brie Chun,Freya Schnabel,Rich Caruana,Jan Witowski,Krzysztof J. Geras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段AI模型：先用跨32种癌型、8,742例患者的组织病理学推断转录组，再基于推断表达与临床变量预测乳腺癌新辅助治疗pCR；在1,412例（9个队列）中汇总AUROC达0.79，优于组织病理学标志物且稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤学深度学习生物标志物的发展受限于标注数据稀缺，亟需能够缓解数据依赖、具备良好泛化能力的方法。

Method: 构建两阶段模型：第一阶段利用8,742例、32种癌型的组织病理图像学习转录组（经病理学家复核并与实测表达在空间上一致）；第二阶段简化为从推断表达与临床变量预测pCR。开发集1,080例（5个队列），评估集1,412例（9个队列）；并进行消融实验。

Result: 模型汇总AUROC为0.79（95% CI 0.73–0.85），可在分子亚型内区分应答者；优于组织病理学生物标志物，在瘤内采样变化及极少量活检组织条件下保持稳定。消融显示转录组全域推断优于仅用临床变量或单阶段病理模型，并规避基因组检测的基因选择限制从而更稳健。

Conclusion: 结果表明，具有生物学信息的压缩（biologically informed compression）可推广至精准肿瘤学中的数据稀疏应用场景。

Abstract: Scarcity of labeled data limits development of deep learning biomarkers in oncology. We develop a two-stage AI model predicting pathological complete response (pCR) to neoadjuvant therapy in breast cancer. The first stage learns the transcriptome from histopathology using 8,742 patients across 32 cancer types, corroborated by pathologist review and spatial agreement with measured expression. This simplifies the second stage to predicting pCR from inferred expression and clinical variables. Developed using 1,080 patients (five cohorts) and evaluated in 1,412 patients (nine cohorts), the model achieves a pooled AUROC of 0.79 (95% CI, 0.73-0.85), discriminating responders within molecular subtypes. It outperforms histopathological biomarkers, remaining stable across intratumoral sampling and with minimal biopsy tissue. Ablations show transcriptome-wide inference improves discrimination over clinical variables alone or one-stage pathology models, and robustness by avoiding genomic assays' gene selection constraints. These results indicate that biologically informed compression may generalize to data-sparse applications in precision oncology.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Hybrid Machine Learning-Assisted Raman Spectroscopy with Generative Feature Augmentation for Pharmaceutical Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02224)
*Quach Thi Thai Binh,Ton Nu Quynh Trang,Thang B. Phan,Vu Thi Hanh Thu,Nguyen Tuan Hung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyMLRaman混合框架，结合深度光谱特征提取、生成模型与经典机器学习分类器，实现六种药物残留的快速拉曼识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物残留的快速可靠识别对公共卫生、食品安全及拉曼筛查应用具有重要意义，但现有方法在表征能力与数据有限条件下仍存在不足。

Method: 将拉曼光谱转为光谱图像，用多种深度骨干网络编码，其中EfficientNet-B3表征最优，输出1536维嵌入；以此训练SVM、KNN、逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost与ANN等下游分类器，采用分层10折交叉验证；并在PCA降维的EfficientNet-B3潜空间中引入DDPM特征增强以应对小样本。

Result: EfficientNet-B3与SVM组合基线最佳，准确率96.31%、宏F1为96.36%，优于独立CNN基线；DDPM增强收益具有选择性，在低训练比例下对KNN提升明显，效果依赖分类器；并实现交互式拉曼药物分析器演示。

Conclusion: HyMLRaman为快速拉曼药物筛查提供了一条实用且可解释的技术路径。

Abstract: Rapid and reliable identification of pharmaceutical residues is important for safeguarding public health, ensuring food safety, and enabling practical Raman-based screening. In this study, we propose HyMLRaman, a hybrid Raman spectroscopy framework that combines deep spectral feature extraction, generative models, and classical machine-learning classifiers to identify six pharmaceutical compounds, including amoxicillin, chloramphenicol, ciprofloxacin, tetracycline, ibuprofen, and paracetamol. Raman spectra are converted into spectral images and encoded with several deep neural-network backbones, among which EfficientNet-B3 yields the most effective representation. The resulting 1536-dimensional embeddings are then used to train downstream classifiers, including SVM, KNN, logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, and ANN, using stratified 10-fold cross-validation. The hybrid EfficientNet-B3--SVM configuration achieves the strongest baseline performance, reaching 96.31% accuracy and a macro-F1 score of 96.36%, outperforming the standalone CNN baseline. To address limited-data conditions, a generative model, a DDPM-based feature augmentation, is introduced in a PCA-reduced EfficientNet-B3 latent space. The low-data ablation results show that DDPM augmentation provides selective benefits, particularly for KNN with reduced training fractions, and that its effect remains classifier-dependent. Finally, an application-level Raman Pharmaceutical Analyzer demonstrates the feasibility of embedding the trained model into an interactive Raman analysis workflow. These results suggest that HyMLRaman provides a practical and interpretable route for rapid Raman-based pharmaceutical screening.

</details>


### [126] [Approximation Property of Dropout Neural Networks: Sobolev Rates and Confidence Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02253)
*Jia-He Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带随机丢弃（dropout）的ReLU网络对Sobolev空间单位球的逼近，给出了高概率意义下的网络规模上界与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有dropout网络的通用逼近性只说明表达能力存在，却无法刻画实现一次随机采样网络所需的具体规模，因此需要量化在固定保留概率p与置信水平1−δ下的网络规模。

Method: 构造常深度网络，结合有界局部子网络、在成功逼近事件上的定位以及多尺度Taylor分解；误差在输入域上一致度量，并以至少1−δ的概率对单个采样网络成立。下界方面用Sobolev容量约束存活边数，并通过逼近固定仿射函数分析输出层代价。

Result: 上界规模为 Õ_{n,d}(p^{-9}ε^{-max{d/n,2}} log(1/δ))；下界显示存活边数存在容量约束，且固定仿射函数需输出层代价量级((1−p)/p)ε^{-2}log(1/δ)。对固定p∈(0,1)与δ<min{1/2,1−p}，在固定或对数深度下精度指数上下界匹配；当d≤2n时置信度依赖也在对数精度内匹配。下界可推广到W^{n,r}目标、L^s误差。

Conclusion: dropout ReLU网络在Sobolev逼近中的规模由保留概率p、精度ε与置信度δ共同决定，上下界在关键指数上匹配，但最优的p依赖与对数因子仍待解决。

Abstract: The universal approximation property of dropout neural networks does not by itself describe the network size required for an accurate random realization. In this work, we study approximation of the unit ball of $W^{n,\infty}([0,1]^d)$ by ReLU networks whose edges are retained independently with probability $p$. The approximation error is measured uniformly over the input domain, and the guarantee holds with probability at least $1-δ$ for a single sampled network. We construct networks of constant depth and size $\widetilde O_{n,d}(p^{-9}\varepsilon^{-\max\{d/n,2\}} \log(1/δ))$. The construction combines bounded local subnetworks, localization on a successful approximation event, and a multiscale Taylor decomposition. Conversely, Sobolev capacity imposes a lower bound on the number of surviving edges, while approximation of a fixed affine function requires an output-layer cost of order $((1-p)/p)\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/δ)$ at sufficiently high confidence. For fixed $p\in(0,1)$ and $δ<\min\{1/2,1-p\}$, the upper and lower bounds match in the accuracy exponent under a fixed or logarithmic depth budget. When $d\leq2n$, they also match in confidence up to logarithms of accuracy. We extend the lower bounds to $W^{n,r}$ targets with $L^s$ error, and distinguish this extension from the upper bound for $W^{n,\infty}$. The optimal retention dependence and logarithmic factors remain open.

</details>


### [127] [The Price of Greenwashing: Algorithmic Verification and Market Discipline using Conformal Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02225)
*Sourav Bose,Taoufik Bouraoui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将SEC财务基本面与EPA设施级温室气体登记数据融合，构建物理排放基线，并以梯度提升+Mondrian保形预测提出CWCD指标量化企业自报排放与算法基线的偏离，实证发现该偏离对后续Tobin's Q与ROA具有显著负向预测力，证明市场并非"失明"，而是将环境信息欺骗定价为公司治理风险的领先指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可持续性监管要求不断扩张，但金融体系对自报排放数据的系统性依赖使市场暴露于普遍的"漂绿"风险。现有文献多依赖主观ESG评级或文本情绪分析，缺乏客观量化物理气候现实的经济计量工具，形成了关键的研究空白与信息不对称。

Method: 融合美国SEC财务基本面数据与EPA设施级温室气体登记数据，建立具有数学保证的物理排放基线；采用梯度提升（Gradient Boosting）架构结合Mondrian保形预测（Mondrian Conformal Prediction），将自报数据与算法基线的缺口量化为"保形加权连续偏离度"（CWCD）指标；随后以横截面领先—滞后（lead-lag）经济计量设计评估该偏离指标的市场效应。

Result: 算法测算的排放偏离与后续市场估值（Tobin's Q）及运营盈利能力（ROA）呈显著且幅度较大的负相关关系，机制稳健。该结果否定了"市场失明"假说，表明机构资本已将环境信息欺骗定价为基本面管理不善的领先指标，而非单纯的伦理瑕疵。

Conclusion: 研究为资产管理机构与监管者大规模部署算法审计基础设施提供了定量依据，说明以物理排放数据为锚、可统计保证的偏离度指标能够有效识别并定价漂绿行为，从而强化市场纪律。

Abstract: While corporate sustainability mandates are expanding, the systemic reliance on self-reported emissions data exposes financial markets to pervasive greenwashing. Current literature relies heavily on subjective ESG ratings or textual sentiment analysis, leaving a critical econometric gap in objectively quantifying physical climate realities. To resolve this information asymmetry, we fuse U.S. SEC financial fundamentals with facility-level EPA greenhouse gas registries to establish a mathematically guaranteed baseline of physical corporate emissions. Leveraging a gradient boosting architecture and Mondrian Conformal Prediction, we quantify the shortfall between self-reported data and this algorithmic baseline into a novel Conformal-Weighted Continuous Divergence (CWCD) metric. Evaluating this divergence via a cross-sectional lead-lag econometric design, we uncover a robust mechanism of market discipline: algorithmic emissions divergence exhibits a severe, statistically significant negative relationship with subsequent market valuation (Tobin's Q) and operational profitability (ROA). Providing definitive evidence against the market blindness hypothesis, this study proves that institutional capital actively prices environmental deception not merely as an ethical lapse, but as a leading indicator of fundamental corporate mismanagement. Ultimately, these findings provide the quantitative justification necessary for asset managers and regulators to deploy algorithmic auditing infrastructure at scale.

</details>


### [128] [State-Space Unlearning for Non-Stationary Bias in Land Surface Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02248)
*Anidipta Pal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SSU-LSF，首个面向地球科学 Mamba 类状态空间模型的机器遗忘框架，用于消除运营性陆表预测系统中的非平稳混淆事件，实现高效且低损伤的混淆去除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运营性陆表预测系统基于 Mamba 族结构化状态空间模型，会将未记录的灌溉激增、水库调度变化、传感器重新校准等非平稳混淆事件吸收进状态转移矩阵，从而在物理成因结束很久之后仍持续隐性偏置 NDVI、LST 与作物物候预测。因此需要一种专门的机器遗忘方法来移除此类混淆影响。

Method: 提出 SSU-LSF 框架：为 Mamba 状态矩阵设计 EKFac 影响函数，借助闭式矩阵指数梯度；采用谱半径加权肘部阈值定位时间混淆足迹 Φ；在 KL 散度信赖域内执行无 Hessian 投影梯度上升，并加入空间全变分（TV）正则化。

Result: 命题 1 证明残余混淆上界为 O((1-ρ(Ā)^{T_c})/((1-ρ(Ā))μ))，随窗口长度 T_c 增长。在三个异构基准、十一个基线对比中，SSU-LSF 混淆降低率分别为 0.773（CropHarvest）、0.821（NDVI-LST）、0.859（ERA5），ERA5 上干净域 RMSE 最坏退化仅 4.2%，3–5 个 epoch 收敛，单位遗忘请求 GPU 成本比全量重训练低 8.4 倍。

Conclusion: SSU-LSF 是首个专为地球科学 Mamba 类 SSM 定制的机器遗忘框架，能在多基准上高效去除混淆、保持干净域性能，且计算成本远低于重训练，并提供开源代码。

Abstract: Operational land surface forecasting systems built on Mamba-family Structured State Space Models absorb non-stationary confounding events (unrecorded irrigation booms, dam-operation shifts, sensor recalibrations) into their state-transition matrices, silently biasing NDVI, LST, and crop phenology predictions long after the physical cause ends. This paper introduces SSU-LSF (State-Space Unlearning for Land Surface Forecasting), the first machine-unlearning framework purpose-built for geoscientific Mamba-based SSMs. We develop EKFac influence functions specialized to the Mamba state matrices via a closed-form matrix-exponential gradient, use spectral-radius-weighted elbow thresholding to localize a temporal confounding footprint $Φ$, and apply Hessian-free projected gradient ascent within a KL-divergence trust region augmented by spatial total-variation (TV) regularization. Proposition 1 establishes that residual confounding is bounded by $\mathcal{O}\big((1-ρ(\bar{A})^{T_c})/((1-ρ(\bar{A}))μ)\big)$, which grows with the window length $T_c$. Across three heterogeneous benchmarks and eleven baselines, SSU-LSF achieves confounding reduction rates of $0.773$ (CropHarvest), $0.821$ (NDVI-LST), and $0.859$ (ERA5), with worst-case clean-domain RMSE degradation of $4.2\%$ on ERA5, converging in 3--5 epochs at $8.4\times$ lower GPU-cost per unlearning request than full retraining. Code: https://github.com/Anidipta/SSU-LSF

</details>


### [129] [Nearest-neighbour baselines for fingerprint prediction from MS/MS spectra under different assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02249)
*Ling Min Serena Khoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本报告系统比较多种最近邻检索变体，揭示推理期可用信息假设的差异如何影响分子指纹预测性能，旨在建立更严格的基线以支持更严谨的基准评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明最近邻检索是MS/MS谱预测分子指纹的强基线，部分变体可匹敌甚至超越深度学习模型；但"最近邻"实际包含一组在推理期信息假设上各不相同的检索方法，这些差异会导致性能不可比，因此需要系统梳理以建立更严格的基线。

Method: 将最近邻检索视为一个方法族，按其推理阶段所假设可用信息的不同，系统地对比多种最近邻变体，并分析这些假设差异对预测性能的影响。

Result: 不同的最近邻变体因推理期可用信息假设不同，其性能表现存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: 通过明确区分并量化各最近邻变体的假设与表现，可建立更严格的基线，从而实现更严谨的基准比较，更准确地衡量该领域的进展。

Abstract: It has recently been shown that nearest-neighbour retrieval provides a strong baseline for molecular fingerprint prediction from MS/MS spectra, with several variants matching or outperforming current deep learning models (Khoo and Barzilay, 2026; Liu et al., 2026; Gupta et al., 2026). Importantly, "nearest neighbour" encompasses a family of retrieval methods that differ in the information assumed to be available at inference. In this report, we systematically compare several nearest-neighbour variants and show how these differing assumptions affect performance. Our goal is to establish stricter baselines that enable more rigorous benchmarking and better measure progress in this area.

</details>


### [130] [Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling for Diffusion Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02251)
*Marcello Bullo,Yanxiao Liu,Öykü Sıla Güner,Arpan Mukherjee,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RASS（Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling），一种基于rank-aware list coupling的验证规则，利用草稿候选间无需额外目标模型评估即可获得的有信息量排序，优化rank权重以最小化选择提案分布与目标分布的总变差距离，并保证对任意rank权重都精确采样；在GMM、FFHQ、CIFAR-10与SD 3.5等多个任务上优于D-GRS，CIFAR-10上同等计算预算下增益约20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的推测采样通过并行验证廉价的草稿状态来加速生成，同时保持目标分布。已有基于树的方案（如D-GRS）比单链草稿更有效地分配并行计算预算：D-GRS在每个节点生成K个条件独立候选，并按生成顺序顺序检验。但这些候选天然具有有信息量的排序，而D-GRS并未利用该排序信息，存在提升空间。

Method: RASS面向推测草稿树的验证规则，基于rank-aware list coupling。其做法：沿提案-目标均值位移对草稿候选排序；采样一个rank，其权重经过优化，以最小化被选提案分布与目标分布之间的总变差距离（TV）；最后将所选候选与目标做最大耦合，并通过残差修正保证对任意rank权重选择下的精确采样。

Result: 在Gaussian-mixture目标、FFHQ无条件像素空间生成、CIFAR-10条件生成，以及使用COCO2014提示词的Stable Diffusion 3.5潜空间扩散上进行评估。以标准采样与推测采样的目标模型评估次数之比为指标，RASS在所有评估设置上均优于D-GRS，在CIFAR-10上同等计算预算下增益约达20%。

Conclusion: 草稿候选的排序信息可在不增加目标模型评估的前提下被有效利用；通过rank-aware list coupling配合权重优化与残差修正，RASS在保持精确目标分布的同时更高效地利用并行验证预算，较D-GRS取得一致的加速提升。

Abstract: Speculative sampling accelerates diffusion generation by verifying inexpensive draft states in parallel while preserving the target law. Recent tree-based methods allocate the parallel compute budget more effectively than single-chain drafts, as demonstrated by Diffusion Greedy Rejection Sampling (D-GRS). D-GRS generates $K$ conditionally independent candidates per node, and sequentially tests them in their generation order. Yet the sampled candidates admit an informative ranking without additional target-model evaluations. To exploit this, we introduce Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling (RASS), a verification rule for speculative draft trees based on rank-aware list coupling. RASS orders draft candidates along the proposal-target mean displacement and samples a rank with weights optimized to minimize total variation between the selected-proposal and target laws. Finally, the selected candidate is maximally coupled with the target, with residual correction ensuring exact sampling for any choice of rank weights. We evaluate RASS on a Gaussian-mixture target, unconditional pixel-space generation on FFHQ, conditional generation on CIFAR-10, and latent diffusion with Stable Diffusion 3.5 using COCO2014 prompts. Measured by the ratio of standard to speculative sampling's target-model evaluation counts, RASS improves on D-GRS across the evaluated settings, with gains reaching approximately 20% on CIFAR-10 at matched compute budgets.

</details>


### [131] [A Manifold-Aware Topic Modeling Approach via Rank-Based Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29630)
*Thiago César Castilho Almeida,Daniel Carlos Guimarães Pedronette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MARETopic是一个免训练的主题发现框架，将主题发现建模为基于排序的原型选择：先降维投影嵌入并构建排序列表，再用贪心算法选出恰好K个真实语料文档作为代表，其邻域覆盖整个语料。两个变体（Corr与Diff）共享该准则，在类别最多的两个基准上Purity与NMI领先，Diff变体速度快1.7–1.9倍，并在三个数据集中的两个上主题连贯性领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主题模型虽引入预训练嵌入，但存在两点缺陷：一是神经架构产生的潜在表示缺乏对具体文本的锚定（无文本根基）；二是基于聚类的流程仅在事后分配代表文档，且依赖绝对距离，而高维空间中的hubness（枢纽性）与各向异性会扭曲绝对距离，导致代表文档选择不可靠。

Method: 提出MARETopic，一个免训练（training-free）框架，将主题发现转化为基于排序（rank-based）的原型选择。流程为：将嵌入投影到低维流形，构建编码序数邻域结构（ordinal neighborhood structure）的排序列表，再用贪心算法选取恰好K个范例文档（真实语料文本），使这些文档的邻域覆盖整个语料。两个变体共享该选择准则：MARETopic_Corr用查询性能预测器（query performance predictor）与排序相关度量（rank correlation measure）对候选打分；MARETopic_Diff用基于排序的扩散矩阵（rank-based diffusion matrix）打分，无需上述两种度量。此外提出一种新的主题间Maximal Marginal Relevance步骤以提升词汇多样性。

Result: MARETopic_Corr在类别最多的两个基准数据集上，Purity与NMI均领先于神经网络主题模型与基于聚类的主题模型；MARETopic_Diff无需额外度量，运行速度快1.7至1.9倍。在不进行任何梯度更新的情况下，MARETopic在三个数据集中的两个上取得最优主题连贯性（topic coherence）。主题间Maximal Marginal Relevance步骤以极小的连贯性代价提升词汇多样性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MARETopic以免训练、基于排序的原型选择方式实现了主题发现，避免了绝对距离受hubness与各向异性扭曲的问题，并使代表文档锚定于真实语料文本；其在聚类质量指标与主题连贯性上达到或超越现有神经与聚类主题模型，同时保持较高效率，证明了排序式原型选择在主题建模中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent topic models leverage pretrained embeddings, but neural architectures produce latent representations without grounding in specific texts, and clustering-based pipelines assign representative documents only post hoc, relying on absolute distances distorted by hubness and anisotropy in high-dimensional spaces. We introduce MARETopic, a training-free framework that casts topic discovery as rank-based prototype selection. After projecting embeddings onto a low-dimensional manifold, MARETopic builds ranked lists encoding ordinal neighborhood structure. A greedy algorithm selects exactly K exemplar documents, real corpus texts, whose neighborhoods cover the corpus. Two variants share this criterion. MARETopic$_\text{Corr}$ scores candidates with a query performance predictor and a rank correlation measure, leading Purity and NMI on the two benchmarks with the most categories, ahead of both neural and clustering-based topic models. MARETopic$_\text{Diff}$ scores them with a rank-based diffusion matrix, needs neither measure, and runs 1.7 to 1.9 times faster. Without a single gradient update, MARETopic leads topic coherence on two of three datasets. A novel inter-topic Maximal Marginal Relevance step raises vocabulary diversity at little cost in coherence. Our code is available at https://github.com/thcastilho/maretopic.

</details>


### [132] [Counterfactual Predictions in Scientific Emulators Without Controlled Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02252)
*Dingling Yao,Kahaan Gandhi,Valentin Duruisseaux,Boris Bonev,Francesco Locatello,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReRoute框架，在无需受控干预数据的情况下，将事实数据与部分机制知识结合，实现科学反事实（what-if）预测，并在平流-扩散系统与气候仿真任务中显著降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学问题常涉及对未观测情形的推理（如条件、干预或历史不同会如何）。模型在观测数据上预测准确，但当相关输入被独立变化时，往往在what-if查询上失败。常见补救是加入受控仿真数据以显式解耦因子，但这需要模拟器、计算昂贵且继承模拟器的建模假设。

Method: ReRoute固定预训练骨干网络中查询输入为参考值，通过已知机制通路重新引入其变化，并在原始事实数据上微调，将下游效应留给学习到的动力学。作者在显式结构假设下给出因果识别结果，核心论证在Lean中机器验证。

Result: 在可获精确响应的受控平流-扩散系统中，ReRoute实现高精度反事实预测；在先进气候仿真中，面对CO2分布严重偏移的留出耦合气候干预，聚合气候误差降低18.2-31.8%，同时在标准条件下保持技能，且成本仅为在额外受控仿真上重训练的一小部分（尚未计入生成此类数据的巨大开销）。在基于历史ERA5再分析训练的仿真器上，固定CO2反事实下，ReRoute保留更多由观测边界条件隐含的地表增暖。

Conclusion: ReRoute为靶向科学what-if预测提供了一种无需受控干预数据适配的框架，结合事实数据与部分机制知识，取得因果可识别性并在气候仿真等任务中显著优于重训练方案，同时大幅降低成本。

Abstract: Many scientific questions require reasoning about what was never observed: What if the conditions, interventions, or history had been different? Models can predict accurately on observed data yet fail on such what-if queries when correlated inputs are varied independently. A common remedy is to add controlled simulation data in which these factors are explicitly disentangled, but this requires access to a simulator, can be computationally expensive, and inherits the simulator's modeling assumptions. We introduce ReRoute, a framework for targeted scientific what-if prediction that combines factual data with partial mechanistic knowledge, without requiring controlled intervention data for adaptation. ReRoute fixes the queried input of a pretrained backbone to a reference value, reintroduces its variation through a known mechanistic pathway, and fine-tunes on the original factual data, while leaving downstream effects to the learned dynamics. We provide a causal identification result for this construction under explicit structural assumptions, with the core argument machine-checked in Lean. After showing that ReRoute achieves highly accurate counterfactual predictions in a controlled advection-diffusion system where exact responses are available, we turn to state-of-the-art climate emulation. On held-out coupled-climate interventions, ReRoute reduces aggregate climate error by 18.2-31.8% under severe CO$_2$ distribution shifts while preserving skill under standard conditions, at a small fraction of the cost of retraining on additional controlled simulations, without even accounting for the substantial expense of generating such data. Finally, on an emulator trained from historical ERA5 reanalysis, where no counterfactual reference exists, ReRoute preserves substantially more of the surface warming implied by the observed boundary conditions under a fixed-CO$_2$ counterfactual.

</details>


### [133] [Overcoming Challenges of Interpretive Structural Modeling with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02254)
*Everett Rush,David J. Icove,Ari Kim,Byung H. Park,Michael A. Langston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索将大语言模型（LLM）与解释结构建模（ISM）结合，用因果图发现方法替代传统依赖专家反复交互的建模过程，并比较了pairwise、k-wise、rowwise与full graph四种发现方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ISM建模需与领域专家反复交互直至达成共识，过程繁琐、劳动密集，且难以扩展到数百个变量的研究，因此需要一种可扩展的自动化方法。

Method: 借鉴将LLM视为"不完美专家"进行大规模因果图发现的已有工作，提出集成的LLM-ISM方法，并对比评估pairwise、k-wise、rowwise和full graph四种因果图发现策略。

Result: 在因果图发现评估中，rowwise方法表现最佳（SHD=160，F1=0.77），full graph方法次之（SHD=135，F1=0.73）。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的因果图发现可有效支撑ISM建模，rowwise与full graph方法具有较好潜力，为ISM向大规模变量研究扩展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Interpretive Structural Modeling (ISM) is a well-known process for multi-criteria decision making. The success of ISM over other methodologies is its ability to model causal relationships, the binary scale of factors, and resulting hierarchical representation. Traditionally, the modeling process is performed by repeated interactions with subject matter experts until consensus is reached. This process is tedious, labor-intense, and most importantly limits the ability of ISM to scale to studies with hundreds of variables.
