Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) supplies dense token-level feedback on trajectories sampled from the student's own policy, a richer training signal than the outcome-level rewards of reinforcement learning. This feedback comes from a teacher conditioned on a full reference solution unavailable to the student. The reference solution specifies the target but not how to move from the student's current error toward it, creating a solution-conditioned shortcut risk. We introduce AIR-OPD, an adaptive iterative repair framework for on-policy distillation that provides error-to-repair supervision. Given a failed response, a guidance generator synthesizes repair guidance for the current error. The student samples an on-policy retry with this guidance. If the retry remains incorrect, the generator produces new repair guidance for the newly observed error. At each round, a fixed teacher receives the guidance as privileged context and supervises the student on an error-aligned region of its latest failed response. Outcome-aware stage weighting favors early repair stages and credits stages whose immediate retry passes verification. We train AIR-OPD on the DAPO-Math-17K dataset and evaluate on AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25, alongside out-of-distribution tests on MMLU-Pro and GPQA. We examine two guidance sources, self-guidance from the current student policy and external guidance from a larger model. For both Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, AIR-OPD attains the best mathematical-reasoning averages, improving over the strongest baseline by up to 3.6 points, while preserving base-model performance on the out-of-distribution benchmarks.

</details>


### [203] [Conditional Capacity and Routing in Mixture-of-Experts Particle Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02701)
*Kaushik Pendiyala,Haris Zia,Trevin Lee,Timothy Legge,Alejandro J. De Leon,Zihan Zhao,Aaron Wang,Abhijith Gandrakota,Jennifer Ngadiuba,Richard Cavanaugh,Javier Duarte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在188类JetClass-II上系统比较稠密与MoE粒子Transformer，发现避免token dropping时top-1 MoE在近同等名义前向算力下优于稠密基线，增加存储专家数收益有限，多专家激活可进一步提升性能但算力增加，路由结构与分类性能非线性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE可在不按比例增加激活计算的前提下提升参数容量，但这一权衡在粒子物理Transformer中尚不明确，需要厘清存储参数容量、激活计算、路由容量与路由组织对喷注分类的影响。

Method: 在188类JetClass-II数据集上训练稠密与MoE粒子Transformer，系统变化专家数量、路由容量、top-K与辅助损失，并进行路由分析。

Result: 避免token dropping时，top-1 MoE在近同等名义前向算力下优于稠密基线；继续增加存储专家数带来的精度增益很小；每token激活多个专家以更高算力换取额外预测提升；路由分配与粒子身份和运动学特征的关联在某些配置下增强，但该结构并不随分类性能单调增加。

Conclusion: 评估喷注分类的稀疏专家模型时，需区分存储参数容量、激活计算、路由容量与路由组织；代码与实验配置开源于https://github.com/kpendiyala/MPT。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models can increase parameter capacity without proportionally increasing active computation, but it is unclear how this trade-off behaves in particle-physics transformers. We study dense and MoE Particle Transformers on 188-class JetClass-II, varying expert count, routing capacity, top-K, and auxiliary loss. We find that, when token dropping is avoided, top-1 MoE models improve over the dense baseline at nearly unchanged nominal forward compute, while further increasing the number of stored experts produces little additional accuracy gain. Activating multiple experts per token yields additional predictive improvements at higher computational cost. Routing analyses show that expert assignments become more strongly associated with particle identity and kinematics in some configurations, but this structure does not increase monotonically with classification performance. These results highlight the need to distinguish stored parameter capacity, active computation, routing capacity, and routing organization when evaluating sparse expert models for jet classification. Code and experiment configurations are available at https://github.com/kpendiyala/MPT.

</details>


### [204] [MuonIO: Principled Norm-Aware Descent for Embedding Tables and Language Model Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02705)
*Linkai Ma,Xinyu Luo,Mengbo Wang,Ananth Grama,Petros Drineas,Brian Bullins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MuonIO，为语言模型输入嵌入层和输出头层设计统一的 Muon 风格更新规则，替代标准 Muon 中使用的 AdamW，在降低内存与计算开销的同时提升验证困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Muon 优化器仅对隐藏线性层使用基于谱范数惩罚的局部线性化更新，而将输入嵌入表与输出语言模型头排除在外，改用 AdamW。这一处理缺乏与 Muon 一致的理论依据，导致优化器规则不统一且 I/O 层仍需维护 Adam 状态。

Method: 针对语言模型头 L∈R^{V×d}，基于 softmax 输出几何的 Lipschitz 连续性，采用 2→∞ 算子范数；针对嵌入表 E∈R^{d×V}，基于 one-hot 输入几何，采用 1→2 算子范数。利用恒等式 ‖L‖_{2→∞}=‖L^T‖_{1→2}，将两者统一到同一词表导向几何，应用单一归一化向量规则：对 E 为列归一化，对 L 为行归一化。

Result: 在 1B LLaMA 于 C4 上的预训练实验中，MuonIO 相比 Muon 将 I/O 优化器状态内存降低 50%，I/O 更新 FLOPs 降低约 46%，同时改善验证困惑度。

Conclusion: 通过选择与各层几何结构匹配的算子范数，MuonIO 为输入嵌入与输出头提供了统一的 Muon 风格更新规则，在保持甚至提升性能的同时显著降低 I/O 层的内存与计算成本。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer derives its update rule for hidden linear layers by solving a local linearization of the loss penalized by the spectral norm, motivated by an RMS-stability argument for dense linear layers. Standard Muon implementations, however, exclude the input (embedding table) and output (language model head) layers from this principled treatment, for which they use AdamW instead. We present MuonIO, a single Muon-style update for both of these layers. For the language model head $\mathbf{L} \in \mathbb{R}^{V \times d}$, we motivate the use of the $2\to\infty$ operator norm, due to the Lipschitz continuity of the softmax output geometry, while for the embedding table $\mathbf{E} \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times V}$, we draw on the $1 \to 2$ operator norm, based on the one-hot input geometry identified by Bernstein & Newhouse (2025). The identity $\lVert\mathbf{L}\rVert_{2\to\infty}=\lVert\mathbf{L}^\top\rVert_{1\to2}$ then puts both matrices in the same vocabulary-oriented geometry: MuonIO applies a single normalized-vector rule, which appears as column normalization for $\mathbf{E}$ and row normalization for $\mathbf{L}$. Empirical evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, with MuonIO reducing I/O optimizer state memory by 50% and I/O update FLOPs by $\sim$46% compared to Muon for 1B LLaMA pretraining on C4, while also improving validation perplexity.

</details>


### [205] [Differential Privacy of Gradient Descent on Perturbed Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02716)
*Austin Watkins,Raman Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究目标扰动下"释放有限步梯度下降迭代"的隐私与风险保证：证明在强凸、光滑且Hessian Lipschitz条件下，z↦w_N为C^1微分同胚并给出Jacobian最小奇异值下界，从而对有限迭代做变量替换分析；在广义线性模型中得到无显式环境维度因子的隐私刻画，有限迭代修正几何递减，并给出超额经验风险与总体风险界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标扰动通常释放精确的扰动最小化器，但实际只能通过有限步优化获得近似解；需刻画"释放第N次迭代"这一有限计算的隐私损失与统计代价，并说明其能否在N增大时逼近精确最小化器的证书。

Method: 对目标 F(w;S)+⟨z,w⟩（z~N(0,σ²I_d)，优化前一次性采样）做确定性梯度下降，研究迭代映射 z↦w_N。在强凸、光滑、Hessian-Lipschitz假设下，证明该映射在隐私论证所用的有界域上是C^1微分同胚，并给出Jacobian最小奇异值的定量下界，据此对有限迭代实施变量替换（change-of-variables）隐私分析。

Result: 在广义线性模型下，一旦迭代条件成立，所得隐私刻画（privacy-profile）界不含显式环境维度因子，且其有限迭代修正项几何递减；令自由截断参数随N缓慢增长，可在极限中恢复精确最小化器对应的证书。同时给出超额经验风险上界 dσ²/(2μ) 加一个几何衰减的优化项，并将结果迁移到总体风险，其主导统计项不含额外的条件数乘性因子。

Conclusion: 有限步梯度下降迭代可视为精确扰动最小化器的可控近似：在适当迭代条件下，隐私界与风险界均只需一个随N几何衰减的修正项，且在截断参数缓慢增长时收敛到精确最小化器的结果，为实际可计算的目标扰动机制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Objective perturbation adds a random linear term to a regularized empirical risk and releases the exact perturbed minimizer. We study the finite computation obtained by releasing the $N$-th iterate of deterministic gradient descent on $w\mapsto F(w;S)+\langle z,w\rangle$, where $z\sim\mathcal N(0,σ^2I_d)$ is drawn once before optimization. For strongly convex and smooth objectives with Lipschitz Hessian, we prove an explicit condition under which the map $z\mapsto w_N$ is a $C^1$-diffeomorphism on the bounded domains used in the privacy argument, with a quantitative lower bound on the smallest singular value of its Jacobian. This permits a direct change-of-variables analysis of the finite iterate. For generalized linear models, the resulting privacy-profile bound has no explicit ambient-dimension factor once the iteration condition holds, and its finite-iteration correction decreases geometrically. By letting the free truncation parameter grow slowly with $N$, we recover the corresponding exact-minimizer certificate in the limit. We also bound the expected excess empirical risk by $dσ^2/(2μ)$ plus a geometrically decreasing optimization term, and transfer the result to population risk without an additional multiplicative condition-number factor in the leading statistical terms.

</details>


### [206] [Structural-Functional Brain Connectivity Generation via Multimodal Hypergraph-based Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02722)
*Chyong Yi Poh,Hwa Hui Tew,Junn Yong Loo,Raphaël C. -W. Phan,Fuad Noman,Pew-Thian Yap,Chee-Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MHG-FM框架，用超图与流匹配实现SC-FC联合生成与跨模态翻译，性能优于SOTA且采样快约8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SC与FC提供互补信息，但大规模配对SC-FC数据稀缺；现有生成方法基于成对图、仅刻画二元交互，且SC与FC独立生成，难以保留高阶结构-功能关系。

Method: 构建模态特异超图，用超图神经网络（HGNN）编码器学习高阶表示，通过双重交叉注意力（DCA）进行双向跨模态融合；用变分自编码器将融合表示映射到紧凑隐空间，在其中以条件流匹配实现连接合成与多模态翻译（隐空间传输）。

Result: 在HCP-YA数据集上，MHG-FM在重建质量、拓扑保持、分布相似性和SC-FC耦合方面优于多个SOTA基线，采样速度约为同构扩散骨干的8倍。

Conclusion: 超图建模与流匹配结合可有效捕捉高阶结构-功能关系，实现高效的SC-FC联合生成与跨模态翻译。

Abstract: Structural connectivity (SC) and functional connectivity (FC) provide complementary information on interactions between brain regions and are widely used in neuroimaging studies of neuropsychiatric disorders. Generative modelling can alleviate the scarcity of large-scale paired SC-FC data, but existing approaches typically use pairwise graphs that capture only dyadic interactions and often generate SC and FC independently, limiting preservation of higher-order structure-function relationships. We propose a Multimodal Hypergraph Flow Matching (MHG-FM) framework for joint SC-FC connectivity generation and cross-modal translation. MHG-FM constructs modality-specific hypergraphs, learns higher-order representations with Hypergraph Neural Network (HGNN) encoders, and performs bidirectional cross-modal fusion using Dual Cross-Attention (DCA). A variational autoencoder maps the fused representations to a compact latent space, where conditional flow matching enables connectivity synthesis and multimodal translation via latent transport. Experiments on the Human Connectome Project Young Adult (HCP-YA) dataset show that MHG-FM outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines in reconstruction quality, topology preservation, distributional similarity, and SC-FC coupling, while achieving approximately 8x faster sampling than a matched diffusion backbone.

</details>


### [207] [Bellman Error Minimization Via Linear Programming Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02730)
*Haining Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的函数逼近方法，以减少高维动态规划与强化学习问题中的Bellman误差，并以收益管理中的网络容量控制问题为动机案例，展示深度神经网络与线性规划逼近算法相结合可求得动态规划问题的近似解，仿真结果表明该算法性能与基准方法具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维动态规划与强化学习问题中存在显著的Bellman误差，传统逼近方法难以有效求解，亟需新的函数逼近途径以提升近似解的精度与可行性。

Method: 提出一种新的函数逼近方法，将深度神经网络与线性规划逼近算法相结合；以经典的动态规划问题——收益管理中的网络容量控制——作为动机案例，构建动态规划问题的近似求解框架，并通过仿真实验与基准方法进行对比评估。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的逼近算法与基准方法相比取得了具有竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络与线性规划逼近算法的结合为高维动态规划问题提供了一条可行的近似求解途径，能够有效降低Bellman误差，并在网络容量控制等实际问题中展现出良好应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new functional approximation approach to reduce Bellman error in high-dimensional dynamic programming and Reinforcement Learning problems. Using a classic dynamic programming problem (network capacity control in revenue management) as the motivational example, the paper illustrates that deep neural networks and linear programming approximation algorithms can be combined to derive approximate solutions to dynamic programming problems. Simulation results show the proposed approximation algorithms achieves competitive performance when compared with benchmark.

</details>


### [208] [Inner Momentum for Differentially Private Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02738)
*Bishnu Bhusal,Minh Vu,Ben Southworth,Geigh Zollicoffer,Rohit Chadha,Manish Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对差分隐私训练中梯度裁剪扭曲Muon优化器奇异向量几何的问题，提出DP-Muon-IM：在裁剪前将每个样本的Muon梯度在当前模型与近期模型历史上做平均，并在理论上证明其能限制裁剪引发的失真且经Newton-Schulz迭代后仍保持极因子，实验上在私密GPT-2微调中稳定提升BLEU/ROUGE-L。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私训练逐样本地按半径裁剪梯度，但各样本裁剪因子不等，会扭曲平均后梯度的相对奇异向量几何。Muon的更新近似为极因子UV^T，仅依赖奇异向量，因此对这种裁剪引发的几何失真尤为敏感。作者希望缓解该退化，保护Muon在隐私训练中的更新方向。

Method: 提出DP-Muon-IM（历史平均）。在裁剪前，将每个采样样本的Muon梯度在当前模型及一段近期模型历史上做平均，再执行裁剪。分析表明，裁剪后的批次矩阵可分解为公共重缩放与一个协方差残差R（样本梯度与裁剪值之间的协方差），且||R||_F <= sigma_lambda sigma_G，从而直接给出裁剪所致失真的上界。作者进一步证明，在此谱条件下，有限步Newton-Schulz迭代保持输入的极因子，说明该修正能经受正交化。

Result: 在E2E与DART上对GPT-2进行差分隐私微调，epsilon取{1,2,4,8}，DP-Muon-IM在每一组相同种子对比中都提升了BLEU与ROUGE-L；非隐私诊断显示噪声前极误差降低2-4%。

Conclusion: 通过裁剪前对Muon梯度做模型历史平均，可有效抑制DP裁剪对奇异向量几何的扭曲，并在有限Newton-Schulz迭代下保持极因子，从而在多个隐私预算下稳定优于DP-Muon，为隐私训练中的Muon优化提供了理论保证与实证支持。

Abstract: Differentially private training clips each per-example gradient before adding noise. This clipping is radial for each example, yet unequal clipping factors can distort the relative singular-vector geometry of their average. Muon is particularly exposed to this effect, since its update is an approximate polar factor UV^T that depends only on the singular vectors that clipping can shift. To curb this degradation, we propose averaging each sampled example's Muon gradient over the current model and a short history of recent models before clipping. The clipped batch matrix then separates into a common rescaling and a covariance residual R between sampled gradients and clipping values, with ||R||_F <= sigma_lambda sigma_G, bounding the clipping-induced distortion directly. We further show that a finite Newton-Schulz iteration preserves the polar factor of its input under these spectral conditions, confirming that our correction survives orthogonalization. In private GPT-2 fine-tuning on E2E and DART at epsilon in {1, 2, 4, 8}, DP-Muon-IM improves BLEU and ROUGE-L over DP-Muon in every seed-matched comparison, and non-private diagnostics show 2-4% lower pre-noise polar error.

</details>


### [209] [Prospective Hindsight: Self-Calibrating Reinforcement Learning via Prediction-Reality Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02740)
*Jiaxin Zhang,Xiangyu Peng,Qinglin Chen,Yu Li,Hiroaki Hayashi,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"前瞻性后见"(Prospective Hindsight, PH)自校准训练原则：利用动作时刻的前瞻预测与事后回报评估之差(惊喜信号)对样本梯度加权，使策略与前瞻预测器共同演化，在提升任务性能的同时实现校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程强化学习仅依赖纯事后训练信号，信用分配只在观察到环境后果后进行，导致智能体在动作时刻的信念对梯度不可见，模型自校准问题被忽视。

Method: 在任意事后基方法上增加一路信号：计算单次 rollout 中"前瞻预测(反馈前)"与"事后评估(反馈后)"之间的差距作为惊喜量；通过 stop-gradient 的惊喜加权优势函数，放大自模型最不准确样本的梯度贡献。前瞻预测器与策略共享参数、协同演化，逐步聚焦剩余盲区。理论上将其与特权信息差距联系，证明最小化惊喜残差可在失校准率上提供下降路径。

Result: 在单轮可验证任务与多轮个人智能体任务上(基于 GRPO、在线策略蒸馏及其组合)均提升任务性能与校准度，且跨模型规模取得一致增益。主要失校准模式随场景结构变化：单轮表现为过度自信的失败，多轮表现为信心不足的成功，而同一训练原则均能有效应对。

Conclusion: PH 将校准作为优化的副产品而非附加目标，无需额外校准损失即可同时改善性能与校准，具备跨任务、跨算法、跨模型规模的通用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for long-horizon agents relies on purely retrospective training signals: credit is assigned only after observing environmental consequences, leaving the agent's belief at action time invisible to the gradient. We introduce Prospective Hindsight (PH), a self-calibrating training principle that augments any retrospective base method with a signal derived from the gap between the agent's prospective prediction (before feedback) and the retrospective evaluation (after feedback). This per-rollout surprise identifies samples where the agent's self-model is most inaccurate and amplifies their gradient contribution through a stop-gradient surprise-weighted advantage. Since the prospective predictor shares parameters with the policy, the two co-evolve, progressively shifting focus to the agent's remaining blind spots. We connect this principle to a privileged-information gap and show that minimizing the surprise residual provides a descent pathway on the agent's miscalibration rate; calibration thus emerges as a byproduct of optimization rather than from an added objective. On single-turn verifiable tasks and a multi-turn personal-agent task (under GRPO, on-policy distillation, and their combination), PH improves both task performance and calibration, with consistent gains across model scales. Notably, the dominant miscalibration mode shifts structurally between regimes, overconfident failures in single-turn, underconfident successes in multi-turn, yet the same training principle addresses both successfully.

</details>


### [210] [Jumping up and down: Denoiser diffusion models for discrete ordinal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02754)
*Yair Shenfeld,Ricardo Baptista,Stefano Peluchetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 JUD（Jumping Up and Down），首个面向离散有序数据的、基于去噪器的扩散模型家族，支持双向（升/降）扰动，训练目标简单，在多种模态上取得有竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续空间（图像、视频）的扩散模型已高度成熟，面向类别数据的离散扩散模型在语言领域也取得重大进展；但面向离散整数型（有序）数据的扩散模型发展不足，尽管该模态广泛存在于图像、音乐、基因计数等领域。因此需要一套专门针对离散有序数据的扩散建模方法。

Method: 提出 JUD（Jumping Up and Down），一类新的基于去噪器（denoiser-based）的扩散模型家族，面向离散有序数据。其核心在于以训练去噪器为中心，并同时支持数据上的双向（向上与向下）扰动。

Result: 得益于训练目标的简洁性与双向扰动的灵活性，JUD 在多种数据模态上取得了具有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: JUD 填补了离散有序数据扩散建模的空白，验证了去噪器中心范式与双向扰动设计的有效性，具备跨模态的通用性与竞争力。

Abstract: Diffusion models are highly developed in continuous spaces for image and video domains. Recently, major advances have been made for discrete diffusion models for categorical data, specifically in the language domain. In contrast, diffusion models for discrete integer-valued data are less developed, despite the prevalence of this modality, ranging from images and music to gene counts. We introduce Jumping Up and Down (JUD)---a new family of denoiser-based diffusion models for discrete ordinal data. This is the first family of diffusion models for ordinal data which centers around training denoisers, which at the same time allows for bi-directional (up and down) perturbations of the data. The simplicity of the training objective, combined with the flexibility of bi-directional perturbations, leads us to obtain competitive results across different data modalities.

</details>


### [211] [A Two-Stage Cascade for Near-Real-Time Forest Anomaly Detection from Sentinel-1 SAR Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02763)
*Pann Thinzar Seint,Subas Chhatkuli,Bryan Atwood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统计检验与自编码器级联的双阶段哨兵-1时序近实时森林损失异常检测系统，兼顾光学云限制与SAR季节性干扰，输出可审计置信度与面积估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带森林监测对全球气候稳定与生物多样性保护至关重要，非法采伐干预、供应链透明、土地利用治理及碳市场标准均需快速可靠地检测森林损失。但遥感监测面临两大障碍：光学观测受云覆盖限制，SAR后向散射季节性变化会使系统将自然湿度变化误判为森林损失。

Method: 采用两阶段“统计—编码器”级联架构，基于Sentinel-1 VH时序。第一阶段：对配准后的VH后向散射，结合同季节历史基线，执行自适应稳健统计z-score检验；第二阶段：以稳定森林斑块训练的卷积自编码器，在潜空间计算结构相似性（SSIM）作为学习式确认门。仅当两级一致同意时，候选扰动才被确认为告警，并依据重复发生历史赋予置信度分数及低/中/高三级风险分层。

Result: 系统可近实时输出逐告警的可审计置信度分数与公顷面积估计，直接兼容MRV（监测、报告与核查）工作流、可持续森林管理、实地核查与环境风险评估。除主要应用外，模型具备灵活性，可扩展至选择性采伐、大规模农业侵占制图、洪涝制图等环境应用。

Conclusion: 统计检验与学习式确认门的双引擎级联能有效克服云覆盖与SAR季节性混淆两大挑战，在保持高保真检测的同时提供可审计、可量化的告警输出，具有良好的可迁移性。

Abstract: Tropical forest monitoring is essential for global climate stability and biodiversity preservation. To address the urgent need for rapid, reliable detection of forest loss which is essential for timely intervention against illegal logging, supply chain transparency, land-use governance and carbon market standards, we introduce a two-stage statistics-encoder cascade for near-real-time anomaly detection using Sentinel-1 time series. Our system is designed to overcome two fundamental challenges in remote sensing: the cloud-cover limitations that restrict optical monitoring and seasonal backscatter variation that causes SAR systems to mistake natural moisture changes for forest loss. The architecture integrates two distinct analytical engines to ensure high-fidelity detection: (1) an adaptive, robust-statistics z-score test on co-registered Sentinel-1 VH backscatter, same-season historical baseline and (2) a learned confirmation gate based on the latent-space structural similarity (SSIM) of a convolutional autoencoder trained on stable-forest patches. A candidate disturbance is confirmed as an alert only when both stages agree, and is assigned a confidence score and a Low/Medium/High risk tier from its repeat-occurrence history. The system produces per-alert auditable confidence scores and area-in-hectares estimates directly compatible with Monitoring, Reporting and Verification (MRV) workflows, sustainable forestry management, operational field checks and environmental risk assessments. Beyond its primary application, the model's flexibility allows for critical environmental applications ranging from selective logging to large-scale agricultural encroachment mapping, flood mapping and so on.

</details>


### [212] [A Controlled Audit of Personal AI Memory for Rating Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02764)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过置换用户历史评分（保留分布、物品支持度与元数据）并对比完整历史、Mem0 记忆抽取与匹配数值读取器，审计结构化评分预测中个性化 AI 究竟依赖历史物品-评分关联还是用户评分倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化评分预测中，个性化 AI 系统的性能来源不明确：它可能真正利用历史物品-评分关联，也可能只是捕捉用户的整体评分倾向。若不区分二者，将无法判断记忆抽取、关联使用、输出可靠性与读取器选择各自的作用，导致评估结论混淆。

Method: 采用用户内历史评分置换作为控制手段，保持评分分布、物品支持度与元数据不变；结合完整历史、原生记忆抽取（Qwen 撰写的 Mem0 流水线）与匹配数值读取器，在公开冻结评估中进行对比，覆盖 Coat 与 MovieLens 的 400 个留出用户画像和 6,160 个目标评分。所有 2,800 次读取调用（含 150 个无效输出）按固定回退规则保留，并由独立实现验证输入、指标及全部十项主要对比。

Result: 在 Coat 上，Mem0 流水线相对完整历史使 user-macro MAE 增加：Qwen 为 0.084，Phi 为 0.149，且经家族调整的 bootstrap 区间均排除零。正确历史赋值对两个读取器在 Coat 上均有帮助；MovieLens 对应效应较小，调整后不具结论性。仅用历史的 ridge 读取器在两个领域均优于 Qwen，在 MovieLens 上优于 Phi；Coat 上 Phi 的对比未决。

Conclusion: 该可复现诊断研究表明，在结构化评分预测中必须将记忆抽取、历史关联使用、输出可靠性与读取器选择分开评估；单纯引入个性化记忆流水线未必优于完整历史，且效应具有数据集依赖性。

Abstract: In structured rating prediction, does a personal AI use historical item-rating associations, or mainly the user's rating tendencies? We audit this distinction by permuting historical ratings within each user while preserving the exact rating distribution, item support, and metadata. We combine this control with full history, native memory extraction, and matched numerical readers in a publicly frozen evaluation of 400 held-out user profiles and 6,160 target ratings across Coat and MovieLens. On Coat, the tested Qwen-written Mem0 pipeline increases user-macro mean absolute error relative to full history by 0.084 for Qwen and 0.149 for Phi; both family-adjusted bootstrap intervals exclude zero. Correct historical assignments help both readers on Coat, but the corresponding MovieLens effects are smaller and inconclusive after adjustment. A history-only ridge reader outperforms Qwen in both domains and Phi on MovieLens, while the Coat Phi comparison is unresolved. All 2,800 reader calls, including 150 invalid outputs, are retained under a fixed fallback rule. A separate implementation verifies inputs, metrics, and all ten primary contrasts. The contribution is a reproducible diagnostic study showing why extraction, association use, output reliability, and reader choice require separate evaluation.

</details>


### [213] [Exact Memory-Time Optimization for Prefix-Cached Language Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02766)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出前缀证书保留(PCR)模型，将静态分组超时缓存保留问题精确建模为最大权闭合问题，可用一次最小割求解；并给出有序超时的线性时间动态规划与转移性实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型前缀状态的保留需要在重计算与存储时间之间权衡。独立优化每个缓存块会高估收益，因为一个常驻块只有在所需的前序前缀也可用时才真正可用，即存在前缀依赖关系。因此需要一种能精确刻画前缀可用性的保留策略优化模型。

Method: 提出Prefix-Certificate Retention(PCR)：针对静态、分组、访问即重置的有限迹超时形式化。将可用前缀收益建模为节点，其前置条件为超时阈值与前序命中证书，从而化为最大权闭合问题并归约为单一最小割，图规模与块查找次数及超时选择数量线性相关。用断点定理解除离散化限制，扩展到任意非负超时；对有序超时导出网格规模线性的动态规划，并给出界定该限制代价的界。用穷举小实例与39,632条Mooncake公开请求的时序回放进行验证。

Result: 在固定网格上，有序超时在120个迹-分组-价格情形中的118个达到无限制训练最优。异构保留改善了若干留出集上的内存-时间权衡，但更细的训练优化并未一致地提升迁移效果。实验以可用前缀块数与存储时间衡量，而非GPU延迟。

Conclusion: 贡献是一个可处理的优化模型与可审计的保留策略基准；PCR能精确处理前缀依赖，避免独立优化导致的收益高估，并提供有序超时的近似与界，为缓存保留策略的优化与评测提供统一框架。

Abstract: Retaining language-model prefix states trades recomputation against storage time. Optimizing each cached block independently can overcount savings: a resident block is usable only when the required preceding prefix is also available. We introduce Prefix-Certificate Retention (PCR), an exact finite-trace formulation for static, grouped, reset-on-access timeouts. Usable-prefix rewards become nodes whose prerequisites are timeout thresholds and preceding hit certificates. The resulting maximum-weight closure reduces to one minimum cut, with graph size linear in the number of block lookups and timeout choices. A breakpoint theorem extends the construction to all nonnegative timeouts without discretization error. We also derive a linear-time-in-grid-size dynamic program for ordered timeouts and bounds that certify the cost of this restriction. Exhaustive small-instance checks and chronological replay of 39,632 public Mooncake requests validate the formulation. On the fixed grid, ordered timeouts attain the unrestricted training optimum in 118 of 120 trace-grouping-price cases. Heterogeneous retention improves several held-out memory-time tradeoffs, but finer training optimization does not uniformly improve transfer. The contribution is a tractable optimization model and an auditable benchmark for retention policies; the experiments measure usable prefix blocks and storage time, not GPU latency.

</details>


### [214] [No-Free-Graph: Learning When Multimodal Data Should Be Graphified](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02768)
*Zekai Chen,Kai Hu,YuXin Zeng,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Yinlin Zhu,Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MAG-SCOUT，一个在构建完整图拓扑之前评估"图化"是否值得的预构建判断框架，通过采集有限关系证据估计预期效用与构建成本，实现build-or-skip决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态图学习研究聚焦于如何构建与优化图，却忽视了更根本的问题：对给定数据集和任务，是否应引入额外关系结构。实证表明图化并非总是有益，收益可能有限甚至为负，因此图构建应作为基于预期效用的选择性决策，而非默认的预处理步骤。

Method: MAG-SCOUT是一种预构建图评估框架：在生成完整拓扑之前，先采集有限的关系证据，分析其针对任务特定目标的潜在贡献，并联合估计图化的预期效用与构建成本，据此做出"构建或跳过"的决策。

Result: 在六个多模态数据集、三个下游任务及多种图构造器上的实验表明，MAG-SCOUT能有效识别何时应引入图结构，在预先设定的下限约束下节省了33.1%的任务宏观图工作量，同时保留96.7%的留出正收益质量。

Conclusion: 图构建应被视为一种基于预期效用的选择性决策，而非默认预处理步骤；MAG-SCOUT通过预构建评估有效避免了无益的图化开销。

Abstract: Multimodal graph learning has recently emerged as an effective paradigm for in corporating inter-entity relationships into multimodal representations. Existing studies have made substantial progress on how to construct and optimize graphs, but rarely consider a more fundamental question: whether additional relational structures should be introduced for a given dataset and task. Through empirical studies across diverse datasets, tasks, and graph constructors, we reveal that graphification is not consistently beneficial: introducing relational structures can provide substantial improvements in some cases, while offering limited or even negative gains. This observation motivates a new perspective that graph construction should be treated as a selective decision based on its expected utility rather than a default preprocessing step. To address this issue, we propose MAG-SCOUT, a pre-construction graph assessment framework that estimates whether introducing graph structures is beneficial before generating the complete topology. MAG-SCOUT collects limited relational evidence, analyzes its potential taskspecific contribution, and estimates the expected utility of graphification together with construction cost to make a build-or-skip decision. Extensive experiments across six multimodal datasets, three downstream tasks, and diverse graph constructors demonstrate that MAG-SCOUT effectively identifies when graph structures should be introduced, saving 33.1% of task-macro graph work while retaining 96.7% of held-out positive-gain mass under the pre-registered floor.

</details>


### [215] [Nearly Optimal Fixed-Confidence Best-Arm Identification with 1-Bit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02771)
*Khang Luong,Dinh Thai Son,Hoang Ta,Hung The Tran,Tuan Quang Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究严格1比特反馈下的固定置信度最优臂识别问题，提出基于随机阈值查询与截断尾积分恒等式的时间一致均值估计原语，并嵌入候选-挑战者算法，给出固定截断与分阶段自适应截断两种算法及其信息论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格1比特反馈（每轮仅收到奖励是否属于查询集的单比特信息）下，无法直接进行经验均值估计，截断不可避免。既有方法依赖完整奖励观测，缺少适用于有限方差、分布无关设定且具有时间一致性的均值估计原语及相应的最优臂识别算法与理论下界。

Method: 1) 建立基于随机化阈值查询和截断尾积分恒等式的时间一致1比特均值估计原语；2) 将该原语嵌入候选-挑战者式最优臂识别算法；3) 提出固定截断算法，给出简洁的anytime (ε,δ)-PAC保证；4) 提出分阶段自适应截断算法，将截断水平匹配当前分辨率，实现间隙自适应样本复杂度；5) 证明K臂最坏情况下的信息论下界。

Result: 固定截断算法给出anytime (ε,δ)-PAC保证；分阶段自适应截断算法获得间隙自适应样本复杂度；所证K臂最坏情况下界表明有限方差1比特反馈带来的对数惩罚是本质性的，且与分阶段算法的主项依赖相匹配，仅相差低阶log log因子。

Conclusion: 在严格1比特反馈与有限方差、分布无关设定下，通过截断尾积分原语与自适应截断策略可实现具有理论保证、间隙自适应的最优臂识别，且其样本复杂度的对数惩罚由信息论下界证明为不可避免。

Abstract: We study fixed-confidence best-arm identification under strict 1-bit feedback constraints. At each round, the learner selects an arm and a query set, and receives only a single bit indicating whether the sampled reward belongs to that set. We consider a distribution-free finite-variance setting with arm-wise localization, where direct empirical mean estimation is no longer available and clipping becomes unavoidable. We first formulate a time-uniform 1-bit mean-estimation primitive based on randomized threshold queries and a clipped tail-integral identity. We then embed this primitive into candidate-challenger best-arm identification algorithms. A fixed-clipping algorithm gives a simple anytime $(ε,δ)$-PAC guarantee, while a phased adaptive-clipping algorithm matches the clipping level to the current resolution and yields a gap-adaptive sample complexity. We also prove a $K$-arm worst-case information-theoretic lower bound showing that the logarithmic penalty caused by finite-variance 1-bit feedback is intrinsic. This bound matches the leading dependence of the phased algorithm up to lower-order $\log\log$ factors.

</details>


### [216] [LatticeSMC: Where to Spend Inference-Time Compute in Chunked Sequence Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02774)
*Xuanchen Wang,Heng Wang,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“预算匹配的分块引导”评测协议与LatticeSMC采样器，在“块索引×去噪步”二维格上构建Feynman-Kac模型，实现长序列生成（音乐、动作、视频）中奖励引导的推理期引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列生成器逐块生成、块内迭代去噪，但奖励定义在整个序列上。现有推理期引导方法（best-of-N、Feynman-Kac步级引导、块级流式剪枝）各自只作用于单一维度，且常在不同算力或不同回报规则下比较，缺乏公平且统一的框架。

Method: 提出LatticeSMC：在块索引与去噪步构成的二维格上定义Feynman-Kac模型。两个“套叠（telescoping）”结论使设计精确：(1) 对块可加奖励，两轴诱导相同权重，故应在前瞻代价最低处重采样；(2) 对终端奖励，任意前缀分数给出精确的中间势函数，使前缀可评估奖励成为无需估计、无需额外去噪调用的twist。采样器在块边界、以及打分免费时在块内，基于这些势函数重采样，返回加权样本或最佳粒子。

Result: 在算力匹配条件下：音乐到舞蹈扩散任务上节拍对齐从0.234提升到0.441（best-of-N为0.354）；40秒文本到音乐生成上提示遵循度从0.470提升到0.560（32个粒子），同时保持留出集质量。在长程奖励上仍保持优势，并在60–77%的成对比较中被人类评分者偏好。

Conclusion: 承诺强度应跟随当前势函数所包含的信息，而前瞻的价值可由未来奖励的“集合内可预测性”来预测。LatticeSMC为长序列生成提供了算力匹配、维度统一的推理期引导框架。

Abstract: Long-form generators for music, motion and video produce sequences chunk by chunk, with each chunk generated by iterative denoising while rewards are defined over the full sequence. Existing inference-time steering methods typically act on one axis at a time: best-of-N at the end, Feynman-Kac steering across denoising steps, or streaming pruning across chunks, and are often compared under unmatched compute or different return rules. We introduce budget-matched chunked steering and propose LatticeSMC, a sampler derived from a Feynman-Kac model on the two-dimensional lattice of chunk index and denoising step. Two telescoping results make its design exact: for chunk-additive rewards, the two axes induce identical weights, so resampling should occur where lookahead is cheapest; for terminal rewards, any prefix score defines an exact intermediate potential, making prefix-evaluable rewards twists with no estimation or extra denoiser calls. LatticeSMC resamples on these potentials at chunk boundaries and, when scoring is free, within chunks, returning either a weighted draw or the best particle. Under matched compute, on music-to-dance diffusion and 40-second text-to-music generation, it raises beat alignment from 0.234 to 0.441 (best-of-N: 0.354) and prompt adherence from 0.470 to 0.560 at 32 particles, while preserving held-out quality. It also retains its advantage on long-range rewards and is preferred by human raters in 60-77 percent of pairwise comparisons. Finally, we show that commitment strength should follow the information in the current potential, while the value of lookahead is predicted by the within-set predictability of future reward.

</details>


### [217] [Controlling Polar Exposure to Delay Memorization in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02780)
*Xuanchen Wang,Heng Wang,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出质量门控去白化控制器QGD与Copy-Budgeted Selection校准方法，旨在延长扩散模型的泛化窗口，在保持质量到达时间的同时扩大可用区间并降低复制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在复制训练样本之前即可达到可用的样本质量，但快速的优化会加速样本特异性的拟合，从而压缩这一泛化窗口。研究希望理解并缓解这一现象，以改善质量与复制之间的权衡。

Method: 通过更新几何的视角研究该效应，提出Quality-Gated De-whitening（QGD）控制器：保留快速的极坐标更新前缀，并逐步恢复固定增益动量。随机特征分析区分了协方差控制、曲率均衡与幅度控制的记忆时钟，在对齐谱假设下给出有限曝光条件，使固定增益尾部恢复与数据集规模成正比的延迟。QGD结合已确认的质量门、有界衰减包络与因果复制反馈；即时切换为保守极限，渐进控制平衡延迟复制与持续质量提升。同时配以Copy-Budgeted Selection（CBS），对冻结的检查点族进行同步二项校准，并对发布检查点做全新评估。

Result: 在2,000张图像的CIFAR-10子集上，QGD在保持极坐标基线质量到达时间的同时，将可用区间扩大8.32倍，并将常见检查点复制减少75.9%。在相同校准与独立质量评估下，QGD达到FID 75.56，而使用相同选择器的SGD为79.37。曝光匹配对照、独立检测器审计以及向流匹配与舞蹈生成的迁移进一步支持了结果。

Conclusion: 自适应曝光控制是改善质量-复制权衡的一种实用方法，可在大幅扩展泛化窗口的同时降低复制而不牺牲质量到达时间。

Abstract: Diffusion models can reach useful sample quality before copying training examples, but fast optimization can compress this generalization window by accelerating sample-specific fitting. We investigate this effect through update geometry and propose Quality-Gated De-whitening (QGD), a controller that retains a fast polar-update prefix and progressively restores fixed-gain momentum. Our random-feature analysis separates covariance-controlled, curvature-equalized and amplitude-controlled memorization clocks. Under aligned spectral assumptions, it establishes a finite-exposure condition under which a fixed-gain tail recovers a delay proportional to dataset size. QGD implements this principle with a confirmed quality gate, a bounded decay envelope and causal copy feedback. Immediate switching is the conservative limit; gradual control balances delayed copying against continued quality improvement. We pair QGD with Copy-Budgeted Selection (CBS), which applies simultaneous binomial calibration to a frozen checkpoint family, followed by a fresh evaluation of the released checkpoint. On 2,000-image CIFAR-10 subsets, QGD preserves the polar baseline's quality-arrival time while expanding its useful interval by 8.32x and reducing common-checkpoint copying by 75.9%. With identical calibration and independent quality evaluation, QGD achieves FID 75.56 versus 79.37 for SGD with the same selector. Exposure-matched controls, independent detector audits and transfer to flow matching and dance generation support adaptive exposure control as a practical way to improve the quality-copying tradeoff.

</details>


### [218] [OPD Before RL: Warm-Starting Rubric-Based RL with On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02781)
*Xinpeng Wang,Wei Shi,Yu-Chia Chen,Maria Zontak,Yun He,Richard Yuanzhe Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架：先以评分标准作为特权教师上下文进行on-policy蒸馏（RP-OPD），再用评分标准奖励做RL，在健康与科学任务上取得最优表现并减少奖励入侵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于评分标准（rubric）的RL可评估开放式回答，但奖励在整段回答结束后才给出，训练信号无法直接定位哪些具体决策贡献了最终分数。

Method: 两阶段训练：第一阶段RP-OPD，让无法访问rubric的学生模型在学生自生成前缀上匹配rubric感知教师的下一词分布，形成稠密token级监督；第二阶段用rubric奖励直接做RL，突破蒸馏平台期。

Result: 在HealthBench、ResearchQA、RubricHub Science上以开放权重模型比较多种后训练方法并改变RL前SFT或RP-OPD的训练量，两阶段框架得分最高；RP-OPD+RL在RubricHub Science上奖励入侵迹象有限，而SFT+RL基线越来越多地仅凭“声称符合rubric”却未提供所需内容而获得高奖励。

Conclusion: 支持先用rubric引导on-policy蒸馏，再进行基于rubric的RL。

Abstract: Many useful language-model tasks cannot be evaluated by exact outcome verification. Rubric-based reinforcement learning (RL) addresses this issue by scoring open-ended responses against explicit criteria. However, because the reward is assigned after the complete response, the training signal does not directly identify which individual decisions contributed to the final score. We propose a two-stage training framework that uses rubrics first as privileged teacher context for dense token-level supervision, then as rewards for further RL. In the first stage, rubric-privileged on-policy distillation (RP-OPD), a student without access to the rubric matches a rubric-aware teacher's next-token distributions at student-generated prefixes. In the second stage, RL directly optimizes the rubric reward and improves beyond the observed distillation plateau. We evaluate the framework on health and science tasks using open-weight models. Across HealthBench, ResearchQA, and RubricHub Science, we compare post-training methods and vary the amount of SFT or RP-OPD training before RL, finding that our two-stage framework achieves the highest scores among the methods evaluated. RP-OPD + RL shows limited signs of reward hacking on RubricHub Science, whereas the SFT + RL baseline increasingly receives high rewards for claims of rubric compliance without providing the required content. These findings support using rubrics to guide on-policy distillation before applying rubric-based RL.

</details>


### [219] [Efficient Memory Crystallization for Graph Learning under Non-Stationary Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02795)
*Yue Hou,Ruomei Liu,Yingke Su,Junran Wu,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EMC：一种免训练的测试时框架，通过闭式解将各图域蒸馏为紧凑记忆，实现高效持续的图域适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实系统中的深度图学习模型常面临非平稳环境，图分布随时间持续漂移。现有方法依赖训练辅助生成模块合成记忆图以进行跨域适应，计算开销大、在长时间分布漂移下扩展性差。作者认为无需生成记忆，而可将其"结晶"。

Method: 提出高效记忆结晶（EMC），一个免训练的测试时框架：通过面向记忆的分布匹配目标的闭式解，将每个到来的图域蒸馏为紧凑且语义忠实的记忆，从而在持续协变量漂移下消除冗余域信息；并通过状态演化记忆建模域间依赖关系。

Result: 在非平稳分布漂移图上，EMC性能优于最先进基线；相比近期竞争方法，平均运行时间减少87.4%，GPU内存消耗减少92.4%。

Conclusion: EMC兼顾泛化性与适应性，具有理论上更紧的泛化误差界，使大规模持续图适应变得实用。

Abstract: Deep graph learning models deployed in real-world systems often need to cope with non-stationary environments, where the underlying graph distribution drifts continually over time. Prevailing solutions rely on training auxiliary generative modules to synthesize memory graphs for cross-domain adaptation, which incurs substantial computational overhead and scales poorly under prolonged distribution shifts. We argue that a more economical path exists: rather than generating memory, one can crystallize it. To this end, we propose Efficient Memory Crystallization (EMC), a training-free test-time framework that distills each incoming graph domain into a compact, semantically faithful memory through a closed-form solution to a memory-oriented distribution-matching objective, thereby eliminating redundant domain information under continual covariate shifts. To preserve both generalizability and adaptability as the model traverses a long sequence of target domains, EMC further models inter-domain dependencies through state-evolving memories and admits a theoretically grounded, tighter generalization error bound than direct adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of EMC over state-of-the-art baselines on graphs under non-stationary distribution shifts, while reducing average runtime by 87.4% and GPU memory consumption by 92.4% relative to the recent competitor, making continual graph adaptation practical at scale.

</details>


### [220] [Muon Learns Facts Better: Understanding the Role of Spectral Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02798)
*Xuheng Li,Qiwei Di,Yuan Cao,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过一个可解析的事实回忆模型，从机制层面刻画了 Muon 优化器（谱梯度流）如何改变特征学习动态：相比普通梯度流，谱梯度流显著缩小了主体特征与关系特征的学习时间差距，并加快误差衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 优化器对矩阵值更新施加谱正交化，在大型神经网络训练中表现优异，但其在特征学习中的机制尚不清楚，尤其缺乏对训练过程中特征分离现象的理论解释。

Method: 构建可解析的事实回忆模型：事实将“主体-关系”对映射到答案，线性 Transformer 学习主体依赖和关系依赖信息。分别用梯度流（GF）、谱梯度流（Spectral GF）和 Sign GF 优化，它们分别是梯度下降、Muon 和 Adam 的连续时间极限。以预测中主体/关系分量达到目标精度的时间刻画特征分离，并分析误差随训练时间的衰减速率及正交等变性。

Result: 当主体数 S 大于关系数 R 时，GF 先学关系信息、后学主体信息，形成特征分离阶段；其学习时间比为 Θ̃(√(S/R))，而 Spectral GF 将该比值降至 Θ̃(1)。固定 S、R 时，GF 下两类误差按 1/(T log T) 衰减，Spectral GF 下按 exp(-poly(T)) 衰减。GF 与 Spectral GF 对 token 嵌入的正交变换具有等变性，Sign GF 则不然，不同标准正交嵌入可能导致无特征分离、极大分离甚至反转学习顺序。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了谱正交化如何从根本上重塑特征学习动态的机制性解释：它能消除主体-关系特征学习的时间尺度差异并带来指数级收敛加速，而 Adam（Sign GF）缺乏对嵌入正交变换的等变性，其学习动态对嵌入选择高度敏感。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer applies spectral orthogonalization to matrix-valued updates and has shown strong performance in large-scale neural network training, yet the mechanisms of this transformation in feature learning remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate this question through a tractable factual-recall model, where a fact maps each subject-relation pair to an answer, and a linear transformer learns the subject- and relation-dependent information required to recover this mapping. The transformer is optimized with gradient flow (GF), spectral GF, or Sign GF, which are continuous-time limits of gradient descent, Muon, and Adam, respectively. Prior studies (Nichani et al., 2025) have shown that when the number of subjects exceeds the number of relations, GF learns relation-dependent information before subject-dependent information, producing a feature-separation phase during training. We characterize this separation with the learning times when the subject- and relation-dependent components of the prediction reach a target accuracy. With $S$ subjects and $R$ relations, GF has a learning-time ratio of $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{S/R})$, whereas Spectral GF reduces this ratio to $\widetildeΘ(1)$. In addition, for fixed $S$ and $R$, the subject- and relation-dependent errors decay as $1/(T\log T)$ in training time $T$ under GF, but as $\exp(-\mathrm{poly}(T))$ under spectral GF. Finally, we show that GF and spectral GF are equivariant under orthogonal transformations of the token embeddings, whereas Sign GF is not: Different orthonormal embeddings can potentially produce no feature separation, a large feature-separation phase, or even a reversed learning order. These results provide a mechanistic view of how spectral orthogonalization can fundamentally reshape feature-learning dynamics.

</details>


### [221] [Gated Slot Attention-2: Two-Sided Associative Memory Correction in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02816)
*Ruijie Li,Shengnan Ding,Weimin Zhang,Derick Tang,Zhanpeng Zeng,Qinsong Zeng,Ming Chen,Jiaxi Hu,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出Gated Slot Attention-2（GSA2），通过将键侧修正的Gated Oja Rule-2与值侧修正的Gated Delta Rule-2经共享隐槽结合，兼顾线性注意力的效率与记忆管理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型的固定规模循环记忆难以有效管理。已有两条改进方向：delta规则类变体用于精确修正与键关联的值，以及槽式架构（如Gated Slot Attention）分两阶段建模键与值记忆。作者观察到二者互补：delta规则提供有效的记忆修正，而两阶段结构天然适合在关联的两侧进行操作。

Method: 提出Gated Oja Rule用于键侧修正，并扩展为带解耦擦除与写入控制的Gated Oja Rule-2；进而构建GSA2，将键侧修正的Gated Oja Rule-2与值侧修正的Gated Delta Rule-2通过共享隐槽（latent slots）结合；同时推导了适用于并行训练的硬件高效分块（chunkwise）算法。

Result: 实验表明，GSA2在多个基准上稳定优于强线性注意力基线，同时保持线性时间序列建模与常数内存的循环解码。

Conclusion: 键侧与值侧修正两种思路具有互补性，将其统一到共享隐槽的槽式框架中可有效提升线性注意力的记忆管理，同时不牺牲效率。

Abstract: Linear attention models have emerged as efficient alternatives to standard attention, but effectively managing their fixed-size recurrent memory remains challenging. To improve memory, recent work has explored two distinct directions: delta-rule variants for precise correction of values associated with keys, and slot-based architectures such as Gated Slot Attention for modeling key and value memories in two stages. We observe that these directions are complementary--the delta rule provides effective memory correction, while the two-stage structure provides a natural way to operate on both sides of an association. Building on this insight, we introduce a new Gated Oja Rule for key-side correction and extend it with decoupled erase and write control to obtain Gated Oja Rule-2. We then introduce Gated Slot Attention-2 (GSA2), which combines Gated Oja Rule-2 for key-side correction with Gated Delta Rule-2 for value-side correction through shared latent slots. We further derive a hardware-efficient chunkwise algorithm for parallel training. Experiments demonstrate that GSA2 consistently improves over strong linear-attention baselines across benchmarks while retaining linear-time sequence modeling and constant-memory recurrent decoding.

</details>


### [222] [Adaptive Spectral-Koopman Dynamics Modeling for Temporal Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02822)
*Tengxue Zhang,Yu Ke,Yang Shu,Chenchen Sun,Yisheng An,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaSpecK，一种结合谱正则化Koopman动力学与自适应上下文提取的时序域泛化框架，在八个分类与回归基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序域泛化（TDG）需应对随时间发生分布漂移的真实流式数据。现有方法存在两类缺陷：一是在数据空间中易过拟合域特定噪声；二是在参数空间中过于复杂且可解释性差。作者旨在弥合这两方面差距。

Method: 提出AdaSpecK谱-Koopman框架，包含两部分：（1）谱正则化Koopman动力学建模，在潜空间中施加谱感知滤波以提取去噪的低频轨迹，并学习Koopman算子以在线性化空间中建模系统动力学，缓解对不规则采样域的噪声拟合；（2）上下文感知的异质模式提取机制，采用目标条件注意力模块关注不同历史窗口，生成动态的目标特定历史摘要，并通过从当前演化模式构建的环境签名与学习路由器，自适应识别对预测最有信息量的历史上下文。

Result: 在八个涵盖分类与回归的多样化基准上开展大量实验，AdaSpecK均达到最先进（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: AdaSpecK通过谱-Koopman动力学建模与自适应上下文提取的结合，有效解决了TDG中噪声过拟合与模型复杂/可解释性差的问题，验证了其有效性与泛化能力，代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Temporal Domain Generalization (TDG) has emerged to address real-world streaming data with distribution shifts over time. However, existing methods are either prone to overfitting to domain-specific noise in the data space or become overly complex and less interpretable in the parameter space. To bridge these gaps, we propose \textbf{AdaSpecK}, a spectral-Koopman framework with adaptive context extraction for TDG. To mitigate noise fitting to irregularly sampled domains, we introduce spectral-regularized Koopman dynamics modeling, which applies spectral-aware filtering in the latent space to extract denoised low-frequency trajectories and learn a Koopman operator to model the system dynamics in a linearized space. To model complex historical environments under non-stationarity, we design a context-informed heterogeneous pattern extraction mechanism. Specifically, we employ a target-conditioned attention module to attend to distinct past windows, producing a dynamic, target-specific historical summary. By constructing an environmental signature from the current evolutionary pattern, our model adaptively perceives which aspects of the past context are most informative for future prediction via a learned router. Extensive experiments on eight diverse classification and regression benchmarks demonstrate that AdaSpecK achieves state-of-the-art performance. The code and datasets are available at \href{}{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Ada-Spec-K}.

</details>


### [223] [All Work And No Play Makes Jack a Dull Boy: Understanding and Preventing Catastrophic Strategy Collapse in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02835)
*Qiyuan Huang,Tianshi Xu,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"策略容量"视角解释RLVR训练后期崩溃：证明主流RLVR目标会将概率集中到单一策略，而维持任务准确率需要最小策略容量，二者冲突导致灾难性崩溃；给出MEI在线预警指标，并提出Mesh Learning方法来防止崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（GRPO类算法）后训练中存在严重的后期崩溃现象。作者通过prompt探测发现这并非良性的策略剪枝，而是有效策略容量有害收缩，导致不同推理策略越来越不可访问，因此需要从机制上刻画并解决这一问题。

Method: 以轨迹级策略更新交互定义"策略"，构建结合优化动力学与信息论的统一理论框架；据此推导出Mirrored Entanglement Index (MEI) 作为轻量级在线崩溃预警信号；并提出Mesh Learning，通过暴露多种推理策略、阻止单一策略主导优化来防止崩溃。

Result: 在AIME26、AIME25、MATH-500、GPQA、LiveCodeBench上，Mesh Learning在Qwen和Phi模型系列上一致优于强基线，最高提升分别达13.4 pp和11.5 pp；MEI可作为有效的在线预警指标。

Conclusion: 策略保持（strategy preservation）是稳定RLVR的关键原则；理论分析揭示崩溃机制，MEI提供预警，Mesh Learning给出去中心化的优化方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: During post-training of large language models (LLMs) with Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), GRPO-style algorithms can exhibit severe late-stage collapse. Prompt-based probing reveals that this is not benign strategic pruning, but a harmful contraction of effective strategy capacity that makes distinct reasoning strategies increasingly inaccessible. To characterize this phenomenon, we define strategies through trajectory-level policy-update interactions and develop a unified theoretical framework combining optimization dynamics and information theory. We prove that major RLVR objectives progressively concentrate probability mass onto a single strategy, while sustaining nontrivial task accuracy requires a minimum strategy capacity. The conflict between these two results provides a mechanistic explanation for catastrophic collapse. We further derive the {Mirrored Entanglement Index (MEI)} as a lightweight online warning signal. To prevent collapse, we propose \textbf{Mesh Learning}, which exposes multiple reasoning strategies and prevents any single strategy from dominating optimization. Across AIME26, AIME25, MATH-500, GPQA, and LiveCodeBench, Mesh Learning consistently outperforms strong baselines across Qwen and Phi model families, with gains of up to 13.4 pp and 11.5 pp, respectively. These results establish strategy preservation as a key principle for stable RLVR. Code is available at https://github.com/Ayanami-0123/Open-Mesh-Learning.

</details>


### [224] [To Explore The Strange New World Beyond Data Distribution: System Behavior, Causality Tax, and Non-causal Base Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02839)
*Xianzhi Zeng,Jiangneng Li,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SBD框架，将系统行为$S$作为不可约的贝叶斯特征纳入ELBO，揭示"因果税"现象，并通过GSH非因果变分族显著降低该税、提升误差界与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型架构以因果性为事实基础，但近期研究显示其与因果性存在持续的不匹配与矛盾。作者认为这些问题的根源主要来自系统行为（数据空间之外的支配性耦合因素$S$），而非数据分布，因此需要重新审视因果性在语言模型中的必要性与最优性。

Method: 提出SBD框架，将系统行为$S$作为证据下界（ELBO）中不可约的组成部分进行贝叶斯建模；通过隐式测量、理论界引导的对照实验以及神经切线核（NTK）评估验证$S$的影响；并构建非因果变分族Green Shell（GSH），用分而治之的划分替代$S$各分量的序列依赖链。

Result: SBD从理论上揭示了"因果税"现象：因果性因忽视$S$而成为带额外结构误差的次优近似。NTK谱在惰性训练机制下表明，GSH始终获得比因果性显著更紧的误差界，信噪比提升$7dB+$；在惰性训练后期，GSH进一步带来更优的泛化能力，多尺度拟合能力提升最高达$20\%$。

Conclusion: SBD将系统行为确立为除因果性与分布拟合之外的互补理论抽象，为设计并优化语言模型基座模型开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: We show that the causality of language models (LMs) may not be necessary nor optimal. This is the case when system behavior (denoted as $S$) is incorporated as a first-principle Bayesian feature. Here, $S$ refers to extra dominant factors beyond the data space, and they involve coupled effects. Despite being the de facto foundation of modern architecture, recent studies indicate persistent mismatches and contradictions with causality. These issues largely stem from system behavior rather than the data distribution. We therefore propose the SBD framework, which incorporates $S$ as an irreducible component of the evidence lower bound (ELBO). SBD theoretically reveals a counter-intuitive Causality Tax phenomenon, where causality emerges as a suboptimal approximation with an additional structural error, due to the obliviousness to $S$. To address the challenge of latent variable analysis, we validate the SBD-predicted impact of $S$ via implicit measurements, theoretical-bound-guided controls, and Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) evaluations. In particular, we construct Green Shell (GSH) to show the possibility of reducing Causality Tax. GSH is a non-causal variational family, and it replaces the sequential dependency chain of $S$ components with a divide-and-conquer partition. NTK spectra in the lazy-training regime confirm that GSH always achieves significantly tighter error bounds than causality, with $7dB+$ improvement in signal-to-noise ratio. In the relatively later stage of lazy-training, GSH further leads to superior generalization (up to $20\%$ richer multi-scale fitting capabilities). Taken together, SBD establishes system behavior as a complementary theoretical abstraction besides causality and distribution fitting, opening new research avenues such as designing and optimizing LM base models.

</details>


### [225] [Understanding Enrichment in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02846)
*Jinwoo Kim,Shraddha Barke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从数学上分析了RLVR中"富集rollout"（加入提示/中间引导）带来的梯度偏差，指出省略或截断重要性权重等价于隐式重加权奖励，并提出基于序贯蒙特卡洛(SMC)的权重修正机制将指数级方差增长降为线性累积，最后用于解释Qwen3-1.7B在稀疏数学推理任务上的微调结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏奖励场景下，RLVR常利用提示或中间引导来生成更成功的rollout，但这种"富集"会偏置策略梯度更新，除非用重要性权重进行修正。现有方法要么忽略修正，要么为避免修正带来的高方差而截断权重，因此人们并不清楚修正富集rollout究竟带来什么收益与代价。论文旨在厘清富集与修正对训练的影响机理。

Method: 1) 数学证明：省略或截断重要性修正等价于对定义奖励进行隐式重加权；2) 将由此产生的梯度误差分解为尺度(scale)、旋转(rotation)与方差(variance)三部分，分别解释其对学习的不同影响；3) 提出一种新的序贯蒙特卡洛(SMC)权重修正机制，在稳定性与粒子排序假设下，把标准修正方差随样本长度指数式复合增长降为加性累积。

Result: 理论上给出了省略/截断修正导致梯度误差的尺度、旋转、方差分解，并证明SMC修正可将方差增长由指数级降为加性线性累积。实验上将该富集分析应用于Qwen3-1.7B在OpenMathReasoning某一稀疏区间的微调，揭示富集（无论是否修正）帮助避免稀疏域训练崩溃的具体机制。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是普遍性地理解富集与修正如何影响训练，而非主张某种操作模式优于未富集的RL。同时提供了一种实用可行的SMC权重修正方案，并为稀疏域中避免训练崩溃提供了机制性解释。

Abstract: When rewards are sparse, reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) often uses hints or intermediate guidance to generate more successful rollouts. This enrichment biases policy-gradient updates unless corrected via importance weights, but existing methods omit correction or truncate importance weights in order to avoid the high variance of correction. It thus remains unclear what exactly is gained or lost in RLVR by correcting enriched rollouts. We show, mathematically, that omitted or truncated correction implicitly reweights the defined reward, and we decompose the resulting gradient error into scale, rotation, and variance to explain their distinct effects on learning. To make correction practical, we develop a novel sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) weight correction mechanism that, under stability and particle-order assumptions, tempers the exponential compounding of standard correction variance over the length of a sample to an additive accumulation. We then apply our analysis of enrichment to interpreting the results of a fine-tune of Qwen3-1.7B on a sparse band of OpenMathReasoning, establishing a concrete mechanism of how enrichment, both corrected and uncorrected, helps avoid collapse in sparse domains. Our main contribution is to understand, in general, how enrichment and correction can affect training, as opposed to claiming that either mode of operation is superior to unenriched RL.

</details>


### [226] [Counterfactual Action Evaluation, Observation Bottlenecks, and Representation Geometry in Joint-Embedding Predictive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02860)
*Arjun Subramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 低潜空间预测误差不能证明世界模型能区分自身动作的后果；本文提出贯穿仿真状态、光栅观测、目标嵌入与预测器输出的干预追踪评估协议，揭示动作路径利用不足、观测不可见、表征几何退化与物理参数弱可辨识等独立瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型评估常以低潜空间预测误差作为学习到动作后果的依据，但低误差可能来自表征几何退化、观测本身不可分辨或预测器对动作不敏感，而非真正建模了动作的物理效果，因此需要区分并分别审计这些来源。

Method: 提出追踪同一干预的评估协议：在受控可形变物理测试台中做精确的仿真状态分叉，令命令改变粒子运动，沿仿真状态→光栅观测→目标嵌入→预测器输出逐级追踪响应；并施加与目标反事实嵌入位移匹配的各向同性状态扰动作为对照，结合方差归一化、MSE-only训练、VICReg目标编码器与刚度解码实验。

Result: 改变命令虽改变粒子运动，但41.5%的一步光栅观测对完全相同；579个高可见度反事实中，预测器对目标的中位响应在两个种子下为0.0051和0.0217，方差归一化后降至0.0027和0.0116；匹配的反事实嵌入扰动在同样可见对上产生190倍和53倍更大的预测器变化，说明是动作路径利用不足而非预测器失效或全局收缩。MSE-only训练10步潜误差低8.36倍但处于频谱集中的空间，某VICReg目标编码器也强烈集中，故误差或秩均不能证明物理状态。刚度即便用全分辨率光栅与机械状态也近随机，而特权材料参数可完美解码。

Conclusion: 低潜预测误差不足以证明世界模型区分动作后果，应分别审计物理效果、观测可见性、表征几何与动作依赖性四类瓶颈；刚度任务表现为该激励下的弱可辨识性而非编码器丢弃。

Abstract: Low latent prediction error does not establish that a world model distinguishes the consequences of its actions. We introduce an evaluation protocol that traces the same intervention through simulator state, raster observations, target embeddings, and predictor outputs. Exact simulator-state forks in a controlled deformable-physics testbed reveal distinct bottlenecks. Changed commands alter particle motion, yet 41.5% of one-step raster pairs are identical. Observation loss is not the whole explanation: among 579 high-visibility counterfactuals, median predictor-to-target response is 0.0051 and 0.0217 across two seeds, falling to 0.0027 and 0.0116 after variance normalization. An isotropic state perturbation matched to the target counterfactual embedding shift produces 190x and 53x larger predictor changes on the same visible pairs, isolating action-path under-use rather than a dead or globally shrunk predictor. MSE-only training gives 8.36x lower 10-step latent error in matched seeds, but in spectrally concentrated spaces; one VICReg target encoder is also strongly concentrated, so neither error nor rank alone certifies physical state. Finally, stiffness remains near chance even from full-resolution rasters and mechanical state while privileged material parameters decode perfectly, indicating weak identifiability under this excitation rather than encoder discard. These results motivate auditing physical effect, observation visibility, representation geometry, and action dependence separately.

</details>


### [227] [NeuroLens: Learning Latent Embeddings of Neural Semantics from Chronic Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02864)
*Hanrui Lyu,Baiyuan Chen,Tianshu Tan,Matthew R. Whiteway,Maxwell D. Melin,Ji Xia,Linyang He,Bradly C. Stadie,Anne Churchland,Liam Paninski,Yizi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroLens，一种基于JEPA自监督框架的神经表征学习模型，将慢性神经记录映射到去噪的共享潜在空间，实现对决策与语义变量的稳健解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经科学长期关注高阶认知的神经表征及其随时间演化，但现有分析工具难以区分表征可塑性与记录不稳定性。慢性神经记录中电极漂移、单元丢失等记录层面的变异会与真实的表征变化混淆，导致解码性能不稳定。

Method: 基于Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture（JEPA）构建自监督模型。采用自适应编码器将变化的神经群体映射到统一潜在空间，并以时间预测器学习支持未来潜在状态预测的时间结构；通过在潜在空间中进行预测来捕获时间可预测结构，降低对瞬时、记录特异性变异的敏感性。

Result: 在小鼠和人类的慢性皮层内记录数据上，学到的表征提升了对决策及语义任务变量的解码。多天预训练可泛化到未来session，支持对未见神经群体的快速少样本适应，且相比SOTA基线在跨时间解码上更稳定。

Conclusion: NeuroLens为研究学习过程及长时程中神经表征如何变化提供了新范式，能够分离表征可塑性与记录不稳定性。

Abstract: Understanding how neural activity represents higher-order cognition and how these representations evolve over time has long been a central pursuit in neuroscience. However, current analytical tools cannot easily distinguish representational plasticity from recording instability in chronic neural recordings. Here, we introduce NeuroLens (Latent Embeddings of Neural Semantics), a self-supervised model based on the Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) framework that learns denoised, semantically informative latents from chronic neural recordings. An adaptive encoder maps changing neural populations into a common latent space, while a temporal predictor learns structure that supports prediction of future latent states. By predicting in latent space, NeuroLens captures temporally predictable structure and reduces sensitivity to transient, recording-specific variability. Across chronic intracortical data in mice and humans, the learned representations improve decoding of decision-making and semantic task variables. Multi-day pretraining enables generalization to future sessions, rapid few-shot adaptation to unseen neural populations, and more stable decoding over time than state-of-the-art baselines. Together, these results establish NeuroLens as a new paradigm for studying how neural representations change during learning and over long timescales.

</details>


### [228] [On Unlearning for Time-series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02865)
*Zeyu Shi,Yanhui Luo,Ziming Hong,Chongyang Gao,Kezhen Chen,Shanshan Ye,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RDTU 残差扩散框架，用于时间序列预测的机器遗忘，使遗忘后模型最接近精确重训练结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序预测模型常基于用户/实体级纵向记录训练，因敏感、专有信息或传感器故障需删除这些数据。重训练代价高，故需机器遗忘机制，但时序遗忘存在独特挑战：被删观测参与多个因果关联的预测窗口，梯度类遗忘不稳定，参数更新会扩散到请求区间之外并损害保留数据的预测效用；标签引导更新更可控，但连续且依赖上下文的预测缺少合适的替换目标，且遗忘时无法获得精确重训练的输出来作监督。

Method: RDTU 先用保留集神经正切核（NTK）预测器得到与删除兼容的基础预测；再用保留参考数据的体积贡献量化每个受影响窗口的全局与局部结构支撑度；随后用扩散模型生成残差修正以估计反事实预测，得到伪标签场，用于引导轻量级模型更新。

Result: 实验表明 RDTU 持续产生与精确重训练最接近的遗忘后模型。

Conclusion: 针对时序预测中删除请求缺乏精确重训练监督、且剩余结构支撑非均匀的问题，RDTU 通过 NTK 基础预测、结构支撑度量化与扩散残差伪标签相结合，实现了接近精确重训练的时序机器遗忘。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting is widely used in sensitive domains. Models in these settings are often trained on longitudinal user- or entity-level records, which may later require removal because they contain sensitive or proprietary information or have been corrupted by sensor failures. To address such deletion requests without costly retraining, machine unlearning has been widely studied as a practical mechanism for privacy protection and data governance. However, the application of machine unlearning to time series prediction has not yet been well realized; this is mainly due to the following unique challenges: Gradient-based unlearning can be unstable because a deleted observation participates in multiple causally connected forecasting windows, causing parameter updates to propagate beyond the requested interval and degrade retained forecasting utility. Label-guided updating offers a more controlled alternative, but continuous and context-dependent forecasts lack a suitable replacement target, while the exact-retrained output is unavailable during unlearning. Moreover, the remaining support for a deleted temporal pattern is highly non-uniform. Some affected windows retain structurally similar counterparts in the retained data, whereas others become underrepresented or isolated. We present RDTU, a Residual Diffusion framework for time-series unlearning. RDTU first uses a retained-set neural tangent kernel predictor to obtain a deletion-compatible base forecast. Then it quantifies the global and local structural support of each affected window using the volume contribution of the retained-reference data. Then a diffusion model generates a residual correction that estimates the counterfactual forecast, yielding a pseudo-label field that guides a lightweight model update. Experiments show that RDTU consistently produces unlearned models that most closely match exact retraining.

</details>


### [229] [DIVINE: Simple Cross-Market Stock Pretraining via Diverse Indicator Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02866)
*Kuan-Yu Chen,Shu-Cheng Zheng,Yu-Chen Den,Wei-Cheng Liao,Tien-Hao Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DIVINE跨市场预训练框架，通过从原始OHLCV历史重构技术指标来学习可迁移的金融时序表征，以0.05M参数编码器在六个股票市场取得最优组合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融时序预训练目标（掩码观测、对比关系、未来结果）难以同时规避未来监督的不确定性与保持收益预测对齐；本文希望找到兼顾监督确定性与收益预测相关性的预训练监督方式。

Method: DIVINE（DIVerse INdicator rEconstruction）：仅以观测到的量价历史计算技术指标作为监督信号，在六个股票市场数据集上联合预训练，重构由16个标准指标衍生的77个目标；下游仅迁移编码器用于股票排序（stock ranking）。

Result: 在全部六个市场上，DIVINE以仅0.05M参数的轻量编码器取得最强的平均组合表现，优于预训练基线，并匹配或超越规模大得多的金融基础模型，同时保持稳健性与数据高效性；指标多样性与市场多样性在迁移中带来互补增益。

Conclusion: 监督信号设计与跨市场多样性，而非模型规模，是获得强且可迁移金融表征的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: Financial time-series pretraining typically learns from masked observations, contrastive relations, or future outcomes---yet existing objectives struggle to simultaneously avoid future-supervision uncertainty and maintain return-prediction alignment. We propose DIVINE (DIVerse INdicator rEconstruction), a simple cross-market pretraining framework that reconstructs technical indicators from raw OHLCV history. Computed from observed price-volume history, technical indicators provide consistently defined supervision across markets while summarizing diverse market dynamics with established relevance to return prediction. Pretrained jointly on six-equity market datasets, DIVINE reconstructs 77 targets derived from 16 standard indicators and transfers only the learned encoder to downstream stock ranking. Across all six markets, DIVINE achieves the strongest average portfolio performance with a lightweight 0.05M-parameter encoder, outperforming pretraining baselines and matching or exceeding substantially larger financial foundation models, while remaining robust and data-efficient. Systematic analyses show that indicator diversity and market diversity provide complementary gains in transfer. Together, these results suggest that supervision design and cross-market diversity---rather than model scale---are the key drivers of strong, transferable financial representations.

</details>


### [230] [Distributionally Robust Survival Models under Subpopulation Shift and Outlier Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02868)
*Seonghwi Kim,Sung Ho Jo,Minwoo Chae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向生存分析的分布鲁棒框架，联合处理潜在亚群偏移与离群值污染，通过外层最小化精炼名义分布与内层最大化聚焦最难亚群，并设计基于KKT条件的交替梯度算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异质人群中，平均表现良好的生存模型在特定亚群上可能表现很差；当训练数据同时受到离群值污染时，该问题进一步加剧。因此需要一种能同时应对潜在亚群偏移和离群值污染的鲁棒生存分析方法。

Method: 提出分布鲁棒框架：外层最小化通过降低污染样本影响来选择精炼的名义分布；内层最大化聚焦最具挑战性的亚群。该形式直接适配不可分解的生存损失，并保留样本间交互（如Cox负偏对数似然的风险集结构）。采用交替梯度算法，外层更新由内层最大化的KKT条件导出。

Result: 在模拟数据和两个生存分析基准上，当亚群偏移与离群值污染同时发生时方法保持鲁棒；在污染环境下稳定训练，显著提升线性与非线性生存模型的最差组性能，同时整体性能具有竞争力，有时更优。

Conclusion: 所提分布鲁棒生存分析框架能有效联合应对潜在亚群偏移与离群值污染，改善最差组表现并稳定训练，适用于线性和非线性生存模型。

Abstract: Learning robust survival models under distribution shift is an important but challenging problem in many applications. In heterogeneous populations, a model that performs well on average may still perform poorly on certain subpopulations, and this issue becomes even more severe when the training data are contaminated by outliers. In this paper, we propose a novel distributionally robust framework for survival analysis that jointly addresses latent subpopulation shift and outlier contamination. The proposed method combines an outer minimization that selects a refined nominal distribution by reducing the influence of contaminated samples and an inner maximization that focuses on the most challenging subpopulation. This formulation directly accommodates non-decomposable survival losses while preserving interactions across samples, including the risk-set structure of the Cox negative partial log-likelihood. We develop an alternating gradient-based algorithm with outer updates derived from the KKT conditions of the inner maximization. Experiments on simulated data and two survival benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method remains robust when subpopulation shift and outlier contamination occur simultaneously. It stabilizes training in contaminated settings and substantially improves worst-group performance across both linear and nonlinear survival models, while maintaining competitive and sometimes superior overall performance.

</details>


### [231] [Peer Effects in Signed Networks: Separating Influence Through Positive and Negative Ties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02872)
*Xiaojing Du,Jiuyong Li,Lin Liu,Debo Cheng,Jixue Liu,Thuc Duy Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于正负关系符号的因果效应框架与SiDE双重稳健估计量，用于评估网络干预中支持性与对抗性关系的差异化影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估网络干预需理解处理如何通过社会关系影响个体。传统方法仅计数被处理的邻居而不区分支持性与对抗性关系，会掩盖两类关系相反的影响方向，导致效应识别偏差。

Method: 定义通过正向与负向关系产生的效应、二者的交互效应，以及在总处理量固定下两类关系间重新分配处理的符号构成效应，并给出识别公式。在符号盲分配下，证明忽略符号会混淆两类关系效应。提出SiDE（Signed-exposure Doubly robust Estimator），将符号特异的结果模型与个体处理分配诱导的暴露概率相结合，证明其得分函数的双重稳健性，并评估考虑邻域重叠的近似置信区间。

Result: 在六个真实符号网络上的半合成实验表明效应估计准确，并考察了区间覆盖率的局限。对已发表学校实验数据的探索性再分析得到通过共处时间关系对手环佩戴的同伴效应为正，但经多重比较调整后，四个效应的区间均包含零。

Conclusion: 该框架通过揭示两类关系的影响何时相互增强或抵消，可为网络干预设计提供指导。

Abstract: Evaluating network interventions requires understanding how treatment affects people through their social relationships. Counting treated neighbors without distinguishing supportive and antagonistic ties can conceal opposing influences. We define effects through positive and negative ties, their interaction, and a sign-composition effect of reallocating treatment between the two types at a fixed total, and give their identification formulas. Under sign-blind assignment, we show how ignoring signs mixes the effects of the two tie types. We propose SiDE (Signed-exposure Doubly robust Estimator), which combines sign-specific outcome models with exposure probabilities induced by individual treatment assignment. We establish double robustness of its score and assess approximate intervals that account for overlapping neighborhoods. Semi-synthetic experiments on six real signed networks demonstrate accurate effect estimation and examine the limits of interval coverage. An exploratory reanalysis of published school-experiment data yields a positive estimate of the peer effect through spend-time ties on wristband wearing, but the intervals for all four effects include zero after adjustment for multiple comparisons. This framework can inform network intervention design by showing when influences through the two tie types reinforce or offset one another.

</details>


### [232] [Toward Omni Multimodal Graph Foundation Model: A Topology-Driven Binding Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02881)
*Xunkai Li,Chenxi Wan,Yinlin Zhu,Wang Luo,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphBind，一种以图拓扑为驱动、将多模态信息绑定到统一共享空间的多模态图基础模型方法，解决节点属性缺失与拓扑未充分引导多模态绑定两大问题，在判别与生成任务上均达领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的多模态属性图常存在节点属性不完整，限制了预训练语料的规模与多样性；同时现有MGFM主要将图拓扑作为结构上下文，忽略了其在引导多模态绑定、塑造统一表示空间方面的作用。

Method: GraphBind基于图拓扑的稳定性（提供结构参照与互补语义），利用拓扑组织自身语义与可靠邻域语义，构建融合结构与语义的全局共享空间，并通过轻量接口将该空间适配到判别式与生成式任务。

Result: 在11个代表性基线对比下，GraphBind在判别式与生成式任务上均取得领先性能，相较最强基线相对提升最高达28.1%。

Conclusion: 以拓扑为驱动的多模态绑定思路能有效缓解属性缺失问题，并为多模态图基础模型构建统一共享表示空间提供新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal graph foundation models (MGFMs) seek to learn generalizable representations from large-scale graphs with heterogeneous node modalities. However, real-world Multimodal-Attributed Graphs (MAGs) often contain incomplete node attributes, limiting the scale and diversity of available pretraining corpora. Besides, existing MGFMs primarily incorporate graph topology as structural context, overlooking its role in guiding multimodal binding and shaping a unified representation space. To address these challenges, we propose GraphBind, a topology-driven approach that uses graph topology to bind rich modality information into a unified shared space. GraphBind is motivated by the stability of graph topology, which provides structural references and complementary semantic information for multimodal binding. Concretely, GraphBind uses topology to organize self semantics and reliable neighborhood semantics into a global shared space that integrates structure and semantics, and adapts this space to discriminative and generative tasks through lightweight interfaces. Extensive experiments against 11 representative baselines demonstrate that GraphBind achieves leading performance on both discriminative and generative tasks, with relative improvements of up to 28.1% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [233] [DyRA: Dynamic Residual Approximation for Efficient Matrix Multiplication in DNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02882)
*Daewon Chae,Hyunwon Chung,Changwoo Lee,Hun-Seok Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DyRA：通过输入自适应地校正结构化权重近似带来的输出残差误差，在同预算下更忠实地逼近全矩阵乘法，改善精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基础模型推理成本高，主因是密集矩阵乘法。现有以低秩等结构化形式替换稠密权重的方法是在权重空间做近似，而非近似决定精度的输出激活，导致小的权重误差被输入激活放大为大的输出误差。

Method: 提出DyRA输入自适应方法：论证直接优化输出的低秩因子能更有效近似矩阵乘法；据此在推理时动态近似并校正结构化权重近似引入的输出误差，将高效结构化计算与输入相关校正结合。

Result: 在视觉、语音、语言模型上一致优于纯结构化权重近似；在DINOv3上实现1.5×端到端GPU加速，同时将精度下降减少3倍以上。

Conclusion: 以输出为中心、输入自适应的校正思路可在同等计算预算下提升结构化近似的保真度，是改善推理精度-效率权衡的有效方向。

Abstract: Large-scale foundation models achieve strong performance across diverse tasks, but their size makes inference costly, largely due to dense matrix multiplications. Prior work reduces this cost by replacing dense weight matrices with efficient structured forms such as low-rank factorizations. However, these methods approximate weights rather than the output activations that determine inference accuracy. Consequently, small weight-space errors can be amplified by input activations, producing large output errors. In this work, we propose DyRA, an input-adaptive method that improves structured matrix multiplication approximation by correcting residual output errors during inference. We show that matrix multiplication can be approximated more effectively by directly optimizing low-rank factors of the output. DyRA builds on this insight by dynamically approximating and correcting the output error introduced by structured weight approximations. This combines efficient structured computation with input-dependent correction, yielding a more faithful approximation of full matrix multiplication under the same computational budget. Across vision, speech, and language models, DyRA consistently improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off over structured weight approximations alone. Notably, DyRA achieves a 1.5$\times$ end-to-end GPU speedup for DINOv3 while reducing accuracy degradation by more than 3$\times$ relative to weight-only baselines.

</details>


### [234] [When Can We Trust the Matching Principle? Robust Deployment Geometry Under Finite-Sample and Model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02894)
*Vishal Rajput*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信任比 tau = epsilon / gamma（估计不确定性除以谱间隔），刻画匹配问题的决策；在线性二次匹配响应下，估计投影器与oracle投影器之间的漂移在Davis-Kahan分离区（tau < 1/2）按 tau^2 缩放。进一步提出置信校准匹配（CCM），将 tau 转化为策略：tau 小则方向性匹配，反之趋向各向同性，阈值来自校准而非定理。实验展示了两种区域，UCI HAR嵌入中始终匹配在每一单元都不如弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匹配决策通常默认应当匹配，但当几何不可辨识时强行匹配会带来更大误差。作者希望量化"何时该匹配、何时该分散惩罚"，即估计不确定性相对于谱间隔是否足以支撑可靠匹配。

Method: 定义信任比 tau = epsilon / gamma（估计不确定性 / 谱分离度），在线性二次匹配响应下分析估计与oracle投影器匹配的相对漂移；提出置信校准匹配（CCM），基于阈值将 tau 转为策略：tau 小时方向性匹配，tau 大时渐进各向同性，阈值由校准而非理论给定。

Result: 理论推导出在所选 top-r 部署子空间中，probe 的 oracle-相对漂移按 tau^2 缩放，Davis-Kahan 分离区 tau < 1/2 时为 O(tau^2)，实际可用性依赖常数。实验观察到两种区域；在 UCI HAR 嵌入上，always-match 在每个 cell 都不如 abstain。

Conclusion: 匹配的可行性由信任比 tau 决定：低 tau 时应方向性匹配，高 tau 时应分散（isotropic/abstain）。CCM 提供了一套校准驱动、而非定理驱动的阈值策略，实验验证了其两种行为模式。

Abstract: Match only geometry you can identify; otherwise spread the penalty. We quantify that decision by the trust ratio tau = epsilon / gamma (estimation uncertainty over spectral separation). Under the linear-quadratic Matching response, oracle-relative drift between estimated and oracle projector matching scales as tau^2 for probes in the chosen top-r deployment subspace -- O(tau^2) in the Davis-Kahan separation region tau < 1/2, with practical usefulness depending on constants. Confidence-Calibrated Matching (CCM) turns tau into a policy -- directional when tau is small, progressively isotropic when not -- with thresholds from calibration, not from the theorem (match sits in the separation region; soft is mostly heuristic). Experiments show both regimes, including UCI HAR embeddings where always-match is worse than abstain on every cell.

</details>


### [235] [Tangent Schrödinger Bridge Matching: Learning Stochastic Transport with Mechanistic Sensitivities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02906)
*Jowaria Khan,Elizabeth Bondi-Kelly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Tangent Schrödinger Bridge Matching（Tangent-SBM），从端点观测与机制敏感性中学习随机传输，实现可复用且对干预响应预测准确的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机系统对黏度、反应速率或外力变化的响应预测通常依赖昂贵仿真，需要可复用的学习模型；但仅匹配观测结果分布并不能保证对干预响应的预测准确。

Method: Tangent-SBM 在轨迹传播过程中同时传播参数导数，并用给定目标监督这些敏感性；针对平均响应目标，采用两次独立 rollout 匹配均值，避免单次 rollout 平方误差对响应方差的额外惩罚；并建立敏感性精度对有限变化预测误差与决策后悔的界。

Result: 在 Gaussian、随机双势井、PDEBench 反应—扩散和随机 Navier–Stokes 系统上，Tangent-SBM 相比匹配的条件桥基线提升了敏感性与有限变化预测，同时保持可比的端点与分布精度；对照实验检验了目标正确性、响应目标与仿真预算分配；在 Navier–Stokes 校准黏度选择中，相比不采取行动降低了跟踪误差。

Conclusion: Tangent-SBM 通过联合学习传输与机制敏感性，提升了随机系统干预响应预测与决策效用，为可复用模型提供了更可靠的敏感性监督与理论保障。

Abstract: Predicting how stochastic systems respond to changes in viscosity, reaction rates, or external forces requires costly simulations, motivating reusable learned models. Yet matching observed outcome distributions does not ensure accurate intervention responses. We introduce Tangent Schrödinger Bridge Matching (Tangent-SBM), which learns stochastic transports from endpoint observations and mechanistic sensitivities. It propagates parameter derivatives alongside trajectories and supervises them against supplied targets. For average-response targets, single-rollout squared error also penalizes response variability; our objective uses two independent rollouts to match the mean without this additional penalty. We establish conditions under which sensitivity accuracy bounds finite-change prediction error and decision regret. Across Gaussian, stochastic double-well, PDEBench reaction--diffusion, and stochastic Navier--Stokes systems, Tangent-SBM improves sensitivity and finite-change prediction over matched conditional-bridge baselines while maintaining comparable endpoint and distributional accuracy. Controls examine target correctness, response objectives, and simulator-budget allocation. To test decision usefulness, we evaluate calibrated viscosity selection in Navier--Stokes: Tangent-SBM reduces tracking error relative to taking no action on every evaluated task.

</details>


### [236] [Do ResNets Route? Sparse Interaction Experts in Residual Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02907)
*Liang Yan,Siying Chen,Kaijie Chen,Bo Li,Jinghao Zhang,Mu Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将训练好的ResNet建模为二进制残差分支掩码上的集合函数，用Möbius反演精确分解输出为各阶残差修正与高阶交互项，发现ImageNet预训练ResNet-18/34的交互质量峰值出现在第5阶和第10阶，且主导交互随输入与类别变化，表明稠密ResNet隐含实现了软路由机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ResNet对每个输入都执行全部残差块，但各块的功能贡献未必与输入无关。作者希望从功能贡献层面刻画残差网络的输入依赖性，探究其是否在无显式路由器、无稀疏执行的情况下隐含路由机制。

Method: 将训练好的ResNet表示为二进制残差分支掩码上的集合函数，通过Möbius反演将其输出精确分解为单块残差修正与高阶交互项；对平滑残差堆叠证明固定k阶交互在残差缩放λ下按O(λ^k)缩放；并对ImageNet预训练的ResNet-18与ResNet-34进行穷举式交互谱分析。

Result: ResNet-18与ResNet-34的交互质量峰值分别出现在第5阶和第10阶，而非低阶；降低残差缩放会使谱向低阶移动，但也会改变预测。交互系数在幅度上集中但并非严格稀疏，保预测的稀疏性随深度减弱。主导交互随输入与预测类别变化，围绕共享全局核心，整体复杂度随样本难度变化不大。

Conclusion: 稠密ResNet实现了一种隐式软路由：每个块都被执行，但不同输入依赖不同的残差交互"专家"。路由可以在功能贡献层面自发涌现，无需显式路由器或稀疏执行。

Abstract: Residual networks execute every block for every input, yet their functional contributions need not be input independent. We formulate a trained ResNet as a set function over binary residual-branch masks and apply Möbius inversion to decompose its output exactly into individual residual corrections and higher-order interactions. For smooth residual stacks, we show that each fixed $k$-way interaction scales as $\mathcal{O}(λ^k)$ under residual scaling. Exhaustive analysis of ImageNet-pretrained ResNet-18 and ResNet-34 reveals that interaction mass peaks at orders five and ten, respectively, rather than at low orders. Reducing the residual scale shifts both spectra toward lower orders, but also changes model predictions. The interaction coefficients are concentrated in magnitude but not hard sparse, and prediction-preserving sparsity weakens with depth. Crucially, the dominant interactions vary across inputs and predicted classes around a shared global core, while their overall complexity changes little with sample difficulty. These results show that dense ResNets implement an implicit form of soft routing: every block is executed, but different inputs rely on different residual interaction experts. Routing can therefore emerge at the level of functional contribution without an explicit router or sparse execution.

</details>


### [237] [Constraint-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02909)
*Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"约束感知"训练目标：既然推理时会用程序分析过滤非法token，就不必在训练中重复教会模型这些规则，从而可将分析"外化"，提升模型与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型生成程序时常用约束解码，借助程序分析剔除违反语法、作用域或类型规则的token。但标准训练本身已让模型学会抑制这些被分析拒绝的token，形成重复；作者追问：既然推理端总要过滤，能否在训练端不再教授该分析，这种外化是否带来更高效的模型。

Method: 作者定义一个满足"外化"要求的通用约束感知目标，并将外化收益形式化为三个关于模型规模与数据效率的定理；随后通过受控的合成实验验证理论在训练动力学下是否成立。

Result: 在匹配参数量与数据量的受控合成实验中，约束感知训练相比普通交叉熵训练取得更低的预测损失，说明定理在存在训练动力学时依然成立。

Conclusion: 约束感知训练目标可将生成阶段所用的分析外化到推理端，减少训练中的冗余学习，为纳入推理所用分析的训练目标提供了理论与实证依据。

Abstract: When generating programs with language models, constrained decoding can apply program analyses to exclude tokens that violate syntax, scope, or typing rules. However, there is a duplication: standard training already teaches the model to suppress the tokens rejected by these analyses. This duplication leads to the question: if we will perform some analysis to filter a set tokens out during inference anyways, can we avoid teaching the model the said analysis altogether during training, and does this externalization lead to more efficient models? This paper defines a general constraint-aware objective satisfying this externalization desideratum and formalizes the benefits of externalization into three concrete theorems about model size and data efficiency.
