We show, through a controlled synthetic experiment, that the theorems survive training dynamics: constraint-aware training yields lower prediction loss at a matched parameter count and data compared to ordinary cross-entropy training, motivating training objectives that incorporate the analyses used during generation.

</details>


### [238] [Probe the Harness: Setup Checks for Stale-Data RL Comparisons in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02911)
*Taiheng Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PTH（Probe The Harness）检查清单，揭示训练框架实现细节可反转陈旧样本下语言模型训练方法的比较结论，并以 SAN 与 TIS 对比为案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陈旧样本（stale samples）训练方法的评价依赖于与重要性修正基线的对比，但实验框架（harness）本身的实现细节往往未被审视，可能导致方法排序被错误反转。

Method: 以 SAN（无行为策略方法）与 TIS（截断重要性采样）为案例，在 verl 与单 GPU 训练器两个栈上复现比较；通过 PTH 检查清单显式暴露框架细节，包括：PPO 比率是否基于 learner 自己重算的概率、数据种子是否传递到 TIS 分支、replay queue 是否复用首批数据达 33 次更新、两个损失归一化器是否与描述一致。

Result: 初始比较中 SAN 在两个栈上均领先；修正上述四处框架细节后，verl 上 TIS 与 SAN 持平，单 GPU 训练器上 TIS 稳定学习而 SAN 保持一定优势。论文还给出每个细节的“特征签名”及其对比较的影响，以及采样器滞后下 TIS 与未修正 GRPO 的参考结果。

Conclusion: 实验框架细节足以反转方法比较的排序；日志中量看似正常，但定义比较的关键量未被检查。作者贡献 PTH 检查清单、各细节签名与参考结果，提醒研究者系统性审查 harness。

Abstract: Methods for training language models on stale samples are judged by comparisons against importance-corrected baselines. We show that details of the experimental harness can reverse the observed ranking of methods, and we introduce PTH (Probe The Harness), a set of checks that makes the harness visible. Our case is a comparison between SAN, a behaviour-free method, and truncated importance sampling (TIS) on verl and in a single-GPU trainer, in which SAN first finished ahead in both stacks. Four details of the harness changed this comparison: the PPO ratio was taken against the learner's own recomputed probabilities, the data seed did not reach the TIS arm, the replay queue reused its first batch for 33 updates, and two loss normalisers differed from their description. In each case the logged quantity looked consistent with a working setup, while the quantity that defines the comparison went unchecked. With the harness checked, TIS matches SAN on verl, and in the trainer TIS learns steadily while SAN keeps a margin. We contribute the signature of each detail and its effect on the comparison, reference results for TIS and uncorrected GRPO under sampler lag, and the PTH checklist.

</details>


### [239] [Dynamic Expert Pruning for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02951)
*Jabin Koo,Soheil Abbasloo,Sungjae Lee,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态专家剪枝（DEP），用系统与任务提示预测每请求的专家掩码，在多智能体异构负载下优于静态剪枝与合并基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 通过仅激活少量专家来高效扩展语言模型，但节省仅限于计算：所有专家必须常驻加速器，内存限制了部署规模。专家剪枝可缓解占用，但现有方法为静态——离线校准出单一掩码，对所有后续请求不变。该假设在异构负载下失效，尤其多智能体系统中单骨干同时服务多任务与角色：分析表明不同任务与角色调用不同专家，而静态方法对所有任务分配同一固定子集。

Method: 本文建立的核心发现是：智能体的系统提示与任务提示本身已足以识别该智能体及其任务所需的专家，因为该文本已描述智能体将执行的行为。基于此提出 DEP：在流程转录上一次性训练轻量预测器，将提示通过单次前向传播转化为每请求的专用掩码，无需针对每种配置进行校准。

Result: 在多种任务与角色、模型规模及 MoE 架构上，DEP 的整体准确率优于静态剪枝与合并基线，并能泛化到训练中未见的流程而无需重训练。在保留专家数较少时，其相对基线的优势最大，说明多智能体系统固有的角色特化允许比静态剪枝更稀疏的服务。

Conclusion: 提示信息足以为每请求生成专家掩码，动态剪枝可突破静态单掩码假设；角色特化带来的条件性专家需求使更稀疏的 MoE 服务成为可能，且方法具备跨任务、跨规模、跨架构的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale language models efficiently by activating only a few experts per token, but the saving is confined to computation: every expert must stay resident on the accelerator, so memory bounds where these models can be deployed. Expert pruning reduces this footprint, yet existing methods are static --- a single mask, calibrated offline, is applied to the model for every subsequent request. This assumption can fail when the workload is heterogeneous, most prominently in multi-agent systems, where one backbone serves many tasks and roles at once: our analysis shows that different tasks and roles recruit different experts, while static methods assign one fixed subset to all of them. We therefore propose Dynamic Expert Pruning (DEP), which rests on a finding we establish here: an agent's system and task prompts are by themselves sufficient to identify the experts that agent and its task require, since that text already describes what the agent will do. A lightweight predictor, trained once on workflow transcripts, turns those prompts into a specialized per-request mask in a single forward pass, with no per-configuration calibration. Across diverse tasks and roles, model scales, and MoE architectures, DEP achieves better overall accuracy than static pruning and merging baselines, and generalizes to workflows unseen in training without retraining. Its margin over those baselines is largest when few experts are retained, suggesting that the role specialization inherent to multi-agent systems permits sparser serving than static pruning allows.

</details>


### [240] [SlimKV: Joint Token-Feature KV Cache Compression with Reconstruction-Free Beacon Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02953)
*Zihan Teng,Jiayu Zhao,Wentao Ren,Minhao Fan,Tianrui Ma,Song Chen,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SlimKV：一种问题无关的联合token-特征KV缓存压缩方法，通过低秩感知训练、层自适应秩分配及K侧免RoPE约束，实现潜在空间注意力，在16x/32x压缩下超越基线，128K长度下解码加速3.38倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM服务受KV缓存内存瓶颈制约，尤其在资源受限场景下。现有压缩策略中，token-wise方法通过驱逐或压缩减少缓存状态但存在信息损失风险；feature-wise方法降低每token的KV维度，但应用位置嵌入时需全维重建，限制了解码加速。

Method: 提出SlimKV，问题无关的联合token-特征KV缓存压缩方法。采用低秩感知训练将长上下文压缩为带潜在KV表示的beacon记忆状态，并进行层自适应秩分配。发现位置不对称性：去除key侧RoPE对beacon token与原始token影响不同，对beacon token退化更小。据此在K-RoPE-free约束下训练beacon KV投影，并在解码时启用潜在空间注意力以减少重建延迟。

Result: 在LongBench上，SlimKV在16x/32x压缩下优于基线，在4x/8x下保持领先并保留未压缩模型96%以上的分数。Needle-in-a-Haystack验证了跨证据位置的鲁棒性。效率评估显示，128K长度下注意力加速最高7.34倍，端到端解码加速3.38倍。

Conclusion: SlimKV通过联合token与特征压缩、层自适应秩分配及利用位置不对称性的K-RoPE-free潜在空间注意力，有效缓解KV缓存内存瓶颈并显著提升长上下文解码效率，同时保持较强性能。

Abstract: Long-context LLM serving is increasingly bottlenecked by KV-cache memory, especially in resource-constrained scenarios. Among existing KV-cache compression strategies, token-wise methods reduce cached states but risk information loss through eviction or condensation, while feature-wise methods reduce per-token KV dimensions but can require full-dimensional reconstruction to apply positional embedding, limiting decoding speedups. We introduce SlimKV, a question-agnostic joint token-feature KV-cache compression method. SlimKV uses low-rank-aware training to compress long contexts into beacon memory states with latent KV representations, together with layer-adaptive rank allocation. We further uncover a positional asymmetry: removing key-side RoPE affects beacon and raw tokens differently, with much smaller degradation for beacon tokens. Exploiting this asymmetry, SlimKV trains beacon KV projections under a K-RoPE-free constraint and enables latent-space attention during decoding, mitigating reconstruction latency. On LongBench, SlimKV outperforms baselines at 16x/32x compression and remains leading at 4x/8x, where it retains over 96% of the uncompressed model's score. Needle-in-a-Haystack confirms robustness across evidence positions, and efficiency evaluation shows up to 7.34x attention speedup and 3.38x end-to-end decoding speedup over the uncompressed model at 128K length.

</details>


### [241] [Hyperparameter selection for equation learning with biologically-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02954)
*William Lavery,Jodie A. Cochrane,John T. Nardini,Sara Hamis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套用于生物信息神经网络（BINNs）超参数选择的诊断工作流，围绕三个核心问题指导选择，并在具有已知真值的合成扩散与增长系统中验证，给出实用经验法则与推荐起始值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BINNs适用于方程高度非线性、先验知识不完整、数据稀疏含噪的生物系统，但其效果严重依赖超参数选择。目前超参数多凭经验选取且记录不充分，导致结果可复现性差、方法难以迁移，亟需在真值方程未知时也能使用的超参数选择流程。

Method: 提出一套诊断式超参数选择工作流，以三个问题为引导：(1) 增大网络容量的收益是否值得其代价；(2) 更多训练轮次是否持续降低验证损失；(3) 随网络容量与训练增加，学到的项是否趋于稳定。将该流程应用于复杂度递增、真值已知的合成系统，涵盖扩散与增长右端项，数据范围从1D+t到2D+t。

Result: 验证损失总体能跟随所学项的真实误差；据此提炼出BINN架构中超参数选择的实用经验法则。

Conclusion: 通过提供结构化工作流、实用指南及超参数推荐起始值，降低基于BINN进行方程学习的门槛，提升结果可复现性与方法可迁移性。

Abstract: Biologically-informed neural networks (BINNs) have emerged as a flexible subclass of physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for learning terms in partial differential equations from data. BINNs are particularly suited for biological systems, where the governing equations are highly nonlinear and only partially known a priori, and where data observations are often sparse, noisy, and incomplete. However, applying BINNs effectively in practice depends critically on hyperparameter selection, which remains a central challenge in equation-learning frameworks. Hyperparameters are often chosen heuristically and only cursorily documented, which limits the reproducibility of results and the transferability of methods. We present a diagnostic workflow for hyperparameter selection that can be used when the ground-truth equations are not known. The workflow is guided by three main questions: (1) Are the benefits of greater network capacity worth the cost? (2) Do more training epochs keep reducing the validation loss? (3) Do the learned terms stop changing as network capacity and training increase? We apply our workflow to synthetic systems of varying complexity with known ground truth, spanning diffusion and growth right-hand side terms and data ranging from 1D+t to 2D+t. We demonstrate that the validation loss generally follows the true error in the learned terms and distil practical rules of thumb for selecting hyperparameters in the BINN architecture. By providing a structured workflow, practical guidelines, and suggested starting values for hyperparameter selection, this work lowers the barrier to BINN-based equation learning.

</details>


### [242] [Understanding Trajectory Heterogeneity in Federated World Model Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02957)
*Yipan Wei,Zhaokun Yan,Ziming Hong,Jiaqi Wu,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过MIMIC-IV八种疾病队列的逐小时动作条件临床预测任务，构建跨时间联邦学习基准，揭示客户端所有权与参与率对时间窗覆盖的限制、细粒度严重度划分对FedAvg的负面影响，以及算法标签掩盖的更新行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型依赖轨迹中的时间上下文进行状态演化学习，而联邦学习虽可利用分布式记录，但轨迹的所有权边界限制了各客户端可构造的样本，导致跨时间的训练需求难以满足。因此需要系统性地评估这一"跨时间"联邦学习设定。

Method: 在MIMIC-IV的八个疾病队列上开展逐小时动作条件预测，包含4087万转移成员关系；设定基于严重度的客户端所有权、患者分离的样本构造、本地历史与未来窗口规则，以及1至32小时的配对rollout评估；在五轮10%参与率下，对十种联邦算法覆盖32种疾病-划分配置进行基准测试。

Result: 1）客户端所有权与参与率共同限制长窗口覆盖：跨疾病平均仅7.55%–21.36%的池化可用32步窗口在训练中被本地完整锚点访问；2）更细的严重度划分在16组配对比较中有15组伴随更高的FedAvg误差，算法增益较小且依赖预测时长，FedProx平均误差降低0.56%，但在32步处无一致改善；3）算法标签掩盖了不同的更新行为，包括不活跃外推和更新尺度数量级差异；相同基准协议下缓存更新性能在不同轨迹划分间差异显著。

Conclusion: 时间可访问性、参与覆盖率、优化行为与时长分辨预测是评估联邦临床世界模型的四个互补维度，仅凭算法标签不足以刻画其真实表现。

Abstract: World models learn state evolution from trajectories, making access to temporal context a central training requirement. Federated learning can use distributed records, while ownership boundaries within a trajectory restrict the examples each client can construct. Our study benchmarks this cross-time setting through hourly action-conditioned clinical prediction on eight MIMIC-IV disease cohorts, comprising 40.87 million transition memberships. We specify severity-based client ownership, patient-separated construction, local history and future-window rules, and paired rollout evaluation from one to 32 hours. A matrix of ten federated algorithms covers 32 disease--partition configurations under five rounds of ten-percent participation. Three findings emerge from existing results and training logs. First, client ownership and participation jointly restrict long-window coverage: only 7.55\%--21.36\% of pooled-available 32-step windows have a locally complete anchor visited during training, averaged across diseases. Second, finer severity partitions accompany higher FedAvg error in 15 of 16 paired comparisons, while algorithm gains are small and horizon-dependent: FedProx reduces mean error by 0.56\%, with no consistent improvement at 32 steps. Third, algorithm labels conceal distinct update behavior, including inactive extrapolation and orders-of-magnitude differences in update scale. Cached-update performance also varies strongly across trajectory partitions under the same benchmark protocol. These results establish temporal access, participation coverage, optimization behavior, and horizon-resolved prediction as complementary dimensions for evaluating federated clinical world models.

</details>


### [243] [Dirac-Interconnected Neural Elements: Discovering Modularity in Physical Systems Without Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02960)
*Reiho Li,Razmik Arman Khosrovian,Takaharu Yaguchi,Hiroaki Yoshimura,Takashi Matsubara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dirac 互联神经单元（DINEs），将物理系统建模为微分代数方程（DAE），其代数约束由核表示的 Dirac 结构给出，可同时从数据中辨识组件间互联结构并学习组件特性，支持子系统隔离与组合复用，并能处理部分可观测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动动力系统建模方法虽通过能量守恒、辛结构等物理先验取得成效，但未充分利用"真实物理系统是组件互联"这一事实：有的方法要求互联结构已知，有的假设系统可约化为 ODE 并仅学习降阶 ODE，从而丢弃了互联带来的代数约束。

Method: 提出 Dirac-interconnected neural elements（DINEs），将系统表示为 DAE，其代数约束由核表示的 Dirac 结构刻画；通过数据同时辨识组件间的互联（Dirac 结构）与组件特性（神经网络），保持子系统为非降阶形式，并支持部分可观测情形。

Result: 实验在现有方法难以处理的物理系统上验证了上述能力：互联与组件的联合辨识、子系统的隔离与组合（无需重新训练）以及对部分可观测系统的处理。

Conclusion: DINEs 以 Dirac 结构显式刻画互联、以神经网络学习组件，为可组合、可复用的物理系统建模提供了统一框架，并拓展了可处理系统的范围。

Abstract: Deep learning has shown remarkable success in the data-driven modeling of dynamical systems. Much of its success is attributed not to the flexibility of neural networks but to inductive biases based on physical prior knowledge, such as energy conservation and symplecticity. However, existing methods do not fully exploit the fact that real-world physical systems are interconnections of components. Some methods require the interconnection to be known a priori, while others assume the system to be reducible to an ordinary differential equation (ODE) and learn only the reduced ODE, discarding the algebraic constraints imposed by the interconnection. Here, we propose Dirac-interconnected neural elements (DINEs), a neural network model that represents a physical system as a differential-algebraic equation (DAE), whose algebraic constraints are given by a Dirac structure in kernel representation. With DINEs, we simultaneously identify from data the interconnection among the components as a Dirac structure and learn the characteristics of the components as neural networks. This allows us to keep the learned subsystems in unreduced form and isolate or compose them to make a new system without retraining. Moreover, DINEs can handle partially observable systems. Experimental results demonstrate these capabilities on physical systems beyond the reach of existing methods.

</details>


### [244] [Differentiable Koopman Operator for Contrastive Learning on Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02990)
*Md Abrar Jahin,Taufikur Rahman Fuad,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KAIROS框架，将可微Koopman算子嵌入动态图对比学习循环，实现嵌入空间中时间演化的显式线性化，在九个动态图基准上取得SOTA异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实交互网络本质动态，边随节点行为变化而生成与消失。多数基于快照的对比方法仅在编码器权重中隐式编码时间依赖，缺乏对节点表示如何演化的显式建模，导致在分布偏移下表现脆弱。

Method: KAIROS（Koopman-Aligned Invariant Representations for Open Dynamic Systems）是一个自监督框架：在动态图对比学习循环中嵌入可微Koopman算子，在学习到的嵌入空间中对时间演化进行线性化；采用双视角编码器，将原始节点特征与图扩散结构视图配对，并在多个时间窗口上以多粒度对比目标优化。异常检测结合Koopman预测残差、时间不一致性与局部邻域偏差。

Result: 在九个动态图基准上评估，KAIROS在全部九个数据集上均取得SOTA异常检测结果，相较先前工作ROC-AUC最高提升23.15个点，同时在无监督节点分类上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 显式的动态建模为时序图表征学习提供了可扩展且有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Real-world interaction networks are inherently dynamic: edges form and dissolve as node behavior shifts over time. Most snapshot-based contrastive methods encode temporal dependencies implicitly in encoder weights, without an explicit model of how node representations evolve, making them brittle under distribution shifts. We propose KAIROS (Koopman-Aligned Invariant Representations for Open Dynamic Systems), a self-supervised framework that embeds a differentiable Koopman operator within a dynamic graph contrastive learning loop to linearize temporal evolution in the learned embedding space. A dual-view encoder pairs raw node features with a graph-diffused structural view and is optimized with multi-granularity contrastive objectives across temporal windows. For anomaly detection, KAIROS uses the Koopman prediction residual together with temporal inconsistency and local neighborhood deviation to separate irregular behavior from predictable graph evolution. Evaluated on nine dynamic graph benchmarks, KAIROS achieves state-of-the-art anomaly detection results on all nine datasets, with gains of up to 23.15 ROC-AUC points over prior work, while remaining competitive for unsupervised node classification. These results show that explicit dynamics modeling provides a scalable and effective inductive bias for temporal graph representation learning.

</details>


### [245] [Sentry: Learning to Recover from LLM Agent Failures at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02994)
*Changxiu Ji,Amy Lu,Qizheng Zhang,Kunle Olukotun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出失败知识是条件性知识、应按需暴露的原则，并据此构建Sentry失败管理模块，仅在检测到失败时检索并注入匹配教训，验证恢复后存回外部手册，显著提升多智能体基准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体常因无效工具调用、重复动作或推理无依据而中途失败，从失败中学习是提升可靠性的途径。但失败知识具有条件性：常驻上下文会在对应失败缺失时误触发、反而降低性能；而运行时干预只在失败发生时起作用，却不从修复中学习。因此需要一种既能按需使用、又能积累学习失败经验的方法。

Method: 将失败知识视为条件性知识并条件性暴露，实例化为Sentry——一个与智能体并行运行的失败管理分层。Sentry检测到失败后，从外部playbook检索匹配教训以引导恢复；在不访问任务奖励的情况下验证智能体是否恢复；仅当确认恢复时才写入新教训；完整playbook从不进入智能体上下文。

Result: 在多个智能体基准上，Sentry在每个基准上均优于最强运行时干预基线，平均提升37%；在两个共同评估的基准上比最强上下文演化基线高39%；与上下文演化结合可进一步提升。所学教训可迁移到留出任务；对照实验表明，即使相关教训仍可按需获取，将完整playbook暴露给智能体会降低性能。

Conclusion: 失败知识应作为条件性知识按需暴露，而非常驻上下文或仅作即时干预。Sentry通过外部playbook的条件检索、无奖励恢复验证与选择性存储，在多个基准上稳定超越现有基线，且教训具备迁移性；将其与上下文演化方法结合还能带来额外收益。

Abstract: LLM agents often fail mid-task due to invalid tool calls, repeated actions, or poorly grounded reasoning, and learning from these failures is a path to reliability. We find that how failure knowledge reaches the agent matters as much as what it contains. Failure lessons are conditional: kept in the agent's context, they misfire when their failure is absent, and removing them from an evolving playbook improves performance. Runtime interventions, in contrast, act only when a failure occurs but do not learn from their repairs. We argue that failure knowledge is conditional knowledge and should be conditionally exposed, and instantiate this principle in Sentry, a failure-management layer that runs alongside the agent. When Sentry detects a failure, it retrieves matching lessons from an external playbook to guide recovery, verifies without access to task rewards whether the agent recovered, and stores a new lesson only if it did; the full playbook never enters the agent's context. Across multiple agentic benchmarks, Sentry outperforms the strongest runtime-intervention baseline on every benchmark, by 37\% on average, and the strongest context-evolution baseline by 39\% on the two benchmarks where both are evaluated; combining Sentry with context evolution yields further gains. Learned lessons transfer to held-out tasks, and controlled experiments show that exposing the full playbook to the agent lowers performance even when relevant lessons remain available on demand.

</details>


### [246] [Temporal Geometry of Deep Networks: Hyperbolic Representations of Training Dynamics for Intrinsic Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03000)
*Ambarish Moharil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双曲几何与时间参数图的元学习框架，将MLP训练过程中的参数快照嵌入Poincaré球，学习具备置换等变/不变性的动态表示，揭示网络随时间的自组织几何演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层感知机在优化环境中需要动态再训练，其内在可解释性仍是难题。传统基于度量（measure-based）的可解释方法仅分析单一检查点，忽略了训练轨迹中编码的信息；而神经网络不仅通过权重、也通过训练过程中的轨迹来编码信息。因此需要一种能捕捉权重拓扑几何演化与自组织过程的动态表示方法。

Method: 1）构造时间参数图：对MLP训练过程按T步采样，获得随时间变化的参数快照。2）采用双曲几何的Poincaré模型，将时间参数图嵌入Poincaré球。3）提出几何+时间图基的元学习框架，学习动态双曲表示，同时保持对快照内神经元置换的等变性（equivariance）与对历史快照置换的不变性（invariance）。4）通过该框架保持跨时间的功能等价性，并恢复网络潜在演化的几何结构。

Result: 在回归与分类任务上，针对训练后的MLP进行实验，元网络性能表现优异，并获得双曲时间表示。结果表明网络结构在特定训练环境下随时间如何涌现，为网络自组织提供了洞见。

Conclusion: 该工作将神经网络训练轨迹表示为时间参数图并嵌入双曲空间，提供了一种内在可解释的几何-时间元学习框架，能够追踪并解释网络拓扑与自组织的动态演化。

Abstract: Intrinsic explainability remains a challenging problem, particularly in contexts where multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) require dynamic re-training within an optimization environment. This paper investigates how MLPs and their training dynamics can be represented and studied in non-Euclidean spaces; our representation features the Poincaré model of hyperbolic geometry. We aim to capture the geometric evolution of their weighted topology and self-organization over time. Instead of restricting the analysis to single checkpoints---as per established measure-based explainability methods---we construct temporal \textit{parameter graphs}, i.e., snapshots over time $T$ steps of the optimization/training process for MLPs. This reflects the view that neural networks encode information not only in their weights but also in the trajectory traced during training. Drawing on the idea that many complex networks admit embeddings in hidden metric spaces where distances correspond to connection likelihood, we present a geometric and temporal graph-based metalearning framework for obtaining dynamic hyperbolic representations of the underlying neural parameter graphs. Our model embeds temporal parameter graphs in the Poincaré model ball, and learns from them while maintaining equivariance to within-snapshot neuron permutations and invariance to permutations of past snapshots. In doing so, the approach preserves functional equivalence over time and recovers the latent evolving geometry of the network. Experiments on regression and classification tasks with trained MLPs show strong meta-network performance, accompanied by hyperbolic temporal representations. This reveals how the network structure emerges over time under specific training environments, thus providing insights into the network's self-organization.

</details>


### [247] [Neural Data Needs Semantic Tokenization: Behavioral Events as Boundaries of Session-Transferable Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03001)
*Sangyoon Bae,Jiook Cha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Tokenization with States (TWS)，以群体状态作为跨会话共享的 token，无需神经元或会话嵌入，实现跨会话、跨物种泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞外电生理每次记录到的神经元集合不同，神经基础模型为每个神经元和会话分别嵌入 token，导致新会话成为未见过的输入而无法泛化。因此需要一个所有会话共享、且携带行为信息的 tokenizer 单元。

Method: 群体活动在低维流形上演化，该流形在神经元更替和跨动物间（对齐后）保持稳定，并在刺激起始、运动起始等任务事件处发生状态切换。TWS 按这些事件将每次试次分割，把每个状态转换为群体几何的 token，不使用任何神经元或会话嵌入。

Result: 在留出的 IBL 会话上，TWS 即使从无目标信息的区间边界也能解码运动，而基于该会话预训练的逐神经元基础模型仅达随机水平；仅用小鼠训练后冻结，TWS 仅经线性探针即迁移到猕猴和 Utah 阵列；在不由边界定义的目标（伸手方向）上，Matthews 相关系数达 0.23，而仅用事件时间仅为 0.01。

Conclusion: 对于跨会话泛化，token 本身比上层模型更关键；以群体状态为基本单元可实现跨会话与跨物种的行为解码。

Abstract: Extracellular electrophysiology records a different set of neurons in every session. Neural foundation models embed each neuron and each session into their tokens, so every new session is an input they have never seen, and they fail to generalize to it. A tokenizer for new sessions needs a unit that every session shares and that carries behavior. Population activity offers such a unit. It evolves on a low-dimensional manifold that persists across neuronal turnover and across animals once sessions are aligned. This manifold changes regime at task events such as stimulus onset and movement onset. Within each regime the population occupies a state, the part of the manifold it spans between two events, and each state carries its own behavioral meaning. We propose Tokenization with States (TWS), which segments each trial, one repetition of the task, at these events and converts every state into tokens of population geometry, with no neuron or session embedding. On held-out International Brain Laboratory (IBL) sessions, TWS decodes movement even from regime boundaries that carry no information about the target, while a per-neuron foundation model pretrained on those sessions decodes at chance. Frozen after training on mice alone, TWS transfers to macaques and Utah arrays with only a linear probe. On reach direction, a target that its boundaries do not define, it achieves a Matthews correlation of 0.23, where the event time alone achieves $0.01$. For cross-session generalization, the token matters more than the model on top.

</details>


### [248] [AvoKV-E: Payload-Aware KV Cache Eviction for Long Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03007)
*Han Yu,Wenhui Zhu,Xiwen Chen,Zhipeng Wang,Hejian Sang,Han Shi,Menglin Zhou,Xuanzhao Dong,Minzhou Huang,Rui Cai,Hao Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 KV-cache 淘汰策略 AvoKV-E，通过延迟新状态的可淘汰资格，并结合候选归一化读取压力、键冗余度与值载荷潜力进行排序，在紧凑缓存下优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长输出推理使 KV-cache 瓶颈从固定提示转移到生成的推理轨迹上。现有推理缓存淘汰方法多将缓存条目视为路由对象，仅估计旧 key 是否仍会被读取、是否复现或可否替换。这种纯路由视角忽略两点：低注意力条目可能携带大 value 载荷，删除会改变后续预测；新生成状态可能在后续查询读取前就已被视为陈旧。

Method: AvoKV-E 是一种免训练淘汰策略：首先对近期状态延迟其淘汰资格，再对符合资格的条目基于三个信号打分排序——候选归一化的读取压力、key 冗余度、value 载荷潜力。该打分与延迟机制配合，兼顾可读性与载荷保留。

Result: 在多种模型与数据集上的实验表明，在相同 active-KV 预算下，AvoKV-E 达到或超过冗余感知、复现驱动及思维自适应等淘汰基线，且在缓存最紧张的场景中收益最大。组件与反事实分析进一步将收益归因于延迟观察、载荷感知打分、冗余度与尺度鲁棒归一化。

Conclusion: 长推理 KV 淘汰不应只保留可能被读取的 key，还应保留维持推理轨迹的 value 载荷。

Abstract: Long-output reasoning shifts the KV-cache bottleneck from the fixed prompt to the generated trace. Existing reasoning-cache eviction methods largely treat cached entries as routing objects, estimating whether an old key will still be read, will recur, or can be replaced. This routing-only view overlooks two effects: low-attention entries can carry large value payloads whose removal changes future predictions, and newly generated states can appear stale before later queries have had a chance to read them. We introduce AvoKV-E, a training-free eviction policy that first delays eligibility for recent states and then ranks eligible entries using candidate-normalized read pressure, key redundancy, and value-payload potential. According to empirical evaluation across different models and datasets, AvoKV-E matches or exceeds redundancy-aware, recurrence-based, and thought-adaptive eviction baselines at matched active-KV budgets, with its largest gains in the tightest-cache regime. Component and counterfactual analyses further connect these gains to delayed observation, payload-aware scoring, redundancy, and scale-robust normalization. Together, the results show that long-reasoning KV eviction should preserve not only keys that are likely to be read, but also the value payloads that sustain the reasoning trajectory.

</details>


### [249] [Signal Simplification Is Not Predictive Simplification: Diagnosing Residual Neural Forecasting in Short-Horizon Volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03019)
*Bingqi Lian,Linfeng Cheng,Mei Lu,Jerry Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文检验"统计第一阶段成功后，残差更干净、更易学"这一混合管线假设。基于信号-预测系统诊断框架，在五种美国流动性资产上，波动对齐HAR模型优于AR/MA/ARIMA；残差方差降约82%、滚动样本外HAR误差方差降约74%且滞后一阶自相关微弱，但残差LSTM增强反而使MSE从0.3049升至0.3594，纯LSTM最低为0.2649。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合统计-神经管线普遍假设：第一阶段统计预测成功会留下更干净、更易学习的残差目标。作者质疑该假设，尤其针对短周期波动率预测场景，考察统计残差简化是否真的能转化为下游神经网络的可用预条件。

Method: 提出信号-预测系统（signal-forecast-system）诊断框架。在五种流动性较好的美国资产上，比较波动对齐的HAR类模型与AR、MA、ARIMA；在扩展训练窗口内，分析用于构造残差-LSTM序列的标准化前拟合残差过程（方差与滞后一阶自相关）；并在滚动伪样本外评估HAR误差统计特征，同时对比残差-only LSTM增强、纯LSTM的伪样本外MSE与端到端运行时间。

Result: 波动对齐HAR类模型优于AR、MA、ARIMA。残差过程方差较对应目标低约82%，滞后一阶自相关近零；滚动伪样本外HAR误差方差降约74%，滞后一阶依赖同样微弱。但残差-only LSTM增强使平均MSE由0.3049升至0.3594，且在每个资产上均恶化；纯LSTM取得最低选定伪样本外MSE 0.2649；残差混合模型需要显著更多端到端运行时间却未提升精度。

Conclusion: 作者将这一现象概括为"预测器-预条件器不对称"（forecaster-preconditioner asymmetry）：第一阶段的预测成功与统计残差简化，未必能转化为对下游神经网络有益的预条件。这提示混合管线中"残差更干净即更易学"的默认假设在短周期波动率预测中不成立。

Abstract: Hybrid statistical-neural pipelines often assume that a successful statistical first stage leaves a cleaner and more learnable residual target. We examine that assumption in short-horizon volatility forecasting through a signal-forecast-system diagnostic framework. Across five liquid U.S. assets, a volatility-aligned HAR-style model outperforms AR, MA, and ARIMA. Within expanding training windows, the pre-standardization fitted residual process used to construct residual-LSTM sequences has about 82% lower variance than the corresponding target and near-zero lag-1 autocorrelation; independently, rolling pseudo-out-of-sample HAR errors show about 74% variance reduction and similarly weak lag-1 dependence. Residual-only LSTM augmentation nevertheless raises mean squared error from 0.3049 to 0.3594 on average, with deterioration on every asset. Pure LSTM records the lowest selected pseudo-out-of-sample MSE, 0.2649, while the residual hybrid requires substantially more end-to-end runtime without improving accuracy. We describe this pattern as forecaster-preconditioner asymmetry: first-stage forecasting success and statistical residual simplification need not translate into useful downstream neural preconditioning.

</details>


### [250] [Tailoring the Quantization Space for 1-Bit KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03027)
*Minsoo Cheong,Donghyun Son,Sungjoo Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TaSQ通过定制向量量化(VQ)的目标空间来改进低比特KV缓存压缩,结合查询引导的通道加权、跨头归一化和协方差感知的通道分组,在1-bit等极端压缩下保持推理性能,并显著提升吞吐与批大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存成为主要内存瓶颈,压力和带宽需求大。向量量化(VQ)是有前景的压缩方法,但现有VQ方法在1-bit极端压缩区间性能大幅下降:每个码本需用有限质心表征更大的通道组,容量利用困难。

Method: 提出TaSQ,通过对VQ目标空间进行定制化变换:(1)查询引导的通道加权,(2)跨头归一化,(3)协方差感知的通道分组,以更好反映缓存激活的误差敏感性与统计结构。这些变换与RoPE兼容,可合并进投影权重和码本中,从而保留传统VQ查表结构并带来可忽略的服务开销。

Result: 在通用、长链思维推理、长上下文检索基准上,TaSQ持续优于现有低比特KV缓存VQ基线,并保持推理稳定性。在单张RTX 6000 Ada GPU上,其SGLang实现支持最高14倍更大的批大小,峰值吞吐相比BF16基线提升1.87倍。

Conclusion: 通过对VQ目标空间进行查询引导加权、跨头归一化与协方差感知分组等定制化变换,TaSQ在极端低比特(1-bit)KV缓存压缩下有效缓解性能退化,兼顾精度与效率,具备实用部署价值。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache becomes a major memory bottleneck in long-context LLM inference, placing substantial pressure on memory capacity and bandwidth. To mitigate this bottleneck, vector quantization (VQ) has emerged as a promising approach for aggressive KV cache compression. However, existing VQ methods degrade substantially in the 1-bit regime. At such extreme compression, each codebook must represent a larger group of channels with a limited set of centroids, making effective use of its capacity increasingly challenging. To address this, we introduce $\textbf{TaSQ}$, which tailors the VQ target space by combining query-guided channel weighting, cross-head normalization, and covariance-aware channel grouping to better reflect the error sensitivity and statistical structure of cached activations. Since these transforms are RoPE-compatible and can be easily merged into projection weights and codebooks, TaSQ preserves the conventional VQ lookup structure and adds negligible serving overhead. Across general, long-chain-of-thought reasoning, and long-context retrieval benchmarks, TaSQ consistently outperforms existing low-bit KV cache VQ baselines while preserving reasoning stability. On a single RTX 6000 Ada GPU, its SGLang implementation supports up to $14\times$ larger batch sizes and achieves $1.87\times$ higher peak throughput compared to the BF16 baseline.

</details>


### [251] [Adaptive Second-Order Solvers for Fast Stochastic Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03034)
*Ella Kemperman,Luca Ambrogioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将比例-积分（PI）步长控制引入扩散模型采样，提出基于噪声归一化误差估计的自适应求解器，并将逐样本轨迹聚合为固定调度；在图像和语言任务上，固定调度可超越EDM调度，PI自适应求解器在多数基线上取得更优FID/困惑度，但逐样本自适应的收益具有问题依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型依赖时间离散化的数值求解器，采样成本与质量的权衡受离散化方式强烈影响。逆向过程的计算难度沿采样轨迹及不同数据分布而变化，因此离散化选择至关重要；现有扩散自适应方法仅响应当前误差，步长调整不够平滑，且逐样本自适应的实际收益尚不明确。

Method: 将比例-积分（PI）步长控制迁移到扩散采样，并设计扩散噪声归一化误差估计器；相比仅依赖当前误差的现有自适应方法，PI求解器同时纳入历史误差以实现更平滑的步长调整。进一步发现逐样本轨迹具有共享结构，可聚合为一个固定调度以保留自适应采样的部分收益；在自然图像与语言数据集上，以匹配NFE下的FID、困惑度等指标与主流随机求解器及调度进行对比。

Result: 图像任务中，固定离散化在配合随机Heun采样器时质量优于常用EDM调度，在低NFE下配合EDM-churn采样器亦更优；PI自适应求解器FID优于多数随机与自适应基线，但低NFE下不及EDM-churn。语言扩散中，求解器在低到中等NFE下困惑度优于EDM与熵调度，代价是token熵降低。逐样本自适应性收益依赖问题：一维玩具例子中收益明显，图像与语言数据上仅边际，平均调度有时甚至优于PI自适应求解器。

Conclusion: PI步长控制为扩散采样提供了更平滑的自适应离散化方案，且逐样本轨迹可聚合为有效固定调度；然而逐样本自适应的价值具有问题依赖性，在复杂高维数据上收益有限，固定调度在部分场景甚至更具优势。代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion models rely on numerical solvers requiring time-discretization, which has a large influence on the tradeoff between sampling cost and quality. However, the computational difficulty of the reverse process varies along the sampling trajectory and across data distributions, making the choice of discretization important. We adapt proportional-integral (PI) step-size control to diffusion, using our diffusion noise-normalised error estimator. Unlike existing adaptive methods in diffusion that respond only to the current error, the PI solver also incorporates the previous error, yielding smoother step adaptation. We further show that these per-sample trajectories exhibit shared structure and can be aggregated into a fixed schedule that retains much of the benefit of adaptive sampling. We evaluate both approaches on natural-image and language datasets, in terms of quality, measured by FID at a matched number of neural network evaluations (NFE), comparing them with widely used stochastic solvers and schedules. For images, our fixed discretization outperforms the commonly used EDM schedule in terms of sample quality when used with the stochastic Heun sampler, and with the EDM-churn sampler at low NFE. Additionally, our PI adaptive solver obtains better FID than most stochastic and adaptive baselines, although it does not beat the EDM-churn sampler at low NFE. Moreover, we find our solver outperforms both the EDM and the entropy schedule on language diffusion at low-to-medium NFE in terms of perplexity, with the drawback of lower token entropy. Lastly, we find that the benefit of per-sample adaptivity is problem-dependent. It is highly beneficial in 1D toy examples, while only marginal for image and language data, where the average schedule sometimes even outperforms the PI-adaptive solver. Code is available at https://github.com/ellakemperman/adaptive-second-order-diffusion-solvers

</details>


### [252] [Balancing Multimodal Learning via Functional Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03035)
*Zhongjing Gu,Fengqiang Wan,Yiming Cui,Yufa Feng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FGMO框架，用函数空间进度信号评估并协调各模态优化进度，缓解多模态学习中的模态失衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态学习方法通常基于预测不确定性或优化统计得到的分数差异来估计模态失衡，但不同模态的预测不确定性与学习动态存在差异，直接比较这类分数会把模态的固有差异误判为进度差距，导致失衡估计有偏。

Method: FGMO包含两部分：Functional Progress Estimation（FPE）度量每个模态更新引起的函数空间响应，并以与损失对齐的单模态参考进行校准，得到可比较的进度信号；Functional Response Control（FRC）据此重新分配模态级函数空间预算，并通过张量级学习率调整实现目标响应。

Result: 理论分析证明FRC在有界控制器状态失配下具有一步目标收缩性质；大量实验表明FGMO在多个多模态基准上有效。

Conclusion: 以函数空间进度信号取代分数差异作为失衡判据，可更准确地刻画模态优化进度，并通过函数空间预算再分配与学习率调节有效缓解模态失衡。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where the joint optimization process is dominated by a single modality. Existing methods typically estimate modality imbalance from score disparities derived from prediction uncertainty or optimization statistics. However, due to distinct prediction uncertainty and learning dynamics across modalities, direct comparison of such scores may misinterpret intrinsic modality differences as progress gaps, leading to biased imbalance estimation. In this paper, we propose Function-Space Guided Multimodal Optimization (FGMO), which leverages a function-space progress signal to assess modality-wise optimization progress and coordinate optimization across modalities to alleviate modality imbalance. Specifically, we introduce Functional Progress Estimation (FPE) to measure each modality's update-induced function-space response and calibrate it against a loss-aligned unimodal reference, producing a comparable progress signal. Based on this signal, Functional Response Control (FRC) redistributes modality-level function-space budgets and realizes the target responses through tensor-wise learning-rate adjustment. Theoretical analysis establishes a one-step target-contraction property of FRC under bounded controller-state mismatch, and extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of FGMO across multiple multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [253] [WebFovea: When the Model Is Right but the Click Is Wrong -- Reliable Round Trips for Vision-Based Web Agents on Live Websites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03036)
*Jiangang Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WebFovea 是一个视觉型网页智能体，在 WebRetriever Challenge 2026 中获第二名（100 分制得 57.0 分）。论文提出“四阶段”视角（解析回复、动作生效、结果回传、信息呈现），指出真实网站上大量失败源于模型与页面之间的 harness，而非模型推理本身；通过加固各阶段并加入 guardrails，团队在相同模型下将隐藏集得分从 31.0 提升到 57.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评测要求智能体从实时网站的入口 URL 出发，操作网站自身界面并返回可验证答案。作者认为，强大的多模态大模型是必要条件但不充分，因为模型的决策需经 harness 到达浏览器，真实环境中许多失败发生在模型与页面之间的工程环节，而非模型推理能力上，因此需要系统性地定位并加固这些环节。

Method: 提出“四阶段”分析框架：模型回复需被解析为预期动作、动作需在页面上生效、结果需被准确回传、模型需被呈现所需信息。围绕该框架对 harness 各阶段进行加固，并加入使智能体遵守规则与预算的 guardrails。同时给出各组件证据（含负面结果）、失败分析、局限性与路线图（如将不同步骤路由到不同模型）。

Result: 观察到多类 harness 层面失败：坐标空间不匹配导致每次点击落在预期坐标的 3/4 处；原生下拉框、iframe 内及文本框中的动作静默失败；自生成的 chat-template token 污染了 4.9% 的任务回合。在四次提交使用同一模型的情况下，官方隐藏集得分从 31.0 提升到 57.0，提升主要归因于 harness 改动（叠加实时网站的运行间方差）。

Conclusion: 四阶段视角不依赖于具体模型（尽管部分具体修复依赖模型）。在模型固定的前提下，harness 层面的系统性加固能显著提升端到端表现，说明工程环节是网页智能体性能的关键瓶颈之一。

Abstract: We present WebFovea, a vision-based web agent that placed 2nd in the WebRetriever Challenge 2026 with a final score of 57.0 out of 100. The challenge evaluates agents end to end on Protocol III of the WebRetriever benchmark (arXiv:2607.06118): starting from an entry URL on a live website, the agent must operate the site's own interface and return a verifiable answer. A capable multimodal large language model (LLM) is necessary for this, but not sufficient. The model's decisions reach the browser through the harness, the code between the model and the page. At every step, four things must go right: the model's reply must be parsed into the intended action, the action must take effect on the page, the result must be reported back accurately, and the model must be shown the information it needs. On real websites, many of the failures we observed occurred at one of these four stages rather than in the model's reasoning. A coordinate-space mismatch placed every click at 3/4 of its intended coordinates; actions on native dropdowns, inside iframes, and in text boxes failed silently; and self-generated chat-template tokens contaminated 4.9% of task episodes. WebFovea hardens each stage and surrounds the loop with guardrails that keep the agent within the rules and its budget. The four-stage view does not depend on the model, although some individual fixes do. Because we used the same model in all four submissions, the rise of our official hidden-set score from 31.0 to 57.0 reflects changes to the harness, up to run-to-run variance on live sites. We describe the design, the evidence for each component (including negative results), a failure analysis, the limitations, and a roadmap that includes routing different steps to different models.

</details>


### [254] [RIPPLE in Still Water: Zero-Shot Clustering in Federated Learning with Wavelet Scattering Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03054)
*Alessandro Licciardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RIPPLE 是一种离线聚簇联邦学习框架，通过小波散射变换与高斯混合 VAE 对客户端本地数据做谱表征，实现零额外通信的簇分配，并可对未参与训练的客户端单次前向推理生成个性化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚簇联邦学习方法需在训练循环内通过梯度相似度、损失评估或 EM 式更新来发现簇结构，导致通信开销增加、梯度暴露于反演攻击，且无法为未参与训练的客户端分配簇。

Method: RIPPLE 完全离线计算簇分配：对每个客户端本地数据构造方差加权的主成分原型，经小波散射变换嵌入，再由服务端在联邦开始前于合成客户端群体上训练的高斯混合 VAE 解码完成聚类。每轮通信成本与 FedAvg 完全相同，缺席客户端仅需一次前向传播即可获得个性化模型，无需梯度计算、模型评估或额外通信轮次。

Result: 理论上证明了 RIPPLE 的代理聚簇目标与最优解之间的差距受一个可计算量约束，该量随客户端样本量增大而衰减且与联邦时长无关；每个簇的收敛速率达到非凸光滑目标下的极小极大最优速率。在五个涵盖可控与真实异质性的基准上，RIPPLE 一致优于所有基线，且在最具真实性的划分上优势更为显著。

Conclusion: RIPPLE 通过离线谱表征实现聚簇联邦学习，在保持与 FedAvg 相同通信成本的同时支持缺席客户端的个性化，并具备理论收敛保证，实验验证其在多种异质性设置下的优越性。

Abstract: Clustered Federated Learning (FL) partitions a client population into groups of similar local distributions and trains one specialized model per cluster, mitigating client drift that degrades single-model methods under non-IID data. Prior methods discover cluster structure inside the training loop through gradient similarity, loss evaluation, or EM-style updates, thus increasing communication overhead, exposing gradients to inversion attacks, and providing no mechanism to assign clients absent from training. We propose RIPPLE, a clustered FL framework in which cluster assignment is computed entirely offline from a spectral characterization of each client's local data: a variance-weighted principal-component prototype embedded via the Wavelet Scattering Transform and decoded by a Gaussian Mixture VAE trained server-side on synthetic client populations before federation begins. Per-round communication cost matches FedAvg exactly, and a client absent from training obtains a personalized model from a single forward pass, without gradient computation, model evaluation, or extra communication round. We prove that the gap between RIPPLE's surrogate clustered objective and the oracle is bounded by a computable quantity decaying with client sample size and independent of federation duration; per-cluster convergence matches the minimax-optimal rate for non-convex smooth objectives. Across five benchmarks spanning controlled and realistic heterogeneity, RIPPLE consistently outperforms all baselines, with margins growing on the most realistic partitions.

</details>


### [255] [When Does Synthetic Relational Data Teach Models to Use Relations? Tracing Predictive Structure from Pretraining Data to Model Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03057)
*Shivam Dubey,Mohamed Bouadi,Nassim Bouarour,Varun Kulkarni,Aditya Tanna,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以数据归因视角研究关系基础模型：在相同架构与算力下，用四个生成器语料训练Relational Transformer，发现当跨表信息对掩码单元预训练目标具有预测必要性时，模型才会习得关系计算机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系基础模型日益依赖合成数据库预训练，但下游基准无法解释为何某合成语料能训练出更好的模型。尤其是强性能可能仅源于逼真的行级统计，而模型从未真正学会利用关系结构。因此需要厘清合成预训练数据的哪种属性诱导了关系计算。

Method: 将问题建模为数据归因：控制架构、初始化、目标函数与算力一致，使用四个关系数据生成器产出的语料训练四个Relational Transformer检查点。假设当跨表信息对掩码单元预训练目标具有预测必要性时关系机制会涌现。通过对比各语料下外键父表带来的预测增益、对未见数据库进行外键干预、随机初始化对照、逐步增加损坏链接比例，以及定位串行跨表通路来验证。

Result: RelDiff从外键链接父表获得的预测增益最大，其对应模型对未见数据库上的外键干预具有独特敏感性。该依赖性在随机初始化对照下依然存在，随损坏链接比例单调增长，并定位于一条串行跨表通路。最终在下游推理中干扰该机制会消除RelDiff在关系任务上的优势，而对结构不敏感模型几乎无影响。

Conclusion: 研究将合成训练数据的某一属性与所学机制联系起来，并通过干预进一步关联到下游行为，表明跨表信息的预测必要性是关系机制涌现的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: Relational foundation models are increasingly pretrained on synthetic databases, yet downstream benchmarks reveal little about why one synthetic corpus produces a better model than another. In particular, strong performance may arise from realistic row-level statistics without the model ever learning to use relational structure. We study this as a data-attribution problem: which property of synthetic pretraining data induces relational computation? Using four Relational Transformer checkpoints trained with the same architecture, initialization, objective, and compute budget on corpora produced by four relational data generators, we trace a measurable property of the data to learned computation and downstream behavior. We hypothesize that relational mechanisms emerge when cross-table information is predictively necessary for the masked-cell pretraining objective. RelDiff exhibits by far the largest predictive gain from foreign-key-linked parents, and its corresponding model is uniquely sensitive to foreign-key interventions on unseen databases. This dependence survives a random-initialization control, grows monotonically with the fraction of corrupted links, and localizes to a serial cross-table pathway. Finally, disrupting the same mechanism during downstream inference removes RelDiff's advantage on relational tasks while leaving structure-insensitive models nearly unchanged. These results connect a property of synthetic training data to a learned mechanism and, through intervention, to downstream behavior.

</details>


### [256] [Learning Transferable Policies from Action-free Time Series Through Dynamical Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03065)
*Niklas Emonds,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层基于模型的强化学习框架，利用相关系统间的共享结构，从无动作记录中学习系统特定的控制策略。分层动力学重建模型通过低维嵌入捕捉共享动力学与个体差异，并复用这些嵌入参数化共享策略与价值网络；策略完全在显式干预模型下通过仿真训练。在Lorenz-63和双摆系统上，分层策略提升了迁移性能，并在Lorenz-63上取得高于重复规划的均值奖励，接近有控制交互训练的方法，且在嵌入推断后能泛化到策略训练中未出现的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从无动作记录中学习控制十分困难：干预效应无法观测，且策略可能利用重建动力学中的误差。作者希望借助相关系统间的共享结构，从未包含控制动作的记录中推断系统特定的控制策略，并保持对干预效应与受控动力学的可解释性。

Method: 提出分层基于模型的强化学习框架：1）分层动力学系统重建模型，通过低维嵌入同时建模共享动力学与个体差异；2）将嵌入复用于参数化共享的策略网络与价值网络，把重建动力学的差异与控制差异关联；3）策略在显式干预模型（含加性潜扰动）下完全通过仿真训练；4）采用分段线性循环神经网络以支持受控动力学的机制分析；5）引入解码器约束，使干预的即时效应在观测空间中可解释，并允许对某一模态施加干预同时保护另一模态免受直接操纵。

Result: 在Lorenz-63与双摆系统上，分层策略相比独立训练策略取得更好的迁移效果；在Lorenz-63上，其平均奖励高于使用相同重建模型的重复规划，并与使用受控交互训练的方法表现相当；在仅进行嵌入推断后，可泛化到策略训练中未出现的系统。应用于神经行为记录时，在受限神经扰动下能够抑制预测的运动。

Conclusion: 共享动力学表征能够支持可迁移的控制，并促进从无动作记录中进行机制性假设生成，同时通过嵌入复用与解码器约束实现了控制差异的关联与干预效应的可解释性。

Abstract: Learning control from action-free recordings is challenging because intervention effects are unobserved and policies may exploit errors in reconstructed dynamics. We present a hierarchical model-based reinforcement learning framework that uses shared structure across related systems to learn system-specific control policies from action-free recordings. A hierarchical dynamical system reconstruction model captures shared dynamics and individual variation through low-dimensional embeddings. These embeddings are then reused to parameterize shared policy and value networks, linking differences in reconstructed dynamics to differences in control. Policies are trained entirely via simulation under an explicit intervention model with additive latent perturbations. Piecewise-linear recurrent neural networks enable mechanistic analyses of the controlled dynamics, while decoder-based constraints make the immediate effects of interventions interpretable in observation space and permit interventions on one modality while protecting another from direct manipulation. On Lorenz-63 and double-pendulum systems, hierarchical policies improve transfer over independently trained policies. On Lorenz-63, they also achieve a higher mean reward than repeated planning with the same reconstructed models, perform comparably to methods trained with controlled interactions, and generalize to systems absent from policy training after embedding inference alone. Applications to neural-behavioral recordings demonstrate suppression of predicted movement under constrained neural perturbations. Together, these findings show how shared dynamical representations support transferable control and mechanistic hypothesis generation from action-free recordings.

</details>


### [257] [Learn Feasibility Once, Optimize All Objectives: Derivative-Free Diffusion Models for Chance-Constrained Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03071)
*Ziwen Liu,Yan Liu,Congying Han,Tiande Guo,Yao Yan,Weichen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 D³Opt，一种无导数、基于扩散的框架，将机会约束建模与目标优化解耦：先用约束过滤后的决策训练风险条件扩散模型作为可复用先验并冻结，再在推理时通过退火粒子式 Feynman–Kac 校正优化事后指定的目标，实现无需重训练的非凸/非光滑无导数优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机会约束规划需在限制违约概率的前提下优化决策，但现有传统与学习方法在优化非凸或非光滑目标、以及在固定机会约束下适配不同目标方面仍存在困难；目标变化往往需要重新训练，缺乏可复用的约束先验。

Method: 方法核心是解耦约束与目标：仅在约束过滤后的决策上训练风险条件扩散模型，学习一次机会可行结构并冻结为可复用先验；推理阶段在冻结的反向扩散过程中引入退火、基于粒子的 Feynman–Kac 校正，仅利用函数评价优化事后指定目标，从而实现无导数优化且无需目标特定重训练。

Result: 理论上证明该校正能在冻结先验满足可行性时保持可行性，并给出将优化误差分解为学习先验覆盖、有限粒子近似与有限温度影响的误差界；实验在线性高斯 CCP、目标迁移任务和机会约束经济调度上验证了对光滑/非光滑及非凸目标的有效优化，以及固定机会约束下无需重训练的目标泛化能力。

Conclusion: D³Opt 通过扩散先验与 Feynman–Kac 校正的结合，实现了约束建模与目标优化的解耦，支持无导数、非凸/非光滑目标优化及目标迁移，为固定机会约束下的多目标优化提供了可复用、无需重训练的通用框架。

Abstract: Chance-constrained programs (CCPs) optimize decisions under uncertainty by limiting the probability of constraint violation. Despite advances in traditional and learning-based approaches, optimizing non-convex or non-smooth objectives and adapting to different objectives under fixed chance constraints remain challenging. In this paper, we propose a \textbf{D}erivative-free \textbf{D}iffusion-based framework that \textbf{D}isentangles constraint modeling from objective optimization, termed \textbf{D$^3$Opt}. We learn the chance-feasible structure once, independently of any particular objective, by training a risk-conditioned diffusion model solely on constraint-filtered decisions and freezing it as a reusable prior for post-specified objectives. At inference time, we propose an annealed, particle-based Feynman--Kac correction along the frozen reverse diffusion process to optimize post-specified objectives using only function evaluations. This enables derivative-free optimization of non-convex and non-smooth objectives without objective-specific retraining. We prove that the correction preserves feasibility when this property holds for the frozen prior, and derive an optimization-error bound separating learned-prior coverage, finite-particle approximation, and finite-temperature effects. Experiments on linear Gaussian CCPs, objective-transfer tasks, and chance-constrained economic dispatch demonstrate effective optimization across smooth and non-smooth objectives, including non-convex cases, and objective generalization under fixed chance constraints without retraining.

</details>


### [258] [Smart Sensing for Safer Bridges: From Sensor Signals to AI-Driven Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03082)
*Rahul Jaiswal,Joakim Hellum,Halvor Heiberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用信号处理与数据驱动的Isolation Forest两种互补方法，对挪威某桥梁iBridge设备采集的真实传感器数据进行异常检测，并通过异常计数、检测时间、处理速率、异常率、可视化与时间一致性等指标及受控异常注入实验进行对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桥梁对交通连通性和城市发展具有重要作用，可靠的桥梁监测对保障公共安全、及早发现结构异常征兆至关重要。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：信号处理方法与数据驱动机器学习模型Isolation Forest；数据来源为挪威某桥梁上安装的iBridge传感器设备采集的实时桥梁传感器数据；评估指标包括异常计数、异常检测时间、处理速率、异常率、可视化与时间一致性，并开展受控异常注入分析以评估各方法灵敏度。

Result: 数值结果表明，两种方法具有不同的检测特性与计算需求；机器学习（尤其是数据驱动的Isolation Forest）与信号处理相结合，在识别桥梁传感器测量异常方面具有潜力。

Conclusion: 信号处理与数据驱动的Isolation Forest方法互补，可用于真实桥梁传感器数据的异常检测，凸显了机器学习方法的潜力。

Abstract: Bridges contribute significantly to transportation connectivity and urban development. Therefore, reliable bridge monitoring is crucial for protecting public safety and detecting anomalous behavior in bridge sensor data that may provide early indications of abnormal structural conditions. This paper investigates anomaly detection in real-world bridge sensor data using two different complementary approaches, namely signal processing and the data-driven machine learning model Isolation Forest. The real-time bridge sensor data is collected from an iBridge sensor device installed on a bridge in Norway. The methods are evaluated using anomaly counts, anomaly detection time, processing rate, anomaly rates, visualization, and temporal agreement. Moreover, a controlled anomaly-injection analysis is performed to evaluate the sensitivity of each method. Numerical results demonstrate distinct detection characteristics and computational requirements, highlighting the potential of machine learning, particularly the data-driven Isolation Forest, alongside signal processing for identifying anomalies in bridge sensor measurements.

</details>


### [259] [Light Entropic Optimal Transport on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03085)
*Xavier Aramayo-Carrasco,Petr Mokrov,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ManifoldLightOT，一种在常见流形上直接学习核诱导熵最优传输耦合的轻量方法，通过几何特定的 Gibbs 核与势参数化实现闭式归一化和可采样条件分布，在合成与真实实验中常优于现有流形 OT 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵最优传输（EOT）已成为学习复杂分布间随机耦合的实用框架，广泛应用于生成建模与域适应。但多数 EOT 求解器面向欧氏空间，流形扩展有限且常依赖高成本迭代方法、模拟动力学或通用神经模型，未能充分利用底层几何结构。

Method: 基于 EOT 解的核形式，为球面、环面、SO(3)、SE(3) 构造几何特定的 Gibbs 核及兼容的势参数化，获得闭式归一化与可直接采样的条件分布；该形式自然推广至流形乘积；势参数通过蒙特卡洛估计学习目标，直接从样本优化。

Result: 在合成与真实世界实验中，ManifoldLightOT 在保留直接采样能力的同时，常优于现有流形 OT 方法。

Conclusion: ManifoldLightOT 提供了一种轻量、几何感知且可直接采样的流形 EOT 学习方案，适用于常见流形及其乘积，在性能与实用性上较现有流形 OT 方法具有优势。

Abstract: Entropic Optimal Transport (EOT) has become a practical framework for learning stochastic couplings between complex distributions, with applications in generative modeling and domain adaptation. However, most EOT solvers are designed for Euclidean spaces, while manifold extensions remain limited and often rely on costly iterative methods, simulated dynamics, or generic neural models that do not fully exploit the underlying geometry. We introduce ManifoldLightOT, a light approach for learning kernel-induced EOT couplings directly on common manifolds. Using the kernel form of the EOT solution, we construct geometry-specific Gibbs kernels together with compatible potential parameterizations for spheres, tori, $\mathrm{SO}(3)$, and $\mathrm{SE}(3)$. These choices yield closed-form normalization and directly sampleable conditional distributions. Our formulation naturally extends to products of manifolds, making it applicable to more complex geometries. The parameters of the potentials are optimized directly from samples using Monte Carlo estimates of the learning objective. Through synthetic and real-world experiments, we show that ManifoldLightOT often outperforms existing manifold OT methods while retaining direct sampling.

</details>


### [260] [Zephon: Elastic Determinism for Online, Stateful Foundation Model Data Loading Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03087)
*Maximilian Böther,Josh Wills,Ties Robroek,Sonnet Xu,Paul Burstein,Daniel Zayas,Cody Blakeney,Siddharth Joshi,Haoli Yin,Rishabh Adiga,Haakon Mongstad,Luke Merrick,Pratyush Maini,Ari Morcos,Matthew Leavitt,Ana Klimovic,Bogdan Gaza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Zephon，一个面向基础模型训练的数据加载器，支持在线、有状态的数据流水线，并提供“弹性确定性”（elastic determinism）与高效的检查点恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性数据加载对基础模型研发至关重要：研究者需要确保在昂贵消融实验中观察到的差异源于所改变的参数，而非训练数据序列的非确定性。数据加载器必须提供弹性确定性，即在 GPU 拓扑变化（如 GPU 资源短缺）、频繁的检查点-恢复周期、以及不同数据处理执行后端下，仍能保持全局训练数据批次的确定性序列。然而现代基础模型数据流水线在线进行分词、打包与混合，引入了有状态的 n 对多（n-to-m）变换，破坏了样本索引。现有加载器大多假设可索引的 1 对 1 流水线，而常用的离线物化（materialization）方案代价高昂，且对视频等模态甚至不可行。

Method: Zephon 将全局数据流划分为与拓扑无关的“车道”（lanes）；将顺序决策串行化，同时在可互换的后端上并行执行无状态工作；并且仅对有限数量的在途状态（bounded in-flight state）进行检查点保存，使恢复成本不随训练进度增长。

Result: 在文本与视觉-语言（vision-language）工作负载上评估 Zephon，结果显示其在实现有竞争力吞吐量的同时，提供了现有加载器对在线、有状态流水线均无法提供的多项保证组合。

Conclusion: Zephon 通过车道划分、顺序决策串行化与有界在途状态检查点等技术，解决了在线有状态流水线的弹性确定性与高效恢复难题，在不牺牲吞吐的前提下提供了超越现有方案的综合保证。

Abstract: Deterministic data loading is important for foundation model development: model researchers need confidence that differences they observe across costly ablations are caused by the parameter they changed rather than non-determinism in the training data sequence. The data loader must provide elastic determinism, i.e., a deterministic sequence of global training data batches despite changes to the GPU topology across runs (e.g., due to GPU scarcity), frequent checkpoint-resume cycles, and different data processing execution backends. Achieving this is difficult because modern foundation model data pipelines tokenize, pack, and mix samples online, introducing stateful n-to-m transformations that break sample indexing. Existing data loaders largely assume indexable 1-to-1 pipelines, and the common workaround of offline materialization is expensive and, for some modalities such as video, infeasible.
  We present Zephon, a data loader for foundation models that supports online, stateful pipelines while providing elastic determinism and efficient resumption from checkpoints. It partitions the global stream into topology-independent lanes, serializes ordering decisions while parallelizing stateless work on interchangeable backends, and checkpoints only bounded in-flight state so recovery cost does not grow with training progress. We evaluate Zephon on text and vision-language workloads and show that it achieves competitive throughput while providing a combination of guarantees that no existing loader offers for online, stateful pipelines.

</details>


### [261] [ULTRADISCOVERY: Abductive Exploration in an Interconnected, Epistemically Open Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03092)
*Weihan Li,Tianshi Zheng,Yangqiu Song,Ginny Y. Wong,Simon See*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 ULTRADISCOVERY 交互式基准，通过 2×2 设计分离"表征开放性"与"证据分布性"两种溯因探索需求，发现当前模型的关键瓶颈在于将累积证据组合为可迁移的表征，而非单纯积累证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现常始于零散线索呼唤新的世界描述方式。这种溯因探索包含两个可区分的要求：一是当世界在认知上开放时，需要构造出解释所依据的表征；二是当世界在结构上相互关联时，需要组合跨情境散布的证据。现有基准很少将这两种需求分离或独立控制，因此无法定位模型究竟卡在哪一步。

Method: 构建 ULTRADISCOVERY：一个含五个领域的交互式世界，智能体需修正一个初始成功的理论，并预测未见过的跨领域干预结果。采用 2×2 设计，分别控制表征是开放还是被披露、证据是分散还是被对齐，同时固定潜在动力学。评估覆盖 11 个模型的智能体，并考察两个厂商自研的 harness 系统，统计干预请求次数、发现的十八条结论中被揭示的数量，以及在 200 次付费动作预算内能否给出精确预测。

Result: 表征开放时，11 个模型中的智能体常常撤回它们被教授的公理，且没有任何一个引入未观测实体或重写替换理论所需的变量。披露表征使干预请求增至三倍，并额外带来约十八条可得结论中的一条，证据对齐带来的增益更小。两个厂商 harness 系统将发现扩展到更多领域，其中一个在开放情节中重写了变量。没有任何系统能在 200 次付费动作内做出精确预测。在更大预算下，同时具备两种辅助时才出现一次精确预测，而所有开放情节仍不精确。

Conclusion: 困难不在于证据的累积，而在于从累积证据到将其组合成一个可迁移表征的那一步。基准因此把溯因发现的核心难点定位在表征构建与迁移环节。

Abstract: Scientific discovery often begins when scattered clues call for a new way of describing the world. Such abductive exploration can require constructing the representation in which an explanation is stated, when the world is epistemically open, and composing evidence scattered across contexts, when it is structurally interconnected. Existing benchmarks rarely separate these two demands or control them independently. We introduce ULTRADISCOVERY, an interactive world of five domains in which an agent revises an initially successful theory and predicts the outcome of an unseen cross-domain intervention. A $2 \times 2$ design leaves the representation open or discloses it, and leaves the evidence distributed or aligns it, with the latent dynamics fixed. With the representation open, agents across eleven models often retract the axiom they were taught, and none introduces the unobserved entity or rewrites the variables that a replacement requires. Disclosure triples intervention requests and adds about one of the eighteen findings the world affords, and alignment adds less. Two vendor-harness systems carry discovery into more domains, and one of them rewrites the variables in Open episodes. No system makes the exact prediction within 200 paid actions. At larger budgets one exact prediction appears with both aids, while every Open episode remains inexact. The results locate the difficulty in the step from accumulating evidence to composing it into a representation that transfers.

</details>


### [262] [LS-AR: Future-Predictive Latent Steering in Autoregressive LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03093)
*Anubha Gupta,Eduardo Pignatelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LS-AR双通道自回归架构，通过FiLM条件将连续目标引导与离散token解码解耦，在长程检索与规划任务上显著优于参数匹配基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归模型将高层任务指令、状态历史与瞬态token混在单一共享序列中处理，缺乏将宏观目标与上下文噪声隔离的架构机制，存在单通道瓶颈。

Method: LS-AR采用双通道架构：连续目标引导与离散token解码解耦，经FiLM条件实现调制。设计并评估两种变体：静态目标编码器P_0用于长rollout中持久宏观目标保持，动态状态跟踪器P_t用于生成过程中的循环隐变量更新。

Result: 长程检索（H=1024, W=500，超出上下文限制）中LS-AR(Static)达100%目标召回，参数匹配基线崩溃至0%，吞吐量提升约35%，峰值VRAM降低52.8%；Blocksworld强制扰动(k=1)下LS-AR(Dynamic)完成率89.0% vs 基线71.0%，但零样本实体扩展(N→N+1)暴露出单向量容量瓶颈(0%)；双通道权限分析显示文本目标dropout可建立隐变量主导控制。

Conclusion: 双通道解耦有效隔离宏观目标与上下文噪声，提升长程任务性能与效率；文本目标dropout为文本提示注入提供结构性防御，但引入隐变量向量攻击面，且单向量容量限制零样本实体泛化。

Abstract: Standard autoregressive (AR) models process high-level task instructions, state history, and transient tokens within a single shared sequence of tokens. Consequently, they lack the architectural mechanisms needed to isolate macro-objectives from context noise. To overcome this single-channel limitation, we introduce Latent-Steered Autoregressive (LS-AR), a dual-channel architecture that decouples continuous goal steering from discrete token decoding via FiLM conditioning. We evaluate a Static Goal Encoder (P_0) for persistent macro-objective retention across long rollouts and a Dynamic State Tracker (P_t) for recurrent latent updates during generation. On long-horizon retrieval past context limits (H=1024, W=500), LS-AR (Static) achieves 100% target recall where parameter-matched baselines collapse (0%), while increasing throughput by ~35% and cutting peak VRAM by 52.8%. In Blocksworld planning under forced perturbations (k=1), LS-AR (Dynamic) sustains an 89.0% completion rate vs. 71.0% for the baseline, though zero-shot entity scaling (N -> N+1) exposes single-vector capacity limits (0%). Finally, dual-channel authority analysis shows that text goal dropout establishes latent-dominant control, offering structural defence against text prompt injection while introducing a latent vector attack surface.

</details>


### [263] [Exploring the Trade-Off Between Structured Pruning and Fault Tolerance in Deep Neural Networks for Space Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03117)
*Toon Vinck,Naïn Jonckers,Jaro De Roose,Jeffrey Prinzie,Peter Karsmakers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究模型宽度与单粒子翻转(SEU)鲁棒性的关系，通过结构化剪枝降低宽度并逐阶段进行故障注入，发现剪枝虽提升单次推理敏感度，但执行时间缩短使遭遇SEU的概率下降，两者相互抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN因分布式信息表示而具有一定位级容错性，理论上宽度越大信息越分散、单比特故障影响越小。但宽度与SEU鲁棒性的实际关系尚缺乏系统实证，且对面向空间应用的可靠AI设计具有指导意义。

Method: 对基线模型进行迭代式结构化剪枝以降低宽度，并尽量保持任务性能；在每个剪枝阶段开展针对性的故障注入实验，评估模型在模拟位翻转场景下的表现。

Result: 结构化剪枝因减少冗余而提高了单次推理对故障的敏感度，但该效应被执行时间缩短所抵消——更短执行时间降低了遇到SEU的概率，二者总体平衡。

Conclusion: 结构化剪枝可在不损害整体可靠性的前提下带来显著的能耗与时延节省，为设计面向空间应用的鲁棒AI系统提供指导。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) inherently exhibit a degree of robustness to bit-level faults due to their distributed representation of information. As a model increases in width, this information becomes more dispersed, theoretically reducing the impact of any single bit fault. In this paper, we empirically investigate the relationship between model width and robustness to Single Event Upsets (SEUs). We conduct a comprehensive experiment in which baseline models undergo iterative structured pruning to reduce their width while preserving task performance as much as possible. At each pruning stage, we run a targeted fault-injection campaign to evaluate the model's performance under simulated bit-flip scenarios. Our results show that, although structured pruning increases per-inference sensitivity to faults by reducing redundancy, this effect is effectively counterbalanced by shorter execution time, which lowers the probability of encountering an SEU. These findings suggest that structured pruning can yield significant energy and latency savings without compromising overall reliability, providing useful guidance for designing robust AI systems for space applications.

</details>


### [264] [How to Find and Reuse Policies for Continuous Adaptation in Lifelong Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03119)
*Saptarshi Nath,Inish M. D'Souza,Antonio Carta,Soheil Kolouri,Andrea Soltoggio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AMSC方法，通过在线经验的非参数Wasserstein任务嵌入估计任务相似度，用z-score归一化sparsemax生成可变支撑集，周期性选择并加权先前策略以构建新任务的先验，在CT-graph与MiniGrid上实现更高的平均性能与前向迁移且无遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在终身强化学习中，仅保留先前策略不足以有效迁移到新任务：有用知识可能分散在多个先前策略中，且其相关性会随学习经验变化。作者假设任务相似度可作为持续学习中选择与组合先前策略的有效依据。

Method: 设计Adaptive Mask Selection and Composition (AMSC)：基于状态-动作-奖励样本构建非参数Wasserstein任务嵌入，从在线经验中估计相似度；对相似度分数做z-score归一化后经sparsemax生成可变大小的支撑集（mask），据此周期性选择并加权若干策略，形成学习新任务时的先验。

Result: 在CT-graph和MiniGrid上，AMSC的平均性能与前向迁移均高于所评估的模块化组合基线，且无遗忘。在Continual World上结果表明，识别相关先验知识及其层特定组合可能需要额外的逐层调优。消融实验显示，选择相关来源与确定复用强度是性能增益的关键；独立测量的成对迁移与任务嵌入相似度呈正相关。

Conclusion: 任务相似度可作为终身强化学习中选择与加权特定知识以复用的有效准则；AMSC验证了该思路在无遗忘前提下的迁移优势，同时提示层特定组合（如Continual World）仍需进一步调优。

Abstract: In lifelong reinforcement learning, retaining previously learned policies is not sufficient for effective transfer to a new task. Useful knowledge may be distributed across several prior policies, and its relevance may change as the learner acquires experience. One hypothesis is that task similarity can be effectively used in a continual learning setting to find and combine previously learned policies. To test it, Adaptive Mask Selection and Composition (AMSC) is designed to estimate similarity from online experience via non-parametric Wasserstein task embeddings from state-action-reward samples. The z-score-normalized sparsemax of the similarity scores are used to derive a variable-size support to periodically choose and weight policies to form a prior when learning a new task. On CT-graph and MiniGrid, AMSC achieves higher mean performance and forward transfer than the evaluated modular composition baselines while exhibiting no forgetting. Results on Continual World suggest that identifying relevant prior knowledge and determining its layer-specific composition may require additional layer-specific tuning. Ablations show that selecting relevant sources and determining how strongly to reuse them are central to these gains. Independently measured pairwise transfer is also positively associated with task-embedding similarity. These results indicate that task similarity can be an effective criterion to select and weight specific knowledge for reuse in lifelong reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [265] [Coverage You Can Steer: Online Conformal Calibration for RL-Driven Hardware-Aware NAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03127)
*Pedro Brandimarte,Nerea Aranjuelo,Marcos Nieto,Oihana Otaegui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用在线反馈控制(Adaptive Conformal Inference)替代一次性分位数估计,修复RL驱动NAS中因候选分布非交换性导致的覆盖失控问题,实现可调、单调、可复现的覆盖率并剪枝25-50%评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件感知NAS的主要瓶颈是评估成本:每个架构都要训练后才能获得奖励。共形预测过滤器通过剪掉预测奖励上界低于阈值的候选来降低成本,并提供至多错误丢弃δ比例的分布无关保证。但该保证依赖校准集与测试候选之间的可交换性,而RL搜索循环恰恰破坏了这一假设:策略的提议随搜索推进而改善,在逐层构建中每个episode内分布也在漂移。因此,静态校准的覆盖保证在NAS场景下失效。

Method: 将一次性分位数估计替换为在线反馈控制,即Adaptive Conformal Inference,并采用其无调参、局部自适应与群条件三种变体,以恢复可操控的覆盖率,使其对任意序列单调、可复现且能精确达到目标水平。实验覆盖三个神经网络架构族、单步与序贯搜索两种设置、三个随机种子,并将同一乐观上界用作采集函数在一个受限测试平台上评估。

Result: 在全部设置下,在线校准将每个请求的覆盖水平跟踪到约10^{-3}以内,同时剪枝25-50%的评估且未测得精度损失;静态校准则失去覆盖控制,高斯过程基线无论请求多少都保持保守。作为采集函数时,该方法优于随机搜索,而这一增益本身来自固定乐观性,在线校准进一步放大了它。

Conclusion: RL驱动的NAS循环破坏了共形预测的可交换性假设,使静态校准不再可靠;用在线自适应共形推断替代可恢复对覆盖率的可控性,在保持精度的同时显著降低评估成本,并可作为有效的采集函数。代码已开源于 https://github.com/Vicomtech/rl-hw-nas。

Abstract: Hardware-aware neural architecture search (NAS) is dominated by evaluation cost: every architecture must be trained before its reward is known. Conformal-prediction filters cut this cost by pruning candidates whose predicted-reward upper bound misses a threshold, with a distribution-free guarantee that at most a fraction $δ$ are wrongly discarded. That guarantee assumes exchangeability between calibration and test candidates, which the surrounding reinforcement-learning (RL) loop violates: the policy's proposals improve as search proceeds and, in layer-by-layer construction, shift within every episode. We replace one-shot quantile estimation with online feedback control (Adaptive Conformal Inference, with tuning-free, locally-adaptive, and group-conditional variants), restoring steerable coverage: dialing the target delivers it, monotonically and reproducibly, for arbitrary sequences. Across three neural-network architecture families and both single-step and sequential search (three seeds), it tracks every requested level to within ${\sim}10^{-3}$ while pruning 25-50% of evaluations at no measured accuracy cost, whereas static calibration loses control of its coverage and a Gaussian-process baseline stays conservative regardless of the request. Finally, used as an acquisition function on one constrained testbed, the same optimistic bound beats random search, a gain that fixed optimism already carries and online calibration sharpens. The source code is available at https://github.com/Vicomtech/rl-hw-nas.

</details>


### [266] [Page-EntroKV: Hardware-Aligned, Entropy-Weighted KV-Cache Eviction under Grouped-Query Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03135)
*Inbasekaran S*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Page-EntroKV，一种在 GQA 物理组粒度上进行 KV cache 淘汰的框架：用 sink 隔离的 Renyi-2 熵权重池化组内各 head 分数，并映射到 PagedAttention 页帧，实现 (layer, group, page) 粒度的精确预算执行，避免 per-head 独立选择导致的并集膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文自回归模型的 serving 受 KV cache 制约。现有动态淘汰方法按 query head 独立打分选 token，与 GQA 中多个 query head 共享同一物理 KV buffer 的分配方式不匹配：per-head 选择的分歧迫使引擎保留各 head 选择的并集，使 cache 膨胀至多约组比 r 倍；而算术平均池化又会稀释承载事实回忆的专用检索 head。因此需要与 GQA 实际分配粒度对齐的淘汰框架。

Method: Page-EntroKV：在每个物理组内，用基于 sink 隔离碰撞（Renyi-2）熵的权重池化各 head 分数，每 head 仅一次内积，prefill 时计算一次、无需校准，使 sink head 无法伪装成检索 head；池化分数投影到 PagedAttention 页帧，在硬件 tuple（layer, group, page）上执行淘汰。形式化定义并集开销比（UOR）与组内分歧，证明二者在双 head 组下的精确恒等式及各组比下的双边界，证明严格预算保持与算术均值池化必然违反的有限上下文 needle 保持界，并给出逐层精确页核算。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct（r=6）上，对 2,240 组测量的 head 独立 replay 显示：2% 预算下并集开销高达 4.75x，而 Page-EntroKV 的 UOR 精确保持 1.000；sink 隔离消除了 13x 的 sink 伪装；20% 预算下 needle recall 为 100% 对比均值池化的 0%；各页大小下保留基数均精确；QA 与代码任务在 20% 保留率下仍可解。

Conclusion: 与 GQA 物理分配粒度对齐的池化淘汰（而非 per-head 独立选择或算术均值池化）可消除并集开销、抑制 sink 干扰并保持精确预算，从而在激进压缩下维持长上下文检索与下游任务性能。

Abstract: Serving long-context autoregressive language models is constrained by the key-value (KV) cache. Most dynamic eviction methods score token importance per query head and choose tokens independently. This fits poorly with grouped-query attention (GQA), where several query heads share one physical KV buffer: divergent per-head selections force the serving engine to retain the union of their choices - inflating the cache by up to the group ratio r - while arithmetic mean pooling dilutes the specialized retrieval heads that carry factual recall. We introduce Page-EntroKV, a formal framework for KV-cache eviction operating at the granularity GQA serving actually allocates. Heads within each physical group are pooled by weights derived from sink-isolated collision (Renyi-2) entropy - one inner product per head, computed once at prefill with no calibration - so sink heads cannot masquerade as retrieval heads. Pooled scores are projected onto PagedAttention page frames, and eviction executes at the hardware tuple (layer, group, page). We formalize the union overhead ratio (UOR) and intra-group disagreement, prove an exact identity linking them for two-head groups alongside two-sided bounds at every group ratio, prove strict budget preservation and a finite-context needle-retention bound that arithmetic mean pooling provably violates, and give exact per-layer page accounting. On a pilot architecture (Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, r=6), head-independent replay over 2,240 group measurements yields union overhead up to 4.75x at a 2% budget, while Page-EntroKV holds UOR exactly 1.000; sink isolation removes a 13x sink masquerade; needle recall is 100% versus 0% for mean pooling at a 20% budget; retained cardinality is exact for every page size; and QA and code tasks remain solvable at 20% retention.

</details>


### [267] [Sample complexity of variance-reduced policy gradient: weaker assumptions and lower bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03165)
*Gabor Paczolay,Matteo Papini,Alberto Maria Metelli,Istvan Harmati,Marcello Restelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出防御性策略梯度算法（Defensive Policy Gradient），基于防御性重要性采样，在不对普通重要性权重方差作任何假设的情况下达到 $O(\epsilon^{-3})$ 样本复杂度，并给出黑盒策略优化模型下的下界，证明该更快收敛速率的紧致性与最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基于重要性采样的方差缩减 REINFORCE 变体虽能达到 $O(\epsilon^{-3})$ 样本复杂度，但依赖于对重要性权重方差的不现实假设；作者希望去除这一假设，并在更一般的模型中验证该速率的本质最优性。

Method: 提出 \algo（Defensive Policy Gradient）算法，采用防御性重要性采样来消除对普通重要性权重方差的假设；同时在隐藏状态与动作、允许参数依赖奖励的广义黑盒策略优化模型中建立下界，并分析其 oracle 条件及策略正则条件下的上界实现。

Result: 在黑盒模型中，有界方差单策略反馈的最优速率为 $\Theta(\epsilon^{-4})$，均方光滑耦合双策略反馈的最优速率为 $\Theta(\epsilon^{-3})$；在标准策略正则条件下，REINFORCE 与 \algo 分别实现对应 oracle 条件并达到 $O(\epsilon^{-4})$ 与 $O(\epsilon^{-3})$ 上界。

Conclusion: 尽管下界不直接适用于算法实际运行的经典 MDP 交互模型，但这一对应关系提供了 oracle 层面的证据，表明 \algo 更快的收敛速率是紧致的，且与朴素策略梯度存在本质分离。

Abstract: Several variance-reduced versions of REINFORCE based on importance sampling achieve an improved $O(ε^{-3})$ sample complexity to find an $ε$-stationary point, under an unrealistic assumption on the variance of the importance weights. In this paper, we propose the \algo (Defensive Policy Gradient) algorithm, based on defensive importance sampling, which achieves the same rate without any assumption on the variance of ordinary importance weights. We also establish lower bounds in a generalized black-box policy-optimization model that hides states and actions and permits parameter-dependent rewards. In this model, the optimal rates are $Θ(ε^{-4})$ with bounded-variance one-policy feedback and $Θ(ε^{-3})$ with mean-square-smooth coupled two-policy feedback. Under standard policy-regularity conditions, REINFORCE and \algo realize the corresponding oracle conditions and attain the $O(ε^{-4})$ and $O(ε^{-3})$ upper bounds, respectively. Although the lower bounds do not apply directly to the classical MDP interaction model in which these algorithms operate, this correspondence provides oracle-level evidence that the faster rate of \algo is optimal and genuinely separated from that of vanilla policy gradient.

</details>


### [268] [Predicting and Repairing Merge Collapse in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03199)
*Jungseob Lee,Seungyoon Lee,Sugyeong Eo,Hyeonseok Moon,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现任务向量在专家间的方差（即"干扰"）既能预测模型合并的坍塌，也能校准其修复；据此提出PRISM算子，无需数据或调参即可在越过阈值的破坏性合并中保持性能接近基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享基座微调的大模型可通过平均任务向量进行合并，但部分合并会严重坍塌至远低于基座模型，且现有合并算子在被评估前不会给出任何预警。因此需要一个能事前预测坍塌并指导修复的统计量。

Method: 1）提出以任务向量在专家间的方差作为"干扰"度量，指出平均所移除的功率恰等于该方差；2）在工作噪声模型下，推导合并注入的扰动随合并系数与干扰增大，得到合并前评分；3）指出符号冲突类统计具有反向预测性；4）提出PRISM算子：先对任务向量取平均，再按各层干扰水平对每层做软阈值处理；5）仅对超过阈值的合并启用PRISM，未超阈值者保留普通平均。

Result: 在来自四个模型家族的22个合并配置中，仅破坏性合并超过该评分阈值。在评估前预测14个合并结果，其中12个预测正确，包括继续预训练导致专家对越阈后的破坏性结果。PRISM在无数据、无调参条件下，使全部5个破坏性合并保持在阈值以上，与基座模型的评估噪声相当；而普通平均至少低14.4分或完全坍塌。15个无害合并均未超阈值。

Conclusion: 任务向量跨专家的方差是可预测合并坍塌并校准修复的有效统计量；PRISM以干扰自适应的分层软阈值实现无数据修复，配合阈值门控可安全地仅对破坏性合并生效，兼顾性能保持与无害合并的原始行为。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned from a shared base can be merged by averaging their task vectors, but some merges collapse far below the base model, and common merge operators give no warning before evaluation. We show that one statistic of the specialists' task vectors both predicts this collapse and calibrates its repair. The power that averaging removes equals the variance of the task vectors across specialists, our measure of interference. Under a working noise model, the disturbance that a merge injects grows with the merge coefficient and with interference, yielding a pre-merge score. In our experiments on twenty-two merge configurations from four model families, only destructive merges exceed a threshold on this score. We find that statistics of sign conflict between specialists, a common target of existing merge operators, are anti-predictive. We then predicted the outcomes of fourteen merges before evaluating them, and twelve predictions were correct, including the destructive outcome of a specialist pair pushed past the threshold by continued pretraining. To address this collapse, we introduce PRISM, an operator that averages the task vectors first and then soft-thresholds each layer at a level set by the layer's interference. Without data or tuning, PRISM keeps all five destructive merges above the threshold within evaluation noise of the base model, where plain averaging falls at least 14.4 points below it or collapses entirely. We apply PRISM only above the threshold and keep the plain average for merges below it, which include all fifteen harmless ones. Code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/PRISM.

</details>


### [269] [HyperFuse: Fast Self-Supervised Node Embeddings for Attributed Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03211)
*Megha P,Harshit Kumar,Srajan Agarwal,Anirban Banerjee,Olaf Wolkenhauer,Saptarshi Bej*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyperFuse是一种无需标签的快速超图表示学习流程，通过谱松弛节点坐标、多尺度特征摘要与有界效用权重，以及轻量编码器，实现高效嵌入生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督超图表示学习方法通常需要深度编码器训练数百轮，即使只有几千个节点的超图，嵌入生成成本也很高，限制了需要为大量或演化超图生成嵌入的应用。

Method: HyperFuse包含三部分：(i) 利用Banerjee超图邻接矩阵与线性代价的无矩阵算子，通过最大化超图模块度谱松弛计算结构节点坐标；(ii) 基于特征与成员掩码下的成员稳定性，构建多尺度特征摘要并为超边分配有界效用权重；(iii) 使用不变性-去相关目标训练轻量效用加权超图编码器100轮。

Result: 在九个公开超图上与TriCL、SE-HSSL、VilLain、HypeBoy比较，使用六种下游分类器与k-means聚类。在全部方法完成的八个数据集上，HyperFuse平均每数据集耗时8.7秒，较基线获得13-179倍几何平均加速；在六种分类器中五种取得最高平均准确率，分类与聚类性能与TriCL和SE-HSSL无显著差异；相比HypeBoy快13倍，所有分类器准确率高2.1-4.1个百分点。

Conclusion: HyperFuse为快速、重复的超图嵌入生成提供了一种实用方法。

Abstract: Self-supervised hypergraph representation learning can produce informative node embeddings, but existing methods often require deep encoders trained for hundreds of epochs, making embedding generation costly even for hypergraphs with a few thousand nodes. This limits applications requiring embeddings for many or evolving hypergraphs. We present HyperFuse, a label-free pipeline for fast hypergraph representation learning. HyperFuse (i) computes structural node coordinates by maximizing a spectral relaxation of hypergraph modularity using Banerjee's hypergraph adjacency and a matrix-free operator with cost linear in node-hyperedge incidences; (ii) constructs multi-scale feature summaries and assigns bounded utility weights to hyperedges based on member stability under feature and membership masking; and (iii) trains a lightweight utility-weighted hypergraph encoder for 100 epochs using an invariance-decorrelation objective. We compare HyperFuse with TriCL, SE-HSSL, VilLain, and HypeBoy on nine public hypergraphs using six downstream classifiers and k-means clustering. On the eight datasets where all methods completed, HyperFuse required 8.7 s per dataset on average, achieving 13-179x geometric-mean speed-ups over the baselines. It achieved the highest average accuracy with five of six classifiers, while classification and clustering performance was not significantly different from TriCL and SE-HSSL. Compared with HypeBoy, HyperFuse was 13x faster and 2.1-4.1 percentage points more accurate across all classifiers. HyperFuse provides a practical approach for fast, repeated hypergraph embedding generation.

</details>


### [270] [Kernel Singular Value Decomposition with Extension to Multiple Data Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03216)
*Xinjie Zeng,Qinghua Tao,Johan Suykens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 eKSVD，将核奇异值分解（KSVD）从两类数据源扩展至多数据源，通过原始形式联合学习非线性特征并结合成对耦合，基于拉格朗日与 KKT 条件在孪生问题中推广移位特征值问题，并给出协方差框架与神经网络显式特征映射，实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KSVD 仅处理由两类数据源（如自注意力的 query 与 key、矩阵的行与列）诱导的非对称核矩阵，无法对多个数据源进行联合非线性特征学习，亟需将其扩展至多数据源场景。

Method: 提出 eKSVD：在原始形式中联合学习各数据源的投影以捕获最大信息并引入成对耦合；借助拉格朗日函数及其 KKT 条件，在孪生问题中得到 KSVD 的 Lanczos 分解定理中移位特征值问题的推广；进一步导出基于协方差的框架，并使用神经网络实现显式特征映射。

Result: 数值实验表明，eKSVD 在处理多数据源任务时优于基于 Mercer 核的方法；同时，引入神经网络为核方法提供了较大的灵活性。

Conclusion: eKSVD 成功将 KSVD 推广到多数据源的非对称核联合非线性特征学习，其核方法与协方差/神经网络两种解释相互补充，兼具理论推广性与实践灵活性。

Abstract: Kernel Singular Value Decomposition (KSVD) learns a pair of singular vectors w.r.t. an asymmetric kernel matrix, which can be induced by two data sources, e.g., the queries and keys in self-attention or the rows and columns of a given matrix. In this work, we extend KSVD to multiple data sources, namely eKSVD, which conducts joint nonlinear feature learning upon asymmetric kernels. In the primal formulation, the projections associated with each data source are jointly learned to capture maximal information, while incorporating pair-wise couplings. With the Lagrangian and its Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions, the optimization in the dual leads to a generalization of the shifted eigenvalue problem in Lanczos decomposition theorem of KSVD. Further, a covariance-based framework is derived together with using neural networks (NNs) for explicit feature mappings, complementary to the kernel-based interpretation and optimization. Numerical experiments verify the effectiveness of our eKSVD compared to methods based on Mercer kernels for tackling multiple data sources, and our innovation of deploying NNs demonstrates great flexibility for kernel methods.

</details>


### [271] [AdaStep: Adaptive Step Credit Weighting for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03223)
