*Xin Wang,Wenhao Wu,Menghao Zhang,Zhi Wang,Kun Shao,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AdaStep，一种自适应逐步信用加权方法，通过将逐步优势估计建模为均方误差问题并推导最优收缩系数，在低计算成本下改善长程 LLM 智能体的轨迹级训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程 LLM 智能体通常仅使用稀疏的结果奖励进行训练，轨迹级目标过于粗糙，无法区分单个决策的贡献；而逐步信用分配虽提供更细粒度监督，但其估计不可靠，因为观测回报还依赖后续动作、环境转移和轨迹长度。

Method: 将逐步信用加权形式化为对潜在逐步优势的均方误差估计问题，在显式的条件采样假设下推导出最优的逐状态收缩系数；该系数具有信号-总方差比的解释：当回报变异可归因于所选动作时保留局部信用，当变异由下游随机性主导时抑制之。仅需轻量标量计算，无需 critic、额外 rollout 或额外模型推理。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 ScienceWorld 三个环境上，使用三种模型骨干进行实验，均一致优于基线，且计算成本低。

Conclusion: AdaStep 通过控制组内局部优势对轨迹级信号的修正强度，实现了可靠且高效的逐步信用分配，为长程 LLM 智能体训练提供了低开销的有效改进。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents are typically trained with sparse outcome rewards, making trajectory-level objectives too coarse to distinguish the contribution of individual decisions. Step-level credit assignment provides finer-grained supervision, but its estimates can be unreliable because observed returns also depend on subsequent actions, environment transitions, and trajectory length. We propose AdaStep, an Adaptive Step-credit weighting method that controls how strongly each group-derived local advantage modifies the trajectory-level signal. We formulate this weighting as a mean-squared-error estimation problem for the latent step advantage and, under an explicit conditional sampling assumption, derive an optimal per-state shrinkage coefficient. The coefficient admits a signal-to-total-variance interpretation: it preserves local credit when return variation is attributable to the selected action and suppresses it when variation is dominated by downstream randomness. AdaStep requires only lightweight scalar computation, with no critic, additional rollouts, or extra model inference. Experiments with three model backbones on ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld show consistent improvements over baselines at low computational cost.

</details>


### [272] [The Neuro-Physical Inverter: A Modular Framework for Magnetotelluric Inversion Coupling Ensemble Conditioning with Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03225)
*Jae Deok Kim,Sai Ravela,Rob. L. Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经-物理反演器（NPI），一个模块化、不确定性感知的地球物理反演框架，将集合条件化与约束残差学习耦合，在一维大地电磁场景中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地球物理反演需要兼顾物理可行性与不确定性量化，而现有方法往往难以在降低误差的同时稳定地传播集合不确定性，尤其缺乏可扩展至更高维参数化的统一框架。

Method: NPI分两阶段：第一阶段用集合条件高斯过程（EnsCGP）将电阻率模型先验集合条件化于观测响应，生成物理可行参考集合；第二阶段用残差学习神经网络预测对该参考的定向修正，基于合成数据训练，并通过物理耦合目标函数逐台站微调以应用于实测数据。

Result: 合成实验表明NPI系统性地降低集合均值误差且不破坏集合稳定性；应用于美国内华达州Gabbs Valley地热区宽带MT数据时，在中间周期频段降低了跨台站平均失配，同时保持相当的集合离散度。

Conclusion: 传播的集合给出不确定性因子，可作为假定模型类内约束程度的操作性度量；两阶段在公式上均与维度无关，所建立的设计原则有望推广至高维参数化。

Abstract: We present the Neuro-Physical Inverter (NPI), a modular, uncertainty-aware framework for geophysical inversion that couples ensemble-based conditioning with constrained residual learning, demonstrated in the 1D magnetotelluric (MT) setting as a controlled testbed. The framework operates in two stages. An Ensemble-Conditional Gaussian Process (EnsCGP) conditions a prior ensemble of resistivity models on the observed response, producing a physically admissible reference ensemble. A residual-learning neural network then predicts targeted corrections to this reference, trained on synthetic data and fine-tuned per station for field application through a physics-coupled objective. Because an ensemble is conditioned, refined, and propagated through both stages, every estimate carries an associated ensemble spread. Synthetic experiments show that NPI systematically reduces ensemble-mean error without destabilizing the ensemble. Applied to broadband MT data from the Gabbs Valley geothermal region (Nevada, USA), NPI reduces the across-station mean misfit over the mid-period band while retaining comparable ensemble spread. The propagated ensemble yields a factor of uncertainty that serves as an operational measure of constraint within the assumed model class. Both stages are dimension-agnostic in formulation, and the design principles established here are intended to scale to higher-dimensional parameterizations.

</details>


### [273] [D2K-Bench: Can LLM Agents Turn Expert Designs into Efficient GPU Kernels?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03226)
*Daifeng Li,Huiqiang Jiang,Chengruidong Zhang,Wei Wu,Xudong Guo,Jianhong Tu,Jianwei Zhang,Binhang Yuan,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D2K-Bench诊断基准（26任务/85工作负载），量化LLM智能体将专家设计指导转化为高效GPU内核的能力；指导使正确率93.1%→98.5%，性能分1.46→1.95，前沿模型几何平均加速1.69×→2.49×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体生成的GPU内核效率常不及专家实现，但仅凭运行时间无法揭示性能差距究竟源于设计发现不足还是实现能力不足，缺少可诊断的评测手段。

Method: 构建D2K-Bench，含26个任务、85个工作负载，按三层专家设计指导建模：L1高层算法洞见、L2数据流设计、L3底层优化技巧，并刻画层间依赖。采用成对实验（有/无指导），任务描述、工作负载、工具、硬件及350轮预算均保持一致；辅以对独立提出的设计与生成代码中实际实现的设计属性的评估。在NVIDIA B200上测试五个模型。

Result: 有指导时，130个模型-任务对的正确率由93.1%升至98.5%，全部26个任务的性能分由1.46升至1.95；对全部26个任务两次运行均正确的三个前沿模型，几何平均加速由1.69×升至2.49×；五个模型的平均综合实现分由57升到70（满分100）。

Conclusion: 结果表明专家设计指导具有显著价值，同时指出部分设计属性仍未被实现对，为诊断LLM生成GPU内核的“设计—实现”差距提供了可复用的基准与指标。

Abstract: GPU kernels generated by large language model (LLM) agents can remain less efficient than expert implementations, but runtime alone does not reveal how the gap relates to design discovery and implementation. We introduce D2K-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 26 tasks and 85 workloads that measures how effectively agents translate expert design guidance into efficient GPU kernels. The guidance covers L1: high-level algorithmic insights, L2: dataflow design, and L3: low-level optimization tricks, including dependencies among these levels. Pairwise runs with and without guidance share task descriptions, workloads, tools, hardware, and a 350-turn budget. Complementary assessments examine independently proposed designs and the design properties implemented in generated code. Across five models on NVIDIA B200 GPUs, guidance raises correctness over 130 model-task pairs from 93.1% to 98.5% and increases the Performance Score over all 26 tasks from 1.46 to 1.95. For the three frontier models with correct submissions on all 26 tasks in both runs (GPT-6-Astra, Claude-Opus-4.8, and GPT-5.6-Sol), geometric mean speedup increases from $1.69\times$ to $2.49\times$. Across all five models, the mean combined implementation score increases from 57 to 70 out of 100. These results show the value of expert design guidance while identifying design properties that remain unimplemented.

</details>


### [274] [Cross-cohort TB classification using clinical data gathered in Uganda and South Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03256)
*Joshua M. Jansen van Vüren,Devendra S. Parihar,Daphne Naidoo,Marisa Klopper,Frank Cobelens,Lutz Kolbe,Kimsey Zajac,Willy Ssengooba,Moses Joloba,Grant Theron,Thomas R. Niesler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次评估将机器学习应用于南非和乌干达两国收集的患者临床与人口学数据以进行结核病筛查，研究发现基于神经网络的分类器在结核病筛查中具有可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别可从昂贵分子检测中获益的人群是结核病筛查的关键目标。本文旨在评估利用两国多中心临床与人口学数据的机器学习方法在结核病筛查中的表现，以辅助分子检测的分流决策。

Method: 基于新近构建的CAGE-TB数据集（含南非和乌干达社区卫生中心疑似结核病患者子队列），采用三种神经网络架构：逻辑回归（LR）、多层感知机（MLP）和卷积神经网络（CNN），并结合贪心特征选择。针对CNN提出一种联合优化特征选择与特征排序的策略。

Result: 特征选择使三种模型在开发集上的AUROC提升2-7%。LR经特征选择后在留出的乌干达和南非数据上AUROC分别为0.8 [0.75,0.86]（95% CI）和0.84 [0.78,0.9]。更深网络（MLP、CNN）在开发队列上优于LR，但在留出队列上趋势不一致，LR在AUROC上仅比最佳结果低1-2%。LR在灵敏度上距WHO最低要求差4-9%。

Conclusion: 所提出的联合优化特征选择与排序策略能为CNN带来一致的发展与测试集改进。LR在跨队列泛化上表现稳定，仅次于更复杂网络1-2%。总体而言，基于神经网络的结核病筛查分类器的开发具有可行性，但灵敏度仍略低于WHO标准。

Abstract: We present a first evaluation of machine learning applied to patient clinical and demographic data gathered in two different countries for the purpose of tuberculosis (TB) screening to identify people who would benefit from expensive molecular testing. Experiments are based on the recently-compiled CAGE-TB dataset, which includes sub-cohorts of people with presumptive TB presenting at community health care centres in South Africa and Uganda. Three neural network architectures (logistic regression (LR), multilayer perceptrons (MLP) and convolutional neural networks (CNN)) are considered in conjunction with greedy feature selection. For the convolutional neural network, a strategy that jointly optimises feature selection and feature ordering is proposed and shown to lead to consistent development and test set improvements. For all three models, development set area under the receiver operating characteristic (AUROC) curve is improved by 2-7% using feature selection. LR after feature selection achieves an AUROC of 0.8 [0.75,0.86] (95% CI) and 0.84 [0.78,0.9] when testing on the held-out Ugandan and South African data respectively. Although outperforming LR on the development cohort, the deeper networks (MLP, CNN) show inconsistent trends on the held-out cohorts, while LR achieves performance within 1-2% of the best achieved in terms of AUROC. LR narrowly misses the WHO minimum requirements by 4-9% in sensitivity even though the network is being evaluated on a completely held-out cohort. The development of neural-network based classifiers for TB screening therefore appears viable.

</details>


### [275] [Mapping and Advancing the Scalability-Accuracy Frontier of Nonlinear Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03258)
*Hendrik Suhr,Sascha Xu,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较四类可扩展非线性因果发现方法，揭示各自精度-运行时间瓶颈，并提出SPADE（基于样条的得分评估方案），通过一次编译充分统计量并在组合搜索中复用，将高斯变体复杂度从O(nd³)降至O(nd²+d³)，在合成与真实基准上显著推进可扩展性-精度前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展非线性因果发现需要兼顾灵活机制估计与大图空间高效搜索，但现有四类算法族（可微结构学习、摊销结构学习、得分匹配、组合搜索）的精度-运行时间权衡缺乏系统理解。作者希望填补这一空白，并针对组合搜索中重复冗余局部评分导致的效率瓶颈提出改进。

Method: 实证比较四类主要方法：可微结构学习、摊销结构学习、得分匹配与组合搜索。基于所发现的瓶颈，提出SPADE——一种基于样条的得分评估方案：一次性编译充分统计量，并在整个组合搜索过程中复用；在入度有界条件下，其高斯变体将算法复杂度从O(nd³)降低至O(nd²+d³)。

Result: 实验揭示互补瓶颈：可微与摊销方法扩展性好但存在精度差距；得分匹配在低维可准确但随特征维度增加迅速退化；组合方法精度高但受重复冗余局部评分拖慢。SPADE在数秒内求解100变量、160K样本问题，在数分钟内求解1600变量、2.5K样本问题，并在合成与真实基准上保持高结构精度，使可扩展性-精度前沿提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果表明组合搜索的实际可处理规模发生实质性转变，并强调应沿完整精度-运行时间前沿评估可扩展因果发现方法，而非仅关注单一维度。

Abstract: Scalable nonlinear causal discovery requires methods that combine flexible mechanism estimators with efficient search over large graph spaces. Several algorithmic families have been proposed to address this challenge, yet their accuracy-runtime trade-offs remain poorly understood. We empirically compare the four major approaches: differentiable structure learning, amortized structure learning, score-matching, and combinatorial search. Our results reveal complementary bottlenecks: differentiable and amortized methods scale well but exhibit an accuracy gap, score-matching methods can be accurate in low dimensions but degrade quickly for increasing feature sizes, and combinatorial methods remain accurate but are slowed by repeated and redundant local scoring. Motivated by this bottleneck, we develop SPADE, a spline-based score-evaluation scheme that compiles sufficient statistics once and reuses them throughout combinatorial search. Under bounded indegree, its Gaussian variant reduces algorithmic complexity from O(nd^3) to O(nd^2+d^3). Empirically, SPADE shifts the observed scalability-accuracy frontier by orders of magnitude: it solves 100-variable problems with 160K samples in seconds and 1600-variable problems with 2.5K samples in minutes, while retaining high structural accuracy across synthetic and real-world benchmarks. These results reveal a substantial shift in the practical scale of combinatorial search and highlight the importance of evaluating scalable causal-discovery methods along the full accuracy-runtime frontier.

</details>


### [276] [PaMIR: Open Benchmark of Public Credit-Default Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03259)
*Mikhail Liashkov,Ilyas Varshavskiy,Shuhratjon Khalilbekov,Azizjon Azimi,Bonu Boboeva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布PaMIR 0.4.0，一个面向标签稀缺且延迟到达情形下信用违约预测的开放基准，整合19个公开数据集（124万条记录），统一构建流程并采用延迟标签流评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域内参照基准通常仅使用8个数据集，且其中仅2至4个为公开数据，导致结果难以复现与横向比较。作者希望提供全公开、可复现且经过防泄漏审计的信用违约预测基准，以支撑标签稀缺与标签延迟场景下的模型评估。

Method: PaMIR整合19个含二值违约标签的公开数据集，共124万条贷款、企业与信用卡账户记录，覆盖9个国家；所有数据基于固定源快照、通过统一且经泄漏审计的流程重建，且不再二次分发。所有模型以单一函数形式实现，在重复独立同分布划分与标签延迟流两种设定下评估：后者在每个申请到达时即打分，并按标签预算报告AUC；仅当所有数据集均完成评分时才公布整体均值。另设合成数据测试框架，在不暴露留出样本的前提下检验生成式训练数据。

Result: 构建了据作者所知目前同类中唯一的全公开信用违约预测基准PaMIR（0.4.0版本），实现跨9国、124万条记录的统一评测，并支持标签预算维度下的AUC报告与生成数据无效性检验。

Conclusion: PaMIR作为持续更新的活基准，为标签稀缺与延迟到达条件下的信用违约预测提供了公开、可复现且防泄漏的评测平台，版本0.4.0为该基准的阶段性发布。

Abstract: We release PaMIR (Public Arrival-ordered Measurement for Inference in Risk), an open benchmark for credit-default prediction when labels are scarce and arrive late. The field's reference benchmark studies use eight datasets each, only two or four of them public. PaMIR brings together 19 public datasets with binary default labels -- 1.24M loans, firms and card accounts from nine countries -- rebuilt from pinned source snapshots by one leakage-audited recipe and never redistributed; to our knowledge it is the one of its kind as of today. Every model is a single function, scored under a repeated i.i.d. split and a label-delayed stream in which each application is scored on arrival, with AUC reported by label budget; fleet means are withheld unless every dataset is scored. A synthetic-data harness tests generated training rows without letting a generator see held-out rows. This report describes release 0.4.0 of this living benchmark.

</details>


### [277] [SPEAR: A Spectral-Disentangled MoE Neural Operator with Knowledge-Guided Expert Aggregation for Large-Scale PDE Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03265)
*Dengdi Sun,Xiaoya Zhou,Xiao Wang,Wanli Lyu,Jin Tang,Bin Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPEAR——谱解耦的MoE神经算子，通过低频/高频特征解耦与知识引导专家聚合，缓解异构PDE中的知识干扰与专家冗余，在12个PDE数据集上取得更优性能，并将专家数减少50%而保持或提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PDE基础模型在大规模预训练下泛化能力增强，但面对异构动力学时，共享表示会引发知识干扰；而MoE架构虽可缓解该问题，却存在专家冗余不断增长、模型效率下降的缺陷。

Method: SPEAR将隐特征解耦为低频与高频分量，低频部分承载可迁移的共享动力学、高频部分用于学习PDE特有的专门模式；同时设计知识引导的专家聚合策略，基于数据集特定学习到的知识与路由偏好度量专家相似性，从而识别并合并相似专家。

Result: 在12个PDE数据集及多个下游基准上，SPEAR在预训练、微调和迁移学习任务中均表现更优；聚合策略在维持或提升预测精度的同时将专家数量减少50%。

Conclusion: 谱解耦与知识引导专家聚合可有效平衡PDE基础模型的效率与泛化能力，为异构PDE的大规模预训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large-scale pre-training has improved the generalization of neural operators across diverse PDEs. However, existing PDE foundation models still struggle with heterogeneous dynamics, where shared representations may cause knowledge interference, while mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures suffer from increasing expert redundancy. We propose SPEAR, a spectral-disentangled MoE neural operator with knowledge-guided expert aggregation for large-scale PDE pre-training. SPEAR decouples latent features into low- and high-frequency components, enabling shared modeling of transferable dynamics and specialized learning of PDE-specific patterns. To address expert redundancy, we design a knowledge-guided expert aggregation strategy that measures expert similarity from dataset-specific learned knowledge and routing preferences, enabling the identification and consolidation of similar experts. Experiments on twelve PDE datasets and multiple downstream benchmarks demonstrate superior performance in pre-training, fine-tuning, and transfer learning. Furthermore, our aggregation strategy reduces the number of experts by 50\% while maintaining or improving prediction accuracy, achieving a balance between model efficiency and generalization for PDE foundation models.

</details>


### [278] [Architecture-Dependent Fusion Pathways in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03289)
*Hebao Zhu,Dongxia Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对拼接式与原生多模态两类MLLM进行机制性分析，揭示其视觉-文本融合路径的差异：拼接模型呈"先文本后视觉"路径，原生模型则表现为更早的模态共适应与特征空间重组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在视觉-语言任务上表现优异，但视觉与文本信息在跨层融合中的内部机制尚不清晰，缺乏对融合过程的机制性刻画与架构感知的诊断手段。

Method: 选取拼接架构与原生多模态架构的代表性MLLM，开展三项递进分析：对齐解耦以识别模态变化；注意力路由与熵刻画跨模态信息分布；内在维度考察融合对特征空间的重塑；并以因果干预实验验证解释，另用visual CKA检验柏拉图表示假说。

Result: 发现两条不同的融合路径：拼接模型遵循先文本、后视觉的融合顺序；原生模型则表现出更早的视觉-文本共适应以及特征空间重组。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解多模态融合提供了机制性视角，并支持面向架构的多模态表示诊断。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance across vision-language tasks, yet the internal mechanisms by which visual and textual information are fused across layers remain insufficiently understood. We investigate representative MLLMs from two architectural paradigms: concatenation architectures and native multimodal architectures. We conduct three progressively connected analyses: alignment decoupling identifies which modality changes, attention routing and entropy characterize how cross-modal information is distributed, and intrinsic dimensionality examines how fusion reshapes feature spaces. Separately, we perform causal intervention experiments as a validation of the resulting interpretation. As a supplementary analysis, we use visual CKA to examine the Platonic Representation Hypothesis. Together, these analyses reveal two distinct fusion pathways: concatenation models follow a text-first, vision-later pathway, whereas native models exhibit earlier visual-textual co-adaptation and feature-space reorganization. This work provides a mechanistic perspective for understanding multimodal fusion and supports architecture-aware diagnostics of multimodal representations.

</details>


### [279] [S$^{2}$-PINN: Stochastic Separable Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03303)
*Zhendong Li,Akwum Onwunta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出S²-PINN，一种随机可分离物理信息神经网络，用于随机偏微分方程的不确定性量化：解以可学习高斯空间字典、Fourier时间特征与gPC随机基表示，并通过低秩CP张量分解耦合，采用强形式与gPC投影残差混合损失训练；在多个基准上以更少参数优于九个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机偏微分方程的不确定性量化在计算科学与工程中普遍存在。经典谱方法面临维数灾难，而现有神经求解器往往忽略随机结构，导致矩估计与校准难以处理。因此需要一种既能避免维数灾难、又能显式利用随机可分离结构的神经求解框架。

Method: S²-PINN将随机PDE解u(t,x,Z)表示为可学习高斯空间字典、Fourier时间特征与广义多项式混沌(gPC)随机基三者的耦合，并通过低秩Canonical Polyadic(CP)张量分解核心连接。训练采用强形式残差与gPC投影残差构成的混合损失。理论分析证明该可分离函数类在温和条件下于L²中稠密，投影残差恰好对应随机Galerkin约束，且小批量投影系数对gPC模态数呈对数依赖，正交性惩罚项控制空间字典的条件数。

Result: 在四个制造的随机PDE基准上，S²-PINN在均值与方差精度及校准方面均优于九个基线，同时参数量显著更少。额外评估涵盖非制造Poisson与Darcy问题、随机Navier–Stokes问题、更高随机维度的扩散缩放研究，以及两个随机反问题，验证了所提结构的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结果支持将随机可分离性作为物理信息神经不确定性量化的有效设计原则，表明该结构在精度、参数效率与泛化性方面具有综合优势。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) for random partial differential equations (PDEs) is ubiquitous in computational science and engineering. However, classical spectral solvers for this class of problems face the curse of dimensionality, and existing neural solvers often ignore the stochastic structure that makes moments and calibration tractable. We introduce a stochastic separable physics-informed neural network, dubbed S$^{2}$-PINN, that represents the solution $u(t,\mathbf{x},\mathbf{Z})$ of a random PDE with a learnable Gaussian spatial dictionary, Fourier temporal features, and a generalized polynomial chaos (gPC) stochastic basis, coupled by a low-rank Canonical Polyadic (CP) tensor decomposition core. The method is trained with a hybrid strong-form and gPC-projected residual loss. Our theoretical analysis establishes that the separable class is dense in $L^2$ under mild conditions, and the projected residual corresponds exactly to a stochastic Galerkin constraint. Furthermore, we show that mini-batch projection coefficients are logarithmically dependent on the number of gPC modes, and that the orthogonality penalty controls the conditioning of the learned spatial dictionary. Using four manufactured random PDE benchmarks, we show that S$^{2}$-PINN outperforms nine baselines in terms of mean and variance accuracy, as well as calibration, while using significantly fewer parameters. Further evaluations on non-manufactured Poisson and Darcy problems, a stochastic Navier--Stokes problem, a diffusion scaling study of higher random dimensions, and two stochastic inverse problems reveal the generalization capabilities of the proposed structure. Together, these results support stochastic separability as an effective design principle for physics-informed neural UQ. The code for the experiments can be found in https://github.com/DMax1314/s2pinn

</details>


### [280] [Training-Loss Guarantees for Muon with Finite-Step Newton--Schulz Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03306)
*Amartya Roy,Souvik Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为Muon优化器在带动量与有限步Newton–Schulz正交化下建立了有限时间训练保证：对足够宽的两层ReLU网络全批量训练，证明Muon能以高概率达到任意目标经验平方损失ε，命中时间为O((1-μ)^{-1}ε^{-1/2})，所需宽度与精度和动量无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有Muon收敛分析要么假设精确正交化，要么只分析经典Newton–Schulz多项式，且仅保证收敛到驻点。因此，Muon实际使用的五步调优Newton–Schulz究竟保留了哪些性质、是否足以达到预设的训练损失，仍是未解问题。

Method: 针对足够宽的两层ReLU网络全批量训练，固定随机输出权重并假设极限神经正切核正定；分析同时考虑正交化前的动量累积与调优的有限步更新。证明调优Newton–Schulz映射在保持与动量缓冲区对齐的同时限制了更新的谱范数，并通过控制初始化附近的梯度变化将该对齐传递到当前梯度，从而实现下降。

Result: 对任意μ∈[0,1)，取与目标相关的常数学习率正比于(1-μ)√ε，可得到O((1-μ)^{-1}ε^{-1/2})的命中时间界；所需宽度与目标精度和动量均无关。数值实验在低于理论阈值的宽度下支持该机制：梯度-更新对齐高于解析参考，六个宽度、五种学生初始化共30次运行均在保持核正定的同时达到目标损失。

Conclusion: 调优的有限步Newton–Schulz正交化无需精确正交化即可保证Muon有效下降并达到预设训练损失；动量与有限步更新的联合效应被纳入统一的有限时间分析框架，理论与实验一致。

Abstract: Existing convergence analyses of Muon either assume exact orthogonalization or analyze classical Newton--Schulz polynomials, and guarantee only stationarity, so it is unresolved what Muon's five tuned Newton--Schulz steps preserve and whether that suffices to reach a prescribed neural-network training loss. We establish a finite-time training guarantee that accounts for both momentum accumulation before orthogonalization and the tuned finite-step update. For full-batch training of a sufficiently wide two-layer ReLU network with fixed random output weights and a positive-definite limiting neural tangent kernel, we prove that Muon reaches any target empirical squared loss $\varepsilon>0$ with high probability over initialization. For every momentum parameter $μ\in[0,1)$, a target-dependent constant learning rate proportional to $(1-μ)\sqrt{\varepsilon}$ yields a hitting-time bound of $O((1-μ)^{-1}\varepsilon^{-1/2})$, with other problem parameters fixed. The sufficient width is independent of both target accuracy and momentum. The analysis shows that the tuned Newton--Schulz map preserves alignment with the momentum buffer while bounding the update's spectral norm. Control of gradient variation near initialization transfers this alignment to the current gradient, ensuring descent until the target is reached without requiring exact orthogonalization. Numerical experiments support these mechanisms at widths below the sufficient theoretical threshold: gradient-update alignment remains above the analytical reference, and all 30 runs across six widths and five student initializations on a fixed teacher-student dataset reach the target loss while maintaining kernel positivity.

</details>


### [281] [Cordial Learning: Distributed Training with Correlated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03330)
*Sarah Shitrit,Ilai Bistritz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出cordial（相关且分布式）学习框架，针对代理间数据相关且受隐私与通信约束的分布式任务，仅共享低维输出并训练局部模型提取同伴信息，在博弈框架下证明线性模型下几乎必然收敛到全局最优，并在相关数据上优于联邦学习等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中代理共享同一环境，其标签依赖于其他代理对同一样本的输入，且输入之间也相互相关。现有去中心化方法（如联邦学习）忽略问题结构，在相关数据上表现差；而集中式方法因隐私和通信约束不可行。因此需要一种兼顾隐私/通信限制与相关数据结构的分布式学习方法。

Method: 提出cordial（correlated and distributed）学习：代理之间只共享低维输出，同时训练局部模型以从同伴处提取有用信号。该分布式学习诱导出一个博弈，其中每个代理的损失函数依赖于其他代理的模型。在假设线性模型的情况下进行理论分析。

Result: 理论证明：在线性模型假设下，尽管全局目标函数非凸，cordial learning仍能以概率1收敛到全局最优解。实验方面，在结构化多数字MNIST任务上，即使在高非线性设置下，cordial learning依然保持高度有效性。

Conclusion: cordial learning填补了去中心化方法与集中式方法之间的空白，在仅共享低维输出的约束下能有效处理相关数据，并在理论（线性收敛保证）与实验（非线性任务有效性）两方面得到验证，优于忽略问题结构的联邦学习等方法。

Abstract: We consider a distributed learning task with agents that have correlated data. Specifically, the label of an agent depends on the input of other agents for the same sample, and these inputs are also correlated. Correlated data is the reality when agents share the same environment. Existing decentralized methods, such as federated learning, ignore the structure of the problem and perform poorly on correlated data. On the other hand, centralized approaches are infeasible due to privacy and communication constraints. We introduce cordial (correlated and distributed) learning to address this gap by sharing only low-dimensional outputs between the agents while training local models to extract informative signals from peers. This distributed learning induces a game in which the loss function of each agent depends on the models of others. Assuming a linear model, we prove that cordial learning converges with probability one to a globally optimal solution, despite the nonconvex global objective. Experiments on structured multi-digit MNIST tasks demonstrate that cordial learning remains highly effective even in highly nonlinear settings.

</details>


### [282] [Follow the Winners: Conservative Policy Improvement with the Cross-Entropy Method for Critic-Free RFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03361)
*Joery Ariën de Vries,Neil David Lawrence,Zhenwen Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Follow the Winners (FTW)，一种无 critic 的强化微调算法，将交叉熵方法适配到 RFT，用回放缓冲区样本上的序数滤波替代组 rollout，实现返回序统计量的多项式集中，并在 agentic LLM 后训练中匹配 GRPO 与 PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 类无 critic RFT 方法依赖对重复 rollout 计算组基线以降低目标方差，但在有状态环境（如在线服务、安全沙箱）中重复 rollout 不可行，且激进更新会固化长且稀疏验证轨迹中的噪声，因此需要一种不依赖组 rollout 的方差控制机制。

Method: FTW 将交叉熵方法适配到 RFT，用回放缓冲区样本上的序数滤波替代组 rollout，从而获得返回序统计量的多项式集中；通过 control-as-inference 视角推导 FTW，并在此框架下把 GRPO 与 DPO 还原为特定建模选择，指出 GRPO 风险中性，而 DPO 与 FTW 共享一个有界风险寻求偏移，且该偏移可由 FTW 控制。

Result: 在 Sokoban 与 Search-R1 基准上，FTW 扩展到 agentic LLM 后训练后与 GRPO 和 PPO 表现相当，表明可以用 CPU 内存换取价值模型或组 rollout，形成一种可行的权衡。

Conclusion: 序数滤波带来的风险寻求偏移是样本方差降低的固有代价，而 critic 模型则引入不同的偏差-方差权衡；FTW 为有状态、难以重复 rollout 的 agentic 场景提供了一种可行的无 critic 策略学习方案。

Abstract: Critic-free reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) for agentic large language models is often done through GRPO-style methods, which compute a group baseline over repeated rollouts to reduce target variance. However, this setup is ill-suited to agents acting in stateful environments such as live services or security sandboxes, where repeated rollouts are impractical to obtain and aggressive updates entrench the noise of long, sparsely verified trajectories. We propose \textit{Follow the Winners} (FTW), a critic-free policy-learning algorithm that adapts the cross-entropy method to RFT, replacing group rollouts with an ordinal filter on replay-buffer samples that yields polynomial concentration in the order statistic of returns. We derive FTW through a control-as-inference lens, which also recovers GRPO and DPO as specific modelling choices, identifying GRPO as risk-neutral while DPO and FTW share a bounded risk-seeking offset that FTW controls. We identify this offset as an inherent trade-off of variance reduction through ordinal filters on samples, whereas a critic model induces a different trade-off between bias and variance. Scaled to agentic LLM post-training, FTW matches GRPO and PPO on Sokoban and Search-R1 baselines, showing a viable trade-off from a value model or group rollouts to CPU memory.

</details>


### [283] [SCAD: Structured Credit Assignment and Distillation for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03372)
*Shangyang Wu,Shuai Zhao,Ziyue Zhu,Jinyang Wu,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCAD 将长程智能体交互分为规划与有界子任务执行，在局部上下文蒸馏执行、通过跨 rollout 子任务前缀树优化规划信用分配，从而结合结果型信用分配与教师引导蒸馏，提升长程智能体的规划与执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练长程智能体解决复杂任务需要对长交互序列进行有效监督，但稀疏的终端奖励难以揭示中间步骤的贡献，而在策略蒸馏又会随着学生生成历史的增长而逐渐丢失教师的有效指导信息。

Method: 提出 SCAD：将交互组织为“规划 + 有界子任务执行”两个层次；在局部上下文中对执行阶段进行蒸馏；并通过跨 rollout 的子任务前缀树来优化规划的信用分配。信用分配上，规划获得完整的终端信用，执行获得正向终端信用以及教师指导。

Result: 在所有评估基准上，SCAD 相比最强训练基线在文本任务上的宏平均准确率提升 4.48 个百分点，在多模态任务上提升 4.19 个百分点。

Conclusion: SCAD 有效结合了基于结果的信用分配与教师引导蒸馏，改进了长程智能体的规划与执行能力。

Abstract: Training long-horizon agents to solve complex tasks requires effective supervision over extended interaction sequences. However, sparse terminal rewards obscure intermediate contributions, while on-policy distillation can lose informative teacher guidance as student-generated histories grow. To address this problem, we introduce SCAD, which organizes interactions into planning and bounded subtask execution, distills execution in local contexts, and refines planning credit through cross-rollout subtask prefix trees, with planning receiving full terminal credit and execution receiving positive terminal credit and teacher guidance. Across all evaluated benchmarks, SCAD improves macro-average accuracy over the strongest training baseline by 4.48 percentage points for text tasks and 4.19 points for multimodal tasks. SCAD effectively combines outcome-based credit assignment with teacher-guided distillation to improve planning and execution in long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [284] [Operator-informed initialization for Fourier features physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03378)
*Juan Molina,Paris Perdikaris,Mircea Petrache,Matías Courdurier,Francisco Sahli Costabal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从NTK角度分析Fourier特征PINN的训练动态，推导出频域残差演化方程，发现收敛速率由微分算子符号与初始化权重谱密度之积决定，据此提出针对特定PDE定制初始权重分布的信息化初始化策略，以缓解算子导致的谱偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN普遍存在谱偏置问题，即目标函数的部分频率收敛速度明显慢于其他频率，限制了网络的逼近精度。作者希望从理论层面理解并缓解这一局限。

Method: 在神经切向核（NTK）框架下分析Fourier特征PINN的训练动态，推导频域残差误差的显式演化方程，揭示收敛速率受微分算子符号与初始化权重谱密度乘积主导，进而提出根据所求解PDE定制初始权重分布的信息化初始化策略。

Result: 理论分析表明特定频率收敛速率由算子符号与初始化权重谱密度之积决定；实验在多个线性与非线性PDE上验证，该初始化策略相比标准初始化改善了各频率的学习动态与逼近精度，且不增加额外训练成本。

Conclusion: 通过定制初始权重分布可削弱算子导致的谱偏置，使各频率收敛速率更加均衡，从而提升PINN的预测精度，且方法无需额外训练开销。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) typically exhibit spectral bias, where some frequencies of the target function converge more slowly than others. In this work, we analyze the training dynamics of Fourier Feature PINNs in the Neural Tangent Kernel regime to address this limitation. We derive an explicit evolution equation to estimate the residual error in the frequency domain, demonstrating that the convergence rate of specific frequencies is primarily governed by the product of the differential operator's symbol and the spectral density of the initialization weights. Leveraging this theoretical insight, we propose an informative initialization strategy that tailors the initial weight distribution to the specific PDE being solved. With this method, we can diminish the operator-induced spectral bias, balancing the convergence rates across the frequency spectrum and achieving better prediction accuracy. Numerical experiments on linear and nonlinear partial differential equations confirm that this initialization strategy improves learning dynamics and approximation accuracy across frequencies compared to standard initialization methods, with no additional training cost.

</details>


### [285] [Bidirectional Voronoi-biased Exploration Curriculum for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03395)
*Juri Pfammatter,Kaixian Qu,Clemens Schwarke,Victor Klemm,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BVER——一种双向Voronoi偏置探索课程，同时从起点和终点两端扩展课程，加速稀疏奖励目标条件强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视界稀疏奖励任务中，策略从初始状态难以到达目标、缺少学习信号；现有参考运动、人工课程和塑形奖励依赖示范与任务特定工程，而自动课程多从单侧扩展，需从该侧覆盖全部距离。

Method: 受双向RRT规划启发，BVER从目标向外生长起始状态、从初始状态分布向外生长目标，二者偏向未探索任务空间并相互引导，在同一目标条件策略上联合训练。

Result: 在点质量迷宫、四足机器人爬箱和机械臂环入销任务上，BVER比所有对比的无参考课程学习更快；爬箱任务中约减少65%迭代达到0.4 m箱95%成功率，是唯一学会爬0.7 m箱的方法，且策略对起始、目标和偏航变化鲁棒；无示范条件下接近参考型课程的样本效率；消融表明双向扩展优于单向。

Conclusion: 双向同时扩展课程能有效缓解稀疏奖励长视界探索瓶颈，无需示范即可逼近参考型课程效率，验证了双向扩展的优越性。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks with sparse rewards pose an exploration bottleneck for goal-conditioned reinforcement learning: a policy started from the initial state rarely reaches the goal and receives no learning signal. Reference motions, hand-designed curricula, and shaped rewards supply this signal but require demonstrations or task-specific engineering; automatic start-state and goal curricula avoid this but typically expand from one side only, so the full distance to the target must be covered from that side. We propose the Bidirectional Voronoi-biased Exploration curriculum for Reinforcement learning (BVER), which expands from both ends at once. Inspired by bidirectional RRT planning, BVER grows start states outward from the goal and goals outward from the initial state distribution, biases both toward unexplored task space, and steers them toward each other, training one goal-conditioned policy on both. On point-mass mazes, quadrupedal box climbing, and robot-arm ring-on-peg transfer, BVER learns faster than all compared reference-free curricula. On box climbing, it reaches 95% success on a 0.4 m box in roughly 65% fewer iterations than the best of them, is the only one of them to learn to climb a 0.7 m box, and yields a policy robust to start, goal, and yaw variation. Without a demonstration, it approaches the sample efficiency of reference-based curricula on the 0.4 m box and on ring-on-peg transfer. Ablations show that expanding from both ends outperforms either direction alone.

</details>


### [286] [A Unified Framework for Bayesian Data Assimilation with Generative Models and Observation Interpolants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03396)
*Nikolaj T. Mücke,Benjamin Sanderse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出观测插值框架，将预训练的随机插值、流匹配和扩散模型无需重训练即可转化为后验采样器，用于贝叶斯数据同化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯数据同化需结合模型预报与含噪观测，但高维、非高斯后验的采样仍然困难。现有生成模型（随机插值、流匹配、扩散模型）虽强大，却难以直接、免训练地用于后验采样。

Method: 将插值路径对观测进行条件化，得到对漂移项或速度场共享的似然得分修正，统一了随机与确定性后验采样；当中间似然得分已知时，所得SDE与ODE可采样精确后验。实际计算中，用闭式高斯代理近似该得分，其均值经偏差校正、协方差由模型源协方差膨胀；并采用免雅可比与系综共享近似以处理高维问题。

Result: 在线性高斯动力学、随机二维Navier-Stokes以及城市气流（自由度高达O(10^4)）上验证了该框架。

Conclusion: 该框架无需重训练即可将预训练生成模型转化为后验采样器，兼顾理论精确性与高维可计算性，适用于复杂高维数据同化任务。

Abstract: Bayesian data assimilation combines model forecasts with noisy observations, but sampling high-dimensional, non-Gaussian posteriors remains challenging. We introduce an observation-interpolant framework that turns pretrained stochastic interpolant, flow matching, and diffusion models into posterior samplers without retraining. Conditioning the interpolant path on observations yields a shared likelihood-score correction to the drift or velocity, unifying stochastic and deterministic posterior sampling. The resulting SDEs and ODEs sample the exact posterior when the intermediate likelihood score is known. For practical computation, we approximate this score using a closed-form Gaussian surrogate with a bias-corrected mean and covariance inflated by the model's source covariance. Jacobian-free and ensemble-shared approximations make the method tractable in high dimensions. We evaluate the framework on linear-Gaussian dynamics, stochastic two-dimensional Navier-Stokes, and urban airflow with up to $O(10^4)$ degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [287] [16-bit Precision of Convolutional Neural Networks on Microcontroller Units for 8-bit Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03402)
*Rui Liu,Benjamin Paaßen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出W16A16——一种面向边缘硬件的16位高精度、高速度、低能耗量化方案，并在Armv7E-M微控制器上验证其优于其他量化方案的速度与能耗表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在边缘硬件上部署深度神经网络，需要既能保持高精度又高效的推理方案；此前的低比特（如8位）量化虽高效但精度损失较大，因此需要兼顾精度与效率的量化方法。

Method: 提出W16A16的16位量化方法，分析Armv7E-M架构，阐明16位方案性能优势的底层原理；在回归与分类任务上评估量化误差，并在MCU部署中测量实际时间与能耗，与替代量化方案在层级和模型级进行对比。

Result: 相比8位量化方案，量化误差约降低10倍，同时推理时间和能耗相当或更优；在Armv7E-M上实现了更快速度与更低能耗。

Conclusion: W16A16是一种适用于边缘硬件的高精度、高速度、低能耗量化方案，在保持或提升推理效率的同时显著降低量化误差。

Abstract: To deploy deep neural networks on edge hardware, highly efficient inference schemes are necessary that retain high accuracy. This work presents W16A16, a high precision (16-bit), fast speed, low energy quantization method. On a widely applied microcontroller architecture Armv7E-M, our proposed approach achieves faster speed and lower energy consumption on layer- and model-level compared to alternative quantization schemes. We analyze the architecture of Armv7E-M, explain the underlying principles behind the performance advantages of 16-bit approaches, and evaluate the empiric quantization errors for regression and classification tasks, as well as empiric time- and energy consumption in MCU deployment. We observe ca.\ 10 times lower quantization errors compared to 8-bit quantization schemes while achieving similar or better inference times and energy consumption.

</details>


### [288] [AIBL: Augmented Instance-Based Learning with Structured Memory and Neural Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03413)
*Radha Poovendran,Andrea Stocco,Linda Bushnell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AIBL（增强型实例学习），将符号式实例学习推广到学习向量空间，用神经嵌入相似度替代人工匹配规则，并引入主动/遗忘/惊奇三层记忆与毕业机制，在5个ML任务和3个仿真任务上比经典IBLT提升6–17个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实例学习理论（IBLT）依赖字典式符号知识表示和人工指定的匹配规则，但文本、图像、交易向量、用户-物品历史等高维数据需要学习得到的相似度；同时数据分布随时间变化（概念漂移），固定符号匹配难以应对。

Method: AIBL在学习到的向量空间中表述实例学习：将符号化情境匹配泛化为神经嵌入相似度，同时保留实例存储、激活加权检索与效用混合。记忆划分为主动、遗忘、惊奇三个存储区；惊奇记忆隔离弱匹配、可能分布外或边角案例以减少对最近案例的强制拟合；采用观测驱动的毕业算法将反复出现的惊奇实例晋升为主动记忆以吸收漂移下的新反复模式。

Result: 同一实现于5个机器学习任务和3个受控仿真任务上评估，并与经典IBLT变体及任务特定基线对比，AIBL准确率提升6–17个百分点；结果表明向量空间检索在何处优于符号匹配，以及新增记忆机制如何调控新异性检测、冷启动处理、漂移适应与奖励学习。

Conclusion: 向量空间中的实例学习可有效替代符号匹配，三层记忆与毕业机制为高维序列数据下的新异性检测、冷启动、概念漂移与奖励学习提供了统一治理手段。

Abstract: Sequential learning systems often make decisions from accumulated experience while receiving high-dimensional inputs whose distribution may change over time. Instance-Based Learning Theory (IBLT) provides a principled case-based framework for such settings through stored situation-decision-utility instances, partial matching, activation, and blending. IBLT relies on symbolic knowledge representation in dictionary-like formats, but text, images, transaction vectors, and user-item histories often require learned similarity rather than hand-specified matching rules. In this paper, we introduce AIBL (Augmented Instance-Based Learning), an instance-learning model formulated in a learned vector space for high- dimensional sequential data. AIBL generalizes symbolic situation matching to neural embedding similarity while retaining instance storage, activation- weighted retrieval, and utility blending. The AIBL model organizes memory into active, forgotten, and surprise stores. Surprise memory separates weakly matched, possible out-of-distribution, or corner-case observations from active memory, reducing forced fitting to the nearest available cases. An observation-driven graduation algorithm promotes recurring surprise instances to active memory, allowing the memory to incorporate repeated novel patterns that may arise under concept drift. We evaluate the same implementation on five machine learning tasks and three controlled simulation tasks, comparing AIBL with classical IBLT variants and task-specific baselines where appropriate. AIBL improves accuracy by 6 to 17 percentage points. The results show where vector-space retrieval improves over symbolic matching and how the added memory mechanisms govern novelty detection, cold-start handling, drift adaptation, and reward learning under the tested protocols.

</details>


### [289] [Rethinking Epistemic Uncertainty in Node Classification through Information Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03418)
*Emma Meneghini,Francesco Ferrini,Bruno Lepri,Andrea Passerini,Veronica Lachi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个研究认知不确定性在信息增长下可约性的统计框架，指出图证据深度学习（EDL）方法无法保证一致性，并提出图自举集成作为候选的一致性认知预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知不确定性应随关于数据生成过程（DGP）的信息增加而减小。然而现有面向节点分类的图EDL方法通常从图特有的性质构造认知不确定性，并在分布外检测等下游任务上评估，这些评估并未检验其随DGP信息增长而可约化的能力。为使可约性可直接检验，需要建立相应框架。

Method: 作者提出一个研究信息增长下认知不确定性的统计框架：包含信息增长实验协议与认知预测器的一致性判据，并用投影图DGP保证不断增长的图构成对同一底层过程的一致观测。在此框架下分析EDL方法并开展受控信息增长实验；作为替代方案，提出图自举集成（graph bootstrap ensembles），通过图重采样与随机化训练同时捕获数据不确定性与过程不确定性。

Result: EDL方法并未显式估计来自单一有限图观测的数据不确定性，而是通过模型超参数调节认知不确定性，从而无法满足一致性，这一点得到受控信息增长实验的佐证。在同一实验协议下，图自举集成相比标准深度集成表现出更强的认知不确定性下降。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，自举集成可作为信息增长下候选的一致性认知预测器，为图不确定性建模提供了可检验的理论与实验基础。

Abstract: Epistemic uncertainty should decrease as additional information about the data-generating process (DGP) becomes available to the predictor. Yet, existing graph evidential deep learning (EDL) methods for node classification typically construct epistemic uncertainty from graph-specific properties and evaluate it on downstream tasks such as out-of-distribution detection, which do not test its reducibility as information about the DGP increases. To make reducibility directly testable, we introduce a statistical framework for studying epistemic uncertainty under information growth. Our framework specifies an information-growth experimental protocol and a consistency criterion for epistemic predictors, while using projective graph DGPs to ensure that growing graphs, which in general need not provide increasing information about the same DGP, constitute coherent observations of the same underlying process. We show that EDL methods do not explicitly estimate data uncertainty arising from a single finite graph observation and instead regulate epistemic uncertainty through model hyperparameters, precluding consistency, as corroborated by controlled information-growth experiments. As an alternative, we propose graph bootstrap ensembles, capturing both data and procedural uncertainty through graph resampling and randomized training. Under the same experimental protocol, these ensembles exhibit epistemic uncertainty reduction beyond standard deep ensembles. These findings support bootstrap ensembles as candidate consistent epistemic predictors under information growth.

</details>


### [290] [Deep Bayesian REFoCUS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03419)
*Simon Penninga,Ruud van Sloun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将超声多基地恢复从任意发射序列建模为贝叶斯推断问题，利用深度生成先验处理经典线性REFoCUS解码器失效的秩亏情形，提出Deep Bayesian REFoCUS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典线性REFoCUS解码器在秩亏（rank-deficient）条件下失效，且仅能给出点估计，无法表达不确定性。需要一种能应对任意发射序列、在秩亏与噪声条件下稳健的恢复方法。

Method: 将超声多基地恢复表述为贝叶斯推断问题；在多基地数据集上训练深度生成先验（deep generative prior），构建Deep Bayesian REFoCUS模型。

Result: 在所有秩亏程度与噪声水平下均优于线性基线；当反演为精确（exact）时退化为线性解码；能在采集的零空间（null space）中表达不确定性，而线性REFoCUS仅提供点估计。

Conclusion: 分析了仿真与在体（in-vivo）采集之间的分布偏移影响，显示模型无需任何微调或自适应即具备显著泛化能力。

Abstract: In this work we formulate ultrasound multistatic recovery from arbitrary transmit sequences as a Bayesian inference problem. To that end, we train a deep generative prior on multistatic data sets to tackle the rank-deficient regime in which classical linear REFoCUS decoders fail. This appproach, which we term Deep Bayesian REFoCUS, outperforms the linear baselines for all regimes of rank-deficiency and noise levels, and regresses to linear decoding when inversion is exact. The model also expresses uncertainty in the null space of the acquisitions, whereas the linear REFoCUS decoders only provide point estimates. Finally, we analyze the impact of distribution shift between simulation and in-vivo acquisitions, showing remarkable generalization ability without any fine-tuning or adaptation.

</details>


### [291] [OptiSelect: How does the Optimizer Shape Data Curriculum?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03432)
*Simin Fan,Alireza Abdollahpoorrostam,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将在线数据选择形式化为"优化器感知选择"(OptiSelect)，首次系统研究优化器如何塑造数据选择：理论上提出选择增益原理（由优化器诱导效用分数的可判别性决定），证明Lion/Muon的符号化与极坐标切向预条件器会导致可判别性塌缩从而限制增益，而AdamW/Sophia等对角自适应优化器具有更优上界，并导出最优候选过采样比；124M与720M预训练实验验证了理论，且AdamW的对角自适应打分几何在Muon为优化器时仍最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线数据选择通过在每批次内挑选最有价值的候选样本来提升LLM预训练效率，但候选的价值最终是通过"有效模型更新"实现的，而优化器会在更新参数前重塑原始梯度。现有选择方法多忽略优化器步骤，导致选择准则与实际增益脱节，因此需要建立优化器感知的选择理论与系统研究。

Method: 将优化器感知的在线选择形式化为OptiSelect范式，把优化器诱导的效用分数纳入候选价值度量；通过理论分析建立"选择增益原理"，用效用分数的可判别性刻画在线选择优势，并据此分析不同优化器（Lion、Muon的符号/极坐标切向预条件器 vs AdamW、Sophia的对角自适应）的增益上界，同时定量推导最优候选过采样比；在124M与720M模型上进行预训练实验，并测试数据改写场景下的鲁棒性。

Result: 理论证明Lion与Muon的符号化及极坐标切向预条件器存在可判别性塌缩，从而封顶OptiSelect的可获得增益，而AdamW、Sophia等对角自适应优化器享有严格更优的上界；该原理还给出最优候选过采样比的定量推导。124M与720M预训练实验与理论一致：即便以Muon作为优化器，AdamW的对角自适应打分几何仍是最强打分几何；OptiSelect在数据改写下依然保持收益。

Conclusion: 优化器与数据选择应协同设计。以优化器诱导效用分数的可判别性为核心的选择增益原理，为在线数据选择提供了理论基础，并为LLM预训练中优化器与选择策略的联合设计提供了实践指导；对角自适应优化器的打分几何在多种设置下表现最优。

Abstract: Online data selection has demonstrated substantial efficiency gains for LLM pretraining by training on the most valuable candidates within each batch. Since a candidate's value is realized through its effective model update, principled selection should account for the optimizer step, which reshapes the raw gradient before it updates model parameters. We formalize this optimizer-aware selection paradigm as OptiSelect and present the first systematic study of how the optimizer shapes data selection. Our theory establishes a selection gain principle in which the advantage of online selection is governed by the discriminability of the optimizer-induced utility scores. We prove that sign-based and polar-tangential preconditioners of Lion and Muon would suffer from a discriminability collapse which caps attainable gains from OptiSelect, whereas diagonal-adaptive optimizers such as AdamW and Sophia admit strictly better upper bounds. The proposed principle also yields a quantitative derivation of the optimal candidate oversampling ratio. Pretraining experiments on 124M and 720M models are consistent with our theoretical analysis and show that AdamW's diagonal-adaptive scoring geometry remains the strongest scoring geometry even with Muon as optimizer. We further demonstrate that OptiSelect retains its benefits under data rephrasing, a technique used in modern data processing pipelines. Our findings provide theoretical foundations and practical guidance for co-designing optimizers and data selection in LLM pretraining.

</details>


### [292] [Causal Representation Learning with Instantaneous and Lagged Relations via Nonstationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03452)
*Tatsuya Yamada,Hiroshi Morioka,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出iCReN框架，利用辅助变量与对比学习，从非平稳时间序列中识别潜在状态及其瞬时（同期）与滞后因果关系，并给出可识别性充分条件，在合成与真实数据上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列因果表示学习需同时刻画跨观测区间的滞后因果效应与区间内的同步（瞬时）因果效应，并应对数据的非平稳性；现有方法难以联合处理这两类因果关系与非平稳性，存在研究空白。

Method: 在存在辅助变量（如时间或条件标签）且其关联转移噪声分布变化的设定下，建立可识别性充分条件，使潜在状态可在分量置换与逐分量可逆变换意义下被识别，同时识别瞬时与滞后因果结构。据此提出iCReN框架，采用针对离散或连续辅助变量的对比学习，学习潜在表征并估计瞬时与滞后因果结构。

Result: 在合成数据上能够较准确地恢复潜在状态及瞬时与滞后因果结构；在真实数据上，所学表征在下游预测任务中具有实用价值。

Conclusion: 通过辅助变量驱动的对比学习，可在非平稳时间序列中联合识别瞬时与滞后因果结构，理论可识别性条件与方法有效性得到验证，为时间序列因果表示学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Causal representation learning for time-series data aims to identify latent states and their causal relations from observations. In this setting, an important challenge is to model both lagged causal relations across observation intervals and faster causal effects that appear as instantaneous relations within an interval, while accounting for nonstationarity in time-series data. However, methods that jointly handle these causal relations and nonstationarity remain limited. To address this gap, we establish sufficient conditions for identifying latent states up to component permutation and component-wise invertible transformations, and their instantaneous and lagged causal structures up to the same permutation, using an observed auxiliary variable, such as time or a condition label, associated with changes in transition-noise distributions. Based on these results, we propose iCReN, a framework that uses contrastive learning with discrete or continuous auxiliary variables to learn latent representations and estimate their instantaneous and lagged causal structures. Experiments demonstrate accurate recovery of latent states and both instantaneous and lagged causal structures on synthetic data and the utility of the learned representations for downstream forecasting on real-world data.

</details>


### [293] [Measure Less, Know More: Self-Supervised Test-Time Feature Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03454)
*Eeshaan Jain,Linus Bleistein,Bart Deplancke,Charlotte Bunne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ECHO-k，一种任务无关、自监督的模态顺序选择方法，用预训练基础模型的内部表示为代理目标，并用强化学习策略实现低成本测试时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型虽能处理异构大规模数据，但测试时获取全部特征或模态代价高昂且常存在冗余；当下游任务与预测目标未知时，如何顺序选择信息量大的模态尤为困难。

Method: 将深度模型（如基础模型）的预训练内部表征作为汇总跨模态信息的代理目标，形成任务无关的自监督学习原则；在简化的线性设定下给出理论保证，并据此设计强化学习策略进行顺序模态选择。

Result: 在多种基础模型后端上，ECHO-k 相比任务无关、无标签的获取基线，在预算受限的下游性能上取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 该方法为代价感知的测试时部署提供了有原则的路径，适用于测量昂贵、时间受限且下游任务事先未知的多模态系统。

Abstract: Recent progress in multimodal, high-dimensional learning has enabled foundation models to process heterogeneous, large-scale data. However, at test time, acquiring all features or modalities can be prohibitively costly and often redundant. Sequentially selecting informative modalities is therefore critical, yet challenging when the downstream task or prediction target is unknown. To this end, we introduce ECHO-$k$, a task-agnostic and self-supervised learning principle for modality acquisition: we use a deep model's internal pretrained representations (e.g., from a foundation model) as proxy targets that summarize cross-modal information. We provide theoretical guarantees in a stylized linear setting that motivate a reinforcement learning (RL) policy for sequential modality selection. Across task-agnostic and label-free acquisition baselines, ECHO-$k$ consistently improves budgeted downstream performance across diverse foundation-model backends. Our method provides a principled route to cost-aware test-time deployment, with implications for any multimodal system where measurements are expensive or time-constrained, and downstream tasks unknown a priori.

</details>


### [294] [Beyond Random Splits: Evaluating Drug-Target Affinity Models Under Chemically and Biologically Motivated Distribution Shifts Copy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03456)
*Minjae Chung,Clara Li,Malar Paavai Muthukumaran,Shaunna Wang,Aniket Ramkrishnan Iyer,Shaun Qien Yeau Tan,Harinishree Sathu,Micky C. Nnamdi,J. Ben Tamo,Benoit Louis Marteau,May Dongmei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建718,800个药物-蛋白配对数据集，比较13种DTA预测架构在4种分布偏移下的表现，发现模型排名高度依赖外推类型，标准基准可能误导架构选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTA预测模型通常在单一数据划分下进行比较，但实际部署常需外推到新的化学系列、新蛋白靶点或两者兼有。作者质疑：用于评估的分布偏移类型是否会改变"哪个架构最优"的结论。

Method: 从ChEMBL和BindingDB整理718,800个唯一药物-蛋白配对；以Morgan指纹+蛋白CNN为基线，对比12种受控架构（4种药物表示×3种ESM-2交互模式）；在scaffold、fingerprint-cluster、protein-cluster、dual四种OOD下评估，并采用held-out评估、重复种子、group-aware bootstrap及规模匹配对照。

Result: 平均验证RMSE从scaffold和fingerprint-cluster OOD的0.950与0.945升至protein-cluster与dual OOD的1.299与1.321。两种化学偏移下排名一致（tau=0.79），但与scaffold OOD的一致性在protein OOD下下降（tau=0.39），在dual OOD下反转（tau=-0.55）。

Conclusion: 架构选择取决于外推形式，而非仅由平均误差或训练集规模决定。DTA基准应匹配部署时预期的化学与靶点偏移。

Abstract: Drug-target affinity (DTA) prediction is widely used to prioritize candidate compounds before costly experimental screening. DTA models are often compared under a single data split, even though deployment may require extrapolation to new chemical series, new protein targets, or both. We ask whether the distribution shift used for evaluation changes which architecture appears best. We curate 718,800 unique drug-protein pairs from the ChEMBL and BindingDB datasets. We compare a Morgan-fingerprint + protein-CNN baseline with 12 controlled architectures that combine four drug representations with three ESM-2 interaction modes. Mean validation RMSE increases from 0.950 and 0.945 under scaffold and fingerprint-cluster OOD to 1.299 and 1.321 under protein-cluster and dual OOD. Model rankings are similar across the two chemical shifts (tau = 0.79), but agreement with scaffold OOD falls under protein OOD (tau = 0.39) and reverses under dual OOD (tau = -0.55). Held-out evaluation, repeated seeds, group-aware bootstrap analysis, and a size-matched control support the same conclusion: architecture selection depends on the form of extrapolation, not only on average error or training-set size. DTA benchmarks should therefore match the chemical and target shifts expected at deployment.

</details>


### [295] [Single or Multiple Policies for Phase-Structured Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03475)
*Guilhem Loussouarn,Nancy Nayak,Kin K. Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文澄清了非平稳强化学习中多策略与单一状态增广策略的性能差异及适用条件，并提出基于"瞬态-准稳态"时长的分相判定方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 很多RL问题非平稳但具有阶段结构，阶段序列已知时可用状态增广+标准RL求解；然而已有工作发现不同阶段各用一策略（multi-policy）可能优于单一共享策略，其原因尚不明确，缺乏理论解释与选用准则。

Method: 首先从理论上证明共享策略可达到任意多策略解的性能上界；进而指出实际优劣取决于函数逼近、学习与优化过程，以及多策略的样本效率与策略切换时的连续性损失。据此提出regime-based（基于体制/瞬态）的分相分解方法：以瞬态动态时长相对于准稳态期时长的比例作为判定依据。

Result: 在多种非平稳RL任务上的数值实验验证了四个假设：(a) 阶段持续时间越长越有利于多策略；(b) 阶段间异质性越强，单一策略负担越大；(c) 多策略需要每个阶段有充足数据；(d) 阶段间环境特定的转移动态会影响哪种策略更优。

Conclusion: 共享策略与多策略并无绝对优劣：理论上共享策略已足够，但实践中受逼近误差、优化与样本限制影响，需依瞬态/准稳态时长的regime分析来选择。该分相判定方法为阶段已知的非平稳RL提供了策略架构选择依据。

Abstract: Many reinforcement-learning (RL) problems are non-stationary yet structured and can be decomposed into phases, each with its own transition probabilities and reward functions. When the phase sequence is known, the common solution augments the state with information to satisfy the Markovian property and applies standard RL techniques. However, prior work finds that the multi-policy approach for different phases can outperform a single state-augmented policy shared among the phases, for reasons that remain unclear. In this work, we first show that the shared policy can theoretically achieve performance of any multi-policy solution. However, whether a multi-policy solution can perform better than the corresponding single shared policy in practice depends on function approximation, learning and optimization processes, as well as, for multi-policy solutions, the sample efficiency and loss of continuity from one policy to another. We propose a regime-based phase decomposition method to identify which policy can provide better performance. The method is based on consideration of the duration of transient system dynamics relative to the duration of the quasi-stationary period. Numerical experiments are conducted with different non-stationary RL problems to validate our four major hypotheses: (a) longer phase durations favor multi-policies, (b) the heterogeneity between phases increases the burden on single policy, (c) multi-policies need sufficient data for each phase, and (d) environment-specific transition dynamics between phases can affect which policy is preferable.

</details>


### [296] [Metropolis-Hastings Dominates Importance Resampling for Policy Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03480)
*Alexey Kurennoy,Ramil Yarullin,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析解码时多奖励策略组合中的采样偏差问题，证明基于独立Metropolis-Hastings（MH）的迭代校正，在任意采样预算下，其输出分布按任意凸f-散度衡量都不劣于同等预算的SIR，并给出MH相对未校正解码器改进的下界及两种渐近情形的采样误差刻画，辅以可枚举与LLM规模实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练LLM常需探索多奖励间的权衡，但为每种权衡重新训练代价高昂。解码时策略组合可在推理阶段合并奖励特定策略来调节权衡，然而标准实现直接组合下一token概率，会引入采样偏差。

Method: 论文分析一种已知的、基于独立Metropolis-Hastings（MH）的迭代校正方法。目标分布是各策略在完整回复上概率的加权乘积，而标准做法在下一token概率上组合。通过凸f-散度度量，将MH与同等rollout预算的采样-重要性重采样（SIR）进行对比分析，并推导共识目标下MH相对未校正解码器的改进下界，以及在两种渐近情形下刻画校正的采样误差。

Result: 主要结果表明：对每个rollout预算，MH产生的输出分布在任意凸f-散度度量下，都至少与同等预算的SIR一样接近目标分布。此外推导出MH相对未校正解码器改进的下界，该下界基于衡量与所给策略一致性的共识目标。并刻画了两种渐近情形下的采样误差：奖励特定策略趋于一致时，以及目标与未校正解码器概率之间的对数比波动愈发剧烈时（长回复中可能出现）。

Conclusion: MH迭代校正为解码时多奖励策略组合提供了一种理论上有保障的采样偏差纠正方法，在任意采样预算下优于SIR，并相对未校正解码器有可量化的改进；同时揭示了在策略趋同与对数比剧烈波动两种渐近情形下的采样误差行为，并通过可枚举与LLM规模实验加以验证。

Abstract: Post-training a large language model (LLM) often requires exploring trade-offs between multiple rewards, but retraining for each trade-off is expensive. Decoding-time policy composition allows these trade-offs to be adjusted by combining reward-specific policies at inference time. This composition targets a weighted product of the policies' probabilities over complete responses, but standard implementations combine their next-token probabilities, generally introducing sampling bias. We analyze a known iterative correction based on independence Metropolis-Hastings (MH). Our main result shows that, for every rollout budget, MH produces an output distribution at least as close to the target as sampling-importance-resampling (SIR) with the same budget, as measured by every convex f-divergence. We also derive a lower bound on MH's improvement over the uncorrected decoder in a consensus objective measuring agreement with the supplied policies. We further characterize the correction's sampling error in two asymptotic regimes: when the reward-specific policies approach agreement, and when the log ratio between target and uncorrected-decoder probabilities fluctuates increasingly widely, as can happen for long responses. We complement our analysis with experiments in enumerable and LLM-scale settings.

</details>


### [297] [Dual-Context Analog Retrieval for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03491)
*Jung Min Choi,Ngoc Son Le,Ibram Abdelmalak,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DuoTS：一种双上下文时间序列预测模型，通过检索相似历史状态及其后续轨迹，逐步精化未来各patch的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期时间序列预测模型将回看窗口一次性直接映射到完整预测窗口，虽然高效，但未能显式识别哪些历史状态与不同未来片段最相关，也未利用这些状态之后的观测结果。类比预测虽可检索相似历史状态并使用其后续轨迹，但单一最近邻不可靠，且重叠patch易产生冗余候选。

Method: DuoTS先通过并行patch编码器与线性预测头生成基础预测，再逐个未来patch进行渐进式精化。每次精化融合两种视图：当前上下文（关注近期token，捕捉最新动态）与细节上下文（提供不同的检索类比及其后续轨迹）。patch级精化使模型能在预测窗口内平衡两种视图，并按时间距离为各未来片段关联合适证据。该精化机制与模型无关，仅需编码后的回看窗口与未来patch位置，可集成到现有预测模型。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上，DuoTS达到最新先进水平（SOTA），消融实验验证了两种上下文各自的贡献。

Conclusion: DuoTS通过双上下文检索与patch级渐进精化，在不过度依赖检索证据的情况下有效提升长期时间序列预测性能，且精化机制具备通用可插拔性。

Abstract: Most long-term time-series forecasting models map the look-back window directly to the full horizon in a single pass. While efficient, this design does not explicitly identify which historical states are most relevant to different future segments or exploit what followed those states. Analog forecasting addresses this by retrieving past states similar to the present and using their observed continuations, but single nearest matches can be unreliable and overlapping patches may produce redundant candidates. We propose DuoTS, a Dual-Context Time Series forecasting model that uses retrieved evidence without relying on it exclusively. DuoTS first produces a base forecast with a parallel patch encoder and linear prediction head, then progressively refines it one future patch at a time. Each refinement combines two views: a current context that attends to recent tokens and captures the latest dynamics, and a detail context that provides distinct retrieved analogs together with their subsequent trajectories. Patch-wise refinement allows the model to balance these views across the forecast horizon and associate each future segment with evidence appropriate to its temporal distance from the present. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets show that DuoTS achieves state-of-the-art performance, while ablations confirm the contribution of each context. The refinement mechanism is also model-agnostic, requiring only an encoded look-back window and the future-patch position, and can therefore be integrated into existing forecasting models.

</details>


### [298] [Most-Recent Anchoring with Recurrent Ordering for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03494)
*Jung Min Choi,Ngoc Son Le,Ibram Abdelmalak,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MARO 将回看窗口从最近到最旧排序，以最近 patch 作为锚点初始化并条件化循环状态，共享单模块逐级折叠历史，实现递归细化顺序上的近因表达，在多数据集长期与短期预测上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期预测模型对回看窗口内所有 patch 采用同一固定计算栈处理，老旧的上下文 patch 与近期证据获得相同计算深度。但靠近预测点的信息与较远上下文贡献并不相等，统一处理使这种区分在架构中未被表达。

Method: 提出 MARO（Most-Recent Anchoring with Recurrent Ordering）：按从最近 patch 到最旧 patch 的顺序扫描回看窗口；最近 patch 作为锚点，初始化隐状态并条件化后续每一步，使更旧 patch 被折叠进一个始终以近期证据为中心的表示；每一步复用同一共享模块，向更久远历史延伸不增加参数；扫描中保留的中间状态供预测头分别权衡历史的短期与长期部分。

Result: 在多个真实世界时间序列数据集上的大量实验表明，MARO 在长期与短期预测任务上均取得 SOTA 性能；消融实验考察了主要架构组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 以递归顺序本身表达近因性（recency）是有效设计：通过最近锚定与共享循环细化，在不增加参数的前提下区分近期与远期信息的贡献，兼顾长短期预测，并获得消融验证。

Abstract: Long-term forecasting models commonly process all patches in a look-back window using the same fixed stack. Older contextual patches and recent evidence therefore receive the same computational depth. Yet the information closest to the forecast and the more distant context do not contribute equally. Uniform processing leaves this distinction unexpressed in the architecture. We propose MARO, a Most-Recent Anchoring with Recurrent Ordering model that processes the look-back window from the most recent patch to the oldest. The most recent patch serves as the anchor. It initializes the latent state and conditions each subsequent step, so older patches are folded into a representation that remains centered on recent evidence. A single shared module is reused at every step, so extending the scan further into the past introduces no additional parameters. Intermediate states retained during the scan allow the forecast head to weigh short and long portions of the history separately. This expresses recency through the order of recurrent refinement. Extensive experiments across multiple real-world time series datasets show that MARO achieves state-of-the-art performance on both long-term and short-term forecasting tasks.Ablation studies examine the contribution of the main architectural components.

</details>


### [299] [Below what training size do deep tabular generators stop beating trivial baselines? A preregistered benchmark on a size ladder of clinical and standard datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03500)
*Shivam Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该预注册基准研究通过规模阶梯子采样实验发现：在小型表格数据（含临床数据）上，深度生成模型在绝大多数情形下无法稳定超越平凡基线，且预注册的"小样本下排名不稳定"假设被证伪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度表格生成模型的基准测试通常使用数万行的大数据集，而临床数据集往往只有数百行，两者规模差异巨大。作者希望找到这两种规模区间在方法表现上的分歧点，并检验深度模型在真实小样本场景下的价值与排名稳定性。

Method: 作者进行预注册的规模阶梯基准测试：对8个公开数据集从200到20,000训练行进行子采样，比较7种生成器（独立边际、高斯copula、SMOTE、无条件SMOTE、CTGAN、TVAE、TabDDPM），固定20次调参预算与5个评估种子；另加4个原生小样本临床数据集按真实规模运行，共2,220次承诺运行。主指标为在合成数据上训练、在真实数据上测试的固定分类器AUROC。同时计算相邻规模档之间的Kendall tau以评估排名稳定性，并比较原生小数据与子采样大数据的方法排名一致性。

Result: 在24个（数据集，深度模型）组合中的23个里，深度模型在任何训练规模下都未能超过最佳平凡基线一个种子噪声以上；最佳基线在49个（数据集，规模）单元中赢下40个。预注册的"深度模型小样本排名不稳定"预测被证伪：5,000行以下相邻档之间平均Kendall tau为0.806，高于0.8阈值，且稳定性在最小规模处最高而非最低。重要限定：81%的单元中前两名方法差距小于种子间变异。原生小临床数据集与子采样大数据集的方法排名仅中等一致（平均tau 0.57–0.64），说明子采样大数据集未必能替代小数据集。

Conclusion: 在数百行量级的表格数据上，深度生成模型相对简单基线的优势无法与种子噪声区分，且方法排名在小样本下反而更稳定。因此，在小型临床数据集上使用深度表格生成模型需谨慎，且以子采样大数据集外推小数据集结论存在效力问题。全部2,220个结果文件、预注册及其哈希、以及可复现全部图表与数字的代码均已公开。

Abstract: Deep tabular generative models are benchmarked on datasets with tens of thousands of rows; clinical datasets have hundreds. We preregistered and ran a size-ladder benchmark to find where the two regimes diverge: 8 public datasets subsampled from 200 to 20,000 training rows, seven generators (independent marginals, Gaussian copula, SMOTE, unconditional SMOTE, CTGAN, TVAE, TabDDPM) with a fixed 20-trial tuning budget and 5 evaluation seeds, plus 4 natively small clinical datasets at true size, for 2,220 committed runs in total. The primary metric is the AUROC of fixed classifiers trained on synthetic and tested on real data. In 23 of 24 (dataset, deep model) pairs no deep model ever beats the best trivial baseline by more than seed noise, at any training size we measured. The best baseline wins 40 of 49 (dataset, size) cells. Our preregistered prediction that the deep models' ranking would be unstable at small sizes is falsified: mean Kendall tau between adjacent rungs below 5,000 rows is 0.806, above our 0.8 threshold, and stability is highest at the smallest sizes rather than lowest. One caveat bounds all of this: in 81% of cells the gap between the top two methods is smaller than the variation between seeds. Finally, method rankings on natively small clinical datasets agree only moderately with rankings on subsampled large ones (mean tau 0.57 to 0.64), which questions whether a subsampled large dataset can stand in for a small one. All 2,220 result files, the preregistration and its hash, and the code that regenerates every figure and number from those files are public.

</details>


### [300] [Certified Mechanistic Edits: Behavioral Guarantees for Skill Removal and Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03502)
*Md Sazid Uddin,Md. Khairul Alam Mazumder,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"机制编辑"（消融、权重编辑、激活引导）的效果验证从"测试"提升为"连续区域上的形式化认证"，给出特征非干扰式保证：对某区域内所有输入，禁用某电路可移除一项技能且可证明保留另一项技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制编辑方法仅通过测试来验证效果，而测试永远无法覆盖连续的输入区域；已有可解释性-验证交叉工作认证的是"模型描述"（电路计算什么、解释是否忠实），而非编辑本身的行为效果。

Method: 在信息流安全意义下定义并构造编辑的认证问题，证明在连续嵌入空间区域上的移除与保留性质；对大规模问题从严密求解器切换为可靠的界传播（sound bound propagation）以扩展可处理维度；并从理论上证明不存在有限的确定性黑盒测试可认证移除，构造一个能通过穷举测试却在任意小"幸存者口袋"上确定失败的编辑。

Result: 从玩具 ReLU 网络到标准 softmax + LayerNorm Transformer 均实现了认证编辑；在连续嵌入空间区域上证明了移除与保留，可达精确求解器约 9 倍的输入扰动维度；并证明了任一有限确定性黑盒测试都无法认证移除的不可行性定理。

Conclusion: 机制编辑的效果可获得形式化、区域级的认证保证，但仅适用于小型标准架构网络；且与任何移除声明一样，其前提是目标技能存在可判定规约，而现实危害可能并不具备该性质。

Abstract: Mechanistic edits (ablations, weight edits, activation steering) are the standard tools for unlearning a harmful capability from a neural network while preserving useful ones. Current approaches validate their effects only by testing, which can never cover an entire continuous region of inputs. Prior work at the interpretability-verification boundary certifies descriptions of a model: what a circuit computes, or whether it faithfully explains the whole. We instead certify the behavioral effect of an edit: that disabling a circuit removes one skill and provably preserves another, for every input in a region; a feature non-interference guarantee in the information-flow-security sense. We demonstrate such certified edits from toy ReLU networks up to a standard softmax + LayerNorm transformer, proving removal and preservation over continuous embedding-space regions and reaching roughly 9x the input-perturbation dimension an exact solver can handle by switching to sound bound propagation. Furthermore, we prove that no finite deterministic black-box test can certify removal, exhibiting an edit that passes exhaustive testing yet provably fails on a survivor pocket that can be made arbitrarily small. Guarantees hold on small, standard-architecture networks and, like any removal claim, presuppose that the target skill admits a decidable specification, a property which real-world harms may not have.

</details>


### [301] [Getting Your Guidance Weights Right in diffusion and flow-matching posterior sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03503)
*Liam Moroy,Jean-François Giovannelli,Yoann Altmann,Steve McLaughlin,Frédéric Champagnat,Guillaume Bourmaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离线自动调节引导权重的策略，将条件去噪分数匹配/条件流匹配目标视为最小二乘问题，通过离线求解二维线性最小二乘获得随时间变化的引导权重，从而提升扩散与流匹配模型的免训练后验采样性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练后验采样方法（Plug-and-Play）大多依赖引导权重来平衡无条件先验信息与测量一致性，但这些权重的调节通常缺乏理论依据，主要依靠启发式设置，缺乏原则性的调优方法。

Method: 关键观察是：每个时间步上，扩散模型的条件去噪分数匹配目标（或流匹配模型的条件流匹配目标）是一个最小二乘目标。当条件预测被表示为无条件网络输出与测量引导项的加权和时，对这些权重的优化即化为一个二维线性最小二乘问题。由此可离线求解随时间变化的引导权重，只需一个采样轨迹小批量，针对给定的测量算子、噪声水平和采样器即可完成，无需重新训练或微调预训练生成模型。该方法以标准 Tweedie 测量一致性项实例化。

Result: 该方法改进了后验采样，在基于扩散和流匹配的方法中取得了最先进的重建性能；同时，优化后的引导权重使扩散采样器能够将采样步数从 1000 步减少到 50 步，而重建质量无显著下降。

Conclusion: 提出了一种简单且有原则的离线引导权重自动调优策略，无需重训练生成模型，仅需单次小批量采样轨迹即可获得随时间变化的引导权重，显著提升后验采样质量并大幅加快采样速度。

Abstract: Training-free posterior sampling methods, also known as Plug-and-Play methods, leverage pretrained unconditional diffusion or flow-matching models to solve inverse problems. Most existing approaches rely on guidance weights to balance, at each time step, prior information from the unconditional score or velocity network with measurement consistency, yet the tuning of these weights is often not discussed and is largely left to heuristics. We introduce a simple and principled offline strategy for automatically tuning these guidance weights. Our key observation is that, at each time step, the conditional denoising score-matching objective for diffusion models, or the conditional flow-matching objective for flow-matching models, is a least-squares objective. Therefore, when the conditional prediction is expressed as a weighted sum of the unconditional network output and a measurement-guidance term, optimizing over these weights reduces to a two-dimensional linear least-squares problem. The resulting time-dependent guidance weights can be optimized offline for a given measurement operator, noise level and sampler at the cost of a single minibatch of sampling trajectories, without retraining or fine-tuning the pretrained generative model. Instantiated with the standard Tweedie-based measurement-consistency term, our approach improves posterior sampling and achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction performance across diffusion- and flow-matching-based methods. Moreover, the optimized guidance weights enable diffusion samplers to reduce the number of sampling steps from 1000 to 50 with no significant degradation in reconstruction quality. Code will be made available.

</details>


### [302] [An Automated and Reproducible Workflow for Crack Identification and Damage Assessment of Fusion Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03505)
*Rinkle Juneja,Viktor Reshniak,Richard K. Archibald,John W. Duggan,Gregory R. Watson,Cory D. Hauck,Gary M. Staebler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于Galaxy的自动化SEM裂纹识别与损伤评估工作流，实现聚变材料表征的可复现、可共享与可移植分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后曝露显微分析是聚变材料合格性评定的核心，但人工分析无法扩展到现代聚变材料研究在数据量、异质性和多分辨率方面的需求，因此需要自动化、可复现的分析方案。

Method: 在Galaxy科学工作流环境中构建端到端工作流，输入SEM图像与实验元数据，自动识别裂纹并量化损伤，输出裂纹掩膜、骨架化裂纹网络、质控可视化及标量损伤描述符；方法设计无需针对具体图像调参，可跨钨牌号、微观组织、放大倍数与损伤状态通用。同时集成下游机器学习预测与物理裂纹模拟模块。

Result: 在稀疏电子束热冲击数据集（114个实验、418张图像、5种钨牌号、3种微观组织状态）上验证了工作流；定义了裂纹密度描述符，为下游机器学习预测和基于物理的裂纹模拟提供标准化输入。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是一个端到端、可共享且计算可移植的工作流，将实验表征、自动图像分析、初步损伤预测与模拟引导的数据采集相衔接，服务于聚变材料研究。

Abstract: Post-exposure microscopy is central to qualification of fusion materials. However, manual analysis does not scale to the volume, heterogeneity, and multiresolution character of modern fusion-materials campaigns. To address this challenge, we present a reproducible workflow, implemented in the Galaxy scientific workflow environment, for automated crack identification and quantitative damage assessment from scanning electron microscopy images. The workflow processes SEM images and experimental metadata to identify cracks, quantify damage, and retain the intermediate products and processing history needed for reproducibility. Outputs include crack masks, skeletonized crack networks, quality-control visualizations, and scalar damage descriptors. The method is designed to operate without image-specific parameter tuning across tungsten grades, microstructures, magnifications, and damage states. We demonstrate the workflow on a sparse electron-beam thermal-shock dataset containing 418 images from 114 experiments spanning five tungsten grades and three microstructural states. We define a crack-density descriptor, which provides standardized inputs for downstream machine-learning prediction and physics-based crack simulation. These predictive components are exposed in the same Galaxy environment and are intentionally treated here as extensible workflow modules. The principal contribution is therefore an end-to-end, shareable, and computationally portable workflow that links experimental characterization, automated image analysis, preliminary damage prediction, and simulation-guided data acquisition for fusion-materials research.

</details>


### [303] [Beyond Trained Models: Compiling GNNs for a Sound Explainer Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03526)
*Steve Azzolin,Francesco Paolo Nerini,Stefano Teso,Francesco Bonchi,Bruno Lepri,André Panisson,Andrea Passerini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出GNN解释器评估中"合理性"范式的隐含假设不成立，提出用编译替代训练来消除混淆，构建首个GNN解释器基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN解释器评估普遍采用"合理性"（plausibility）指标，即衡量解释能否恢复预设的ground truth（如数据中植入的motif）。该协议隐含假设：在此类数据上训练的GNN确实依赖预期motif。作者首先验证该假设在多个常用基准上被违反（例如仅凭度统计即可完成任务），且尽管已有工作质疑，该协议仍被广泛使用，因此需要消除这一混淆因素。

Method: 作者用"编译"（compilation）替代"训练"以消除混淆：提出Gracr，首个将分级模态逻辑（graded modal logic）公式编译为GNN权重的编译器，所得模型行为与对应公式一致。由于模型行为由构造已知，可形式化定义其ground truth解释并精确计算。基于此构建GracrBench，一个由编译得到的GNN组成的基准，用于对照精确ground truth评估解释器。



Result: 在六个任务、十一个解释器上的实验表明，GracrBench可进行细粒度诊断评估；尤其发现大多数解释器对间接影响（indirect influences）和同一公式的替代实现（alternative implementations）不具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GracrBench为图后验可解释性提供了一个新颖且严格的评估框架。

Abstract: Explainers for Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are commonly evaluated by their plausibility, i.e., how well their explanations recover a predefined ground truth, such as a motif planted in the data. This protocol implicitly assumes that a GNN trained on such data relies on the intended motif. Although prior work has questioned this assumption, plausibility remains widespread. First, we show that the assumption is violated on several widely used benchmarks, where, e.g., degree statistics alone suffice to solve the task. Then, we remove this confounder by replacing training with compilation. We achieve this by introducing $\mathsf{Gracr}$, the first compiler translating graded modal logic formulas into GNN weights, yielding models that replicate the behaviour of the corresponding formulas. Since the behaviour of the model is now known by construction, we can define its ground truth explanation formally and compute it exactly. Building on this, we introduce $\mathsf{Gracr}\mathsf{Bench}$, a benchmark of compiled GNNs for the evaluation of explainers against this exact ground truth. Experiments on eleven explainers across six tasks show its effectiveness for fine-grained diagnostic evaluation: notably, we discover that most explainers are not robust to indirect influences or alternative implementations of the same formula. These results position $\mathsf{Gracr}\mathsf{Bench}$ as a novel, rigorous evaluation setting for graph post-hoc explainability.

</details>


### [304] [Divergence controls entropy in distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03529)
*Nicolas Zucchet,Scott W. Linderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从熵的角度研究大语言模型蒸馏,证明前向KL会使学生熵膨胀高于教师,并揭示不同散度作为隐式熵正则化器的作用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏已成为大语言模型训练的核心手段,但其性质尚未被充分理解,尤其是散度选择如何影响学生模型的熵特性缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 采用熵视角,分析学生熵如何依赖数据及定义蒸馏目标的散度;通过理论证明前向KL导致学生熵膨胀,并考察反向KL及两者插值对熵的影响,结合预训练和监督微调进行定量验证。

Result: 前向KL使学生的熵高于教师,交叉熵作为特例给出可定量验证的恒等式;反向KL则使熵收缩直至师生差距过大;两者插值在训练早期平滑改变熵,但在收敛时突变。on-policy蒸馏的低熵来源于token级反向KL而非on-policy采样。

Conclusion: 散度充当隐式熵正则化器,该作用在自蒸馏中最为明显:当特权信息条件化使熵收缩时,最优的散度超参数正是能够补偿这一效应者。

Abstract: Distillation has become a core primitive of large language model training, but its properties are not yet well understood. We take an entropic perspective, studying how the entropy of the student depends on the data and the divergence that define the distillation objective. We prove that forward KL inflates the entropy of the student above that of the teacher. Since cross-entropy training is a special case, this yields an identity that we verify quantitatively in pretraining and supervised finetuning. Other divergences come with no such guarantee: reverse KL deflates entropy until the gap between student and teacher gets too large, and interpolating between the two changes entropy smoothly early in training but abruptly at convergence. The lower entropy of on-policy distillation comes from token-level reverse KL, not from on-policy sampling. The divergence therefore acts as an implicit entropy regularizer, whose role is clearest in self-distillation: as conditioning on privileged information deflates entropy, the divergence hyperparameters that work best are those that compensate for it.

</details>


### [305] [ZeroMAG: Zero-Shot Multimodal Adapter Generation for Plug-and-Play EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03546)
*Yubo Wang,Jingying Ma,Xinliang Zhou,Yangxuan Zhou,Jiquan Wang,Sha Zhao,Yiyuan Yang,Yi Ding,Ziyu Jia,Chenyu Liu,Cuntai Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZeroMAG是一个零样本多模态适配器生成框架，无需目标标签或目标端优化，即可将冻结的EEG基础模型扩展至多模态记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型（EFMs）只能利用EEG单模态信息，而实际记录常伴有提供互补信息的伴随生理信号。核心挑战在于：如何在保留预训练知识的同时，通过从无标签目标数据推断出的适配，将EFM扩展至异构多模态记录。

Method: ZeroMAG围绕"配置不变适配器"组织伴随模态，从无标签记录与任务上下文构建"模态-受试者-任务条件"，并在从源适配器学习到的函数约束潜空间中生成适配器权重。整个流程不使用目标标签，目标数据集在全部训练与选择阶段均被留出。

Result: 在六个留出目标数据集、三种EFM骨干上，ZeroMAG相比仅EEG推理提升平衡准确率7.22个百分点，相比直接权重回归提升4.89个百分点，平均仅落后有监督多模态适配0.50个百分点。消融实验显示，去除表征学习或条件生成中的任一函数监督均会降低生成适配器性能。

Conclusion: ZeroMAG能够在零标签、零目标端优化的条件下有效实现多模态扩展，性能接近有监督适配，且其中函数监督的两个组成部分均对生成适配器性能有实质贡献。

Abstract: EEG foundation models (EFMs) capture reusable knowledge from large-scale EEG data, while many EEG recordings also include companion physiological signals that provide complementary information beyond the EEG-only interface. The challenge is to preserve this pretrained knowledge while extending the EFM to heterogeneous multimodal recordings through an adaptation inferred from unlabeled target data. We introduce ZeroMAG, a zero-shot multimodal adapter generation framework that extends a frozen EEG encoder and prediction head using unlabeled target recordings, without target labels or target-side optimization. The target datasets are held out from all model training and selection in the ZeroMAG pipeline. ZeroMAG organizes companion modalities around a configuration-invariant adapter, constructs a modality-subject-task condition from unlabeled recordings and task context, and generates adapter weights in a function-constrained latent space learned from source adapters. Across six held-out target datasets and three EFM backbones, ZeroMAG improves balanced accuracy by 7.22 percentage points over EEG-only inference and 4.89 points over direct weight regression, while coming within 0.50 points of supervised multimodal adaptation on average. Ablations further show that removing functional supervision from either representation learning or conditional generation degrades generated-adapter performance, confirming the contribution of both components.

</details>


### [306] [Objects Without Morphisms: What LLMs for Mathematics Do Not Represent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03551)
*Yanli Wang,Suijin Wang,Xiaopeng Yuan,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究检验大语言模型在缺乏外部判定标准时能否保持数学翻译的忠实性。作者开发了一套盲审式评测工具，将真值、内容与论域范围分开编码。在四个家族七个模型上，向"一般框架"翻译时60.6%的改写扩大了量化域而无一个缩小；向"具体框架"翻译时28.3%缩小、0.3%扩大。假说显式陈述率仅21.6%（模型）与4.2%（人类）。该不对称性不随能力提升而消失，在最强的模型上反而最弱，且在预注册留出集与数学家文本上均可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在竞赛数学上的高水平表现主要依赖外部搜索与筛选（大量采样+外部准则保留），这只能改善幸存解的质量，并不改变模型内部的表征方式。作者想探究：当不存在外部判定标准时，模型是否真正掌握了内容。具体选择"相邻子领域方言间的陈述翻译"这一任务，因为忠实性取决于内容被断言时的"一般性层级"，而该层级隐含在源文本词汇中，必须借助理论之间的关系才能恢复。

Method: 构建了一个评测工具：将真值、内容与论域范围分别放入独立的盲审队列，采用无裁判（judge-free）指标衡量改写是否陈述了源文本所隐含的假说，并用植入阳性对照（planted-positive control）确立其灵敏度。任务为相邻数学子领域方言间的陈述互译，分别向"一般框架"和"具体框架"方向翻译，在来自四个家族共七个模型上进行测试，并在预注册规则划出的留出集与数学家撰写的陈述上验证复现性与稳健性。

Result: 向一般框架翻译：60.6%的改写扩大了量化域，无一个缩小；向具体框架翻译：28.3%缩小，0.3%扩大。能阻止该行为的假说仅在21.6%的模型改写与4.2%的人类陈述中被显式陈述。该不对称性在所有测试模型中均出现，且能力越强的模型扩大反而最小，说明能力并非其成因。结果在预注册留出集与数学家文本上均复现。指示模型陈述其所需要的全部假说可提高假说陈述率，但并未提升其对翻译方向的敏感性。

Conclusion: 作者认为，这些系统已获得子领域词汇之间的对象级对应关系，却缺乏"翻译须将假说映射为假说"这一理论间翻译所需的核心约束条件。因此，其翻译忠实性问题不能仅靠增加搜索量或提升能力来解决。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have reached expert-level performance on competition mathematics largely through the volume of search placed around them: candidate solutions are sampled in quantity and retained only when an external criterion accepts them. Such a procedure improves the outcome that survives it while leaving untouched what the model represents. We examine that question where no external criterion exists: translating statements between the dialects of neighbouring subfields, where fidelity turns on the level of generality at which content is asserted. The source leaves that level implicit in its vocabulary, so a faithful translation must recover it from the relation between the theories. We introduce an instrument that codes truth, content and scope in separate blind queues, with a judge-free measure of whether a rewrite states the hypothesis implicit in its source, and establish its sensitivity with a planted-positive control.
