Across seven models from four families, translating towards the general framing widens the domain of quantification in 60.6% of rewrites and narrows it in none; translating towards the concrete framing narrows it in 28.3% and widens it in 0.3%. The hypothesis that would prevent it is stated in 21.6% of model rewrites and 4.2% of human statements. Capability does not govern the asymmetry: it appears in every model tested, and the most capable widens least. It replicates on the half of the benchmark held out by a pre-registered rule, and on statements written by mathematicians. Instructing a model to state every hypothesis it requires raises that rate but not its sensitivity to direction. We argue that these systems have acquired an object-level correspondence between subfield vocabularies without the constraint under which a translation between theories carries hypotheses to hypotheses.

</details>


### [307] [Get a GRIP, this will be a long TRIP: A Quantifiable Long-Range Framework for Verifying Over-squashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03556)
*Ferran Hernandez Caralt,Simon Heilig,Adrián Bazaga,Asja Fischer,Moshe Eliasof,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出图神经网络长程交互（over-squashing）研究缺乏对“长程基准”的严格刻画，提出四条可验证公理（Predictability、Tightness、Strictly k-Range、Topology-Invariance），并基于此构造 TRIP/GRIP 基准生成方法及闭式逐距离 MLE oracle，用于审计和构建真正的长程任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实证结论依赖基准是否真正需要长程交互，但事实标准 Long Range Graph Benchmark 已被证明可被调优的短程模型饱和，而现有合成基准又与特定拓扑绑定，导致任意图上缺乏有原则的长程性证明，根源在于缺少对长程基准的精确刻画。

Method: 提出四条公理：Predictability（可预测性）、Tightness（紧致性）、Strictly k-Range（严格 k 距离）、Topology-Invariance（拓扑不变性），并形式化证明违反任一公理会引入削弱结论的失效模式；据此构造 TRIP（Truly Ranged Interactions Problem）及其推广 GRIP（Generally Ranged Interactions Problem），通过从稳定分布采样特征，将任意图转化为可证明的长程任务，且 GRIP 由构造天然具备闭式、逐距离的最大似然 oracle，给出首个先验的逐距离测试误差下界。

Result: (i) 依据公理审计了 4 个常见长程基准，识别其失效模式；(ii) 在 TRIP 实例化拓扑上，发现一种流行的曲率概念与 GNN 性能不相关，支持拓扑瓶颈与计算瓶颈的区分；(iii) 展示一个新基准的 over-squashing 度量反映了超越纯长程性的其他因素。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该框架为长程图基准提供了可验证的理论准则与构造工具，揭示现有基准的缺陷，并厘清 over-squashing 与长程交互、拓扑瓶颈之间的关系，为公平评估 GNN 长程建模能力奠定基础。

Abstract: Empirical claims about the connection between over-squashing and long-range interactions in GNNs, can only be trusted if the benchmarks used to validate them genuinely require long-range interactions. The de-facto standard, the Long Range Graph Benchmark, has been repeatedly shown to be saturated by tuned short-range models, with existing synthetic alternatives being tied to specific topologies. As such, there is a lack of principled certificate of long-rangedness on arbitrary graphs. This state reflects the absence of a precise characterization of long-ranged benchmarks. We address this fundamental gap by introducing four verifiable axioms: Predictability, Tightness, Strictly $k$-Range, and Topology-Invariance, that any task claiming to test $k$-hop interactions must satisfy. We formally prove that violating any one of them admits failure modes that undermine conclusions drawn from the task. Based on these axioms, we introduce TRIP (Truly Ranged Interactions Problem) and its generalisation GRIP (Generally Ranged Interactions Problem), constructive procedures that turn any graph into a provably long-ranged task by drawing features from stable distributions. Moreover, by construction, GRIP admits a closed-form, per-range Maximum-Likelihood oracle that yields the first a priori per-range lower bound on test error available on any benchmark. Using our framework, we: (i) audit 4 common long-range benchmarks and identify their failures modes with respect to our axioms; (ii) on TRIP-instantiated topologies, we find a popular notion of curvature is uncorrelated with GNN performance, supporting topological-vs-computational bottleneck distinction; and (iii) we show that a novel benchmark's over-squashing measures factors beyond pure long-rangedness. Code to use the framework and reproduce experiments is released https://github.com/ferranhernandezc/graph-grip.

</details>


### [308] [Cephalonauts One: A deep fMRI dataset for decoding naturalistic speech in the human brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03558)
*Antoine Collas,Louis Jalouzot,Géraud Ilinca,Corentin Caris,Romain Valabrègue,Ahmed Hassayoune,David Goncalves,Madeleine Hueber,Thaddée Delebarre,Julien Savatovsky,Clara Fonteneau,Charles Maussion,Bertrand Thirion,Alexis Thual*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cephalonauts One 是一个全脑 3T fMRI 数据集，记录被试聆听音频播客时的脑活动，每名被试 30 小时数据，并附带解码基准（音频片段检索）及基线模型和缩放分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 fMRI 数据集在使用自然语音刺激方面深度不足，缺乏大规模、长时程的配对数据以及标准化的脑解码评测基准，难以支撑基于自然语音的脑活动解码研究。

Method: 3 名健康被试在多次扫描中进行全脑 3T fMRI 采集，每次含 5 个 15 分钟 run，同时聆听母语播客；数据与播客音频、转录标注及派生刺激嵌入配对。提出音频片段检索式脑解码基准，给定留出 session 的 fMRI 活动，从候选片段中识别时间对齐的播客音频，并提供标准划分、评价指标与基线解码器。

Result: 每名被试约 30 小时 fMRI 数据，为使用自然语音刺激的最深 fMRI 数据集；提供了标准划分、评价指标与基线解码器；缩放分析显示解码性能随每名被试训练数据量增加而持续提升。

Conclusion: 该数据集与基准为自然语音条件下的脑解码研究提供了大规模资源与标准化评测框架，并揭示了数据规模对解码性能的正向缩放效应。

Abstract: Cephalonauts One is a whole-brain 3 Tesla (3T) functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) dataset recorded while subjects listened to audio podcasts. Three healthy subjects underwent multiple scanning sessions, each consisting of five 15-minute runs, while listening to podcasts in their native language. With 30 hours of fMRI data per subject, the current release is the deepest available fMRI dataset using naturalistic speech stimuli. The dataset pairs brain activity with the corresponding podcast audio, transcript annotations, and derived stimulus embeddings. Furthermore, we introduce a brain decoding benchmark formulated as audio segment retrieval: given fMRI activity from a held-out session, the decoder must identify the corresponding time-aligned podcast audio segment among candidate segments. We provide standardized splits, evaluation metrics, and baseline decoders for this task. Finally, a scaling analysis shows that decoding performance improves continuously with the amount of training data per subject.

</details>


### [309] [What Should World Models Forget? Stratified Retention for Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03713)
*Nishit Anand,Ramani Duraiswami,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出世界模型的预测目标是非平稳环境，因此"遗忘"不应一概视为失败；提出按不变性时间尺度分层的保留观，并以"差分保留"指标（不变量回归测试+修订时延，不做聚合）替代传统遗忘指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习沿用平稳预测目标的惯例，把对旧数据的性能下降当作失败。但世界模型的预测目标是会变化的环境，曾被正确习得的知识可能过时，丢弃它是应有行为而非缺陷；同时世界模型还编码了永不修订的知识（如物理规律、客体永久性）。概念漂移与语言模型时序事实性已有研究，但尚未针对世界模型这一兼具"必改"与"永不改"知识的特殊性进行形式化。

Method: 提出以不变性时间尺度分层的保留（stratified retention）：将物理规律、客体永久性等不变量（永不修订）与实例级事实（环境变化即应修订）区分开。进而提出 differential retention 评估方式：在适应流上联合报告不变量回归测试与修订时延，且不做聚合。

Result: 标准遗忘指标无法区分"已正确修订过时知识的世界模型"与"发生灾难性遗忘的世界模型"，因而会把冻结模型排为最优；现有物理推理基准也只评估冻结的检查点。

Conclusion: 世界模型的持续学习需要按不变性时间尺度分层评估，应放弃单一聚合的遗忘分数，改用不变量回归测试与修订时延的联合、非聚合报告，以正确刻画"该改的改了、不该改的没改"这一双重目标。

Abstract: Continual learning treats degradation on previously seen data as evidence of failure, a convention inherited from settings with a stationary prediction target, where a correct label remains correct indefinitely. World models do not satisfy this condition. Their prediction target is the environment, which changes, so knowledge that was accurate when acquired may later become false, and discarding it is required behavior rather than a defect. Non-stationary ground truth is well studied in the concept drift literature and in the temporal factuality of language models, but has not been formulated for world models, which are distinctive in that they also encode knowledge that must never be revised. We argue that continual world models require retention stratified by invariance timescale, separating invariants such as physics and object permanence, which must never be revised, from instance-level facts that should be revised as soon as the environment changes. Standard forgetting metrics cannot distinguish a world model that has correctly revised outdated knowledge from one that has suffered catastrophic forgetting, and consequently rank a frozen model highest, while existing physical-reasoning benchmarks evaluate only frozen checkpoints. We propose differential retention, which reports invariant regression testing across the adaptation stream jointly with revision latency, without aggregation.

</details>


### [310] [A Path Integral Surrogate for Multi-Step Gradient Inversion in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03597)
*Agnivo Ghosh,Saumik Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PI-SME，将FedAvg中累积的客户端更新视为梯度场的路径积分，用沿可学习Bézier路径的高斯-勒让德求积进行近似，从而改进梯度反演攻击中对私有图像的重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习声称客户端只共享模型更新而非原始数据，从而保护隐私。梯度反演攻击直接从单次更新中重建客户端私有输入图像，对这一前提构成威胁。在FedAvg下，客户端更新累积了多个本地训练步，服务器只能看到隐藏权重轨迹的两个端点；现有攻击虽在两端点之间拟合代理模型，但仅在单点读取其梯度，未能充分利用轨迹信息。

Method: 提出Path-Integral Surrogate Model Extension (PI-SME)：将累积更新建模为梯度场的路径积分，并用沿可学习Bézier路径上多个节点的高斯-勒让德求积来近似该积分，从而更完整地刻画权重轨迹上的梯度信息。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和FEMNIST图像上，覆盖多种轨迹长度与类别受限批次设置，PI-SME在多项反演指标及匹配损失上均比最强的代理基线更忠实地重建私有输入。

Conclusion: 将累积更新视为路径积分并采用多点求积近似，能显著提升梯度反演攻击的重建质量，进一步说明仅依赖更新共享不足以保证客户端隐私。

Abstract: Federated learning lets many clients train a shared model together without ever sending their private data to a central server. Each client shares only a model update, and this update should reveal far less about the client than its raw training examples would. This premise is what protects the privacy of the clients. Gradient inversion attacks challenge it directly by trying to reconstruct a client's private input images from the single update it shared. Under FedAvg, a client's update accumulates several local training steps, so the server sees only the two endpoints of a hidden weight trajectory. Recent gradient inversion attacks fit a surrogate model along the path between these two endpoints but they still read its gradient at a single point. We propose the Path-Integral Surrogate Model Extension (PI-SME) which treats the accumulated update as a path integral of the gradient field and approximates it by Gauss--Legendre quadrature over several nodes along a learnable Bézier path. On CIFAR-100 and FEMNIST images across a range of trajectory lengths and class-restricted batches PI-SME reconstructs the private inputs more faithfully than the strongest surrogate baseline on several inversion metrics and the matching loss.

</details>


### [311] [Mastering Atari 2600 Games with Discovered Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03604)
*Erik M. Lintunen,Marlos C. Machado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Wayfarer 是一种通用的在线深度强化学习智能体，通过从高维观测中学习拉普拉斯表示来自动发现选项，并利用这些选项提升探索、信用分配与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间抽象（如选项）被认为能加速信用分配、促进探索并实现泛化，但在大规模高维领域中，通用且有效的选项发现方法仍是基本挑战。现有方法要么局限于简单领域，要么依赖手工或准符号表示，要么相比无选项学习提升有限。

Method: 提出 Wayfarer：一种通用、领域无关的在线深度强化学习智能体。它从高维观测中通过拉普拉斯表示学习发现选项，并将这些选项用于控制。

Result: 所发现的选项同时改善了探索、加速了信用分配，并能有效泛化到未见过的环境，显著加快复杂策略的学习。Wayfarer 在最难的 Atari 2600 游戏中达到单流智能体的最先进性能，在需要长时程探索和策略行为的游戏（如 Montezuma's Revenge 和 Private Eye）中提升最大。

Conclusion: Wayfarer 证明了从高维观测中通过拉普拉斯表示学习可自动发现通用选项，并将探索、信用分配和泛化统一受益，为大规模高维领域中的选项发现提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Temporal abstractions, often instantiated as options, have long been regarded as a mechanism for accelerating credit assignment, facilitating exploration, and enabling generalisation in reinforcement learning (RL). However, developing general option discovery methods that are effective in large-scale, high-dimensional domains remains a fundamental challenge. Existing option discovery methods are either confined to relatively simple domains, depend on handcrafted or quasi-symbolic representations, or offer little improvement over learning without options. We present Wayfarer, a general, domain-agnostic, online deep RL agent that discovers options through Laplacian representation learning from high-dimensional observations and leverages them for control. We show that the resulting options simultaneously improve exploration, accelerate credit assignment, and generalise effectively to unseen settings, enabling substantially faster learning of complex policies. Wayfarer achieves state-of-the-art performance among single-stream agents on the most challenging Atari 2600 games, with the largest gains in games that require long-horizon exploration and strategic behaviour, such as Montezuma's Revenge and Private Eye.

</details>


### [312] [UniIntervene++: An Adaptive Intervention Agent for Efficient Real-World Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03620)
*Yudong Lin,Haoyuan Deng,Zhuoxuan Yuan,Zaijia Yang,Yuanjiang Xue,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 UniIntervene++，一种自适应干预智能体，在在线强化学习中动态分配自主执行与多种辅助行为之间的控制权，联合决定何时干预、如何干预及何时交还控制权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 RL 使机器人策略可通过物理交互持续改进，但其所需辅助随能力演化而变化；现有基于离线估计或固定决策规则的干预策略会与当前策略失配，导致干预不足或冗余。

Method: 将演化的 RL 策略、轨迹修正与任务结构化 CodePolicy 建模为统一半马尔可夫决策过程（semi-MDP）中的 Options 并在线学习其相对价值；引入能力自适应干预，通过无辅助执行定期探测 RL 策略以保持控制分配对其能力变化的响应；采用耦合经验学习，使辅助行为改进 RL 策略，而策略演化结果反过来重塑未来干预决策。

Result: 在五个真实世界操作任务上，平均成功率达 89.67%，优于所有基线至少 6 个百分点；人工干预降至 0.77%，相较最佳基线相对减少至少 94.6%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: UniIntervene++ 通过统一 Options 建模、能力自适应探测与耦合经验学习，实现了随策略能力协同演化的自适应干预，在真实操作任务中同时显著提升成功率并大幅降低人工干预成本。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) enables robot policies to improve through physical interaction, but the assistance they require changes as their competence evolves. Existing intervention strategies based on offline estimates or fixed decision rules can therefore become mismatched to the current policy. To address this, we propose UniIntervene++, an adaptive intervention agent that learns to allocate control between autonomous execution and heterogeneous assisted behaviors during online RL. Specifically, UniIntervene++ first formulates the evolving RL policy, trajectory correction, and a task-structured CodePolicy as Options in a unified semi-Markov decision process and learns their relative values online. Building on this, competence-adaptive intervention periodically probes the RL policy through unassisted execution, keeping control allocation responsive to its evolving capability. Finally, coupled experience learning allows assisted behaviors to improve the RL policy, whose evolving outcomes in turn reshape future intervention decisions. In this way, UniIntervene++ jointly determines when to intervene, how to intervene, and when to return control as the RL policy improves. Across five real-world manipulation tasks, UniIntervene++ achieves an average success rate of 89.67%, outperforming all baselines by at least 6 percentage points, while reducing human intervention to 0.77%, a relative reduction of at least 94.6% from the best baseline. Code is available in our \href{https://github.com/dannyyudong/An-Adaptive-Intervention-Agent-for-Efficient-Real-World-Reinforcement-Learning}{GitHub repository}.

</details>


### [313] [Broken scale symmetries in undercomplete linear autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03640)
*Farhad Pashakhanloo,Jacob A. Zavatone-Veth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究欠完备线性自编码器中SGD对尺度对称性的破坏，发现SGD在PCA解流形上偏好较大的解码器权重，并给出可解析的有效动力学描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络损失景观存在诸多对称性，梯度流保持这些对称性，而有限步长SGD会破坏它们。已有工作记录了SGD打破尺度对称性以平衡梯度噪声或最小化波动的案例，但对称性破缺在解流形上的方向选择机制尚不清楚。本文旨在探究损失几何如何将残余梯度噪声转化为沿功能等价解流形的有向运动。

Method: 以欠完备线性自编码器为模型，分析其尺度对称性在SGD下的破缺行为。通过在PCA解流形上考察SGD的尺度漂移方向，建立可解析处理的慢时间尺度有效动力学描述，并追踪漂移所趋向的有限步长稳定性边界。

Result: 在PCA解流形上，SGD偏好较大的解码器权重，形成有向尺度漂移；该漂移发生在慢时间尺度上，且其动力学可解析描述。漂移不能无限持续，会趋向有限步长稳定性边界。所得解在损失Hessian最大特征值意义上比平衡基线更尖锐，但不同尖锐度度量可能呈相反方向变化。

Conclusion: 欠完备自编码器提供了一个具体例证，说明损失几何如何将残余梯度噪声转化为沿功能等价解流形的有向运动，并揭示了对称性破缺方向由解几何选择这一机制。

Abstract: Neural network loss landscapes have many symmetries, which are preserved by gradient flow but broken by finite-stepsize stochastic gradient descent (SGD). A canonical example of such a symmetry is scale in homogeneous networks: one can scale up the parameters in one layer and down in the next without changing the network output. Previous work has documented cases in which SGD breaks this symmetry in favor of balancing gradient noise or minimizing fluctuations. Here, we show that the solution geometry of undercomplete linear autoencoders instead selects a preferred sign for scale drift: on the PCA solution manifold, SGD favors large decoder weights. This directed scale drift occurs on a slow timescale, and its dynamics admit an analytically-tractable effective description. However, it cannot continue indefinitely: increasing scale eventually drives the dynamics towards a finite-stepsize stability boundary. The resulting solutions are sharper than a balanced baseline in the sense of the maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian, but different sharpness measures can move in opposing directions. Thus, undercomplete autoencoders give a concrete illustration of how loss geometry can convert residual gradient noise into directed motion along a manifold of functionally-equivalent solutions.

</details>


### [314] [IDRF: Inverse-Distilled Reward Fine-tuning of Masked Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03641)
*Vladislav Gromadskii,David Li,Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Eric Moulines,Maxim Panov,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IDRF: 面向少步掩码离散扩散生成器的奖励微调框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码离散扩散模型是自回归生成的有前景替代方案，但其迭代采样代价高昂，且序列似然难以计算，导致奖励微调困难。

Method: 从标准反向KL正则化目标出发，用逆蒸馏正则替代难算的序列级KL惩罚；在最优辅助去噪器下证明总体逆蒸馏损失上界于对参考分布的序列级KL散度。优化该损失的轨迹级替代目标，无需参考模型rollout，使学生保留自身少步采样器；将少步生成建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程，并用裁剪策略梯度目标在轨迹上优化奖励。

Result: 在DNA、图像和文本生成任务上，IDRF取得高奖励，去噪步数较参考模型最多减少32倍，同时缓解奖励攻击并保持样本质量。

Conclusion: IDRF为少步掩码离散扩散生成器提供了有效的奖励微调方案，兼顾效率、奖励优化与样本质量。

Abstract: Masked discrete diffusion models offer a promising alternative to autoregressive generation, but iterative sampling can be costly, and intractable sequence likelihoods complicate reward fine-tuning. We introduce IDRF, a framework for reward fine-tuning of few-step masked discrete diffusion generators. Starting from a standard reverse-KL-regularized objective, IDRF replaces the intractable sequence-level KL penalty with inverse-distillation regularization. With an optimal auxiliary denoiser, we prove that the population inverse-distillation loss upper-bounds the sequence-level KL divergence to the reference distribution. IDRF optimizes a trajectory-based surrogate of this loss without reference-model rollouts, so the student keeps its own few-step sampler. We view few-step generation as a finite-horizon Markov decision process and optimize reward with a clipped policy-gradient objective over the student's trajectories. Across DNA, image, and text generation, IDRF achieves high reward with up to $32\times$ fewer denoising steps than the reference while mitigating reward hacking and preserving sample quality.

</details>


### [315] [On the Convergence of Success Conditioning for Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03642)
*Matthew Brun,Xu Andy Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究成功条件化策略在随机环境中的收敛性，证明其收敛到最优策略，并给出折扣MDP与单周期MDP的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功条件化是强化学习中常用的改进策略方法，但其极限行为与收敛速率尚缺乏理论理解。

Method: 通过理论分析，在广泛的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）类上研究成功条件化的收敛性质，并针对折扣MDP和单周期MDP推导收敛速率。

Result: 折扣MDP可在O(1/ε^p)次迭代内收敛到ε-最优策略（指数p依赖问题数据）；单周期MDP可在O(log(1/ε))次迭代内获得该策略。

Conclusion: 成功条件化在广泛MDP类上收敛到最优策略，并给出具体收敛速率，为其实践应用提供理论保证。

Abstract: Success conditioning is a strategy for improving decision-making policies in stochastic environments; it updates a policy by increasing the probability of taking actions that yield successful outcomes. Success conditioning is common to many reinforcement learning applications, yet its limiting behavior and convergence rates are not well understood. In this work, we demonstrate that success conditioning converges to an optimal policy on a broad class of Markov decision processes (MDPs). We also derive convergence rates in some common settings. For discounted MDPs, we prove convergence within $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon^p)$ iterations to an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy, where the exponent $p$ depends on problem data. For single-period MDPs, such a policy is obtained within $\mathcal{O}(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ iterations.

</details>


### [316] [When May a Bandit Leave Its Anchor? E-Process-Authorized Thompson Sampling under Non-stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03646)
*Mayand Gulati,Kerong Wang,WeiChen Au*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 e-ATS：用 anytime-valid e-process 授权是否启用折扣（遗忘）状态，在授权前严格等价 OTS，并给出偏离概率上界 α_E。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平稳环境下历史信息有价值（记忆带来收益），但环境发生改变后，同一段历史可能产生误导，因此需要回答"何时应当允许遗忘"这一问题，并避免依赖拟合阈值来人为判断。

Method: e-ATS 为每条臂同时维护全历史 Beta 状态与折扣 Beta 状态；先用 anytime-valid e-process 对折扣状态进行授权检验，再通过可逆的相关性得分（relevance score）控制折扣状态的影响权重。未获授权时完全退化为乐观 Thompson 采样（OTS）。在 Beta-Bernoulli 先验预测的平稳模型下分析其性质。

Result: 在 Beta-Bernoulli 平稳模型下，e-ATS 任何时刻偏离 OTS 的概率不超过所选 α_E，且无需拟合阈值。相对 e-ATS，去掉授权机制使注册数据集上的平均归一化动态伪遗憾上升 38.4%，但在文献衍生的重放数据集上下降 7.5%。

Conclusion: 证据决定的是"遗忘/自适应何时开始"，而非保证自适应总会带来收益；授权机制可提供风险可控的触发条件，但其收益依赖环境是否真的发生非平稳变化。

Abstract: Stationarity rewards memory, but after a change the same history can mislead. We ask when forgetting should be permitted. E-process-authorized Thompson sampling (e-ATS) gives each arm full-history and discounted Beta states. An anytime-valid e-process first authorizes the discounted state, then a reversible relevance score controls its influence. Before authorization, e-ATS exactly follows optimistic Thompson sampling (OTS). Under a Beta-Bernoulli prior-predictive stationary model, e-ATS's probability of ever departing from OTS is at most the chosen $α_E$, without fitted thresholds. Relative to e-ATS, removing authorization increased mean normalized dynamic pseudo-regret by $38.4\%$ on the registered suite but reduced it by $7.5\%$ on the literature-derived replay suite. Therefore, evidence controls when adaptation begins, not whether it always helps.

</details>


### [317] [Forecasting from Counterfactual Simulator Rollouts: A Sim2Real Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03662)
*Angel Wang,Dominique Perrault-Joncas,Alvaro Maggiar,Dean Foster,Carson Eisenach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出利用仿真生成反事实数据训练预测模型，以解决新决策策略部署时的冷启动问题，并通过两个真实库存控制部署案例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新策略部署带来冷启动问题：历史观测反映旧策略，而新策略下的真实观测尚不可得，导致依赖策略动作的预测模型目标难以训练。仿真可跨反事实场景展开目标策略，用生成轨迹学习系统对控制的响应，从而填补这一数据缺口。

Method: 通过仿真器围绕目标策略进行反事实展开，生成轨迹并训练预测模型；再从三个角度评估Sim2Real迁移过程：仿真器保真度、零样本迁移到真实行为、以及随真实目标策略观测累积的适配。方法以两个真实库存控制部署为案例进行回测。

Result: 仿真器训练的预测器点估计MAPE低于在历史真实数据上训练的相同架构：研究1降低1.2–3.1个百分点，研究2降低12.5–18.7个百分点；部署后利用早期真实观测进行轻量校准，误差进一步降低最多2.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 实证表明，仿真生成的反事实数据能够支撑新策略下的冷启动预测，且所得模型可随真实部署数据积累而持续改进。

Abstract: Deploying a new decision policy creates a cold-start problem for prediction models whose targets depend on the policy's actions: historical observations reflect earlier policies, while real observations under the new policy are not yet available. Simulation offers a way to address this gap by rolling out the target policy across counterfactual scenarios and using the resulting trajectories to learn how the system responds to those controls. The simulation-to-reality (Sim2Real) transfer of this simulator-trained model can then be backtested by evaluating it against real observations from past deployments. Using two real-world inventory-control deployments, we evaluate this process from three angles: simulator fidelity, zero-shot transfer to real behavior, and adaptation as real target-policy observations accumulate. The simulator-trained forecaster achieves lower point-estimate mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) than the same architecture trained on historical real data, reducing MAPE by 1.2-3.1 percentage points in Study 1 and 12.5-18.7 points in Study 2. After deployment, lightweight calibration using early real observations further reduces error by up to 2.5 percentage points. These results provide empirical evidence that simulator-generated counterfactual data can support cold-start forecasting under a new policy, and the resulting model can be further refined as real deployment data become available.

</details>


### [318] [Pivot-SD: Efficient Self-Distillation for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03665)
*Seo Hyun Kim,Sunwoo Hong,Younwoo Choi,Chen-Hao Chao,Se-Young Yun,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pivot-SD：一种针对掩码扩散语言模型的离线自蒸馏框架，仅对去噪过程中高影响力的"关键承诺"（pivots）进行监督，用200题即可超越全序列SFT与预算匹配的扩散RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（dLMs）在复杂推理上可并行解码，但存在独特的信用分配难题：去噪过程中少数承诺会大幅降低其余掩码位置的不确定性并塑造大部分回复。现有后训练方法未利用该信号——它们多对最终文本训练，或将奖励分配给整个去噪步骤，而非选择真正塑造回复的单个承诺。

Method: 提出 Pivot-SD，高效的离线自蒸馏框架，仅监督高影响力承诺（pivots）。用信息增益度量（衡量对剩余掩码位置的不确定性降低程度）来选取 pivots。对成功轨迹中的 pivots 用交叉熵训练；对失败轨迹中的 pivots 用定向无似然（targeted unlikelihood）训练，失败轨迹其余部分保持不动。

Result: 仅用200个问题、每题4次采样（rollout），在数学与代码基准上，Pivot-SD 使 LLaDA-8B-Instruct 优于全序列 SFT 以及预算匹配的扩散 RL 基线。

Conclusion: 以信息增益定位去噪中的关键承诺并做稀疏、方向性的监督，是一种数据高效、优于全序列微调与扩散RL的dLM后训练策略。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLMs) offer a promising parallel alternative to autoregressive models for complex reasoning. However, they face a distinct credit-assignment challenge, since a few commitments during denoising sharply reduce the uncertainty over the remaining masked positions and shape much of the response. Most post-training recipes for dLMs do not use this signal to decide which tokens to train on: they typically train on the final text or assign rewards to whole denoising steps, rather than selecting the individual commitments that shape the response. We introduce Pivot-SD, an efficient offline self-distillation framework that supervises only these high-impact commitments (pivots). Pivot-SD selects pivots using an information-gain metric measuring uncertainty reduction over the remaining masked positions. Pivots from successful trajectories are trained with cross-entropy, and pivots from failed trajectories with targeted unlikelihood, leaving the rest of the failed trajectory untouched. Using only 200 questions and four rollouts each, Pivot-SD improves LLaDA-8B-Instruct over full-sequence SFT and budget-matched diffusion RL baselines across math and code benchmarks.

</details>


### [319] [Planning to Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03667)
*Ian Osband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示精确策略梯度在分类任务中反而不如交叉熵，原因在于其短视性；通过引入"视界损失"（horizon loss），在MNIST与ImageNet上提升了top-1准确率，且标签噪声越大增益越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代强化学习（含LLM后训练）中的策略梯度方法常受限于探索、信用分配与动作采样噪声，而分类任务恰好不含这三者：分类器作为一种策略，其期望奖励即期望准确率，等于分配给正确标签的概率，因标签已知，策略梯度是精确且光滑的。然而即便如此，精确策略梯度在期望准确率上仍劣于交叉熵。作者试图解释这一悖论并加以改进。

Method: 作者指出精确策略梯度是短视的（myopic）：它仅以当前收益衡量更新，但每次更新也决定了下一步的起点，故更新的价值取决于剩余的学习量。据此，交叉熵被解释为"耐心的准确率"——即假设样本对数几率以单位速度无限上升时累计的总误差，而精确策略梯度是其零视界极限。将这一累计量在剩余学习量处截断，得到视界损失（horizon loss），仅需一行改动，随训练推进从交叉熵向精确策略梯度过渡。作者还在一个简单分配模型中证明了该方法能避开两端点各自陷入的陷阱。

Result: 在MNIST以及使用ResNet-50、ResNet-101和ViT-S/16的ImageNet上，视界损失在固定学习率下相比交叉熵提升了top-1准确率，且增益随标签噪声增加而增大。

Conclusion: 精确策略梯度的短视性是其劣于交叉熵的根源；视界损失通过按剩余学习量截断累计误差，在理论与实践上均优于交叉熵与精确策略梯度两个端点，并在标准视觉基准及标签噪声场景下取得一致的准确率提升。

Abstract: Policy-gradient methods are central to modern reinforcement learning, including LLM post-training. When they struggle, the usual suspects are exploration, credit assignment and action-sampling noise. Classification has none of them. A classifier is a policy whose expected reward, its \emph{expected accuracy}, is the probability it assigns to the correct label, and because that label is known, the policy gradient is exact and smooth. Yet exact policy gradient loses to cross-entropy, even on expected accuracy. The exact gradient is myopic: it values an update only by what it buys now, but each update also sets where the next one starts, so an update's value depends on how much learning remains. Viewed this way, cross-entropy is patient accuracy, the total error an example would pay if its log-odds rose at unit speed forever, while exact policy gradient is the zero-horizon limit. Truncating this total at the learning that remains yields the horizon loss, a one-line change that moves from cross-entropy toward exact policy gradient as training runs out. In a simple allocation model, it provably escapes the trap that catches each endpoint. On MNIST and on ImageNet with ResNet-50, ResNet-101 and ViT-S/16, the horizon loss improves top-1 accuracy over cross-entropy at a flat learning rate, and the gain grows with label noise.

</details>


### [320] [Simulation-Free Learning of Population Dynamics with Wasserstein Lagrangian Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03679)
*Fedor Sergeev,Markus Heinonen,Daniel Waxman,Tim Cooijmans,Ricardo Baptista,Dmitry Batenkov,Eli Bingham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Double-Stitch，一种无模拟（simulation-free）方法，用于从非配对快照中学习Wasserstein空间中的拉格朗日力学，通过惩罚学习到的种群路径上运动方程的残差，训练速度比基于模拟的WLM快4-14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞、生物体和流体的动力学常被建模为随时间演化的概率分布。从非配对快照中重建和推断该演化需要关于潜在过程的假设。Wasserstein梯度流是常见选择，但无法描述保守或周期性动力学；Wasserstein空间中的拉格朗日力学虽能覆盖二者，但现有学习方法基于模拟：每个训练步都要运行数值求解器，导致训练昂贵。

Method: 提出Double-Stitch，一种无模拟方法：通过学习到的种群路径上运动方程的残差进行惩罚，从而学习上述力学。从不需要梯度速度（gradient velocities）的Clebsch变分原理推导该运动方程，并证明当方程成立时残差恰好为零。

Result: 在合成数据集、单细胞数据集和海洋涡旋数据集上测试Double-Stitch，发现在大多数任务上其表现与梯度流方法和基于模拟的WLM相当或更优，同时训练速度比WLM快4-14倍。提供了JAX实现。

Conclusion: Double-Stitch通过无模拟的残差惩罚框架，在不依赖数值求解器的前提下学习Wasserstein空间中的拉格朗日力学，兼具对保守/周期性动力学的建模能力与显著提升的训练效率，为从非配对快照推断分布演化提供了实用方案。

Abstract: The dynamics of cells, organisms, and fluids are often modeled as probability distributions evolving over time. Reconstructing and extrapolating this evolution from unpaired snapshots requires assumptions about the underlying process. Wasserstein gradient flows are a common choice, but they cannot describe conservative or periodic dynamics. Lagrangian mechanics in Wasserstein space covers both, but existing methods for learning it are simulation-based: they run a numerical solver at every training step, which makes training expensive. We propose Double-Stitch, a simulation-free method that learns these mechanics by penalizing the residual of the equation of motion along a learned population path. We derive this equation from a Clebsch variational principle that does not require gradient velocities, and show that the residual vanishes exactly when the equation holds. We test Double-Stitch on synthetic, single-cell and ocean vortex datasets and find that it matches or outperforms gradient-flow methods and simulation-based WLM on most tasks, while training $4$-$14$ times faster than WLM. We provide a JAX implementation of Double-Stitch at https://github.com/BasisResearch/stitching.

</details>


### [321] [LESSER: Post-Training Data Selection with Output-Layer Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03702)
*Lyuxin David Zhang,Eric Wong,Surbhi Goel,Anton Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LESSER，用输出层梯度替代全参数梯度进行数据选择，在保持下游性能的同时大幅降低特征提取的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的数据选择方法能通过样本梯度与小型验证集梯度的对齐程度来排序训练数据，提升大语言模型后训练效果。但全参数梯度需要对每个样本进行昂贵的反向传播，候选池较大时计算不可行。因此作者希望以更低成本近似全梯度特征。

Method: 作者发现输出层梯度足以支持有效的数据选择，且只需较便宜的前向传播即可获得。据此实现 LESSER，作为选择方法的即插即用封装器，用输出层梯度特征替代全参数梯度特征。

Result: LESSER 将特征提取的 FLOP 成本降低了 9.7 倍（SFT）和 3.0 倍（RL 基准），同时在下游任务上追踪全梯度的性能。即使输出层梯度与全梯度对单个样本排序不同，二者选出的批次梯度仍然对齐。

Conclusion: 输出层梯度可作为全参数梯度的廉价且有效的代理，用于后训练数据选择。LESSER 通用、即插即用，在显著降低计算成本的同时保持数据选择质量。

Abstract: The choice of post-training data for large language models substantially affects downstream performance. Gradient-based data selection is a popular approach that ranks training data by how well their gradients align with those of a small validation set. However, ranking with full-parameter gradients requires an expensive backward pass on every sample, making computation intractable for large candidate pools. This raises a natural question: can we approximate full-gradient features at a fraction of the cost? Conveniently, we find that output-layer gradients suffice for effective data selection, yet require only the cheaper forward pass. We implement this as LESSER, a drop-in wrapper for selection methods that reduces the feature-extraction FLOP cost by $9.7\times$ for SFT and $3.0\times$ for RL benchmarks, while tracking full-gradient performance on downstream tasks. Empirically, we find that even when output-layer and full gradients rank individual samples differently, they select batches with aligned gradients.

</details>


### [322] [RNADyn: A Benchmark for Generating and Understanding RNA Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03712)
*Yiming Huang,Lennart Bastian,Hanqun Cao,Luis Vollmers,Tolga Birdal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出标准化的RNA分子动力学基准RNADynBench（2585条质控100ns全原子轨迹，防泄漏划分）及统一模型RNADynNet，用共享骨干同时实现轨迹生成与动力学指纹提取，在RMSF相关性上分别达0.875和0.766。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNA功能依赖构象变化，静态结构无法充分刻画；然而大规模标准化RNA动力学数据稀缺，且现有方法通常将轨迹生成与动力学理解割裂为两个独立目标。

Method: 构建RNADynBench标准化MD基准，包含2585条质控100ns全原子轨迹及防泄漏数据划分；据此提出RNADynNet统一模型，采用共享骨干网络，从单一构象出发同时完成轨迹生成与动力学指纹提取，通过坐标去噪、单帧到轨迹对齐及物理约束三项机制，将全原子轨迹生成与动力学表征学习相连接。

Result: 物理约束同时提升了生成动力学的质量及指纹中可恢复的物理信息；在两个测试集（含高柔性挑战集）上，生成轨迹的RMSF相关性分别为0.875和0.766，单构象预测与MD推导动力学达到相当一致性。

Conclusion: RNADynBench与RNADynNet共同建立了用于生成和理解RNA动力学的基准与统一建模框架，为RNA动力学的标准化研究与联合建模提供了基础。

Abstract: Ribonucleic acid (RNA) functions through conformational changes that are not fully captured by static structures. However, large-scale standardized RNA dynamics data remain limited, and existing approaches typically treat trajectory generation and dynamics understanding as separate objectives. Here, we introduce RNADynBench, a standardized RNA molecular dynamics (MD) benchmark with 2585 quality-controlled 100-ns all-atom trajectories and leakage-controlled splits. Building on RNADynBench, we develop RNADynNet, a unified model for RNA dynamics learning that uses a shared backbone for both trajectory generation and dynamics fingerprint extraction from a single conformer. It combines coordinate denoising, single-frame-to-trajectory alignment, and physical grounding to connect all-atom trajectory generation with dynamics representation learning. Physical grounding improves both generated dynamics and the physical information recoverable from these fingerprints. Across both test sets, including the high-flexibility challenge set, the generated trajectories achieve RMSF correlations of 0.875 and 0.766, while single-conformer predictions show comparable agreement with MD-derived dynamics. RNADynBench and RNADynNet together establish a benchmark and unified modeling framework for generating and understanding RNA dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [323] [Adaptive Sparsity Optimization with Learnable Soft Top-K and Per-Term Thresholding for Efficient Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02572)
*Wentai Xie,Parker Carlson,Shanxiu He,Tao Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过可学习软Top-K、逐项阈值和FLOPs正则化协同作用的稀疏化优化方案，在保持检索相关性的同时显著缩短查询与文档向量长度，降低检索时延和存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的神经稀疏检索虽相关性较强，但受大词汇表影响，查询与文档向量过长，导致检索时间与空间效率面临严重挑战。

Method: 通过自适应策略协同优化模型稀疏性，包括可学习软Top-K、逐项阈值化以及FLOPs正则化，以提升查询和文档向量的稀疏度。

Result: 在MS MARCO与BEIR数据集上，基于Lion-SP模型的实验表明，该方案显著降低平均查询与文档长度，优于基线，并在保持高竞争力的相关性下实现更短检索时延与更低存储成本。

Conclusion: 该稀疏化协同优化方案可在维持高度竞争性相关性的同时大幅提升神经稀疏检索的效率。

Abstract: Recent work on neural sparse retrieval has demonstrated strong relevance by leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic term expansion. However, learned models paired with previous sparsification techniques still yield overly long document and query vectors partly due to a large LLM vocabulary, imposing a serious challenge to retrieval time and space efficiency. This paper proposes a scheme for optimizing model sparsity through a synergy of adaptive strategies, including learnable soft top-K, per-term thresholding, and FLOPs regularization to increase the sparsity of query and document vectors. Experimental results with Lion-SP model on the MS MARCO and BEIR datasets demonstrate that the proposed scheme can outperform the baselines by significantly reducing the average query and document lengths. Our scheme can achieve much shorter retrieval latency and lower storage cost while maintaining highly competitive relevance.

</details>


### [324] [When History Misleads: Asymmetric Margin Supervision for Instruction-Guided LLM Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02600)
*Ming Yin,Yuhan Yang,Chen Chen,Xinyu Lin,Wentao Shi,Fangcong Yin,Chaofei Yang,Chao Yang,Jiyan Yang,Hui Zhang,Ning Jiang,Yiran Chen,Qifan Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 AIMS（非对称干预引导的间隔监督），将删除单条历史事件对推荐排序的影响转化为监督信号，用于指令引导的生成式推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在指令引导的生成式推荐中，LLM 推荐器需同时满足用户当前请求与历史偏好；两者冲突时历史事件可能压过请求。作者希望将单条历史事件的影响转化为监督，但面临两个障碍：(1) 影响最大的历史事件未必是支持目标物品的事件；(2) 删除误导性事件虽能提升目标物品得分，却可能使竞争物品得分提升更多，因此目标得分升高并不代表排序更优。

Method: 提出 AIMS，将删除单条历史事件的效果转化为排序监督。对已排序正确的训练请求，用冻结参考模型找出"针对该请求"的删除操作，使目标物品得分及其相对截止线附近竞争物品的间隔同时提升。这些间隔作为训练目标，而输入仍保留完整历史。训练结合交叉熵与一种非对称辅助损失：惩罚间隔不足，且梯度仅通过竞争物品得分回传。推理阶段不变，无需历史编辑或删除搜索。

Result: 在工业数据集与两个公开基准上、覆盖六种 LLM 骨干，AIMS 在 Recall 与 NDCG 上均优于强基线。消融实验支持请求特定间隔与非对称监督的有效性，且所选删除优先移除违反约束的历史事件。

Conclusion: 将历史事件删除的排序效应（而非仅目标得分）作为监督信号，可有效缓解历史与请求冲突问题；非对称、仅经竞争物品回传梯度的辅助损失是关键。方法不改变推理流程、无需删除搜索，具备较好的实用性与泛化性。

Abstract: In instruction-guided generative recommendation, LLM-based recommenders need to balance two goals: responding to the user's current request and aligning with the preferences in their interaction history. When the two conflict, history events can override the request. We show that turning the effect of individual history events into supervision faces two obstacles. First, the events that most influence a recommendation are not necessarily the ones that support the target item. Second, removing a misleading event can raise the target's score but a competing item's score even more, so a higher target score alone does not guarantee a better ranking. We propose Asymmetric Intervention-Guided Margin Supervision (AIMS), which converts the effect of removing individual history events into ranking supervision. For training requests already ranked correctly, a frozen reference model identifies request-specific deletions that improve both the target's score and its margin over a competitor near the recommendation cutoff. These margins serve as training targets, while the complete history is retained as input. Training combines cross-entropy with an asymmetric auxiliary loss that penalizes margin shortfalls and routes its gradient only through the competitor score. Inference is unchanged, requiring no history editing or deletion search. Across six LLM backbones on an industrial dataset and two public benchmarks, AIMS improves Recall and NDCG over strong baselines. Ablations support request-specific margins and asymmetric supervision, and the selected deletions preferentially remove constraint-violating history.

</details>


### [325] [Learning Query Encoders Can Be Hard Even When Vector Retrieval Is Geometrically Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02749)
*Anders Wikum,Nina Mishra,Amin Saberi,Tal Wagner*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"冻结文档索引下的最大召回率"这一新的几何容量概念，发现现有查询编码器远未达到该上限，并从理论上证明学习查询编码器可能是计算困难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效向量检索既依赖支持向量相似度检索的语料几何结构，也依赖能将查询嵌入到目标文档附近的查询编码器。已有工作从"实现所有top-k答案集所需最小嵌入维度"的角度研究几何容量，但作者希望从另一个角度——冻结文档索引可达到的最大召回率——来理解几何容量，并探究学习到的查询编码器能否触及这一天花板。实证观察显示单向量查询编码器的检索质量远低于文档索引本身能支持的水平，这促使作者研究其可学习性。

Method: 作者在多个真实检索基准上测量单向量查询编码器的实际召回率与冻结文档索引几何容量之间的差距。进而在理论上构造一个检索任务：(1)存在一个由小型单隐层ReLU网络表示的具有完美召回率的查询编码器；(2)任何统计查询（SQ）学习器都需要指数级数量的统计查询才能获得相对随机基线k/n的非平凡召回优势。

Result: 在多个真实世界检索基准上，单向量查询编码器的检索质量往往远低于文档索引所能支持的召回上限。理论结果表明，虽然存在紧凑的完美查询编码器，但SQ学习框架下的学习器需要指数级统计查询才能实现非平凡召回优势。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明检索基准中存在大量未被实现的几何容量，并将查询编码器的可学习性确立为基于嵌入检索的一个潜在瓶颈。

Abstract: Efficient vector retrieval requires both a corpus geometry that supports retrieving the right documents through vector similarity, and a query encoder that can embed queries near their desired documents in the embedding space. Recent work has studied geometric capacity through the lens of the minimum embedding dimension needed to realize all top-$k$ answer sets of $n$ documents. We study a different notion of geometric capacity--the maximum recall achievable for a frozen document index--and explore whether learned query encoders can reach this ceiling. On several real-world retrieval benchmarks, we show that retrieval quality of single-vector query encoders often lies far below what the document indices can support.
  Motivated by this observation, we give theoretical evidence that learning query encoders can be computationally hard. In particular, we construct a retrieval task that (1) admits a query encoder with perfect recall which is representable by a small one-hidden-layer ReLU network, but (2) any statistical-query learner (a class capturing learners that access training data through aggregate statistics) provably requires exponentially many statistical queries to achieve non-trivial recall advantage over the random baseline $k/n$. Taken together, our results suggest substantial unrealized geometric capacity in retrieval benchmarks and establish query encoder learnability as a possible barrier in embedding-based retrieval.

</details>


### [326] [Benchmarking Literature Retrieval for a Model Organism: A Dictyostelium Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03130)
*Yun Wang,Gad Shaulsky,Tomaž Curk,Blaž Zupan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文基于dictyBase构建了针对Dictyostelium（盘基网柄菌）这一模式生物的文献检索基准，包含策展人撰写的生物查询、对应的PubMed文章及结构化基因注释，并据此研究交叉编码器重排序、基因感知查询扩展以及仅摘要与全文检索三个因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物文献检索系统多基于宽泛的生物医学语料库和通用检索任务进行开发与评估；然而许多策展型知识库运行在更狭窄的模式生物领域，文献稀疏且术语具有物种特异性，缺乏针对性的检索基准。

Method: 从dictyBase构建文献检索基准，由策展人生成的生物查询与PubMed索引文章及结构化基因注释相关联；在该基准上评估三个因素：交叉编码器重排序、基因感知查询扩展、仅摘要与全文检索的对比。

Result: 重排序与基因感知查询扩展对检索有选择性提升：当模型适合生物证据匹配时，重排序最有效；策展注释有助于澄清简短生物查询、减少词汇不匹配。全文分块在摘要缺失支持证据时显著提升检索效果，同时提高候选召回与顶位排序表现，但此类查询难度高于有摘要支持的查询。

Conclusion: 针对模式生物等狭窄领域的检索需要专门基准与策略：结合重排序、基因感知查询扩展和全文检索可有效应对文献稀疏、术语特异及摘要证据不足的挑战；数据与代码已公开并提供基准数据集存档。

Abstract: Biological literature retrieval systems are often developed and evaluated using broad biomedical corpora and general-purpose search tasks. However, many curated knowledge bases operate in narrower model-organism domains, where the literature is sparse and terminology is organism-specific. We introduce a retrieval benchmark from dictyBase for Dictyostelium, a model organism in cell and developmental biology. The benchmark consists of curator-generated biological queries linked to PubMed-indexed articles, together with structured gene annotations. Using this benchmark, we study three factors in niche biological retrieval: cross-encoder reranking, gene-aware query expansion, and abstract-only versus full-text retrieval. We report that reranking and gene-aware query expansion improve retrieval selectively: reranking is most useful when the model is well suited to biological evidence matching, whereas curated annotations help clarify compact biological queries by reducing vocabulary mismatch. Full-text chunks substantially improve retrieval when abstracts omit supporting evidence, increasing both candidate recall and top-rank performance, although these cases are harder than queries supported by abstracts. Data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/fulaibaowang/dictycite, and the benchmark dataset is additionally archived on Zenodo.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [327] [SimuVerity: Benchmarking Agents for Engineering-Grade Simulink Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02304)
*Ruiqi Zhang,Jiahao Wang,Mingxuan Li,Haichen Luo,Chaoting Wang,Guoyu Mou,Keyu Lai,Hanchao Lv,Jiaxu Wang,Yibo Zheng,Aijun Yang,Xiaohua Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SimuVerity基准，包含跨十个工程领域的101个文本到可执行Simulink模型生成任务，采用分层评估器检验工程合格性并做六维打分；六个智能体系统中最高分仅42.86，表明结构相似性无法代表工程性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Simulink基准主要评估生成模型能否编译、执行或是否与参考模型相似，这些标准无法判定模型是否满足工程需求，因此需要一个能系统评估工程能力与诊断失败的基准。

Method: 构建SimuVerity基准：覆盖十个工程领域的101个文本到可执行Simulink模型生成任务；每个任务用可执行系统画像锚定工程规格与四类原生仿真场景；提出分层评估器，先检查交付产物、原生可执行性和工程合格性，再对合格模型按六个维度打分（准确性、输出质量、机理保真度、控制与因果完整性、工况鲁棒性、动态响应）；并用其对六个智能体系统进行评估。

Result: 最佳系统总体得分仅42.86；结构相似性对工程性能的代理性很差；能力瓶颈既出现在生成合格实现阶段，也出现在合格后满足多维需求阶段；部分高分模型仍存在严重的视觉布局混乱。

Conclusion: SimuVerity为评估智能体工程能力、诊断可执行Simulink模型生成中的失败提供了系统化依据，并揭示结构相似度指标不足以衡量工程性能。

Abstract: Existing Simulink benchmarks mainly evaluate whether generated models compile, execute, or resemble a reference model. These criteria do not establish whether a model satisfies its engineering requirements. We introduce SimuVerity, a benchmark of 101 text-to-executable Simulink model-generation tasks across ten engineering domains. For each task, executable-system profiles ground the engineering specification and four families of native simulation scenarios. A hierarchical evaluator first checks artifact delivery, native executability, and engineering qualification, then scores qualified models across six dimensions covering accuracy, output quality, mechanistic fidelity, control and causal integrity, operating-domain robustness, and dynamic response. We evaluate six agent systems with SimuVerity. The best system achieves an overall score of only 42.86. The results show that structural similarity is a poor proxy for engineering performance: capability bottlenecks arise both in producing qualified implementations and in satisfying multidimensional requirements after qualification. Meanwhile, some high-scoring models still exhibit severe visual-layout disorder. SimuVerity provides a systematic basis for assessing agents' engineering capabilities and diagnosing failures in executable Simulink model generation.

</details>


### [328] [Compound AI System Reliability: A Failure Taxonomy and Resilience Pattern Catalog from 150 Production Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02503)
*Rudrendu Kumar Paul,Sourav Nandy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过分析150个复合AI系统的生产事故报告，构建了23种失效模式的分类体系，并提出经故障注入实验验证的韧性模式，显著降低级联传播、静默退化和故障影响范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合AI系统的可靠安全部署需要理解在组件边界处涌现的失效模式，而非单个模型内部的问题。级联错误跨组件边界传播、静默质量退化逃避标准监控、协调失效导致个体正确但集体错误的行为，这些都构成了现有监控难以应对的挑战。

Method: 分析150份来自开源复合AI项目及匿名企业部署的生产事故报告，构建包含五大类（检索失效、生成失效、工具失效、编排失效、集成失效）共23种失效模式的分类体系；针对每类提出韧性模式，并通过受控故障注入实验测量其有效性。

Result: 断路器将级联传播减少89%；输出质量门在用户受影响前捕获73%的静默退化；组件隔离将影响范围减少64%；实施三个及以上韧性模式的系统相比无结构化监控基线将平均恢复时间（MTTR）降低71%。

Conclusion: 组件边界处的失效是复合AI系统可靠性的核心问题，所提出的失效分类体系与韧性模式目录可作为实践者资源发布，多模式组合应用能显著提升系统韧性。

Abstract: Deploying compound AI systems reliably and safely requires understanding failure modes that emerge at component boundaries, not within individual models. Cascading errors propagate across component boundaries, silent quality degradation evades standard monitoring, and coordination failures yield incorrect collective behavior from individually correct parts. We analyze 150 production incident reports from open-source compound AI projects and anonymized enterprise deployments to construct a taxonomy of 23 failure modes organized into five categories: retrieval failures, generation failures, tool failures, orchestration failures, and integration failures. For each category, we propose resilience patterns with measured effectiveness from controlled fault injection experiments. Circuit breakers reduce cascade propagation by 89%, output quality gates catch 73% of silent degradation before user impact, and component isolation reduces blast radius by 64%. Systems implementing three or more resilience patterns from our catalog reduce mean-time-to-recovery (MTTR) by 71% compared to unstructured monitoring baselines. We release the incident taxonomy and pattern catalog as a practitioner resource.

</details>


### [329] [An Extensive Empirical Study on Evaluation Metrics for Combinatorial Interaction Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02560)
*Lisha Qin,Chenhui Cui,Tao Li,Rubing Huang,Shikai Guo,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了组合测试套件的黑盒评估指标，并通过8个项目、32个场景、295,624个测试套件的实证研究，分析各静态评估指标与故障检测有效性的相关性，给出了指标选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估指标是组合交互测试(CIT)过程中的基础组成部分，用于评估和比较组合测试套件、评价各类测试生成技术。然而可用指标种类繁多，从业者难以选择合适的评估指标，且此前没有工作系统性地解决该问题。

Method: 首先对组合测试套件的黑盒评估指标进行综述，给出严格定义、清晰分类、示例说明与复杂度分析；随后开展实证研究，涉及8个开源项目、32个测试场景和295,624个组合测试套件，使用两种相关性度量考察各静态评估指标与故障检测有效性之间的相关性。

Result: 实验结果表明，值组合覆盖(VCC)指标可作为测试套件评估的有效预测指标；基于分布的指标通常比基于交互覆盖的指标计算成本更低；指标选择还应考虑测试套件自身特性，因为这些特性会显著影响评估的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了组合测试套件评估指标缺乏系统性研究的空白，提供了实践指南，帮助CIT从业者为评估或比较组合测试套件选择合适的评估指标。

Abstract: Combinatorial interaction testing (CIT) is a black-box testing method that has received extensive attention in both research and practice over recent years. Its primary objective is to construct an effective combinatorial test suite that detects software failures caused by parameter interactions. As a fundamental component of the CIT testing process, the evaluation metric plays a critical role in assessing and comparing combinatorial test suites, as well as in evaluating various test generation techniques. For CIT practitioners, selecting an appropriate evaluation metric is both important and challenging, given the wide variety of available options. Nevertheless, no prior work has systematically addressed this problem. To fill this gap, this paper first provides a comprehensive survey of black-box evaluation metrics for combinatorial test suites, offering rigorous definitions, clear classifications, illustrative examples, and complexity analyses. We then conduct an extensive empirical study involving eight open-source projects, encompassing 32 test scenarios and 295,624 combinatorial test suites. In this study, we examine the correlation between each static evaluation metric and fault-detection effectiveness using two correlation measures. Experimental results show that the Value Combination Coverage (VCC) metric serves as a valid predictor for test-suite evaluation. However, distribution-based metrics generally incur lower computational costs than interaction coverage-based ones. The choice of an appropriate metric should also account for the test suite's inherent properties, as these characteristics can substantially influence the effectiveness of the evaluation. Finally, we provide practical guidelines to assist CIT practitioners in selecting suitable evaluation metrics for assessing or comparing combinatorial test suites.

</details>


### [330] [Improving the Energy-Efficiency of the Code Generated by LLMs through Effective Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02571)
*Ritika Rekhi,Bing Zhang,Md Arman Islam,Jaya Krishna Pasham,Yeswanth Chitturi,Akshay Paramesha,Isha Valiveti,Asif Imran,Bekir Turkkan,Tevfik Kosar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了21种提示策略对LLM生成代码能效的影响，筛选出8种策略并在10个开源与闭源模型上验证。结果显示，提示策略可使Python代码能耗最多降低25%、C++最多降低17%，模型层面Python降低幅度可达50%、C++可达56%，表明提示工程可显著影响生成代码的能耗，但效果因模型和语言而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI辅助编程日益普及，AI生成软件的环境影响成为重要议题，仅评估功能正确性已不足。然而，尽管代码生成取得显著进展，前沿LLM很少从其生成代码的能效角度进行评估，这促使作者开展能耗导向的评估研究。

Method: 作者首先对21种面向节能代码生成的提示策略进行综合评估，筛选出8种策略；随后在10个广泛使用的开源权重与专有LLM上，针对Python和C++代码生成，以基线提示为参照评估这些策略的有效性。

Result: 所选提示策略在Python上实现最多25%的能耗降低，在C++上最多17%。模型层面，Python能耗降低最高达50%（Granite-4.0-H-Small）、39%（Claude 4.5 Haiku）、28%（MiniMax M3）；C++降低最高达56%（Granite-4.0-H-Small）和7%（Qwen3-Coder-480B-A35B-Instruct）。

Conclusion: 提示策略能显著影响LLM生成代码的能耗，但其有效性因模型和编程语言而异。研究强调了将能效纳入LLM代码生成评估与优化的重要性，并为设计更可持续的AI辅助编程提示提供了实践启示。

Abstract: As AI-assisted programming becomes increasingly mainstream, the environmental impact of AI-generated software has emerged as an important consideration. This motivates evaluating LLM-generated code beyond functional correctness by considering execution efficiency and energy consumption. However, despite substantial advances in code generation, frontier LLMs are rarely evaluated based on the energy efficiency of the code they produce. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 21 prompting strategies for energy-efficient code generation and identify 8 strategies for evaluation across 10 widely used open-weight and proprietary LLMs. We evaluate their effectiveness for both Python and C++ code generation relative to a baseline prompt. Across the evaluated models, the selected prompting strategies achieved energy reductions of up to 25% for Python and 17% for C++ code generation. At the model level, Python energy reductions reached up to 50% for Granite-4.0-H-Small, 39% for Claude 4.5 Haiku, and 28% for MiniMax M3, while C++ reductions reached up to 56% for Granite-4.0-H-Small and 7% for Qwen3-Coder-480B-A35B-Instruct. These results demonstrate that prompting strategies can substantially influence the energy consumption of LLM-generated code, although their effectiveness varies across models and programming languages. Our findings highlight the importance of incorporating energy efficiency into the evaluation and optimization of LLM-based code generation and provide practical insights into designing prompts for more sustainable AI-assisted programming.

</details>


### [331] [WebUIProof: Benchmarking WebUI Code Generators with UI-Agent Execution Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02617)
*Yun-Yun Tsai,Yuning Mao,Shiqi Wang,Junfeng Yang,Sinong Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WebUIProof：面向执行的WebUI生成基准，提供结构化规格与可执行交互测试，覆盖通用WebUI与3D交互仿真两类任务，并配套UI-agent测试框架及基于其奖励信号的RL训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WebUI代码生成评测存在两大缺陷：一是大规摸评测困难，生成结果可能通过编译且外观合理，但在实际用户交互下失效；二是既有基准多依赖自由形式提示与静态检查（构建成功、截图），无法衡量功能正确性。

Method: 提出WebUIProof执行导向基准：1）提供结构化规格说明与密集的可执行交互测试，覆盖通用WebUI（仪表盘、游戏、交互工具）与3D交互仿真（粒子/星系系统、物理动力学）两类任务；2）构建UI-agent测试框架，在无头浏览器中通过"规划—执行—观察"迭代循环运行交互测试，定位DOM元素、执行动作、观察UI/DOM变化并校验指定断言；3）利用该框架的输出层奖励信号对紧凑模型（Qwen2.5 14B、MIMO 7B）进行RL训练。

Result: 在八种商用LLM上评测，发现即使页面渲染成功，仍频繁出现交互类需求失败，3D仿真界面尤为明显；使用由可执行交互测试导出的RL奖励训练紧凑模型后，功能完成度提升，同时构建失败率下降。

Conclusion: 以执行为中心的交互测试能有效揭示静态检查遗漏的功能缺陷，且该UI-agent框架可作为可扩展的结果级奖励来源，用于改进WebUI生成模型的功能正确性。

Abstract: Evaluating WebUI code generation at scale is difficult: outputs may compile and look plausible yet fail under user interaction, and prior benchmarks largely rely on free-form prompts with static checks (build success, screenshots) that miss functional correctness. We introduce WebUIProof, an execution-oriented benchmark that provides structured specifications and dense, executable interaction tests for WebUI generation across two task families: general WebUIs (e.g., dashboards, game, interactive tools) and 3D interactive simulation (e.g., particle/galaxy systems, physics dynamics). WebUIProof includes a UI-agent harness that runs executable interaction tests in a headless browser using an iterative plan--act--observe loop: it locates DOM elements, performs actions, observes resulting UI/DOM changes, and checks the specified assertions. We evaluate across eight commercial LLMs and observe frequent failures on interaction-based requirements even when pages render successfully, especially on 3D simulation interfaces. Finally, we show the UI-agent harness can provide outcome-level training signals. Training compact models (e.g., Qwen2.5 14B and MIMO 7B) with RL rewards derived from executable interaction tests improves functional completion while reducing build failures.

</details>


### [332] [Self-Supervised Scaling of Terminal Environments for Scientific Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02710)
*Zhongzhi Li,Yucheng Shi,Zongxia Li,Junyao Yang,Ruhan Wang,Yu Wang,Jingyuan Huang,Jichao Yu,Ninghao Liu,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"software-in-the-loop reconstruction (SWR)"自监督框架：通过执行现有软件工作流、划分公开观测与隐藏评测，让智能体根据指令、输入模式和公开输入-输出样本重建可编辑程序，并用分层验证器（语义比较、结构合法性、反捷径检查）在隐藏配置上评估，从而无需为每个任务人工编写参考解即可获得可扩展、行为可验证的监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体的应用已从软件工程扩展到科学与专业领域，但构建训练环境需要可执行的参考行为与领域特定的验证器，以区分语义正确与表面合理的产物；逐任务编写这些组件需要重复工程且限制复用。

Method: 核心为 software-in-the-loop reconstruction 自监督框架：将现有可执行工作流（结构化输入→输出）作为监督来源；对每个工作流执行多种输入配置，划分为 public observations 与 hidden evaluations；智能体仅获得指令、输入模式与公开输入-输出观测，在无法访问源工作流的情况下构建可编辑程序；候选程序在隐藏配置上对照工作流输出评测；采用分层验证器组合领域语义比较、结构有效性与 anti-shortcut 检查，公开反馈支持迭代修订；框架可扩展至新工作流与配置而无需为每任务编写参考解。实例化含 500 个工作流、6 个领域、46 个软件族。

Result: 每任务三次尝试下，Qwen3.8-Max 解决 838 个任务并产生 1,422 条已验证轨迹，过采样为 3,000 条 reconstruction-only 训练样本。对 Qwen3.8-27B 进行监督微调，使 Terminal-Bench 2 平均性能从 47.94% 提升至 53.56%（三个随机种子）；在全部四项报告评测中，于四个等 token 语料对照中取得最高均值。

Conclusion: 结果表明，现有科学软件可为终端智能体提供可扩展且经行为验证的监督信号。

Abstract: Terminal agents are increasingly deployed beyond software engineering in science and other specialized domains. Constructing training environments requires executable reference behavior and a domain-specific verifier that distinguishes semantic correctness from superficially plausible artifacts. Authoring these components for each task requires repeated engineering and limits reuse. We introduce software-in-the-loop reconstruction, a self-supervised framework that obtains reference outputs and verification targets from existing software workflows, executable programs mapping structured inputs to outputs. For each workflow, we execute multiple input configurations and partition cases into public observations and hidden evaluations. Given the instruction, input schema, and public input--output observations, an agent constructs an editable program without access to the source workflow. The candidate is evaluated on hidden configurations against workflow outputs. A hierarchical verifier combines domain-specific semantic comparison, structural validity, and anti-shortcut checks, while public feedback supports iterative revision. The construction admits additional workflows and configurations without authoring a reference solution for each task. We instantiate SWR with 500 workflows and 46 software families across six domains. Across three attempts per task, Qwen3.8-Max solves 838 tasks and produces 1,422 verified trajectories, which we oversample to 3,000 reconstruction-only training examples. Supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.8-27B improves mean Terminal-Bench 2 performance from 47.94% to 53.56% across three seeds and achieves the highest mean among four matched-token corpus controls on all four reported evaluations. These results indicate that existing scientific software can provide scalable, behaviorally verified supervision for terminal agents.

</details>


### [333] [VeriPy Source-Preserving Verification and Compatibility Checking for Python Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02814)
*Naing Oo Lwin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VeriPy 提出一种统一工作流，让带注解的 Python 组件在源码层面同时进行形式化验证与向后兼容性检查，并通过编码器生成 Dafny/Lean 伪迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python 程序与其形式化保证之间存在持续的维护难题：规范须描述实际运行的实现，而更新又须保持既有调用者依赖的行为。作者希望把这两项义务整合进同一工作流。

Method: 开发者与智能体以 Python 注释表达契约、不变式与证明钩子，并借助源码定位诊断细化被检查的辅助引理；直接编码器在保留可执行体（admitted executable bodies）及其依赖的显式模型的前提下，生成 Dafny 或 Lean 产物；再用关系型乘积（relational product）检查更新后的组件是否仍接受旧输入，并保持返回值与建模异常。

Result: 该工作流将源码级证明开发与功能验证、向后兼容性连接起来，并记录每项保证所依赖的假设；代码已开源于给定仓库。

Conclusion: VeriPy 为带注解 Python 组件提供了一条把证明开发、功能验证与兼容性检查统一起来的可维护路径。

Abstract: Keeping a Python program and its formal guarantees aligned is a continuing maintenance problem. Specifications must describe the implementation that actually runs, and updates must preserve the behavior on which existing callers depend. VeriPy brings these obligations into a common workflow for annotated Python components. Developers and agents express contracts, invariants, and proof hooks as Python comments, then refine checked auxiliary lemmas using source-located diagnostics. Direct encoders produce Dafny or Lean artifacts while retaining admitted executable bodies and explicit models of their dependencies. A relational product checks whether an updated component still admits old inputs and preserves returned values and modeled exceptions. The resulting workflow connects source-level proof development with functional verification and backward compatibility, recording the assumptions under which each guarantee applies. The code is available at https://github.com/astrio-labs/veripy.

</details>


### [334] [Discriminating Fixture Coverage in Agent-Infrastructure Verification Suites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02928)
*Xin Xu,Siru Tao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文用变异分析衡量"不变式套件通过一个正确实现、失败一个错误实现"这一单一观测证据的价值：修复后的十二项检查套件在十个对抗性构造的变异体上仅杀死五个，五个存活变异体失败原因分为"从未被激活"和"内部状态损坏无法被任何预言机观测"两类，通过预先登记七个判别维度并补充夹具后五个全部被杀死。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不变式套件与运行时监视器日益成为智能体部署决策的门槛，但支撑某一套件的证据几乎总是单一的通过/失败观测。作者希望量化这种观测究竟值多少，并检验标准验证流程能否真正证明套件的有效性。

Method: 对多会话智能体状态投影层的不变式套件应用变异分析。先发现标准验证给一个存在"移除事件身份去重的一阶变异体仍能通过全部检查"的套件颁发了合格证明。随后冻结修复后的十二项检查套件、记录其哈希，并对由未参与夹具设计的对抗性读者指定的十个变异体运行一次，得到杀死五个的结果。再对五个存活者进行插桩，识别失败模式；将缺失输入视为覆盖问题，枚举输入空间的七个判别维度，预先登记哪些维度未覆盖及应解释哪些存活者，每个未覆盖维度补一个夹具并原样复用既有预言机。

Result: 标准验证会认证一个可被移除事件身份去重的一阶变异体穿透的套件。冻结的十二项检查套件在十个对抗性变异体上杀死五个。五个存活者失败模式分为两类而非一类：三个因无夹具提供变异代码行为不同的输入而从未被激活；两个损坏了套件中任何预言机都无法观测的内部状态。两种模式需要不同修复，且都无从通过通过/失败报告看出。按预登记的七个维度补夹具后，五个存活者全部死亡，且各自死在其预测维度对应的检查上。

Conclusion: 单一通过/失败观测不足以证明不变式套件的有效性，套件验证需要更严格的变异分析。失败（存活）模式具有异质性，需区分"未激活"与"预言机盲区"并分别修复。作者将其报告为同一挑战集上的修复结果而非第二次留出估计，并公开包含冻结哈希、登记预测、全部变异体与运行日志的工件，使这一区分可被核查。

Abstract: Invariant suites and runtime monitors increasingly gate agent deployment decisions, and the evidence offered for any particular suite is almost always a single observation: it passes an implementation believed correct and fails one believed broken. We measure what that observation is worth. Applying mutation analysis to an invariant suite for a multi-session agent state-projection layer, we first find that this standard validation certifies a suite in which a first-order mutant removing event-identity deduplication survives every check. We then freeze the repaired twelve-check suite, record its hash, and run it once against ten mutants specified by an adversarial reader who designed none of its fixtures: it kills five. Instrumenting the five survivors against the reference shows they fail in two distinct ways, not one. Three are never activated, because no fixture supplies an input on which the mutated code behaves differently at all. The other two corrupt internal state that no oracle in the suite can observe. The two modes need different repairs, and neither is visible from a pass/fail report. Treating the missing inputs as a coverage question, we enumerate seven discriminating dimensions of the input space, register in advance which are uncovered and which survivors they should explain, and add one fixture per uncovered dimension while reusing the existing oracles verbatim. All five survivors then die, each to the check written for its predicted dimension. We report this as a repair result on the same challenge set rather than a second held-out estimate, and give the artifact, including the frozen hash, the registered predictions, all mutants and the run logs, so the distinction is checkable.

</details>


### [335] [GTDD: Generative Test-Driven Development for AI Coding Agents with Adversarial Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02952)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出生成式测试驱动开发（GTDD）：测试代理在每轮候选实现固定后基于行为契约与历史反馈生成新输入，可信评估器校验并返回缩减后的反例用于回归测试，从而持续暴露初始用例之外的失败。该过程通过有限总体分析刻画自适应候选选择下的错误接受边界，并在有状态键值存储配对实验中验证其降低平均失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TDD为AI编程代理提供可执行需求，但代理会针对已观察到的示例调整实现，因此仅通过一组预定测试可能仍遗留大量预期行为未实现（即"通过测试但不满足意图"）。需要一种机制持续生成超越初始示例的新测试，以约束自适应候选选择带来的错误接受风险。

Method: GTDD将TDD重构为含独立测试代理的迭代流程：人类指定行为契约；每轮候选实现固定后，测试代理生成新输入；可信评估器检查这些输入，向编码代理返回缩减后的反例，并保存为回归测试。理论上采用有限总体分析，在候选自适应选择情形下刻画错误接受，推导测试可见性与重复反馈对接受概率的界，并分析候选确定后随机审计的作用。实证上在有状态键值存储任务中开展配对实验，比较"开发中重新生成测试"的策略、"同模型一次性生成测试"的策略，以及是否向测试者提供候选源码，并控制测试数量。

Result: 两种在开发过程中重新生成测试的策略，其最终平均失败率均低于同模型一次性生成测试的策略；向测试者提供候选源码未带来可检测的额外改进。在要求测试数量相等的进一步条件下，未能将策略差异归因于任一单一特征。理论界表明，候选确定后的新鲜随机审计可跨开发轮次控制错误接受。

Conclusion: GTDD将自适应开发反馈与既有回归测试及独立接受规则相结合，可在有限总体意义下量化并控制自适应候选选择下的错误接受，并在实验中降低平均失败率；但具体致效机制尚不清晰，仍需进一步消融以识别各设计要素的独立贡献。

Abstract: Test-driven development gives AI coding agents executable requirements for implementing software. Because these agents can adapt their implementations to the examples they observe, passing a predetermined collection of tests can leave substantial parts of the intended behavior unimplemented. We propose Generative Test-Driven Development (GTDD), a formulation of test-driven development in which a separate testing agent generates new inputs after each candidate implementation is fixed, using a human-specified behavioral contract and the feedback from earlier rounds. A trusted evaluator checks these inputs, returns reduced counterexamples to the coding agent, and saves them for regression testing, so development continually confronts failures beyond the initial examples. We characterize the evidence that this process provides through a finite-population analysis of false acceptance under adaptive candidate selection. The resulting bounds quantify how test visibility and repeated feedback affect acceptance, and show that fresh random audits after candidate commitment control false acceptance across development rounds. In a paired experiment on a stateful key-value store, both policies that regenerated tests during development ended with lower mean failure rates than the policy whose tests were generated once by the same language model, and giving the tester the candidate's source produced no detectable additional improvement. Further conditions requesting equal numbers of tests did not isolate any single feature of the policies as the source of this difference. GTDD combines this adaptive development feedback with established regression tests and an independent acceptance rule.

</details>


### [336] [BISCEPTER: Probability-Driven Bisection for Large-Scale System Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02995)
*Mingyan Gao,Celine Wüst,Zuming Jiang,Zhendong Su*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文发现真实BIC具有强烈时间偏斜（50%的BIC集中在最近0.69%的提交历史内），据此提出概率驱动的BISCEPTER二分法，用历史BIC延迟作为先验选择加权中位数枢轴，平均减少25.75%的二分迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定位bug诱导提交（BIC）是回归调试与BIC感知故障定位的基础步骤，实践中常用二分法获取。标准二分法选择剩余good-bad区间的中位数提交，以提交数量均分为目标，这在"每个提交等概率为BIC"的假设下最优。但论文指出该假设与真实BIC历史不符，存在可利用的时间偏斜。

Method: 1) 构建来自GCC、Linux内核和MariaDB的8,172个bug报告数据集，统计BIC时间分布；2) 提出BISCEPTER：概率驱动的二分法，将历史BIC延迟作为轻量先验，选择切分"估计BIC概率质量"的加权中位数枢轴，而非切分剩余提交数量；3) 保持与标准二分法相同的good-bad oracle与接口；4) 在三个大规模系统上评估，并进行噪声历史数据下的鲁棒性实验。

Result: 发现强烈时间偏斜：三个系统中50%的BIC位于报告时间提交历史最近0.69%范围内。BISCEPTER相比标准中位数二分法平均减少25.75%的二分迭代（最高55.55%），在91.26%的测试用例中优于基线；鲁棒性实验表明在噪声历史数据下收益保持稳定。

Conclusion: BIC分布并非均匀，标准二分法的等概率假设不成立；利用历史BIC延迟先验的概率驱动二分法可显著降低二分迭代次数，从而节省实际调试成本，并为未来软件工程研究提供BIC分布方面的洞见。

Abstract: Identifying the bug-inducing commit (BIC) is a fundamental step in regression debugging and a key input to emerging BIC-aware fault-localization pipelines. In practice, BICs are commonly obtained with bisection. Standard bisection selects the median commit of the remaining good-bad interval, thereby balancing commit count. This strategy is optimal under the assumption that each commit is equally likely to be the BIC.
  This paper shows that this assumption does not match real-world BIC histories. We construct a dataset of 8,172 bug reports from GCC, the Linux kernel, and MariaDB. We find a strong temporal skew: across the studied systems, 50% of BICs lie within the most recent 0.69% of the report-time commit history. Motivated by this observation, we introduce BISCEPTER, a probability-driven bisection approach that uses historical BIC latency as a lightweight prior. Instead of selecting pivots that split the number of remaining commits, BISCEPTER selects weighted-median pivots that split estimated BIC probability mass, while preserving the same good-bad oracle and interface as standard bisection. We evaluate BISCEPTER on three large-scale systems. The evaluation results show that BISCEPTER reduces bisection iterations by 25.75% on average (up to 55.55%) compared with standard median bisection, while improving over the baseline in 91.26% of test cases. Robustness experiments further demonstrate that the benefit remains stable under noisy historical data. We expect that our research can effectively save effort in debugging software in practice and, more broadly, benefit future software engineering research by bringing insights about BIC distribution.

</details>


### [337] [Engineering Sustainable Agents: A Systematic Comparison of Agentic LLMs for Developer Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03010)
*Merve Astekin,Yan Naing Tun,Arda Goknil,Erik Johannes Husom,Lwin Khin Shar,Hasan Sözer,Ratnadira Widyasari,Hui Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对五种软件工程任务中的智能体式LLM系统进行了大规模实证研究，发现多智能体设计平均能耗为非智能体基线的6.36倍、运行时间为6.07倍，最坏情况达160倍，而精度提升有限且任务特定；66个帕累托最优配置中有59个由轻量级非智能体或单智能体配置占据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM（尤其是协调多智能体的智能体式系统）在软件工程中应用日益广泛，但带来更高的计算与环境成本，而智能体复杂度与能效之间的权衡缺乏系统性实证证据。

Method: 针对代码生成、技术债识别、代码漏洞检测、日志解析与日志分析五项任务，构建从非智能体单查询基线到多智能体工作流的多种LLM配置进行对比；采用六种开源权重LLM、两种提示策略和三种硬件平台，并从准确率、推理延迟和能耗三个维度评估各配置。

Result: 多智能体设计平均能耗为非智能体基线的6.36倍、运行时间为6.07倍，单个任务—硬件组合最坏减速达160倍；额外智能体带来的精度增益有限且任务特定（多智能体提升漏洞检测平均准确率）；轻量级非智能体和单智能体配置仍主导帕累托前沿，在66个帕累托最优配置中占59个；模型与提示选择是任务特定的调节杠杆，其有效方向因任务而异，而非全局默认。

Conclusion: 智能体复杂度与能效之间存在显著权衡，应据此制定面向可持续、任务感知的LLM软件开发工具的设计指南。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in software engineering, including agentic systems that coordinate multiple agents, but impose higher computational and environmental costs. In this paper, we present a comprehensive empirical study of agentic LLM systems across five software engineering tasks: code generation, technical debt identification, code vulnerability detection, log parsing, and log analysis. For each task, we compare LLM configurations that range from a non-agentic single-query baseline to multi-agent workflows, using six open-weight LLMs, two prompt strategies, and three hardware platforms. We assess each configuration in terms of accuracy, inference latency, and energy consumption. Our results reveal substantial trade-offs between agentic complexity and energy efficiency: multi-agent designs consume on average 6.36$\times$ as much energy and run 6.07$\times$ as long as the non-agentic baseline, with worst-case slowdowns of up to 160$\times$ for individual task--hardware pairs. Accuracy gains from additional agents are limited and task-specific: multi-agent improves average vulnerability-detection accuracy, but lightweight non-agentic and single-agent configurations still dominate the Pareto front, accounting for 59 of 66 Pareto-optimal configurations. Model and prompt choice act as task-specific levers whose effective direction varies between tasks rather than as global defaults. We translate these findings into design guidelines for sustainable, task-aware LLM-based development tools.

</details>


### [338] [MintEval: Do LLMs Implement the Trading Strategy You Asked For? A Behavioural-Equivalence Benchmark for Natural-Language-to-Strategy Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03080)
*Siyu Wang,Yifan Wang,Yuecheng He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MintEval 是一个面向"从交易者口头描述生成策略代码"这一任务的基准，通过程序化生成参考策略、反向翻译为口语化指令、要求被测模型重新实现，并在相同数据与摩擦下逐 bar 比较动作一致性（而非代码相似度或收益），揭示现有代码基准与金融基准都无法捕捉的"静默失败"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正从"产生交易信号"转向"编写执行信号的代码"，而后者的失效模式是静默的：代码能跑、回测能画图，但实际执行的风险逻辑并非交易者所描述的逻辑。现有代码基准只测单元测试上的功能正确性，金融基准只测预测能力，二者均未衡量"实现是否表现得像被要求的那个策略"。因此需要一个以"行为是否匹配所描述的策略"为核心的评测基准。

Method: 1) 从可组合构件库中程序化生成参考策略；2) 将其反向翻译为口语化的交易者指令；3) 由被测模型据此重新实现。评估时，把生成程序与参考程序在相同市场数据与摩擦下逐 bar 执行，比较二者的"动作"而非代码相似度或利润——从而把 alpha 差分掉。MintEval v0 包含 800 个任务，基于 BTCUSDT 15 分钟数据，按"执行测得的状态跨度复杂度 τ"分层，且 τ 与描述长度解耦。

Result: 低成本模型平均 ActionMatch 至多 0.544，完全复现的任务至多 0.087；在 200 个任务的分层子集上，前沿模型（Claude Opus 5.5）达到 0.889、完全复现 0.575，但仍在 0.275 的任务上静默失败。给定构件菜单时，模型几乎完美地识别出策略，然而在"规范被正确读懂"的实现中，79.2% 在超过 10% 的活跃 bar 上发生偏离。此外，将近期某策略生成基准所用的 LLM 评判器原样套用，会接受所有这些静默失败。

Conclusion: 仅靠"能否识别策略"或"代码是否跑通"不足以衡量策略代码生成的可靠性；真正的问题在于行为一致性。MintEval 以动作级比对暴露了普遍存在的静默失败，并证明常用 LLM 评判器对此完全失效，说明亟需以行为对齐为核心的评测范式。

Abstract: Large language models are moving from producing trading signals to writing the code that executes them. The failure mode of the second role is silent: generated code runs, a backtest plots, yet the risk logic that the trader described is not the logic being executed. Existing code benchmarks test functional correctness on unit tests and finance benchmarks test forecasting; neither measures whether an implementation behaves like the strategy that was asked for. We introduce MintEval, a benchmark in which reference strategies are generated programmatically from a library of composable building blocks, back-translated into colloquial trader instructions, and re-implemented by the model under test. Generated and reference programs are executed bar by bar on identical market data and frictions, and compared on their actions rather than on code similarity or profit: alpha is differenced away. MintEval v0 contains 800 tasks on BTCUSDT 15-minute data, stratified by an execution-measured state-span complexity tau that is decoupled from description length. Low-cost models reach a mean ActionMatch of at most 0.544 and reproduce at most 0.087 of tasks exactly; on a stratified subset of 200 tasks a frontier model (Claude Opus 5.5) reaches 0.889 and reproduces 0.575 exactly, yet still fails silently on 0.275 of tasks. Given a menu of building blocks, models identify the strategy almost perfectly, yet 79.2% of the implementations whose specification was read correctly diverge on more than 10% of active bars. The LLM judge of a recent strategy-generation benchmark, applied verbatim, accepts every one of these silent failures.

</details>


### [339] [Pinning Decisions Before Failure: Executable Records of Underspecified Choices in AI-Assisted Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03237)
*Takeharu Mitsui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出"决策钉"（decision pin）方法：先枚举自然语言需求中的欠指定点，再为每点构造两个仅在该点不同的临时实现，仅当二者在某个输入上产生分歧时才保留该输入，从而把"隐含选择"外化为可执行记录，用于约束生成与判定合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言需求本身留有未决之处，模型在实现时会默默替人做决定。对三个模型生成的600个测试套件中，42.7%完全不含能区分两种竞争性解读的测试；常规补救办法（实现前写验收示例）也无法奏效，因为一个被两种解读同时满足的示例并不能区分它们。

Method: 在"先写验收示例"的实践中插入两步：(1) 按名称枚举需求中的欠指定点；(2) 对每个点构造两个仅在该点不同的临时实现，仅当执行两者显示它们结果不一致时，才保留该候选输入。结果记为 decision pin（决策钉），包含三个要素：被命名的点、经确认的输入、以及人在两种展示结果中选择的取值。该记录随后既作为生成约束，也通过执行来判定合规。文中另有1个补充步骤：尝试回填决策钉，但很快放弃（该步骤未采用）。

Result: 在含配对参考实现、两个模型的40任务基准上：92.5%的决策点能获得区分性输入，85–90%能获得指明预期决策的钉。对703个独立生成的实现作核查，钉与基准分类一致率为96–97%，分歧仅出现在三个任务上，其中一个任务两个分类器都判错。作为生成约束，钉在全部210次生成中于被钉输入上均被遵守；在留出输入上，38个格子中不劣于散文式规则，但与陈述相同范围的散文相比并无更优。散文规则下的所有合规失败都源于模型自行决定规则范围：其中一例静默推翻了另一条已记录决策，经两模型上的留一法归因于单一规则，文本层面的调和未能发现，而重放所记录输入则捕获了它。该设定下此类冲突过少，无法评估回归步骤，文中说明了原因。

Conclusion: 通过"枚举欠指定点＋构造仅单点不同的实现对＋保留可区分输入"得到的决策钉，能把需求中的隐含决策转为可执行、可复现的约束，在分类一致性与生成约束上表现可靠；但其相对散文式规则的优势主要体现在固定被钉输入上，在留出输入上并不自动更强，且范围界定仍是模型自由裁量的失败根源，当前冲突样本量不足以支撑回归评估。

Abstract: A natural-language requirement leaves questions open, and a model asked to implement it settles them silently: across 600 generated test suites from three models, 42.7% contain no test that distinguishes the competing readings. An acceptance example written before implementing is the usual remedy, but an example both readings satisfy resolves nothing.
  We insert two steps into that practice: enumerate the requirement's underspecified points by name, then for each construct two throwaway implementations differing only in that point and keep a candidate input only if executing both shows they disagree. The result is recorded as a decision pin: the named point, the confirmed input, and the value the person chose between the two exhibited results. The same record then constrains generation and decides compliance by execution.
  On a benchmark of 40 tasks with paired reference implementations and two models, a separating input is obtained for 92.5% of decision points and a pin identifying the intended decision for 85-90%. As checks on 703 independently generated implementations, pins agree with the benchmark's classification on 96-97%, with disagreements on three tasks, one where both classifiers erred. As generation constraints, pins are honoured at the pinned input in all 210 generations and are never worse than a prose rule on held-out inputs in 38 cells, though no better than prose stating the same scope.
  Every compliance failure under a prose rule came from the model deciding the rule's scope itself; one such case silently overturned another recorded decision, was attributed to a single rule by leave-one-out on both models, and was missed by text-level reconciliation but caught by re-running the recorded input. The setting yields too few such conflicts to evaluate a regression step, and we say why.

</details>


### [340] [IntentCoding: Amplifying User Intent in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00066)
*Zheng Fang,Yihong Dong,Lili Mou,Dongming Jin,Zhi Jin,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出IntentCoding解码策略，通过掩码与多强度集成放大用户意图影响，无需训练即可提升LLM代码生成中的约束遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代码生成能力强，但在满足多条细粒度用户约束时表现不佳：约束数量增加会导致性能快速下降；用户意图虽影响logits，但不足以有效引导解码过程。

Method: IntentCoding：通过屏蔽（masking）用户意图来捕获其影响，并采用多强度集成机制在生成过程中放大用户意图的作用；模型无关、无需额外训练、可无缝集成现有解码流程。同时构建CodeConstraints基准，测试不同约束数量下的意图遵循。

Result: 在CodeConstraints、IFEvalCode、HumanEval和LiveCodeBench上，相比标准解码，约束满足度与功能正确性显著提升：CodeConstraints相对提升最高71.0%，IFEvalCode最高67.3%，HumanEval与LiveCodeBench的pass@1相比贪心解码最高提升29.3%。

Conclusion: IntentCoding能有效增强LLM对多约束用户意图的遵循，兼顾约束满足与代码正确性，具备通用性与实用价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in code generation, but their adherence to fine-grained user intent with multiple constraints remains a significant challenge. Our empirical analysis reveals two key observations: 1) Model performance deteriorates quickly as the number of constraints in the user intent increases, and 2) While user intent does influence the model's logits, such an influence may not be strong enough to effectively steer the decoding process. To this end, we propose Intent-Amplified Code Generation (IntentCoding), a novel decoding strategy that enhances an LLM's ability to follow user intent. IntentCoding captures the influence of user intent by masking out the intent, and applies a multi-strength ensemble mechanism to amplify the effect of user intent during generation. IntentCoding is model-agnostic, requires no additional training, and integrates seamlessly with existing decoding procedures. To enable systematic evaluation, we also construct CodeConstraints, a benchmark dataset specifically designed to test user intent compliance under varying numbers of constraints. Experiments on our constructed Constraints, as well as popular IFEvalCode, HumanEval and LiveCodeBench datasets, show that our IntentCoding model significantly improves both constraint satisfaction and functional correctness compared to standard decoding approaches. IntentCoding achieves up to 71.0% relative improvement on CodeConstraints, achieves up to 67.3% relative improvement on IFEvalCode and achieves up to 29.3% relative improvement in pass@1 on HumanEval and LiveCodeBench compared with greedy decoding.

</details>


### [341] [ClarifyCodeBench: Evaluating LLMs on Clarifying Ambiguous Requirements for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00711)
*Zheng Fang,Dongming Jin,Yihong dong,Yongmin Li,Kechi Zhang,Zhi Jin,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ClarifyCodeBench，一个评估大语言模型（LLM）解决需求歧义能力的交互式基准，并揭示LLM在需求澄清上存在的三大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已能充当编程助手，但代码生成效果受制于输入需求的质量，现实需求常模糊、不完整或欠规范。现有基准多假设提示词已完美指定，忽视了需求获取的迭代性与交互瓶颈，LLM主动澄清意图的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建ClarifyCodeBench交互式基准，取材真实编程任务，配备高质量人工标注，包括N种独特歧义类型、对应澄清问题及标准答案；形式化两个评估指标：Turn-discounted Key Question Rate（惩罚低效提问）与Optimal Round Adherence（衡量需求获取精度）；并对六个先进LLM进行系统评测。

Result: 实验得到三点关键发现：1）能力解耦——强代码生成性能并不必然带来有效需求澄清；2）推理悖论——增加计算思考提升代码正确性，但对识别歧义收益甚微；3）多歧义天花板——随歧义密度上升，澄清性能急剧下降，暴露处理复杂真实规格的显著瓶颈。

Conclusion: 研究强调未来AI4SE应从静态综合转向交互式需求获取，ClarifyCodeBench为推动该方向提供了基准与方法支撑。

Abstract: Large Language Models have emerged as programming assistants. However, the efficacy of code generation is constrained by the quality of input requirements, which are frequently ambiguous, incomplete, or underspecified.
